śrī: ||
atha saptamaṃ maṇḍalam ||
atha saptamaṃ maṇḍalamārabhyate |
tasmin maṇḍale ṣaḍanuvākāḥ |
prathame'nuvāke saptadaśa sūktāni |
tatra 'agniḥ naraḥ' iti pañcaviṃśatyṛcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—'agni pañcādhikā virājo'ṣṭādaśādyāḥ' iti |
'saptamaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vasiṣṭho'paśyat' ityuktvāt maṇḍaladraṣṭā vasiṣṭha ṛṣiḥ |
ādito'ṣṭādaśa virājo'yekādaśakāḥ |
śiṣṭā anādeśaparibhāṣayā triṣṭubhaḥ |
maṇḍalā- diparibhāṣayā agnidevatā |
viśvajitīdaṃ sūktamājyaśastram |
sūtritaṃ ca—'viśvajito'gni nara ityājyam' (āśva. śrau. 8. 7) iti |
atreṣatūrvīrākhye' catūrātre' caturthe'hanīdameva sūktamājya- śastram |
sūyate hi—'agni nara iti caturthe' (āśva. śrau. 10. 2) iti |
vyūhe daśarātre caturthe'hanīdaṃ sūktaṃ jātavedasyanividvānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—'agni nara ityāgnimārutam' (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) iti |
mahāvrate'gnidamājyaśastram |
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake sūyate—'ājyaprauge viśvajitaḥ' (ai. ā. 5. 1. 1) iti |
avivākye'hani prātaranuvāke 'tvamagne vasūnū' ityādinā- manuṣṭubhāṃ sthāna ādyastṛcaḥ prakṣepaṇīyaḥ |
sūyate hi—'daśame'hanyanuṣṭubhāṃ sthāne'gniṃ naro dīdhiti- bhararaṇyoriti tucamāgneye kratau' (āśva. śrau. 8. 12) iti |
ādyāḥ ṣarcaḥ tasminnevāhani āgni- mārute śastre stotriyānurūpāthāḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'agni naro dīdhitibhiraraṇyoriti stotriyānurūpau' (āśva. śrau. 8. 12) iti |
maṇḍalādihome'pyeṣā |
āghāne tṛtīyāyāmiṣṭau 'preddhaḥ' iti sviṣṭakṛto'nuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'preddho agna imo agna iti saṃyājye' (āśva. śrau. 2. 1) iti |
evamanyatrāpi dīkṣaṇīyādiṣu sviṣṭakṛto'nuvākyā |
prāyaṇīyeṣṭau 'sedabhiragnīn' ityādike dve sviṣṭakṛto yājyānuvākye |
sūtritaṃ ca—'sedabhiragnīrtyasvantyāniti dve saṃyājye' (āśva. śrau. 4. 3) iti |
āghāne tṛtīyāyāmiṣṭau 'imo agne' iti sviṣṭakṛto yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'imo agna iti saṃyājye' (āśva. śrau. 2. 1) iti |
evamanyatrāpi dīkṣaṇīyādiṣveṣā sauviṣṭakṛtī yājyā |
prātaranuvāka āgneye kratau traiṣṭubhe chandasyāśvinaśastre ca 'tvamagne suhavaḥ' ityādyāḥ pañcarcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'tvamagne suhavo raṇvasaṃdak' (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti ||
agni naro dīdhitibhiraraṇyorhastacyutī janayanta praśastam |
dūredṛśaṃ gṛhapatimatharyum ||
1 ||
agnim | naraḥ | dīdhitibhiḥ | araṇyoḥ | hastacyutī | janayantaḥ | praśastam | dūredṛśam | gṛhapatim | atharyum || 1 ||
naraḥ netāra ṛtvijaḥ praśastaṃ prakarṣeṇa stutaṃ dūredṛśaṃ dūre dṛśyamānaṃ dūre paśyantaṃ vā gṛhapatiṃ gṛhāṇāṃ pālakam atharyum āgamyamatanavantaṃ vā agnim araṇyoḥ vidyamānaṃ hastacyutī hasta- pracyutyā hastagatyā dīdhitibhiḥ aṅgulibhiḥ janayantaḥ |
'dīdhitayo'ṅgulayo bhavanti dhīyante karmasu |
araṇī pratyuta ene agniḥ samaraṇājāyata iti vā |
hastacyutī hastapracyutyā janayanta praśastaṃ dūredarśanaṃ gṛhapatimatanavantam' (niru. 5. 10) iti ||
1. kha-jha-ta-bha-'śrutarākhye catūrātre; na-'śrutvārākhye catūrātre; śa-'śruturvātrātre catūrātre |
2. kha-jha-na-ma-maṇḍalādihotā |
3. jha-atharyumadhvaryu |
4. kha-jha-na-ma-vāgni vā; śa-agni vā |
5. jha-janayaṃta atra yāskaḥ; ta2-janayata tathā ca yāskaḥ; śa-janayaṃta ajanayata |

he adhvaryavaḥ ⱽvaḥ yūyam ⱽīḻenyaṃ stutyam ⱽasuraṃ balavantaṃ ⱽsudakṣaṃ suprajñaṃ ⱽrodasī rodasyoḥ ⱽantaḥ madhye ⱽdūtaṃ devānāṃ havirvahanātha carantaṃ ⱽsatyavācaṃ ⱽmanuṣ‌vat manuṣyavat 5 ⱽmanunā ⱽsamiddhaṃ yathedānīṃ manuṣyāḥ samindhate tathā pūrvaṃ manunā prajāpatinā samiddham ⱽagnim ⱽadhvarāya yajñāya ⱽsadam it sadaiva ⱽsaṃ ⱽmahema saṃpūjayata ||

ᐁsapardharva: parivaraṇamicchantaḥ ᐁabhijñu abhigatajānukaṃ ᐁbharamāṇā: pādau bharantaḥ ᐁbarhi: ᐁnamasā haviṣā saha ᐁagnau ᐁpra ᐁvṛate prabharanti | tadeva viśadayati | he ᐁadhvaryava: ᐁghṛtapṛṣṭhaṃ ā bhāratī bhāratībhiḥ sajoṣā iḻā dairvaimanuṣyaibhiragniḥ | sarasvatī sārasvatebhirvārvāk tisro devīrbarhiredaṃ sadantu || 8 || ā | bhāratī | bhāratībhiḥ | su'joṣāḥ | iḻā | daivaiḥ | manuṣyaibhiḥ | agniḥ | sarasvatī | sārasvatebhiḥ | arvāk | tisraḥ | devīḥ | barhiḥ | ā | idam | sadantu || 8 || etadāyuktacatuṣṭayaṃ dvitīyāṣṭakasyāṣṭamādhyāye (ṛ. saṃ. 3. 4. 8-11) yadyapi vyākhyātaṃ tathāpi vyavadhānāt saṃkṣepato'trāpi vyākhyāyate | ˅bhāratī bharatasyādityasya patnī ˅bhāratībhiḥ ˅sajoṣāḥ sahitā ˅iḻā ˅manuṣyebhiḥ manuṣyalokabhavaiḥ ˅daivaiḥ sārdham ˅agniḥ ˅ā gacchatu | ˅sarasvatī ˅sārasvatebhiḥ sārasvataiḥ madhyamasthānadaivaiḥ sārdham ˅arvāk asmadabhimukhamāgacchatu | āgatya ˅tisro ˅devīḥ devyaḥ || prathamārye dvitīyā || ˅barhiḥ ˅idam ˅ā ˅sadantu ||
tannasturīpamardha poṣayittu deva tvaṣṭrvi rarāṇaḥ syasva | yatau vīraḥ karmaṇyaḥ sudakṣau yuktagrāvā jāyate devakāmaḥ || 9 || tat | naḥ | turīpam | ardha | poṣayittu | deva | tvaṣṭaḥ | vi | rarāṇaḥ | syasvati syasva | yataḥ | vīraḥ | karmaṇyaḥ | su'dakṣaḥ | yukta'grāvā | jāyate | deva'kāmaḥ || 9 || ˅deva dyotamāna he ˅tvaṣṭaḥ ˅rarāṇaḥ ramamāṇastvaṃ ˅naḥ asmākaṃ ˅turīpaṃ tvaritamāmuvat ˅poṣayittu poṣakaṃ ˅tat retaḥ ˅vi ˅syasva viśeṣeṇāvasānaṃ prāpaya | vimocayetyarthaḥ | ˅yataḥ retasaḥ ˅karmaṇyaḥ karmasu sādhuḥ ˅sudakṣaḥ subalaḥ ˅yuktagrāvā somasut ˅devakāmaḥ ˅vīraḥ putraḥ ˅jāyate ||
vanaspate'va sṛjopa devānhaviḥ śamitā sūdayati | sedu hotā satyataro yajāti yathā devānāṃ janimāni veda || 10 || vanaspate | ava | sṛja | upa | devān | agniḥ | haviḥ | śamitā | sūdayati | saḥ | it | ū~ iti | hotā | satya'taraḥ | yajāti | yathā | devānām | janimāni | veda || 10 || he ˅vanaspate ˅devān ˅upa ˅ava ˅sṛja | atha parokṣastutiḥ | ˅agniḥ vanaspatiḥ ˅śamitā śāmitrarūpaḥ san ˅haviḥ ˅sūdayati prerayatu | ˅set sa eva vanaspatiḥ ˅hotā devānāmāhvātā ˅satyataraḥ api san ˅yajāti yajatu | ˅devānāṃ ˅janimāni jananāni ˅yathā svayaṃ ˅veda tathā ||
ā yāhyame samidhāno arvāḍindreṇa daivaiḥ sarathaṃ turebhiḥ | barhirna āstāmaditiḥ suputrā svāhā devā amṛtā mādayantām || 11 || ā | yāhi | agne | sam'idhānaḥ | arvāṅ | indreṇa | daivaiḥ | sa'ratham | turebhiḥ | barhiḥ | naḥ | āstām | aditiḥ | su'putrā | svāhā | devāḥ | amṛtāḥ | mādayantām || 11 ||

he <sup>v</sup>agne <sup>v</sup>samidhānaḥ <sup>v</sup>arvāk asmadabhimukhasvam <sup>v</sup>indreṇa <sup>v</sup>turebhiḥ tvaritaiḥ <sup>v</sup>devaiḥ ca <sup>v</sup>sarathaṃ samānarathaṃ yathā bhavati tathā <sup>v</sup>ā <sup>v</sup>yāhi āgaccha |
<sup>v</sup>naḥ asmākaṃ <sup>v</sup>barhiḥ adhi <sup>v</sup>āstām <sup>v</sup>aditiḥ ca <sup>v</sup>suputrā kalyāṇaputrā |

2 ||
‘agni vo devam’ iti daśarcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
agneyamanukramaṇikā- ‘agni vo daśa’ iti |
‘agni vo devam’ ityetādīni daśa sūktāni tṛtīyacaturthavarjitāni prātaranuvāke āgneye kratau traiṣṭubhe chandasyāśvinaśastre ca viniyuktāni |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘agni vo devamiti daśānāṃ tṛtīyacaturthe uddharet’ (āśva. śrau. 4.13) iti |
vyūhe daśarātre'ṣṭame'hanīdaṃ sūktamājyaśastram |
sūyate hi- ‘dvitīyasyāgni vo devamityājyam’ (āśva. śrau. 8.10) iti ||
agni vo devamagnibhiḥ sajoṣā yajiṣṭha dūtamadhvare kṛṇudhvam |
yo martyeṣu nidhruvirṛtāvā tapūmūrdhā ghṛtaannaḥ pāvakaḥ ||
1 ||
agnim | vaḥ | devam | agnibhiḥ | sajoṣāḥ | yajiṣṭham | dūtam | adhvare | kṛṇudhvam | yaḥ | martyeṣu | nidhruviḥ | ṛtavā | tapūmūrdhā | ghṛtaannaḥ | pāvakaḥ || 1 ||
he devāḥ <sup>v</sup>vaḥ yūyaṃ <sup>v</sup>devaṃ dyotamānam <sup>v</sup>agnibhiḥ anyairagnibhiḥ <sup>v</sup>sajoṣāḥ sajopasaṃ sahitam |
dvitīyārthe prathamā |
<sup>v</sup>yajiṣṭhaṃ yaṣṭatamam <sup>v</sup>agnim <sup>v</sup>adhvare kauṭilyarahite yajñe <sup>v</sup>dūtaṃ <sup>v</sup>kṛṇudhvaṃ kuruta |
<sup>v</sup>yaḥ agnirdevo'pi san <sup>v</sup>martyeṣu <sup>v</sup>nidhruviḥ nitarāṃ dhruvastiṣṭhati <sup>v</sup>ṛtāvā yajñavān satyavān vā <sup>v</sup>tapūmūrdhā tāpatejāḥ <sup>v</sup>ghṛtānnaḥ <sup>v</sup>pāvakaḥ śodhakaśca bhavati ||
prothadaśvo na yarvase'viṣyanyudā mahaḥ saṃvaraṇādvyasthāt |
ādyasya vāto anu vāti śociradha sma te vrajanaṃ kṛṣṇamasti ||
2 ||
prothat | aśvaḥ | na | yarvase | aviṣyan | yadā | mahaḥ | sam'avarṇāt | vi | asthāt | āt | asya | vātaḥ | anu | vāti | śociḥ | adha | sma | te | vrajanam | kṛṣṇam | asti ||2||
<sup>v</sup>yavase ghāse <sup>v</sup>aviṣyan bhakṣayan <sup>v</sup>prothat śabdaṃ kurvan saṃcaran vā <sup>v</sup>aśvo <sup>v</sup>na aśva iva <sup>v</sup>mahaḥ mahataḥ <sup>v</sup>saṃvaraṇāt nirodhāt dāvarūpo'gniḥ <sup>v</sup>yadā <sup>v</sup>nyasthāt saṃtateṣu vṛkṣepu vitiṣṭhate <sup>v</sup>āt tadā <sup>v</sup>asya agneḥ <sup>v</sup>śociḥ arciḥ <sup>v</sup>anu <sup>v</sup>vāta: <sup>v</sup>vāti |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |
<sup>v</sup>adha anantaraṃ he agne <sup>v</sup>te tava <sup>v</sup>vrajanaṃ vartma <sup>v</sup>kṛṣṇamasti bhavati |
<sup>v</sup>sma iti pūraṇaḥ ||
udyasya te navajātasya vṛṣṇo'gne carantyajarā idhānāḥ |
acchā ghārmaruṣo dhūma eti saṃ duto agma īyase hi devān ||

ut | yasya | te | nava'jātasya | vṛṣṇaḥ | agne | caranti | ajarāḥ | idhānāḥ | acchā | ghārma'ruṣaḥ | dhūmaḥ | eti | sam | dūtaḥ | agne | īyase | hi | devān || 3 ||
1. ta-samidhānaḥ : samidhyamānaḥ |

he <sup>v</sup>agne <sup>v</sup>etā etāni paridṛśyamānāni <sup>v</sup>saubhagā saubhagāni śobhanāni dhanāni <sup>v</sup>naḥ asmabhyaṃ <sup>v</sup>didīhi dīpaya dehi vā |
<sup>v</sup>api api ca <sup>v</sup>kratuṃ karma yajñānāṃ kartāraṃ vā <sup>v</sup>sucetasaṃ śobhanaprajñānayuktaṃ suprajñānaṃ putraṃ vā <sup>v</sup>vatema saṃbhajemahi ||

<sup>v</sup>viśvā viśvāni dhanāni <sup>v</sup>stotṛbhyaḥ udgātṛbhyaḥ <sup>v</sup>gṛṇute śaṃsate <sup>v</sup>ca <sup>v</sup>santu |

4 ||
‘pra vaḥ śukrāya’ iti daśacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
‘pra vaḥ’ ityanukrāntam |
prātaranuvākādhinaśastrayordeśasūktamadhye dvitīyatvenokaḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ |
ekādaśina āgneye paśau ‘pra vaḥ śukrāya’ ityepā vapāyā yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pra vaḥ śukrāya bhānave bharadhvaṃ yathā viprasya manuṣo havirbhiḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 3.7) iti ||
pra vaḥ śukrāya bhānave bharadhvaṃ havyaṃ matiṃ cāgnaye supūtam |
yo daivyāni mānuṣā janū~ṣyantarviśvāni vidmanā jigāti ||
1 ||
pra | vaḥ | śukrāya | bhānave | bharadhvam | havyam | matim | ca | agnaye | supūtam | yaḥ | daivyāni | mānuṣā | janū~ṣi | antaḥ | viśvāni | vidmanā | jigāti || 1 ||
he haviṣāṃ voḍhāraḥ <sup>v</sup>vaḥ yūyaṃ <sup>v</sup>śukrāya śubhrāya <sup>v</sup>bhānave dīptāya <sup>v</sup>agnaye <sup>v</sup>supūtaṃ suśuddhaṃ <sup>v</sup>havyaṃ <sup>v</sup>matiṃ stutiṃ <sup>v</sup>ca <sup>v</sup>pra <sup>v</sup>bharadhvam |
<sup>v</sup>yaḥ agniḥ <sup>v</sup>daivyāni <sup>v</sup>mānupā mānuṣāṇi ca <sup>v</sup>viśvāni <sup>v</sup>janū~ṣi jātāni <sup>v</sup>antaḥ antarā <sup>v</sup>vidmanā prajñānena mārgeṇa vā <sup>v</sup>jigāti gacchati |
devān manuṣyāṃśca antarā havīṃṣi netuṃ vartata ityarthaḥ ||
sa gṛtsau agnistaruṇaścidastu yato yaviṣṭho arjaniṣṭa mātuḥ |
saṃ yo vanā yuvate śucidanbhūri cidannā samidatti sadyaḥ ||
2 ||
saḥ | gṛtsaḥ | agniḥ | taruṇaḥ | cit | astu | yataḥ | yaviṣṭhaḥ | arjaniṣṭa | mātuḥ | sam | yaḥ | vanā | yuvate | śuci'dan | bhūri | cit | annā | sam | it | atti | sadyaḥ ||2||
<sup>v</sup>saḥ <sup>v</sup>cit sa eva <sup>v</sup>gṛtsaḥ medhāvī |
tathā ca yāskaḥ—‘gṛtsa iti medhāvināma’ (niru. 9.5) |
<sup>v</sup>agniḥ <sup>v</sup>taruṇaḥ tārako bhavati<sup>2</sup> |
tadā <sup>v</sup>yataḥ yadā <sup>v</sup>mātuḥ araṇyāḥ <sup>v</sup>yaviṣṭhaḥ yuvatamaḥ san <sup>v</sup>ajaniṣṭa |
<sup>v</sup>yaḥ agniḥ <sup>v</sup>śucidan dīsadantaḥ <sup>v</sup>vanā vanāni <sup>v</sup>saṃ <sup>v</sup>yuvate ātmanā saṃyojayati |
kiṃca <sup>v</sup>bhūri <sup>v</sup>cit bhūrīṇyapi <sup>v</sup>annā svīyānyannāni <sup>v</sup>sadyaḥ <sup>v</sup>it sadya eva <sup>v</sup>sam <sup>v</sup>atti samyak bhakṣayati ||
asya devasya saṃsadhanīke yaṃ martāsaḥ śyetaṃ jagṛbhre |
ni yo gṛbhaṃ pauruṣeyīmuvoca durokaṃmirāyave śuśoṣa ||

ᵛasya ᵛdevasya agreḥ ᵛanīke ᵛsaṃsadi sthāne ᵛiyetaṃ śvetaṃ śubhraṃ ᵛyam ᵛagniṃ ᵛmartāsaḥ manuṣyāḥ ᵛjagṛbhe parigṛhṇanti ᵛ|

niṣevata ityarthaḥ |

he agne tvam adhvarasya praketaḥ prajñāpanaḥ san mahānasi |
tvat ṛte tvayā vinā amṛtāḥ devāḥ na mādayante na mādyanti1 |
viśvebhiḥ viśvaiḥ devaiḥ sarathaṃ yathā bhavati2 ā yāhi ca |
iha āstīrṇaṃ barhiṣi prathamaḥ mukhyaḥ hotā āhvātā san ni sada niṣīda ca ||
vājapeye bārhaspatyacaroḥ sviṣṭakṛto'nuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘tvāmīḻate ajiraṃ dūtyāyābhi suditiṃ sudṛśaṃ gṛṇantaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 9. 9) iti ||
tvāmīḻate ajiraṃ dūtyāya haviṣmantaḥ sadaminmānuṣāsaḥ |
yasya devāsado barhirame'hānyasmai sudinā bhavanti ||
2 ||
tvām | īḻate | ajiram | dūtyāya | haviṣmantaḥ | sadam | it | mānuṣāsaḥ | yasya | devaiḥ | ā | asadaḥ | barhiḥ | agne | ahāni | asmai | sudinā | bhavanti ||2||
he agne ajiraṃ pragāminaṃ tvāṃ mānuṣāsaḥ mānuṣāḥ haviṣmantaḥ yajamānāḥ sadamit sadaiva dūtyāya dūtakarmaṇe havirvahanāya īḻate yācante |
kimarthamityata āha |
yasya haviṣmataḥ barhiḥ devaiḥ sārdham āsadaḥ tvamadhi tiṣṭhasi asmai haviṣmate ahāni sudinā sudināni śobhanadināni bhavanti ||
triṃśadduktaḥ pra cikiturvasūni tve antardāśuṣe martyāya |
manuṣpadma iha yakṣi devānbharvā no dūto abhiśastipāvā ||
3 ||
triḥ | cit | akkoḥ | pra | cikituḥ | vasūni | tve iti | antaḥ | dāśuṣe | martyāya | manuṣpat | agne | iha | yakṣi | devān | bharvā | naḥ | dūtaḥ | abhiśastipāvā || 3 ||
he agne tve tvayi antaḥ madhye akkoḥ ahaḥ |
yadyapyakuriti rātrernāma3 tathāpyatrājyante vyajyante rūpādīnyasminnihyaho nāma |
triḥ trivāraṃ triṣu savaneṣu vasūni havīṃṣi dāśuṣe haviṣāṃ pradātre martyāya manuṣyāya |
tadarthamityarthaḥ |
pra cikituḥ pravedayantyṛtvijaḥ |
yadvā |
akkoriti triṣbīn agniśśramajaṃ ca tvayyantarnihitāni pra cikituḥ |
avidannityarthaḥ |
api ca manuṣpat manoriva iha mamāsmin yajñe dūtaḥ tvaṃ devān yakṣi yaja |
naḥ asmākam abhiśastipāvā abhiśasteḥ abhiśaṃsāt śatravāt pāvā rakṣitā bhava ||
agnīśe bṛhato adhvarasyāgniviśvasya haviṣaḥ kṛtasya |
kratuṃ hasya varco juṣantāthā devā dadhire havyavāham ||
4 ||
agniḥ | īśe | bṛhataḥ | adhvarasya | agniḥ | viśvasya | haviṣaḥ | kṛtasya | kratum | hi | asya | varcaḥ | juṣante | atha | devāḥ | dadhire | havyavāham || 4 ||
bṛhataḥ mahataḥ adhvarasya kauṭilyarahitasya yajñasya agniḥ īśe īṣṭe |
viśvasya sarvasya kṛtasya saṃskṛtasya haviṣaḥ ca agniḥ eveṣṭe |
hi yasmāt asya agneḥ kratuṃ karma vasavaḥ devāḥ juṣanta sevante |
atha api ca devāḥ agniṃ havyavāhaṃ havyānāṃ voḍhāraṃ dadhire cakrira ityarthaḥ ||
1. śa-mādayanti |
2. jha-bhavati tathā |
3. kha-bha-rātrināma |
āgna vaha haviradhāya devānindrajyeṣṭhāsa iha mādayantām |
imaṃ yajñaṃ divi deveṣu dhehi yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
5 ||
ā | agne | vaha | haviḥ'adhāya | devān | indra'jyeṣṭhāsaḥ | iha | mādayantām | imam | yajñam | divi | deveṣu | dhehi | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 5 ||
he agne haviradhāya haviṣāṃ bhakṣaṇāya devān ā vaha |
kimarthamityata āha |
iha asmin yajñe indrajyeṣṭhāsaḥ indrapramukhā devāḥ mādayantām |
imaṃ yajñam idaṃ yajñavyaṃ haviḥ divi sthiteṣu deveṣu dhehi nidhehi |
devānvaha nyedaṃ havirvā deveṣu nayeti bhāvaḥ |
antimaḥ pādo vyākhyātacaraḥ ||
5 ||
‘agnama mahā’ iti tvātmakaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
tathā cānukrāntam- ‘agnama tracam’ iti |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayorukto viniyogaḥ |
vyūhe daśarātre navame'hanīdaṃ sūktamājyaśastram |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘tṛtīyasyāgnama mahatyājyam’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 11 ) iti ||
agnama mahā narmasā yaviṣṭhaṃ yo dīdāya samiddhaḥ sve duroṇe |
citrabhānuṃ rodasī antarurvī svāhutaṃ viśvataḥ pratyañcam ||
1 ||
agnama | mahā | narmasā | yaviṣṭham | yaḥ | dīdāya | sam'iddhaḥ | sve | duroṇe | citra'bhānum | rodasī iti | antaḥ | urvī iti | su'āhutam | viśvataḥ | pratyañcam || 1 ||
yaḥ agniḥ sve duroṇe sve sthāna āhavanīye samiddhaḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ samiddhaḥ san dīdāya dīpyate tamimaṃ yaviṣṭhaṃ yuvatam urvī vistīrṇayoḥ rodasī rodasyoḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ antaḥ madhye antarikṣe citrabhānuṃ citrajvālaṃ svāhutaṃ su āhutibhirhutaṃ santaṃ viśvataḥ sarvataḥ pratyañcaṃ pratigacchantarmagniṃ mahā mahatā namasā namaskāreṇa agnama vayamupagacchāma ||
sa mahā viśvā duritāni sāhnābhiḥ ṣṭave dame ā jātavedāḥ |
sa nau rakṣipaduritādavadyādasmāngṛṇata uta nau maghonaḥ ||
2 ||
saḥ | mahā | viśvā | duḥ'itāni | sāhvān | agniḥ | stuve | dame | ā | jāta'vedāḥ | saḥ | naḥ | rakṣipat | duḥ'itāta | avadyāt | asmān | gṛṇataḥ | uta | naḥ | maghonaḥ || 2 ||
yo'gniḥ mahā mahattvena viśvā viśvāni duritāni sāhvān abhibhavan jātavedāḥ jātadhanā jātaprajño vā dame yajñagṛhe stuve asmābhiḥ stūyate saḥ agniḥ asmān duritāt pāpāt avadyāt ninditācca karmaṇaḥ rakṣipat rakṣatu |
asmān gṛṇataḥ stuvato'pi rakṣipat |
uta api ca so'gniḥ naḥ maghonaḥ haviṣmataḥ rakṣipat ||
tvaṃ varuṇa uta mitro agne tvāṃ vardhanti matibhirvasiṣṭhāḥ |
tve vasu suṣaṇāni santu yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
3 ||
tvam | varuṇaḥ | uta | mitraḥ | agne | tvām | vardhanti | mati'bhiḥ | vasiṣṭhāḥ | tve iti | vasu | su'sananāni | santu | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 3 ||
1. jha-ānayedam |
he ᵛagne ᵛtvaṃ ᵛvaruṇa: asi |
ᵛuta api ca ᵛtvaṃ ᵛmitra: asi jagata: pramītekhātāsi |
ᵛtvāṃ ᵛvasiṣṭhā: ᵛmatibhi: stutibhi: ᵛvardhanti ᵛvardhayanti |
ᵛtve tvayi vidyamānāni ᵛvasu vasūni ᵛsusananāni susaṃbhajanāni² ᵛsantu iti |
spaṣṭamanyat |
15|
‘prāgnaye’ iti tṛcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vaiśvānarābhidevatākam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ- ‘prāgnaye vaiśvānarīyam’ iti |
viniyogo laiṅgika: |
prāgnaye viśvaśuce dhiyaṃdhe'suragne manma dhīti bharadhvam | bhare havirna barhiṣi prīṇāno vaiśvānarāya yataye matīnām || 1 || pra | agnaye | viśvaśucai | dhiyam'dhe | asura'gne | manma | dhītim | bharadhvam | bhare | havi: | na | barhiṣi | prīṇāna: | vaiśvānarāya | yataye | matīnām || 1 ||
he sakhāya: ᵛviśvaśuce viśvaṃ yo dīpayati tasmai ᵛdhiyaṃdhe dhiyāṃ karmaṇāṃ yo dhātā tasmai ᵛasuragne asurāṇāṃ yo hantā tasmai ᵛagnaye ᵛmanma mannīyaṃ stotraṃ ᵛdhītiṃ karma ca ᵛpra ᵛbharadhvam |
ᵛmatīnām abhimatānāṃ kāmānāṃ ᵛyataye dātre ᵛvaiśvānarāya viśvanarahitāyāgniviśeṣāya ᵛbarhiṣi yajñe ᵛhavirna haviriva stutiṃ ᵛprīṇāna: prīyamāṇo'haṃ ᵛbhare bharāmi |
yadvā |
havi: prīṇāna: prīṇayannahaṃ barhiṣi havirbhare saṃbharāmi |
neti saṃpratyarthe |
tvamagne śociṣā śośucāna ā rodasī apṛṇā jāyamāna: | tvaṃ devā~ abhiśsteramuśco vaiśvānara jātavedo mahitvā || 2 || tvam | agnaye | śociṣā | śośucāna: | ā | rodasī iti | apṛṇā: | jāyamāna: | tvam | devān | abhi'śste: | amuñca: | vaiśvānara | jāta'veda: | mahitvā || 2 ||
he ᵛagne ᵛtvaṃ ᵛśociṣā dīptyā ᵛśośucāna: dīpyamāna: ᵛjāyamāna: jāyamāna eva ᵛrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵛā ᵛapṛṇā: āpūraya: |
api ca ᵛjātaveda: jātaprajña jātadhana vā ᵛvaiśvānara viśvanarahita he agne ᵛtvaṃ ᵛdevān ᵛmahitvā mahattvena ᵛabhiśste: abhiśaṃsāt śatro: ᵛamuñca: amocaya: |
jāto yadagne bhuvanā vyakhya: paśūna gopā irya: parijmā | vaiśvānara brahmaṇa vinda gātuṃ yūyaṃ pāta svastibhi: sadā na: || 3 || jātaḥ | yat | agne | bhuvanā | vi | akhya: | paśūna | na | gopā: | irya: | pari'jmā | vaiśvānara | brahmaṇa | vinda | gātum | yuyam | pāta | svasti'bhi: | sadā | na: || 3 ||
he ᵛagne ᵛjātaḥ sūryātmanā jātastvam ᵛirya: svāmī prerayan vā ᵛparijmā parito gantā san ᵛpaśūna ᵛgopā: |
yathā gavāṃ pālaka: paśūna paśyati tadvat |
ᵛyat yadā ᵛbhuvanā bhūtāni ᵛvyakhya:
1. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-śa- ‘vardhanti’ nāsti |
2. jha-susaṃbhajanīyāni |
rakṣārthaṃ paśyasi tadā brahmaṇa| brahma stotram| tadarthaṃ gātuṃ gatiṃ phalaprāptiṃ vinda| yadvā| brahmaṇo brāhmaṇārthaṃ gātuṃ vinda| yena brāhmaṇā upadravācchagacchanti taṃ¹ gātuṃ vindetyarthaḥ| spaṣṭamanyat || 16 ||
‘samidhā jātavedase’ iti tvacaṃ caturdaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣamāgneyam|
ādyā bṛhatī dvitīyā- tṛtīye triṣṭubhau|
tathā cānukramyate—‘samidhā bṛhatyādi’ iti|
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
samidhā jātavedase devārya devahūtibhiḥ |
havirbhiḥ śukraśociṣe namasvinīḥ vayaṃ dāśemāgnaye ||
1 ||
sami'dhā| jāta'vedase| devārya| devahūti'bhiḥ| haviḥ'bhiḥ| śukra'śociṣe| namasvinīḥ| vayam| dāśema| agnaye || 1 ||
jātavedase jātavedasaṃ jātaprajñam agnaye agni samidhā vayaṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ dāśema paricarema|
devāya devaṃ stutyamagni devahūtibhiḥ devastutibhiḥ dāśema|
śukraśociṣe śukraśociṣaṃ śubhradīptiṃ namasvinīḥ haviṣmanto vayaṃ havirbhiḥ dāśema|
atra dāśatiyogāt karmaṇi caturthī|
prāyeṇa sarvatra dāśatiyoge karmaṇi caturthī dṛśyate ||
vayaṃ tai agne samidhā vidhema vayaṃ dāśema suṣṭutī yajatra |
vayaṃ ghṛtenādhvarasya hotarvyaṃ deva haviṣā bhadraśoce ||
2 ||
vayam| te| agne| sam'idhā| vidhema| vayam| dāśema| su'stutī| yajatra| vayam| ghṛtena| adhvarasya| hotaḥ| vayam| deva| haviṣā| bhadra'śoce || 2 ||
he agne te tvāṃ vayaṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ samidhā vidhema paricarema|
he yajatra yaṣṭavyāgne vayaṃ suṣṭutī śobhanayā stutyā dāśema tvāṃ paricarema|
adhvarasya yajñasya hotaḥ agne vayaṃ ghṛtena ājyena tvāṃ dāśema|
he bhadraśoce kalyāṇajvala deva dyotamānāgne tvāṃ vayaṃ haviṣā dāśema ||
ā nau devebhirupa devahūtimagne yāhi varṣaṭkṛti juṣāṇaḥ |
tubhyaṃ devāya dāśataḥ syāma yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
3 ||
ā| naḥ| devebhiḥ| upa| deva'hūtim| agne| yāhi| varṣaṭ'kṛtim| juṣāṇaḥ| tubhyam| devāya| dāśataḥ| syāma| yūyam| pāta| svasti'bhiḥ| sadā| naḥ || 3 ||
he agne naḥ asmākaṃ devahūti stotraṃ yajñaṃ vā devebhiḥ devaiḥ sārdhaṃ vaṣaṭkṛti haviḥ juṣāṇaḥ sevamānastvam upa ā yāhi|
devāya dyotamānāya tubhyaṃ vayaṃ dāśataḥ paricarantaḥ syāma bhavema|
siddhamanyat ||
17 ||
‘upasadyāya’ iti pañcadaśaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ gāyatramāgneyam|
tathā cānukāntam— ‘upasadyāya pañconā gāyatram’ iti|
prātaranuvāka āgneye kratau gāyatre chandasyāśvinaśastre vedaṃ sūktam|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘upasadyāya tvamagne yajñānāmiti tisra uttamā udvaret’ (āśva. śrau. 4.13) iti|
1. kha-bha-‘taṃ’ nāsti |
2. jha-na-bha-śa-śubhradīsinm |
upasadi' paurvāhkyām 'upasadyāya' ityādyāstisra ṛcaḥ sāmidhenyaḥ |
sūyate ca—'upasadyāya mīḻahuṣa iti tisra ekaikāṃ triranavānam' (āśva. śrau. 4. 8) iti |
pavitreṭyām 'agnī rakṣāṃsi' ityepā prathamājyābhāgā'nuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'pāvakavantāvājyabhāgābhyī rakṣāṃsi sedhati' (āśva. śrau. 2.12) iti |
anvārambhaṇīyāyāmagnerbhāgino yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'ā savaṃ savituryathā sa no rādhāṃsyā bhara' (āśva. śrau. 2.8) iti |
svastyanyānvāmiṣṭau 'agre rakṣā ṇaḥ' iti prathamājyābhāgā- nuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'svastyanyāṃ rakṣatvantābhre rakṣā ṇo aṃhasaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 2.10) iti ||
upasadyāya mīḻahuṣa āsyai juhutā haviḥ | yo no nediṣṭhamāpyam || 1 || upa'sadyāya | mīḻahuṣe | āsyai | juhuta | haviḥ | yaḥ | naḥ | nediṣṭham | āpyam || 1 || he adhvaryavaḥ ᵥupasadyāya upasadanīyāya ᵥmīḻahuṣe kāmānāṃ varṣitre'gnaye tatprītyartham ᵥāsye tasyaiva mukhe ᵥhaviḥ ᵥjuhuta | ᵥyaḥ agniḥ ᵥnediṣṭham āsannatam ᵥāpyaṃ bhavati | āsannatamo bandhurbhavatītyarthaḥ | āpyamiti svārthikastaddhitaḥ ||
yaḥ pañca carṣaṇīrabhi niṣsāda dame dame | kavirgṛhapatiryuvā || 2 || yaḥ | pañca | carṣaṇīḥ | abhi | ni'sāda | dame'dame | kaviḥ | gṛha'patiḥ | yuvā || 2 || ᵥyaḥ agniḥ ᵥkaviḥ prājñaḥ ᵥgṛhapatiḥ gṛhāṇāṃ pālayitā ᵥyuvā nityataruṇaḥ san ᵥpañca ᵥcarṣaṇīḥ pañca janān manuṣyān ᵥabhi abhimukhaṃ ᵥdame'dame gṛhegṛhe ᵥnipasāda niṣīdati | uttarayā vākyaparisamāptiḥ ||
sa no vedoṃ amātyamagnī rakṣatu viśvataḥ | utāsmān pātvaṃhasaḥ || 3 || saḥ | naḥ | vedaḥ | amātyam | agniḥ | rakṣatu | viśvataḥ | uta | asmān | pātu | aṃhasaḥ ||3|| ᵥsaḥ ᵥagniḥ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥvedaḥ dhanam ᵥamātyam antike bhavaṃ sahabhūtaṃ vā ᵥviśvataḥ sarvataḥ bādhakāt ᵥrakṣatu | ᵥuta api ca ᵥasmān vasiṣṭhān ᵥaṃhasaḥ pāpāt ᵥpātu rakṣatu ||
navaṃ nu stomamagnaye divaḥ śyenārya jījanam | vasvaḥ kuvid‌nārti naḥ || 4 || navaṃ | nu | stomam | agnaye | divaḥ | śyenārya | jījanam | vasvaḥ | kuvit | vanāti | naḥ ||4|| ᵥdivaḥ dyulokasya ᵥiyenāya śyenasadṛśāya ᵥnu kṣipraṃ gantre ᵥagnaye yasmai ᵥnavaṃ nūtanaṃ ᵥstomaṃ ᵥjījanaṃ janayāmi so'gniḥ ᵥnaḥ asmabhyaṃ ᵥkuvit bahu ᵥvasvaḥ vasu dhanam | karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | ᵥvanāti | dadātvityarthaḥ ||
spārhā yasya śriyau dṛśe rayivīrva̭vato yathā | agne yajñasya śorcataḥ || 5 || spārhā | yasya | śriyaḥ | dṛśe | rayiḥ | vīra'vataḥ | yathā | agne | yajñasya | śorcataḥ || 5 || ᵥyajñasya ᵥagne purastādbhāge ᵥśorcataḥ dīpyamānasya ᵥyasya agneḥ ᵥśriyaḥ dīsayaḥ ᵥvīravataḥ putravataḥ ᵥrayiḥ dhanaṃ ᵥyathā tadvat ᵥdṛśe draṣṭuṃ cakṣuṣe vā ᵥspārhāḥ spṛhaṇīyā bhavanti tasmai navaṃ stomaṃ jījanamityanuṣaṅgaḥ | uttaratra sambandho vā || || 18 ||
1. kha-bha-upasada saumikyām |
2. jha-ta-śa-jījanamarjānam |

tvama̭gne॒ vīra̭va॒dyaśo̭ de॒vaśca̭ sa॒vitā॒ bhaga̭: | di॒tiśca̭ dā॒ti vā॒rya̭m || 12 || tvam | agne | vīra'vat | yaśaḥ | devaḥ | ca | savitā | bhagaḥ | ditiḥ | ca | dāti | vāryam ||12||
he ᐁagne ᐁtvaṃ ᐁvīravat putrapautropetaṃ ᐁyaśaḥ annaṃ dehīti śeṣaḥ |

agne॒ rakṣā॒ ṇo aṃha̭sa॒ḥ prati̭ ṣma de॒va rī̭ṣataḥ | tapi̭ṣṭhaira॒jaro̭ da॒ha || 13 || agne | rakṣa | naḥ | aṃhasaḥ | prati | sma | deva | riṣataḥ | tapiṣṭhaiḥ | ajaraḥ | daha || 13 ||

api ca he ᐁdeva dyotamānāgne ᐁajaraḥ jarārahitastvaṃ ᐁripataḥ hiṃsataḥ śatrūn ᐁtapiṣṭhaiḥ atiśayena tāpakkaistejobhiḥ ᐁdaha bhasmīkuru ||
adhā̭ ma॒hī na॒ ā॒ya॒sya̭nā॒dhṛṣṭḙ nṛ॒pīta̭ye | pū॒rvā śa॒tabhṷjiḥ || 14 || adha | mahī | naḥ | āyasī | anādhṛṣṭe | nṛpītaye | pūḥ | bhuva | śatabhujiḥ || 14 ||

ᐁbhava |

ᵥūrjaḥ : balasya ᵥnapātaṃ putram |
‘sūnuḥ napāt’ ityapatyanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵥpriyaṃ priyasmākaṃᵡ ᵥcetiṣṭham atiśayena jñātāraṃ prajñāpakaṃ vā ᵥaratiṃ gantāraṃ svāminaṃ vā ᵥsvadhvaraṃ suyajñaṃ ᵥviśvasya sarvasya yajamānasya ᵥdūtam ᵥamṛtaṃ nityam ᵥagnim ᵥenā enena ᵥnamasā stotreṇa |

ᵥvaḥ : yuṣmadartham ᵥ
he sahasa: suno balasya putrānne yaśastamam atiśayena yaśasvinaṃ taṃ prasiddhaṃ yaṃ tvā tvāṃ dūtaṃ kṛṇmahe kurma: sa tvaṃ devān bītaye haviṣāṃ bhakṣaṇāya ā vaha |
kiṃca yat yadā tvā tvām īmahe yācāmahe tadaiva viśvā viśvāni martabhojanāni manuṣyāṇāṃ bhogyāni kalyānāni dhanāni rāsva asmabhyaṃ dehi ||
tvamagne gṛhapatistvaṃ hotā no adhvare | tvaṃ potā viśvavāra pracetā yakṣi verṣi ca vāryam || 5 || tvam | agne | gṛha'pati: | tvam | hotā | na: | adhvare | tvam | potā | viśva'vāra | pra'cetā: | yakṣi | verṣi | ca | vāryam || 5 ||
he viśvavāra viśvairvarṇanīya agne tvaṃ na: asmākam adhvare yāge gṛhapati: asi yaja- māno'si |

tvaṃ tvameva potā asi |
ata: pracetā: prakṛṣṭamatistvaṃ vārya varaṇīyaṃ havi: yakṣi yaja |
vepi ca kāmayasva bhakṣaya vā ||

taṃ hotāramadhvarasya pracetasaṃ vahniṃ devā akṛṇvata | dadhāti ratnaṃ vidhate suvīryamagniryanāya dāśuṣe || 12 || tam | hotāram | adhvarasya | praḍacaitasam | vahnim | devāḥ | akṛṇvata | dadhāti | ratnam | vidhate | su'vīryam | agniḥ | janāya | dāśuṣe || 12 ||
ᵛdevāḥ ᵛpracetasaṃ prakṛṣṭamatimagnim ᵛadhvarasya yajñasya ᵛvahniṃ voḍhāraṃ ᵛhotāraṃ ca ᵛakṛṇvata akurvan |
kimarthamityata āha |

22 ||

1. śa-vistṛṇītā0 |

tvā॒mu tḙ dadhire॒ havya॒vāha̭ṃ de॒vāso̭ a॒gna ū॒rja ā na̭pā॒tam || 6 || tvām | ūṃ iti̭ | te | da॒dhi॒re | havya॒'vāha̭m | de॒vāsa̭ḥ | a॒gne | ū॒rjaḥ | ā | na॒pā॒tam ||6||

akurvannityarthaḥ ||

imā u tvā paspṛdhānāso atra mandrā girau devayantīrupa sthuḥ | arvācīṃ te pṛthyā rāya etu syāma te sumatāvindra śarman || 3 || imāḥ | ūṃ iti | tvā | paspṛdhānāsaḥ | atra | mandrāḥ | giraḥ | deva'yantīḥ | upa | sthuḥ | arvācīṃ | te | pṛthyā | rāyaḥ | etu | syāma | te | sumatau | indra | śarman || 3 ||
he indra tvā tvām atra yajñe stotari vā pravartamānāḥ paspṛdhānāsaḥ spardhamānāḥ mandrāḥ modamānāḥ imāḥ giraḥ stutayaḥ upa sthuḥ upatiṣṭhanti |

dhenuṃ na tvā sūyavase dugdhanupa brahmāṇi sasṛje vasiṣṭhaḥ | tvāminme gopartiṃ viśvaṃ āhā na indraḥ sumatiṃ guntavaccha || 4 || dhenum | na | tvā | su'yavase | dugdhan | upa | brahmāṇi | sasṛje | vasiṣṭhaḥ | tvām | it | me | go'patim | viśvaḥ | āha | ā | naḥ | indraḥ | sumatim | guntu | accha ||4||

atha sudāsaḥ śatruḥ ᐁpathaḥ mārgān ᐁpatyamānaḥ gacchan ᐁvartini palāyanamārgaṃ ᐁbheje prāpa ||
āgheṇaṃ cittadekaṃ cakāra siheṃ citpetvainā jaghāna | ava saktīrveśyāvavṛścadindraḥ prāyacchadviśvā bhojanā sudāsai || 17 || āgheṇa | cit | tat | oṃ iti | ekam | cakāra | siham | cit | petvena | jaghāna | ava | saktīḥ | veśyā | avṛścat | indraḥ | pra | ayacchat | viśvā | bhojanā | sudāsai ||17||

na | te | indra̭ | su'ma॒taya̭ḥ | na | rāya̭ḥ | sa॒m'cakṣḙ | pū॒rvīḥ | u॒ṣasa̭ḥ | na | nūnā̭ḥ | de॒va॒kam | ci॒t | mā॒nyamā̭nam | ja॒ghanvā̭n | a॒rva | ta॒manā̭ | bṛ॒hata̭ḥ | śa॒mbara̭m | bhet || 20 ||

pra ye gṛhādarmadamustvayā parāśaraḥ śatayāturvasiṣṭhaḥ | na tai bhojasya sukhyaṃ mṛṣantādhā sūrībhyaḥ sudinā vyucchān || 21 || pra | ye | gṛhāt | armadamuḥ | tvā | parāśaraḥ | śatayātuḥ | vasiṣṭhaḥ | na | tai | bhojasya | sukhyam | mṛṣanta | ardha | sūrībhyaḥ | sudinā | tri | uccchān || 21 ||

dve namudervarvataḥ śate gorddhā rathāṃ vadhūrmantā sudāsaḥ | arhannagre paijavanasya dānaṃ hotaiva sanna paryaimi rebhan || 22 || dve iti | naptuḥ | devavrataḥ | śate iti | goḥ | dvā | rathā | vadhūmantā | sudāsaḥ | arhan | agre | paijavanasya | dānam | hotā iva | sanna | pari | ebhi | rebhan || 22 ||

paijavanasya pivanaputrasya sudāsaḥ rājñaḥ smadiṣṭayaḥ praśatātisarjanīḥ śraddhādidānājñayuktāḥ kṛśininaḥ hiraṇyālaṃkāravantaḥ nireke durgatāau satyām ṛjāsaḥ ṛjugāminaḥ pṛthiviṣṭhāḥ pṛthivyāṃ supratiṣṭhitāḥ dānāḥ deyabhūtāḥ catvāraḥ aśvāḥ tokaṃ putravatpālanīyaṃ māṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ rathe sthitaṃ tokāya tokasya putrasya |

punaḥ mā iti pūraṇaḥ ||
1. kha-samādhiṣṭhitāḥ |
yasya śravo rodasī antarurvvī śīrṣṇe'śīrṣṇe vibbhajā vimuktā | sasedindraṃ na sravato gṛṇanti ni yudhyāmadhimaśiādabhīke || 24 || yasya | śravaḥ | rodasī iti | antaḥ | urvī iti | śīrṣṇe'śīrṣṇe | vi'babhāja | vi'muktā | sat | ita | indram | na | sravataḥ | gṛṇanti | ni | yudhyāmadhim | aśiāt | abhīke ||24|| ^yasya sudāsaḥ ^śravaḥ yaśaḥ ^urvī vistīrṇe ^rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^antaḥ | vistīrṇayo- rdyāvāpṛthivyormadhye vartata ityarthaḥ | yaśca sudāḥ ^vibhaktā dhanasya pradātā ^śīrṣṇe'śīrṣṇe śreṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya ^vibbhaja dhanaṃ pradadau taṃ sudāsaṃ ^saset sasaiva lokāḥ ^indraṃ ^na indramiva ^gṛṇanti stuvanti | kiṃca ^savataḥ nadyaḥ ^abhīke yuddhe ^yudhyāmadhiṃ yudhyāmadhināmakaṃ sapatnaṃ ^ni ^aśiā
sa tvaṃ ᐡsupivatarāyaᐡ atiśayena somābhipavaṃ kurvate janāya ᐡvedaḥ dhanaṃ ᐡprayantā pradātā ᐡasi ||
tṛbhantatvādatra ṣaṣṭhyā abhāvaḥ |
asi ityasyākhyātāsyānudāttatvāt yadvṛttayogādyanudāttatvāsaṃbhavāt yadvṛttayuktākhyātāntaramadhyāhvaya yojanā kṛtā ||
tvaṃ ha tyadindra kutsamāvaḥ śuśrūmāṇastanvā̭ samaryeṃ |
dāsaṃ yacchuṣṇaṃ kurvaṃ nyasmā arandhaya ārjuneyāya śikṣan ||
2 ||
tvam | ha | tyat | indra | kutsam | ātraḥ | śuśrūmāṇaḥ | tanvā̭ | sa'marye | dāsam | yat | śuṣṇam | kurvam | ni | asmai | arandhayaḥ | ārjuneyāya | śikṣan ||2||
he ᐡindra ᐡtvaṃ ᐡha tvaṃ khalu ᐡtyat tadā ᐡtanvā śarīreṇa ᐡśuśrūmāṇaḥ upacaran ᐡsamar

sa॒nā tā tḙ indra॒ bhojanā̭ni rātaha̭vyāya dā॒śuṣḙ su॒dāsḙ|

6||
sanā| tā| te| indra| bhojanāni| rātahavyāya| dāśuṣe| sudāse| vṛṣṇe| te| harī iti| vṛṣaṇā| yunajmi| vyantu| brahmāṇi| puruśāka| vājam||6|| he indra te tava rātahavyāya dattahavyāya dāśuṣe yajamānāya sudāse tā tāni tvayā dattāni bhojanāni bhogyāni dhanāni sanā sanāni sanātanāni babhūvuriti śeṣaḥ| he puruśāka bahukarmannindra vṛṣṇe kāmānāṃ varṣitre te tubhyam| svāmānetumityarthaḥ| vṛṣaṇā vṛṣaṇau harī aśvau yunajmi rathe yojayāmi| brahmāṇi asmadīyāni stotrāṇi vājaṃ balinaṃ tvāṃ vyantu gacchantu||

7||
mā| te| asyām| sahasāvan| pariṣṭhai| aghārya| bhūma| harivaḥ| parādai| trāyasva| naḥ| avṛkebhiḥ| varūthaiḥ| tava| priyāsaḥ| suriṣu| syāma||7|| he sahasāvan balavan harivaḥ harivaścindra te tava asyāṃ stotreṇāsmābhiḥ kriyamāṇāyāṃ pariṣṭhau anveṣaṇāyāṃ parādai parādānāya aghāya a(ā)hantre vayaṃ mā bhūma| kiṃca naḥ asmān avṛkebhiḥ abādhaiḥ varūthaiḥ| vārayantyupadravebhya iti varūthāni rakṣaṇāni| taiḥ trāyasva pāhi| tava suriṣu stotṛṣu madhye vayaṃ priyāsaḥ priyāḥ syāma bhūyāsma||
pri॒yārsa̭ itte॒ magha̭vanni॒bhiṣṭai॒ naro̭ made॒ma śa̭ra॒ṇe sa॒khāya̭:|

8||
priyāsaḥ| it| te| maghavan| abhiṣṭau| naraḥ| madama| śaraṇe| sakhāyaḥ| ni| turvaśam| ni| yādvam| śiśīhi| atithigvāya| śaṃsyam| kariṣyan|| 8|| he maghavan dhanavannindra te tatra abhiṣṭau abhyapaṇe naraḥ stotrāṇāṃ netāro vayaṃ sakhāyaḥ samānakhyātāḥ priyāsaḥ priyāśca santaḥ śaraṇe ita gṛha eva madema modema| kiṃca atithi- gvāya| pūjayātithīn gacchatītyatithigvaḥ| tasmai sudāse divodāsāya vāsmadīyāya rājñe śaṃsyaṃ kariṣyan||
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa- 'tā' nāsti|
2. kha-aghāya; gha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-aghāya ahantre|
3. jha- ta-na-bha-śa- vā madīyāya|
śaṃsanīyaṃ sukhaṃ kariyan kurvan turvaśaṃ rājānaṃ ni śiśīhi vaśaṃ kuru |
yādṛk ca rājānaṃ ni śiśīhītyarthaḥ ||
sadyaścinnu tai maghavanbhittrai naraḥ śaṃsantyukthaśāsa ukthā |
ye te harvebhirvai paṇīradāśansmānvṛṇīṣva yujyāya tasmai ||
9 ||
sadyaḥ | cit | nu | te | maghavan | abhittau | naraḥ | śaṃsanti | uktha'śaṃsaḥ | ukthā | ye | te | harvebhiḥ | vi | paṇīn | adāśan | asmān | vṛṇīṣva | yujyāya | tasmai ||9||
he maghavan dhanavannindra te tava nu atha abhittau abhyepaṇe ye naraḥ ukthaśāsaḥ ukthānāṃ śaṃsitāraḥ ukthā ukthāni śastrāṇi sadyaścit sadya eva śaṃsanti |
kiṃca te tava harvebhiḥ stotraiḥ paṇīn apradānaśīlān vaṇijo'pi vi adāśan |
dhanāni viśeṣeṇādāpayannityarthaḥ |
tān asmān tasmai yujyāya sakhyāya tatsahyamanuvartayituṃ vṛṇīṣva parigṛhāṇa ||
ete stomā nṛṇāṃ nṛtama tumyamasmadrayaśco dadato maghāni |
teṣāmindra vṛtrahatyai śivo bhūḥ sakhā ca śūroṃ'vitā ca nṛṇām ||
10 ||
ete | stomāḥ | nṛṇām | nṛtama | tumyam | asmadrayaḥ | dadataḥ | maghāni | teṣām | indra | vṛtra'hatyai | śivaḥ | bhūḥ | sakhā | ca | śūraḥ | avitā | ca | nṛṇām ||10||
he nṛtama netṛtama indra tumyaṃ narāṃ netṛṇāṃ ye ete stomāḥ saṃghāḥ maghāni mahanīyāni havīṃṣi dadataḥ dadantaḥ asmadrayaḥ asmadabhimukhāḥ abhūvanniti śeṣaḥ |
teṣāṃ nṛṇāṃ vṛtrahatye saṃgrāme śivaḥ kalyāṇakṛt bhūḥ bhava |
sakhā ca bhūḥ |
avitā rakṣitā ca bhūḥ ||
nū indra śūra stavamāna ūtī brahmajūtastanvā vāvṛdhasva |
urpa no vājāṃnmimīhipa stīnyūṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
11 ||
nu | indra | śūra | stavamānaḥ | ūtī | brahma'jūtaḥ | tanvā | vāvṛdhasva | urpa | naḥ | vājān | mimīhi | urpa | stīna | yuyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||11||
he śūra indra nu atha stavamānaḥ asmābhiḥ stūyamānaḥ brahmajūtaḥ brahmaṇā stotreṇa preritaḥ tanvā śarīreṇa utthā rakṣaṇena vāvṛdhasva |
api ca naḥ asmabhyaṃ vājān annāni upa mimīhi |
prayacchetyarthaḥ |
stīn gṛhāṃśca upa mimīhi |
spaṣṭamanyat ||
30 ||
vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan |
pumarthāṃścaturo deyāddidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ ||
iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukabhūpālasāmrājyadhuraṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśe ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye pañcamāṣṭake dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
1. jha- śūraḥ śauryopetaḥ avitā |
2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-jhā- 'ūtyā rakṣaṇena' nāsti |
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat |
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram ||
atha1 tṛtīyo'dhyāya ārabhyate |
‘ugro jajñe’ iti daśarcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
tathā cānukramyate—‘ugro daśa’ iti |
ābhilavike caturthe'hani niṣkevalya etatsūktaṃ nividdhānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘caturthasyogro jajña iti niṣkevalyam’ (āśva. śrau. 7.7) iti |
mahāvrate'pi niṣke- valya etatsūktam |
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake sūtritam—‘ugro jajñe vīryāya svadhāvānudu brahmāṇyaita śravasyā’ (ai. ā. 5. 2. 2.) iti |
saumikacāturmāsyeṣu vaiśvadevasya sthāne prathamaṃ pṛṣṭhyamahaḥ |
tatrāpi2 niṣkevalya etatsūktaṃ nividdhānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘janiṣṭhā ugra ugro jajña iti madhyaṃdinaḥ3’ (āśva. śrau. 9.2) iti ||
ugro jajñe vīryāya svadhāvāścakirapo naryo yatkariṣyan |
jagmiryuvā nṛṣadatamavaubhistrātā na indra enaso mahaścit ||
1 ||
ugraḥ | jajñe | vīryāya | svadhā'vān | cakiḥ | arpaḥ | naryaḥ | yat | kariṣyan | jagmiḥ | yuvā | nṛṣadatam | avaḥ | bhiḥ | trātā | naḥ | indraḥ | enasaḥ | mahaḥ | cit ||1||
˅svadhāvān balavān ˅ugraḥ ojasvī uddūrṇo vā ˅indraḥ ˅vīryāya vīryaṃ kartuṃ ˅jajñe babhūva |
˅naryaḥ narahitaḥ san ˅yat karma ˅kariṣyan bhavati tat ˅apaḥ karma ˅cakiḥ kataiva ˅cakiḥ iti kinpratyayasya liṅṅadbhāvāt ˅na lokāvyayaniṣṭhākhalarthatṛnām’ iti ṣaṣṭhīpratiṣedhaḥ |
api ca ˅nṛṣadataṃ yajñagṛhaṃ ˅yuvā nityataruṇaḥ san ˅avobhiḥ rakṣaṇaiḥ sādhaṃ ˅jagmiḥ gantā ˅mahaścit mahato'pi ˅enasaḥ pāpāt ˅naḥ asmākaṃ ˅trātā rakṣitā ca bhaved ˅||
hantā vṛtramindraḥ śṛṇuvānaḥ prāvīnnu vīro jaritāramūtī |
kartā sudāse aha vā u lokaṃ dātā vasu muhurā dāśuṣe bhūta ||
2 ||

tubhyantvādyatra ṣaṣṭhyabhāvaḥ ||
˅vīraḥ vīraḥ san ˅jaritāraṃ stotāraṃ ˅nu kṣipram ˅ūtī ūtyā rakṣayā ˅prāvīt prārakṣaca |
˅sudāse rājñe ˅lokaṃ janapadaṃ ˅kartā ca |
yadvā |

ihāpyuttaratrāpi tubhyantvāt ṣaṣṭhyabhāvaḥ ||

˅ā iti cārthe ||

tadvān vśūraḥ śauryopetaḥ vjanuṣā janmanā svabhāvata eva vsatrāṣāṭ bahūnāmabhibhavitā vapāḻhaḥ svayaṃ ca kenāpyanabhibhūtaḥ vsvojāḥ subalaḥ vīm ayam vindraḥ vpṛtanāḥ śatrūṇāṃ senāḥ vvyāse vikṣipati |

he vtuviṣmaḥ bahudhana vindra vmahitvā mahattvena vtarvibhīḥ balaiśca vubhe vcit ubhe api vrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau vā vpaprātha āpūritavānasi |
atha parokṣastutiḥ |
vharirvān aśvavān vindraḥ vvajraṃ vni vmimikṣan śatrupu prāpayan vmadeṣu yajñeṣu nivitsu vā vanvasā somena vsam vuvoca saṃsedhyate saṃgacchate vā ||
‘uca samavāye’ iti dhātuḥ |
vvai iti pūraṇaḥ ||

pra yaḥ senānīratha nṛbhyo astīnaḥ satvā gaveṣaṇaḥ sa dhṛṣṇuḥ ||
5 ||
vṛṣā | jajāna | vṛṣaṇam | raṇāya | tam | ū~ itiṃ | cit | nārī | naryam | sasūva | pra | yaḥ | senā'nīḥ | ardha | nṛ'bhyaḥ | asti | inaḥ | satvā | go'eṣaṇaḥ | saḥ | dhṛṣṇuḥ ||5||
vvṛṣā sektā pitā kaśyapaḥ vvṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāramindraṃ vraṇāya yuddhārthaṃ vjajāna |
‘jana janane’ iti dhātuḥ |
vnaryaṃ narahitaṃ vtamu tamevendraṃ vnārī vcit aditirapi vsasūva suṣuve |
vadha api ca vyaḥ vindraḥ vnṛbhyaḥ nṛṇāṃ vsenānīḥ senānāṃ netā san vpra asti prabhavati vsaḥ vindraḥ vinaḥ sarvasya jagata īśvaro bhavati |
‘niyutvān inaḥ’ iti īśvaranāmasu pāṭhāt |
vsatvā śatrūṇāṃ sādhakaśca vgaveṣaṇaḥ gavāmanveṣṭā ca vdhṛṣṇuḥ śatrūṇāṃ dharṣakaśca bhavatīti śeṣaḥ ||
5 ||
nū citsa bhrepate jano na raiṣanmano yo asya ghoramāvivāsāt |
yajñair indre dadhate duvā~si kṣayatsa rāya ṛtapā ṛtejāḥ ||
6 ||
nu | cit | saḥ | bhrepate | janaḥ | na | repat | manaḥ | yaḥ | asya | ghoram | ā'vivāsāt | yajñaiḥ | yaḥ | indrai | dadhate | duvā~si | kṣayat | saḥ | rāye | ṛta'pāḥ | ṛte'jāḥ ||6||
vyaḥ janaḥ vasya indrasya vghoraṃ śatrūṇāṃ bādhakaṃ vmanaḥ vyajñaiḥ vāvivāsāt paricarati vsaḥ vjanaḥ |
vnu iti pratiṣedhe vartate |

bhavedityarthaḥ ||
1. kha-gha-ta-bha-ya-kṣīyate |
yadi̭ndra॒ pūrvo̭ a॒rpāya॒ śikṣa̭nnu॒jjayā॒nkanī̭yaso de॒ṣṇam | a॒mṛta̭ i॒tyarpā̭sī॒ta dū॒ramā ci॒traṃ ci॒tryaṃ bha̭rā ra॒yiṃ na̭ḥ || 7 || yat | indra̭ | pūrva̭: | a॒rpāya̭ | śikṣa̭n | a॒ryat | jyāyā̭n | kanī̭yasaḥ | de॒ṣṇam | a॒mṛta̭: | it | pari̭ | ā॒sī॒t | dū॒ram | ā | ci॒tram | ci॒tryam | bha̭ra | ra॒yim | na̭ḥ ||7||
he citra cāyanīya indra yat dhanaṃ pūrvaḥ pitā jyeṣṭho bhrātā vā aparāya putrāya kanīyase vā śikṣaṇa prayacchan |

2 ||
‘asāvi devam’ iti daśacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
tathā cānukrāntam- ‘asāvi’ iti |
mādhyandinasavane maitrāvaruṇasyokṣīyamānamidaṃ sūktam |

indrasya vṛtraghnaḥ paśau ‘abhikatvā’ iti vapāyā anu- vākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca-‘abhi katvendra bhūradha oṃstvaṃ mahā~ indra tumyaṃ ha kṣāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 3.8) iti ||
asāvi devaṃ goṛjīkamandho nyasminnindro januṣemuvoca |
bodhāmasi tvā haryaśva yajñairbordhā naḥ stomamandhaso madeṣu ||
1 ||
asāvi | devam | go'ṛjīkam | andhaḥ | ni | asmin | indraḥ | januṣā | īm | uvoca | bodhāmasi | tvā | hari'aśva | yajñaiḥ | bodha | naḥ | stomam | andhasaḥ | madeṣu || 1 ||

gavyena miśritamityarthaḥ |

ᵛīm ayam ᵛindraḥ ᵛasmin abhipute somarūpe'ndhasi ᵛjanuṣā svabhāvata eva ᵛni ᵛuvoca nitarāṃ saṃgato bhavati |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |
he ᵛharyaśva ᵛtvā tvāṃ ᵛyajñaiḥ stotrai- rhavirbhirvā ᵛbodhāmasi bodhayāmaḥ |
ᵛandhasaḥ somasya ᵛmadeṣu ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛstomaṃ stotraṃ ᵛbodha budhyasva ca ||
pra yanti yajñaṃ viparyanti barhiḥ somamādau vidathai dudhravācaḥ |
nyu dhriyante yaśaso gṛbhādā dūraupabdo vṛṣaṇo nṛpārvāḥ ||
2 ||

caturthe'hani mādhyandinasavane hotrakaśastreṣu sapta virājau- stṛcān kṛtvaikaikastṛcaḥ śaṃsanīyaḥ |
tatra ‘na te giraḥ’ ityathāśrātasra ṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘na te giro'pi mapye turasya pra vo mahe mahivṛdhe bharadhvam’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 11) iti ||
pibā̭ so॒mamindra̭ mandatu tvā॒ yaṃ tai su॒pāva̭ ha॒ryaśvā̭driḥ | so॒turbā॒hubhyā̭ṃ su॒yato॒ nārvā̭ || 1 || piba̭ | so॒mam | indra̭ | manda̭tu | tvā॒ | yam | te | su॒pāva̭ | ha॒ri'a̭śva | a॒driḥ | so॒tuḥ | bā॒hu'bhyā̭m | su॒'yata̭ḥ | na | a॒rvā || 1 || he indra somaṃ piba | sa somaḥ tvāṃ tvāṃ mandatu mādayatu | he haryaśva te tvadarthaṃ sotuḥ abhiṣavakartuḥ bāhubhyām arvā na raśmibhyām iva suyataḥ suṣṭhu parigṛhītaḥ adriḥ grāvā yaṃ somaṃ supāva ||
yaste॒ mado̭ yu॒jyaścā̭ru॒rasti॒ yena̭ vṛ॒trāṇi̭ ha॒ryaśvā̭ ha॒ṃsi | sa tvā̭mindra॒ prabhṷ̄vaso ma॒mattu || 2 || yaḥ | te | mada̭ḥ | yu॒jyaḥ | cā॒ruḥ | a॒sti | yena̭ | vṛ॒trāṇi̭ | ha॒ri'a̭śva | ha॒ṃsi | saḥ | tvā॒m | indra̭ | pra॒bhū॒va॒so iti̭ pra॒bhu'va̭so | ma॒mattṷ || 2 || he haryaśva te tava yaḥ yujyaḥ anuguṇaḥ cāruḥ samīcīnaḥ madaḥ madakaraḥ somaḥ asti vidyate yena ca pītena somena vṛtrāṇi haṃsi he prabhūvaso prabhūtadhana indra tvāṃ saḥ somaḥ mamattu mādayatu ||
bodhā̭ su॒ me ma̭gha॒vannā̭ca॒memā॒ṃ yāṃ tḙ vasi॒ṣṭho a̭rcati॒ praśa̭stim |
i॒mā brahma̭ sadha॒mādḙ juṣasva ||

1 kha-ta-na-bha-jñā-yaḥ ’ nāsti |
bodha | su | me | maghavan | vācam | ā | imām | yām | te | vasiṣṭhaḥ | arcaiti | pra'śastim | imā | brahma | sudha'mādai | juṣasva || 3 ||
he maghavan dhanavannindra te tava praśastiṃ stutirūpāṃ yāṃ vācaṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ arceti vadati tām imāṃ me vasiṣṭhasya saṃbandhinīṃ vācaṃ su ā bodha suṣṭhu abhibudhyasva |
kiṃca imā imāni brahma brahmāṇi sadhamāde yajñe jupasva sevasva ||
śrudhī havaṃ vipipānasyādrerbodhā viprasyārcaito manīṣām | kṛṣvā duvāṃsyantamā sacemā ||
4 ||
śrudhi | havam | vi'piṣānasya | adreḥ | bodha | viprasya | arcaitaḥ | manīṣām | kṛṣva | duvāṃsi | antamā | sacā | imā || 4 ||
he indra vipipānasya vipivato vipito vā mama adreḥ grāvṇaḥ havam āhvānaṃ śrudhi |

viprasya prājñasya vasiṣṭhasya arcataḥ stuvataḥ manīṣāṃ stutiṃ bodha budhyasva ca |
imā imāni kriyamāṇāni duvāṃsi paricaraṇāni antamā antikatamāni buddhisthāni sacā saha sahāyabhūtaḥ san vā kṛpva kuru ca ||

5 ||

tubhyedimā sarvanā śūra viśvā tubhyaṃ brahmāṇi vardhanā kṛṇomi | tvaṃ nṛbhirhavyairviśvadhāsi || 7 || tubhya | it | imā | sarvanā | śūra | viśvā | tubhyam | brahmāṇi | vardhanā | kṛṇomi | tvam | nṛ'bhiḥ | havyaiḥ | viśvadhā | asi || 7 || he vśūra vtubhyat tubhyameva vimā imāni vviśvā viśvāni vsavanā somābhiṣavaṇāni mayā kriyanta iti śeṣaḥ | vtubhyaṃ tvadarthameva vvardhanā vardhanāni vbrahmāṇi stotrāṇi vkṛṇomi karomi | vtvam eva vnṛbhiḥ yajñānāṃ netṛbhiḥ vviśvadhā sarvaprakāraiḥ vhavyaḥ hrātavyaḥ stutyo vā vasi ||

6 ||
‘udu brahmāṇi’ iti ṣaḍ‌caṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
anukramyate ca— ‘udu pad’ iti |
agniṣṭome mādhyaṃdine savane brāhmaṇācchaṃsiśastra etatsūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘udu brahmāṇyu- jīṣī vajrī vṛṣabhasturāpāliti yājyā’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 16 ) iti |
cāturviṃśike'hani mādhyaṃdina- savane brāhmaṇācchaṃsiśastra etadaharahaḥśaṃsyasaṃjñakaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘udu brahmāṇyabhi taṣṭevetītarāvaha- rahaḥśasye’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 4 ) iti |
ahargaṇeṣu dvitīyādipvahaḥsu etadeva sūktam |
mahāvrate'pi niṣkevalya etatsūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘udu brahmāṇyaita śravasyā te maha indrotyuyeti pañca sūktāni ’ ( ai. ā. 5. 2. 2 ) iti ||
udu brahmāṇyaita śravasyendraṃ samūrye mahayā vasiṣṭha | ā yo viśvāni śavasā tānaupaśrotā m īrvato vacāṃsi || 1 || ut | ū~ iti | brahmāṇi | aita | śravasyā | indram | su'mūrye | mahaya | vasiṣṭha | ā | yaḥ | viśvāni | śavasā | tāna | upa'śrotā | me | īrvataḥ | vacāṃsi || 1 ||
vśravasyā asecchayā vbrahāṇi stotrāṇi havīṃṣi ca indrārtham vut vairata' sarva ṛpatha iti śeṣa: |
vu iti pūraṇa: |
he vvasiṣṭha svamapi vsamarye yajñe vindraṃ vmahaya stotreṇa haviṣā ca pūjaya |
api ca vya: indra: vviśvāni bhuvanāni vśavasā balena vā vtatāna sa:2 vīṃvata: upagamanavata: vme mama vvacāṃsi stutirūpāṇi vākyāni vupaśrotā bhavatu ||
ayāmi ghoṣa indra devajāmiririjayanta yacchurudho vivāci |
nuhi svamāyuścikite janeṣu tānīdahāṃsyati parṣyasmān ||
2 ||
ayāmi | ghoṣa: | indra | deva'jāmi: | irajyanta | yat | śurudha: | vi'vāci | nuhi | svam | āyu: | cikite | janeṣu | tāni | it | ahaṃsi | ati | parṣi | asmān ||2||
vyat yadā vśurudha: |
śucaṃ rudhantīti śurudha oṣadhya: |
virajyanta vardhante tadā he vindra tvadartha vvivāci stotari vdevajāmi: devānāṃ bandhu: vghoṣa: |
stutirūpa: śabdo ghopa: |
vayāmi akāri |
api ca vjaneṣu madhye kenāpi vsvamāyu: svajjīvitaṃ3 vnahi vcikite na jñāyate |
yai: vāyu: kṣīyate vtānīt tāni sarvāṇyeva vaṃhaṃsi pāpāni vasmān vati vparṣi atipāraya ||
yuje rathe gaveṣaṇaṃ haribhyāmupa brahmāṇi jujuṣāṇamasthu: |
vi bādhiṣṭa sya rodasī mahitvendro vṛtrāṇyaprati jaghanvān ||
3 ||
yuje | ratham | go'evarṇam | hari'bhyām | upa | brahmāṇi | jujuṣāṇam | asthu: | vi | bādhiṣṭa | sya | rodasī iti | mahi'tvā | indra: | vṛtrāṇi | aprati | jaghanvān ||3||
vgaveṣaṇaṃ gavāṃ prāpakamandrasya vrathaṃ vharibhyām indravāhābhyāṃ vyuje stotrairahaṃ yunajmi |
vbrahāṇi stotrāṇi vjujuṣāṇaṃ parivārai: sevyamānamindram vupa vasthu: upatiṣṭhanta |
vsya: so'ya- mindra: vmahitvā mahattvena vrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau vvi vbādhiṣṭa vyabādhiṣṭa ca |
api ca vindra: vvṛtrāṇi śatrūna apratidvandvāni5 vjaghanvān hatavān ||
ārpaśrcitpippu: staryo na gāvo nakṣata ṛtama jaritāraste indra |
yāhi vāyurna niyuto no acchā tvaṃ hi dhībhirdayase vi vājān ||
4 ||
ārpa: | cit | pippu: | starya: | na | gāve: | nakṣan | ṛtam | jaritāra: | te | indra | yāhi | vāyu: | na | ni'yuta: | na: | accha | tvam | hi | dhībhi: | dayase | vi | vājān ||4||
he vindra svaprasādāt vāpaśrcit āpa: vstaryo vna vgāva: staryo vaśā gāva iya vpippu: vardhantām |
aprasūtā gāvo māṃsalā bhavanti hi |
vte tava vjaritāra: stotāraśca vṛtam udakaṃ vnakṣan vyāpnuvan |
api ca tvaṃ vna: asmān vniyuta: vāyurna vāyumiva vaccha vyāhi abhiyāhi |
vtvaṃ vhi vdhībhi: prajñābhi: karmabhirvā vvājān annāni vvi vdayase stotṛbhya: prayacchasi ||
1. jha-airata preritavantaḥ | 2. ta-vyāptavān | 3. ta-na-bha-śa-svaṃ jīvitam | 4. kha-bha- sevyamindram | 5. kha-apratidvandvino; jha-pratidvandvāni; na-bha-iti dvandāni |

eko devatrā dayase hi martīnsminśūra sarvane mādayasva |

he 'indra 'tvā tvāṃ 'te ete 'madāḥ madakarāḥ somāḥ 'mādayantu |

dayatiranukampārthaḥ |

'vasiṣṭhāsaḥ vasiṣṭhāḥ 'vajrabāhuṃ vajrakalpabāhuṃ 'vṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāram 'indram 'evet uktena prakāreṇaiva 'arkaiḥ arcanīyaiḥ stotraiḥ 'abhyarcanti abhipūjayanti |

7 |
‘yoniṣṭa indra sadane akāri’ iti ṣaḍ ṛcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
anukramyate ca—‘yoniḥ’ iti |
mahāvrate niṣkevalya etatsūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘yoniṣṭa indra sadane akārītye- tasya catasraḥ śasvottamāmupasaṃtatyopottamayā paridadhāti’ (ai. ā. 5. 3. 1) iti |
yoniṣṭa indra sadane akāri tamā nṛbhiḥ puruhūta pra yāhi |
aso yathā no'vitā vṛdhe ca dado vasūni mamadaśca saumaiḥ |
1 |
yoniḥ | te | indra | sadane | akāri | tam | ā | nṛ'bhiḥ | puru'hūta | pra | yāhi | asaḥ | yathā | naḥ | avitā | vṛdhe | ca | dadaḥ | vasūni | mamadaḥ | ca | saumaiḥ || 1 ||
he 'indra 'te tava 'sadane sadanārthaṃ 'yoniḥ sthānam 'akāri |
he 'puruhūta 'nṛbhiḥ marudbhiḥ sārdhaṃ 'taṃ yonim 'ā 'pra yāhi |
'naḥ asmākaṃ 'yathā 'avitā rakṣitā 'asaḥ bhavasi no'smākaṃ 'vṛdhe vardhanāya 'ca asaḥ |
tathā ca 'vasūni 'dadaḥ asmabhyaṃ dehi |
'saumaiḥ asmadīyaiḥ 'mamadaḥ mādayasva 'ca |
gṛbhītaṃ te mana indra dvibarhāḥ sutaḥ somaḥ pariṣiktā madhūni |
visṛṣṭadhenā bharate suvṛktiryamindraṃ johuvatī manīṣā |
2 |
1. kha-na-bha-śa-goyutam; ta-goyutaṃ kam |
gṛbhītam | te | manaḥ | indra | dvi'barhāḥ | sutaḥ | somaḥ | pari'siktā | madhūni | visṛṣṭa'dhenā | bharate | su'vaktiḥ | iyam | indram | johuvatī | manīṣā || 2 || he ᵥindra ᵥdvibarhāḥ | ṣaṣṭhyarthe prathamā | dvibarhaso dvayoḥ sthānayoḥ 1 paribṛḍhasya ᵥte tava ᵥmanaḥ ᵥgṛbhītam asmābhiḥ parigṛhītam | ᵥsomaḥ ca ᵥsutaḥ abhiputaḥ | ᵥmadhūni ca ᵥpariṣiktā pātreṣu pariṣiktāni | ᵥvisṛṣṭadhenā visṛṣṭajihvā madhyamasvareṇoccāryamāṇā ᵥsuvaktiḥ susamāptiḥ ᵥiyaṃ ᵥmanīṣā stutiḥ ᵥindraṃ ᵥjohuvatī bhṛśamāhvayantī ᵥbharate saṃbhriyate ca ||
ā nau diva ā pṛthivyā ṛjīṣinnidaṃ barhiḥ saumapeyāya yāhi | vahantu tvā harayo mayaścamāṅgupamacchā tavasaṃ madāya || 3 || ā | naḥ | divaḥ | ā | pṛthivyāḥ | ṛjīṣin | idam | barhiḥ | soma'peyāya | yāhi | vahantu | tvā | harayaḥ | mayaścam | āṅgupam | accha | tavasam | madāya || 3 || he ᵥṛjīṣin indra ᵥnaḥ asmākam ᵥidaṃ ᵥbarhiḥ imaṃ yajñaṃ ᵥsomapeyāya ᵥdivaḥ svargāt ᵥā ᵥyāhi āgaccha | ᵥpṛthivyāḥ antarikṣācca | 'āpaḥ pṛthivī' ityantarikṣanāmasu pāṭhāt | ᵥā yāhi | api ca ᵥtavasaṃ pravṛddhaṃ balavantaṃ vā ᵥmayaścaṃ madabhimukhaṃ ᵥtvā tvām ᵥāṅgupaṃ stotram ᵥaccha abhi ᵥmadāya madārtha ᵥharayaḥ aśvāḥ ᵥvahantu ||
ā no viśvābhirūtibhiḥ sajoṣā brahma juṣāṇo haryaśva yāhi | varīvṛtastathvirebhiḥ suśigrāsme dadhadṛṣaṇaṃ śuṣmamindra || 4 || ā | naḥ | viśvābhiḥ | ūti'bhiḥ | su'joṣāḥ | brahma | juṣāṇaḥ | hari'aśva | yāhi | varīvṛtat | sthavirebhiḥ | su'śipra | asme iti | dadhat | vṛṣaṇam | śuṣmam | indra ||4|| he ᵥharyaśva harināmakaaśva ᵥsuśipra śobhanahano ᵥindra ᵥviśvābhiḥ sarvābhiḥ ᵥūtibhiḥ rakṣābhiḥ ᵥsajoṣāḥ saṃgata: ᵥsthavirebhiḥ vṛddhairahadbhiḥ saha ᵥvarīvṛtat śatrūn bhṛśaṃ hiṃsan ᵥasme asmabhyaṃ ᵥvṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāraṃ ᵥśuṣmaṃ balavantaṃ putraṃ ᵥdadhat prayacchan ᵥbrahma stotraṃ ᵥjuṣāṇaḥ sevamānaḥ ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥā ᵥyāhi ||
eṣa stomo maha ugrāya vāhai dhurī'vātyo na vājayenadhāyi | indre tvāyamarka īṭṭe vasūnāṃ divīva ghāmadhi naḥ śromataṃ dhāḥ || 5 || eṣaḥ | stomaḥ | mahe | ugrāya | vāhai | dhuri'iva | atyaḥ | na | vājayen | adhāyi | indra | tvā | ayam | arkaḥ | īṭṭe | vasūnām | divi'iva | ghām | adhi | naḥ | śromatam | dhāḥ ||5|| ᵥmahe mahate ᵥugrāya udgūrṇāya ojasvine vā ᵥvāhai viśvasya voḍhe indrāya ᵥdhurīva rathasya ᵥatyo ᵥna aśva iva ᵥvājayen balaṃ kurvan ᵥeṣa ᵥstomaḥ ᵥadhāyi vyadhāyi | atha pratyakṣastutiḥ | he ᵥindra ᵥtvāṃ ᵥayamakaḥ stotā ᵥvasūnāṃ vasūni dhanāni ᵥīṭṭe yācate sa tvaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmāsu ᵥyāṃ ᵥdivīva ᵥśromataṃ śravaṇīyamannaṃ putraṃ vā ᵥadhi ᵥdhāḥ adhidhehi ||
1. śa-sthānayoḥ |
tathā ca nighaṇṭu-'parivṛḍho madhyame ca sthāne uttame ca' ( nigha. 6. 17 ) |
2. śa-prayacchaṃstvam |
evā ne indra vāryasya pūrdhi pra tai mahīṃ sumatiṃ vevidāma |
iṣaṃ pinva maghavadbhyaḥ suvīrāṃ yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
6 ||
eva | naḥ | indra | vāryasya | pūrdhi | pra | te | mahīṃ | su'matim | vevidāma | iṣam | pinva | maghavat'bhyaḥ | su'vīrām | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||6||
he indra naḥ asmān eva evaṃ vāryasya |
tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
varaṇīyena dhanena pūrdhi pūraya |
te tava mahīṃ mahatīṃ sumatim anugrahabuddhiṃ vevidāma bhṛśaṃ labhemahi |
maghavadbhyaḥ haviṣmadbhyo- 'smabhyaṃ suvīrāṃ śobhanaputrādiyutām iṣam annaṃ pinva prayacchetyarthaḥ |
spaṣṭamanyat ||
8 ||
‘ā te maha indra ’ iti ṣaṭkamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ā te ’ ityanukrāntam |
mahāvrate niṣkevalya idamādīni pañca sūktāni |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā te maha indrotyumeti pañca sūktāni ’ (ai. ā. 5. 2. 2) iti ||
ā tai maha indrotyugra samanyavo yatsamarantu senāḥ |
patāti dvidyut naryasya bāhmormā te manau viśvadṛk vi cārīt ||
1 ||
ā | te | mahaḥ | indra | ūtī | ugra | sa'manyavaḥ | yat | sam | aranta | senāḥ | patāti | dvidyut | naryasya | bāhroḥ | mā | te | manau | viśva'dṛk | vi | cārīt || 1 ||
he ugra udbhaṭa ojasvin indra yat yadā samanyavaḥ |
samo manpurabhimāno yāsāṃ tāḥ samanyavaḥ |
senāḥ samaranta yudhyante saṃgacchante vā tadā naryasya narahitasya mahaḥ mahataḥ te tava bāhroḥ sthitā dvidyut āyudham |
‘dvidyut hetiḥ ’ iti vajranāmasu pāṭhāt |
ūtī ūthai asmadrakṣāyai ā patāti āpatatu |
tava viśvadṛk viśvagantṛ manaḥ ca mā vi cārīt asmātkṣetra sthiraṃ bhavatu ||
ni durga indra śrathihyamitrāṃnibhe ye no martīso amanti |
āre taṃ śaṃsaṃ kṛṇuhi ninitsorā nau bhara saṃbharaṇaṃ vadhrnām ||
2 ||
ni | duḥ'ge | indra | śrathihi | amitrān | abhi | ye | naḥ | martīsaḥ | amanti | āre | tam | śaṃsam | kṛṇuhi | ninitsoḥ | ā | naḥ | bhara | sam'bharaṇam | vasūnām ||2||
he indra durge yuddhe ye martīsaḥ martāḥ abhi abhimukhāḥ santaḥ naḥ asmān amanti abhibhavanti tān amitrān śatrūn ni śrathihi nijahi |
api ca ninitsoḥ asmānninditu- micchato narasya taṃ śaṃsam āśaṃsanam āre dūre kṛṇuhi kuru |
api ca naḥ asmabhyaṃ vasūnāṃ dhanānāṃ saṃbharaṇaṃ samūham ā bhara āhara ||
śataṃ tai śipiviṣṭayaḥ sudāse sahasraṃ śaṃsā uta ratirastu |
jahi vardharvanuṣo martyasyāsme ghumadhi ratnaṃ ca dhehi ||
3 ||
1. jha-yuddhārthaṃ saṃgacchante; ta-śa-yuddhārthaṃ saṃgacchante |
2. tara-te tava |
śatam | te | śiprin | ūtayaḥ | sudāse | sahasram | śaṃsāḥ | uta | rātiḥ | astu | jahi | vadhaḥ | vanurṣaḥ | martyasya | asme iti | dyumnam | adhi | ratnam | ca | dhehi ||3||
he śipren uṣṇīpiṣkindra te tvadīyāḥ śataṃ bahvyaḥ ūtayaḥ rakṣāḥ sudāse śobhanadānāya mahyaṃ santu |
sahasraṃ śaṃsāḥ śaṃsanīyāḥ kāmāśca santu |
uta api ca rātiḥ dhanam astu |
vanupaḥ hiṃsakasya martyasya vadhaḥ hiṃsāsādhanamāyudhaṃ ca jahi |
api ca asme adhi asmāsu dyumnaṃ dīptimadannaṃ yaśo vā ratnaṃ ca dhehi |
tathā ca yāskaḥ—‘dyumnaṃ dyotateryaśo vā annaṃ vā |
asme dyumnamadhi ratnaṃ ca dhehi |
asmāsu dyumnaṃ ca ratnaṃ ca dhehi’ (niha. 5. 5) iti ||
tvāṃrvato hīndra kratve asmi tvāṃrvato'vituḥ śūra rātau |
viśve'hāni taviṣīva ugraṃ orkaḥ kṛṇuṣva harivo na mardhīḥ ||
4||
tvā'rvataḥ | hi | indra | kratvai | asmi | tvā'rvataḥ | avituḥ | śūra | rātau | viśvā | it | ahāni | taviṣīvaḥ | ugra | orkaḥ | kṛṇuṣva | harivaḥ | na | mardhīḥ ||4||
he indra tvāvataḥ tvatsadṛśasya kratve karmaṇe asmi bhavāmi hi |
he śūra avituḥ viśvasya rakṣituḥ tvāvataḥ tvatsadṛśasya rātau dāne cāsmīti śeṣaḥ |
he taviṣīvaḥ balavan ugra ojasviñchindra viśvet viśvānyeva ahāni okaḥ asmākaṃ sthānaṃ kṛṇuṣva kuru |
he harivaḥ harivan na mardhīḥ asmān hiṃsyāḥ ||
kutsā ete haryaśvāya śuṣamindre sahau devajūtamiyānāḥ |
satrā kṛdhi suhanā śūra vutrā vayaṃ tarutrāḥ sanuyāma vājam ||
5 ||
kutsāḥ | ete | hari'aśvāya | śuṣam | indrai | sahaḥ | deva'jūtam | iyānāḥ | satrā | kṛdhi | su'hanā | śūra | vutrā | vayam | tarutrāḥ | sanuyāma | vājam ||5||
ete vayaṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ haryaśvāya harināmakaśvāyendrāya śuṣaṃ sukhakaraṃ stotraṃ kutsāḥ kurvāṇāḥ |
karoteḥ kutsāśabdaniṣpattiḥ |
indre devajūtaṃ devaiḥ preritaṃ sahaḥ balam iyānāḥ yācamānāḥ tarutrāḥ durgāṇi tīrṇāḥ santaḥ vājaṃ balaṃ sanuyāma labhemahi |
api ca he śūra vṛtrā vṛtrāṇi śatrūn suhanā hantuṃ suśakāni satrā sarvadā kṛdhi kuru ||
evā ne indra vāyryasya pṛdhi pra tai mahīṃ sumatiṃ vevidāma |
iṣaṃ pinva maghavadbhyaḥ suvīrāṃ yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
6 ||
eva | naḥ | indra | vāyryasya | pṛdhi | pra | te | mahīm | su'matim | vevidāma | iṣam | pinva | maghavadbhyaḥ | su'vīrām | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||6||
iyaṃ vyākhyātacarā ||
9 ||
‘na soma indram’ iti pañcamaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
tathā cānukramyate— na somaḥ pañca’ iti |
mahāvrata ukto viniyogaḥ ||
1. śa-tejasvicindra |
na soma̭ indra॒masṷto ma॒māda॒ nābra̭hmaṇo ma॒dhavā̭naṃ su॒tāsa̭: | tasmā̭ u॒kthaṃ ja̭naye॒ yajjṷjoṣa॒nnavī̭yaḥ śṛ॒ṇvadha॒thā na̭: || 1 || na | soma̭: | indra̭m | asṷtaḥ | ma॒māda̭ | na | a॒bra̭hmaṇaḥ | ma॒gha'vā̭nam | su॒tāsa̭: | tasmai̭ | u॒ktham | ja॒na॒ye॒ | yat | ju॒joṣa̭t | nṛ॒'vat | na॒vī̭yaḥ | śṛ॒ṇvat | yathā̭ | na̭ḥ || 1 ||
maghavānaṃ dhanavantam indram asutaḥ nābhiputaḥ somaḥ na mamāda na tarpayati |
sutāsaḥ abhiputā api somāḥ abrahmaṇaḥ stotrahīnāḥ na mamaduḥ |
mamāda ityetadākhyātaṃ bahuvacanāntatayā vipariṇataṃ sadatra sambadhyate |
ata eva trivapi savaneṣu pāvamānaiḥ stotraiḥ stutā eva somā hūyante |
api ca naḥ asmadīyaṃ yat uktham indraḥ jujoṣat seveta yathā ca nṛvat rājevādareṇa śṛṇvat śṛṇuyāt tathā navīyaḥ navataram ukthaṃ śastraṃ tasmai indrāya janaye |
paṭhāmītyarthaḥ ||
u॒ktha'ṷkthe soma̭ indraṃ ma॒māda॒ nīthḙnīthe ma॒dhavā̭naṃ su॒tāsa̭: | yadī̭ṃ sa॒bādha̭: pi॒tara॒ṃ na pu॒trāḥ sa॒māna॒dakṣā̭ a॒vasḙ ha॒vantḙ || 2 || u॒kthe'ṷkthe | soma̭: | indra̭m | ma॒māda̭ | nīthḙ'nīthe | ma॒gha'vā̭nam | su॒tāsa̭: | yat | īm | sa॒'bādha̭: | pi॒tara̭m | na | pu॒trāḥ | sa॒māna'da̭kṣāḥ | a॒vasḙ | ha॒vantḙ || 2 ||
yat yasmāt ukthe'ukthe śastraśastre kriyamāṇe somaḥ maghavānam indraṃ mamāda mādayati nīthenīthe stotrestotre kriyamāṇe sutāsaḥ abhiputāḥ somāḥ mādayanti tasmāt īm enamindraṃ sabādhaḥ parasparaṃ militāḥ samānadakṣāḥ samānotsāhā ṛtvijaḥ putrāḥ pitaraṃ na pitaramiva avase tarpāya svarakṣaṇāya vā havante |
śastraiḥ stotraiśca stuvanti ||
cukāra̭ tā kṛ॒ṇava̭nbhūna॒manyā॒ yāni̭ bruvanti vedha̭saḥ su॒teṣṷ | janī̭riva॒ pati̭rekaḥ sa॒māno॒ ni mā̭mṛje pu॒ra indra̭: su sa॒rvāḥ || 3 || cukāra̭ | tā | kṛ॒ṇava̭t | nu॒nam | a॒nyā | yāni̭ | bruvanti̭ | vedha̭saḥ | su॒teṣṷ | janī̭:'iva | pati̭: | eka̭: | sa॒māna̭: | ni | ma॒mṛ॒je | pu॒ra: | indra̭: | su | sa॒rvāḥ || 3 ||
vedhasaḥ stotrāṇāṃ vidhātāraḥ suteṣu someṣvabhiputeṣu yāni karmāṇi bruvanti tāni vṛtravadhādīni karmāṇīndraḥ pūrvasmin kāle cakāra |
nūnaṃ sampratyapi anyā anyāni karmāṇi kṛṇavat kuryāt |
api ca saḥ indraḥ sarvāḥ puraḥ śatrungarīḥ samānaḥ samavṛttiḥ ekaḥ asahāyaḥ patiḥ janīriva jāyā iva su ni māmṛje samyak śodhayet ||
e॒vā ta̭rmāhu॒ta śṛ॒ṇva indra॒ eko̭ vi॒bhaktā॒ tara̭ṇi॒rdhanā̭nām |
mi॒thastura̭ ū॒tayo॒ yasya̭ pu॒rvī ra॒sme bha॒drāṇi̭ sa॒śvata॒ priyā̭ṇi ||
4 ||
1. kha-na-bha-navam |
2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa- 'ekaḥ asahāyaḥ' nāsti |
eva | tam | āhuḥ | uta | śṛṇve | indraḥ | eke | vi'bhaktā | taraṇiḥ | maghānām | mithaḥ'turaḥ | ūtayaḥ | yasya | pūrvīḥ | asme iti | bhadrāṇi | saśvata | priyāṇi || 4 ||
^yasya indrasya ^mithaḥ parasparaṃ ^turaḥ bādhamānāḥ saṃśliṣṭā vā ^pūrvīḥ pūrvyo bahvayaḥ ^ūtayaḥ rakṣāḥ santi ^tam ^eva evamuktaguṇam ^āhuḥ pūrve ṛṣayaḥ |
^uta api cācāpi saḥ ^indraḥ ^maghānāṃ maṃhanīyānāṃ dhanānāṃ ^vibhaktā dātā iti ^taraṇiḥ āpadastārayiteti ^śṛṇve śrūyate |
tasya ca prasādāt ^asme asmān ^priyāṇi ^bhadrāṇi kalyāṇāni ^saśvata sevantām ||
evā vasiṣṭha indramūtaye nṛnkṛṣṭīnāṃ vṛṣabhaṃ sute gṛṇāhi |
sahasriṇa urṣa no māhi vājānyūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
5 ||
eva | vasiṣṭhaḥ | indram | ūtayai | nṝn | kṛṣṭīnām | vṛṣabham | sute | gṛṇāti | sahasriṇaḥ | urṣa | naḥ | māhi | vājān | yuyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||5||
^vasiṣṭhaḥ ^nṝn nṝṇām |
patyarthe dvitīyā |
^ūtaye rakṣāyai ^kṛṣṭīnāṃ prajānāṃ ^vṛṣabhaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāram ^indram ^eva evaṃ pūrvoktaprakāreṇa ^gṛṇāti stauti |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |
haṃ indra ^naḥ asmabhyaṃ ^sahasriṇaḥ sahasrasaṃkhyākān ^vājān annāni ^upa ^māhi |
prayacchetyarthaḥ |
spaṣṭa- manyat ||
10 ||
‘indraṃ naraḥ’ iti pañcarcaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘indraṃ naraḥ’ ityanukrāntam |
mahāvrate niṣkevalya etatsūtamuktaṃ tṛtīyatvena |
aindre paśau vapāpuroḍāśahaviṣāmādyāstisraḥ krame ānu- vākyāḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘indraṃ naro nemadhitā havanta ityuhaṃ’ no lokamanu nepi vidvān’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
indraṃ narau nemadhitā havante yatpārthā yunajate dhiyastāḥ |
śūro nṛpātā śavasaścakāna ā gormati vraje bhajā tvaṃ naḥ ||
1 ||
indram | naraḥ | nema'dhitā | havante | yat | pārthāḥ | yunajate | dhiyaḥ | tāḥ | śūraḥ | nṛ'sātā | śavasaḥ | cakānaḥ | ā | goḥ'mati | vraje | bhaja | tvam | naḥ || 1 ||
^yat yadā ^pārthāḥ yuddhabharaṇanimittāḥ ^tāḥ prasiddhāḥ ^dhiyaḥ karmāṇi ^yunajate prayujyante tadā ^naraḥ netāraḥ yam ^indraṃ ^nemadhitā nemadhitau saṃgrāme ^havante hūyanti saḥ ^tvaṃ ^śūraḥ ^nṛpātā nṝṇāṃ saṃbhaktā ca ^śavasaḥ balasya balaṃ ^cakānaḥ kāmayamānaśca san ^gomati^ vraje goṣṭhe gosamūhe ^naḥ asmān ^ā ^bhaja prāpaya^ ||
1. jha-iti tisra ukm |
2. kha-jha-na-bha-śa- ‘tadā naro netāro yamindraṃ havante; ta-ya-nemadhitā nemadhitau saṃgrāme naraḥ karmaṇāṃ netāra indraṃ havanta āhvayantītyarthaḥ |
yamindraṃ havante |
3. kha-jha-bha-śa- ‘gomati...prāpaya’ nāsti; gha- naḥ asmākaṃ gomati goyukte vraje goṣṭhe goptamābhaja saṃrakṣa; jha-gomati gāvaḥ santyasminniti gomat tasmin vraje goṣṭhe samūhe no'smānābhaja prāpaya; tara-gomati gāvaḥ santyasminniti gomat vraje goṣṭhe samūhe no'smānā bhaja dehītyarthaḥ |
ya indra śuṣmoṃ maghavante asti śikṣa sakhibhyaḥ puruhūta nṛbhyaḥ | tvaṃ hi dṛlhā maghavannicaitā apā vṛdhi parivṛtaṃ na rādhaḥ || 2 || yaḥ | indra | śuṣmaiḥ | magha'van | te | asti | śikṣa | sakhibhyaḥ | puru'huta | nṛbhyaḥ | tvam | hi | dṛlhā | magha'van | vi'caitāḥ | apa | vṛdhi | pari'vṛtam | na | rādhaḥ ||2|| he puruhūta bahubhirāhūta indra te tava yaḥ śuṣmaḥ balam asti taṃ śuṣmaṃ sakhibhyaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ nṛbhyaḥ śikṣa dehi | api ca he maghavan hi yasmāt dṛlhā dṛḍhāni | purāṃ dvārāṇi bibheditheti śeṣaḥ | tasmāt saḥ tvaṃ vicaitāḥ viviktaprajñaḥ san parivṛtaṃ tirohitaṃ rādhaḥ dhanam apa vṛdhi asmabhyamapavṛṇu | na iti saṃgrahārthe ||
indro rājā jagataścarṣaṇīnāmadhi kṣami viṣṭurūpaṃ yadasti | tatau dadāti dāśuṣe vasūni codadrādha upastutaścidvāk || 3 || indraḥ | rājā | jagataḥ | carṣaṇīnām | adhi | kṣami | viṣṭu'rūpam | yat | asti | tataḥ | dadāti | dāśuṣe | vasūni | codat | rādhaḥ | upa'stutaḥ | cit | arvāk || 3 || saḥ indraḥ jagataḥ jaṅgamasya paścāderyataḥ rājā īśvaro bhavati carṣaṇīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ca rājā bhavati adhi kṣami kṣamāyām viṣṭurūpaṃ nānārūpaṃ yat dhanam asti tasyāpi rājā bhavati tataḥ dāśuṣe yajamānāya vasūni dhanāni dadāti | sa indro'smābhiḥ upastutaḥ eva san rādhaḥ dhanam arvāk asmadabhimukhaṃ codat prerayatu ||
nū ciṃnna indro maghavā sahūtī dāno vājaṃ ni yamate na ūtī | anūnā yasya dakṣiṇā pīpāya vāmaṃ nṛbhyo abhīvītā sakhibhyaḥ || 4 || nu | cit | naḥ | indraḥ | magha'vā | sa'hūtī | dānaḥ | vājam | ni | yamate | naḥ | ūtī | anūnā | yasya | dakṣiṇā | pīpāya | vāmam | nṛbhyaḥ | abhi'vītā | sakhibhyaḥ || 4 || maghavā dhanavān dānaḥ dadānaḥ saḥ indraḥ naḥ asmākaṃ sahūtī sahūtyā mahadbhiḥ sahāhvānena vājam annaṃ naḥ asmabhyaṃ ūtī ūtyai rakṣāyai nū cit kṣiprameva ni yamate prayacchatu | yasya indrasya anūnā sampūrṇā abhivītā abhiprāptā dakṣiṇā dānaṃ sakhibhyaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ nṛbhyaḥ vāmaṃ vananīyaṃ dhanaṃ pīpāya dogdhi ||
nū indra rāye varivaskṛdhī na ā te manau vavṛtyāma maghāya | gomadaśvāvadrathavadvṛdhanto yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 5 || nu | indra | rāye | varivaḥ | kṛdhi | naḥ | ā | te | manaḥ | vavṛtyāma | maghāya | go'mat | aśva'vat | ratha'vat | vyantaḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||5||
1. kha-bha- puradvārāṇi |
2. kha-kṣa-tajña-na-ma-śa- yaḥ |
he ᵛindra ᵛnaḥ asmabhyaṃ ᵛrāye dhanaprāsaye ᵛnu kṣipraṃ ᵛvarivaḥ dhanam |
‘vedo varivaḥ’ iti dhananāmasu pāṭhāt |
tvaṃ ᵛkṛdhi dehi |
vayaṃ ᵛte tava ᵛmanaḥ ᵛmaghāya maṃhanīyāya stutvā ᵛā ᵛvakṣyāma āvatayāma |
spaṣṭamanyat ||
11 ||
‘brahma ṇa indra’ iti pañcarcemekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘brahma ṇaḥ’ ityanukrāntam |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye pañcamasvenāsya sūktasya viniyoga uktaḥ |
aṣṭame'hani praugaśāstra ādyasṛca aindraḥ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 10 ) ||
brahma ṇa indropa yāhi vidvānarvāñcaste harayaḥ santu yuktāḥ | viśve ciddhi tvā vihavanta martā asmākamicchṛṇuhi viśvaminva || 1 || brahma | naḥ | indra | upa | yāhi | vidvān | arvāñcaḥ | te | harayaḥ | santu | yuktāḥ | viśve | cit | hi | tvā | vi'havanta | martāḥ | asmākam | ita | śṛṇuhi | viśvam'inva || 1||
he ᵛindra tvaṃ ᵛvidvān jānan ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛbrahma stotraṃ ᵛupa ᵛyāhi |
ᵛte tava ᵛharayaḥ aśvāśca ᵛarvāñcaḥ asmadabhimukhāḥ ᵛyuktāḥ ᵛsantu |
he ᵛviśvaminva viśvaprīṇayitarindra ᵛsvā tvāṃ ᵛviśve sarve ᵛmartāḥ manuṣyāḥ ᵛcit ᵛhi yadyapi ᵛvihavanta pṛthaghavante tathāpi ᵛasmākam asmākameva havaṃ ᵛśṛṇuhi śṛṇu ||
haveṃ ta indra mahimā vyānd brahma yatpāṃsi śavasinṛṣīṇām | ā yadyajñaṃ dadhiṣe hastaṃ ugra ghoraḥ sanakratvā janiṣṭhā arṣāḻhaḥ || 2 || havam | te | indra | mahimā | vi | ānud | brahma | yat | pāṃsi | śavasin | ṛṣīṇām | ā | yat | vajram | dadhiṣe | hastai | ugra | ghoraḥ | san | kratvā | janiṣṭhāḥ | arṣāḻhaḥ ||2||
he ᵛśavasin balavan ᵛindra ᵛyat yadā ᵛṛṣīṇāṃ ᵛbrahma stotraṃ ᵛpāsa rakṣakasi |
stotrasya rakṣaṇaṃ nāma phalapradānam |
tadā ᵛte tava ᵛmahimā ᵛhavaṃ havaḥ stotā taṃ ᵛvyānad vyāpnotu |
he ᵛugra ojasvinnindra ᵛyat yadā ᵛhaste pāṇau ᵛvajram ᵛā ᵛdadhiṣe dhārayasi tadā ᵛṛtvā śatruvadhādinā karmaṇā ᵛghoraḥ ᵛsan ᵛaṣāḻhaḥ śatrubhiranabhibhūtaḥ ᵛjaniṣṭhāḥ ajaniṣṭhāḥ abhavaḥ ||
tva praṇītīndra johuvānānsaṃ yabhna rodasī ninertha | mahe kṣatrāya śavase hi jajñe'tūturji citturjiraśiśrat || 3 || tava | pra'nītī | indra | johuvānān | sam | yat | nṛn | na | rodasī iti | ninertha | mahe | kṣatrāya | śavase | hi | jajñe | atūtujim | cit | tūtujiḥ | aśiśrat || 3 ||
he ᵛindra ᵛyat yasvaṃ ᵛtava ᵛpraṇītī praṇītyā praṇayanena ᵛjohuvānān bhṛśaṃ stuvataḥ ᵛnṛn ᵛrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵛsaṃ ᵛninetha saṃgamayasi |
divi pṛthivyāṃ ca stotṛn pratiṣṭhāpayasītyarthaḥ |
sa tvaṃ ᵛmahe mahate ᵛkṣatrāya dhanāya |
‘rathiḥ kṣatram’ iti dhananāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵛśavase balāya ca |
1. kha-na-bha-pradānam |
2. kha-vyāpnoti |
3. kha-kṣa-ta-na-bha-pratiṣṭhāpayatītyarthaḥ; śa-prati- ṣṭhāpayantītyarthaḥ |
yajamānebhyo mahaddanaṃ balaṃ ca dātumityarthaḥ | ᵥyajñe jajñiṣe | ᵥhi iti hetvarthe | yata evamataḥ kāraṇāt ᵥatūtajim adātāramyajamānaṃ ᵥtūtajiḥ dātā yajamānaḥ ᵥaśibhat | bhayatihiṃsākarmā | tasmā- lluḍarthe luḍ | hinasti | ᵥcit ityevakārārthe ||
ebhir indrāhamirdaśasya durmitrāso hi kṣitayaḥ parvante |
prati yaccaṣṭe anṛtamanenā arba dvitā varuṇo māyī naḥ sāt ||
4 ||
ebhiḥ | naḥ | indra | aharbhiḥ | daśasya | duḥmitrrāsaḥ | hi | kṣitayaḥ | parvante | prati | yat | caṣṭe | anṛtam | anenāḥ | arba | dvitā | varuṇaḥ | māyī | naḥ | sāt ||4||
he ᵥindra ᵥdurmitrāsaḥ duṣṭamitrabhūtāḥ bādhakāḥ ᵥkṣitayaḥ janāḥ ᵥpavante abhigacchanti |
pavati- gatikarmā |
tebhyo dhanamācchidya ᵥnaḥ asmabhyam ᵥebhiḥ sāttvikaiḥ ᵥahabhiḥ ahobhiḥ ᵥdaśasya dehi |
kiṃca ᵥanenāḥ enasāṃ nihantā ᵥmāyī prajñāvān ᵥvaruṇaḥ ᵥyat ᵥanṛtaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmāsu ᵥprati ᵥcaṣṭe abhipaśyati tadanṛtaṃ he indra tvatprasādāt ᵥdvitā dvidhā ᵥava ᵥsān avasyatu vimocayatu |
tathā ca yāskaḥ—‘ syatirupasṛṣṭo vimocane ’ ( niru. 1. 17 ) iti ||
vocemedindraṃ mṛdhavānamenaṃ maho rāyo rādhaso yahadantaḥ |
yo arcato brahmakṛtimaviṣṭho yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||

ᵥyat ya indraḥ ᵥmahaḥ mahataḥ ᵥrādhasaḥ saṃrādhakasya ᵥrāyaḥ dhanasya |
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
saṃrā- dhakaṃ mahaddanaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmabhyaṃ ᵥdadat prāyacchat ᵥyaḥ cendraḥ ᵥarcataḥ stuvataḥ ᵥbrahmakṛrti kriyamāṇaṃ brahma stotram ᵥaviṣṭhaḥ atiśayena rakṣitā gantā bhavati tam ᵥenaṃ ᵥmaghavānaṃ dhanavantam ᵥindraṃ ᵥvoce- met stuvemaiva |

‘ atha soma indra ’ iti pañcarcaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ ayaṃ somaḥ ’ ityanukrāntam |
vyūlahe daśarātre navame'hani ‘ atha soma indra ’ iti praugaśastra aindrastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ atha soma indra tubhyaṃ sunva ā tu pra brahmāṇaḥ ’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 11 ) iti |
ṣoḍaśiśastre ‘ brahmanvīra ’ ityepā triṣṭup |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ brahmanvīra brahmakṛrti jupāṇa iti triṣṭup ’ ( āśva. śrau. 6. 2 ) iti ||
ayaṃ soma indra tubhyaṃ sunva ā tu pra yāhi harivastadaukāḥ |
pibā tvasya subhutasya cārordadaṃ maghāni maghavāniyānaḥ ||

1. kha-bha-duṣṭabhūtāḥ; ya-duṣṭamitrāḥ; śa-duṣṭā mitrabhūtāḥ |

he vbrahan parivṛḍha vvīra indra vbrahmakṛti kriyamāṇaṃ stotraṃ vjuṣāṇaḥ sevamānaḥ varvācīnaḥ asmadabhimukhaḥ san vharibhiḥ aśvaiḥ vtūyaṃ kṣipraṃ vyāhi |

vnaḥ asmadīyāni vimā imāni vbrahmāṇi stotrāṇi ca vupa vśṛṇavaḥ upaśṛṇu ||
kā tai astyaraṃkṛtiḥ sūkteḥ kadā nūnaṃ tai maghavandāśema |

he indra vte tava vsūkteḥ asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇaiḥ stotraiḥ varaṃkṛtiḥ alaṃkṛtiḥ vkā vasti kīdṛśī bhavati |
he vmaghavan vte tava vkadā vnūnaṃ kadā khalu vdāśema prītimutpādayema |
vsvāyā tvaskāmanayaiva vviśvāḥ vmatīḥ sarvāḥ stutīḥ vā vtatane karomi |

uto ghā te puruṣpā idāsanyeṣāṃ pūrveṣāmaśṛṇotṛṣīṇām |

eṣā siddhā ||
14 ||
‘pra va indrāya’ iti dvādaśarcaṃ caturdaśaṃ sūktaṃ vamiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ gāyatramaindram |
daśamyādyāstisro virājaḥ śiṣṭā gāyatryaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ- ‘pra vaṃ dvādaśa gāyatraṃ trivirāḻantam’ iti |
sūktavini- yogo laiṅgikaḥ |
prathame rātriparyāye maitrāvaruṇaśastre ‘pra va indrāya’ ityādyastṛcaḥ stotriyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘pra va indrāya mādanaṃ pra kṛtānmṛjīpiṇaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 4) iti |
anirāye prathame paryāye brāhmaṇācchaṃsiśastre ‘vathamindra tvāyavo'bhi’ ityanurūpasūtracaḥ |
tathā ca sūtritaṃ- ‘vathamindra tvāyavo'bhi vātrahatyāyetyuttamāsuddharet’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 4) iti |
caturthe'hani mādhyandinasavane hotrakaśastra ārambhaṇīyābhya ūrdhvaṃ vairāja ekastṛca āhavanīyaḥ |
tadyathoḥ ‘pra vo mahe mahivṛdhe bharadhvam’ ityādyāstisraḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘pra vo mahe mahivṛdhaṃ bharadhvamiti canastimaśra trirājaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 11) iti ||
pra va indrāya mādanaṃ haryaśvāya gāyata |
sakhāyaḥ somapātre ||

pra | vaḥ | indrāya | mādanam | harya'aśvāya | gāyata | sakhāyaḥ | soma'pātre || 1 ||

pra vo mahe mahidhṛdhe bharadhvaṃ pracetase pra sumatiṃ kṛṇudhvam | viśaḥ pūrvīḥ pra carā carṣaṇīprāḥ || 10 || pra | vaḥ | mahe | mahidhṛdhe | bharadhvam | pracetase | pra | sumatim | kṛṇudhvam | viśaḥ | pūrvīḥ | pra | cara | carṣaṇīprāḥ || 10 ||
he madīyāḥ purupāḥ vaḥ yūyaṃ mahidhṛdhe mahatāṃ dhanānāṃ vardhayitre saha mahate indrāya pra bharadhvaṃ somān praṇayata |
pracetase prakṛṣṭamataye indrāya sumatiṃ sumatiṃ ca pra kṛṇudhvaṃ prakuruta |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |

1. kha-jha-ta-śa-ato |
he indra na: asmabhyaṃ kratuṃ karma prajñānaṃ vā ā bhara āhara|
api ca yathā pitā putrebhya: dhanaṃ prayacchati tathā na: asmabhyaṃ śikṣa dhanaṃ dehi|
he puruhūta bahubhirāhūta yāmani yajñe jīvā: vayaṃ jyoti: sūryam aśmīmahi pratidinaṃ prāpnuyāma||
mā no ajñātā vṛjanā durādhyo mā śvāso arba kramu: |
tvayā vayaṃ pravataḥ śaśvatīrapo'ti śūra tarāmasi ||
27 ||
mā | na: | ajñātā: | vṛjanā: | du:ādhya: | mā | śvāsa: | arba | kramu: | tvayā | vayam | pra'vata: | śaśvatī: | apa: | ati | śūra | tarāmasi || 27 ||
he indra ajñātā: ajñātagamanā: vṛjanā: hiṃsakā: durādhaya: na: asmān mā ava kramu: māvacakramu:|
he śūra tvayā vayaṃ vamiṣṭhā: pravanta: pravaṇakā: santaḥ śaśvatī: bahvī: apa: ati tarāmasi atitarāma ||
27 ||
'śrityaśva:' iti caturdaśarcaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ sūktam|
atreyamanukramaṇikā—'śrityaśva: palunā saṃstabo vamiṣṭhasya maputrasyendraṇa vā saṃvāda:' iti|
ādito navānāṃ vamiṣṭha ṛṣi:|

ato vamiṣṭho devatā|
na eva ṛṣaya:|
'yā tenocyate' iti nyāyāt|
anuktvāt triṣṭup||
śrityaśvo mā dakṣiṇataskapardā dhiyaṃjinvāso abhi hi pramandu: |
uttiṣṭhanvoce pari barhiṣo nṛnna me durādavitave vasiṣṭhā: ||
1 ||
śrityaśva: | mā | dakṣiṇata: | kapardā: | dhiyam | jinvāma: | abhi | hi | pra'mandu: | uta | tiṣṭhan | voce | pari | barhiṣa: | nṛn | na | me | durāta | avitave | vasiṣṭhā: || 1 ||
'śrityaśva: |
śriyaṃ śvetavarṇamañcantīti śrityaśva: |
śvetavarṇā ityartha: |
dhiyaṃjinvāsa: karmaṇāṃ pūrayitāra: dakṣiṇataskapardā: |
dakṣiṇe śiramo bhāge kapardāśca yeṣāṃ te dakṣiṇataskapardā: |
cūḍākarmaṇi dakṣiṇato vasiṣṭhānāmiti smaryate |
mā mām abhi pramandu: vidyābalenābhi- prāhapayan |
yato māmabhi pramandurana: barhiṣa: yajñāt |
pari iti pañcamyarthānuvāda: |
uttiṣṭhan ahaṃ nṛn yajñasya netṛn voce bravīmi |
me mata: durāta vamiṣṭhā: vamiṣṭhasya mama putrā: avitave gantuṃ na ahantīti śeṣa: ||
durādindramanayannā sutenaṃ tiro vaiśantamati pāntamugram |
pāśadyumnasya vāyatasya somātsutādindro'vṛṇīta vasiṣṭhān ||
2 ||
durāta | indram | anayana | ā | sutena | tira: | vaiśantam | ati | pāntam | ugram | pāśadyumnasya | vāyatasya | somāt | sutāt | indra: | avṛṇīta | vasiṣṭhān || 2 ||

yadā vasiṣṭhasya putrāḥ sudāsaṃ rājānamayājayan tadaiva pāśayugmasyā'pi rājā somān yaṣṭumudyamaṃ cakāra|
tadā te vasiṣṭhaputrāḥ pāśayugmaṃ tiraskṛtya tadīye yāge somaṃ pibantamindraṃ mantrabalena tasmādācchidya sudāso yajñe sthāpayāmāsuḥ|
tadetadvṛttāntaṃ kīrtayan vasiṣṭhaḥ svasutānanena mantreṇa stauti|
ᵥvaiśāntam|
veśāntaḥ palva lam|
atra veśāntaśabdena somādhāraśramaso lakṣyate|
tasthaṃ somaṃ ᵥpāntaṃ pibantam ᵥugram udvarṇam ᵥindraṃ ᵥsutena sudāmo yajñe'bhipatena somena hetunā ᵥtiraḥ pāśayugmaṃ tiraskṛtya ᵥtūrāta ᵥā ᵥanayana vasiṣṭhā mantrabalenānītavantaḥ|
ᵥindraḥ api ᵥvāyatasya nayataḥ putrasya ᵥpāśayugmasya|
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī|
vāyataṃ pāśayugmam ᵥati atihāya sudāso yajñe'bhipatāt ᵥsomāt hetoḥ ᵥvasiṣṭhāna vasiṣṭhasya putrāna ᵥavṛṇīta|
pāśayugmasya somayāge camasasthaṃ somaṃ pibanpīndrastaṃ pāśayugmaṃ tiraskṛtya mantramāmarthyabalena sudāso yajña āhvanakāle vasiṣṭhānājagāmetyarthaḥ||
evenu kaṃ sindhumebhistatārevenu kaṃ bhedamebhirajaghāna |
evenu kaṃ dāśarājñe sudāsaṃ prāvadindro brahmaṇā vo vasiṣṭhāḥ ||
3 ||
eva| ita| nu| kam| sindhum| ebhiḥ| tatāra| eva| ita| nu| kam| bhedam| ebhiḥ| jaghāna| eva| ita| nu| kam| dāśarājñe| sudāsam| pra| āvat| indraḥ| brahmaṇā| vaḥ| vasiṣṭhāḥ||3||
ᵥevet yathā pāśayugmasya matvādye somayāge camasasthaṃ somaṃ pibantamapīndraṃ vasiṣṭhaiḥ sudāḥ prāptavān evameva ᵥsindhuṃ nadīm ᵥebhiḥ vasiṣṭhaiḥ ᵥkaṃ sukhena ᵥtantāra tīrṇa ātmān|
ᵥnu iti pūraṇaḥ|
tathā ca nigamantaram- 'arṇāmi cittaprathānā sudāsaṃ' (ṛ. saṃ. 7. 18. 5) iti|
ᵥevet evameva ᵥbhedaṃ bhedanāmakaṃ śatrumapi ᵥebhiḥ vasiṣṭhaireva ᵥjaghāna|
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ|
ᵥevet evameva he ᵥvasiṣṭhāḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmadīyena ᵥbrahmaṇā stotreṇa ᵥdāśarājñe daśarbhī rājabhiḥ saha yuddhe pravṛtte sati ᵥsudāmaṃ rājānam ᵥindraḥ ᵥprāvat prārakṣat|
tathā ca nigamantaraṃ- 'daśa rājānaḥ samitā ayajvaḥ sudāsamindrāvaruṇā na yuyudhuḥ' (ṛ. maṃ. 7. 83. 7) iti 'dāśarājñaṃ pariyattāya viśvataḥ' (ṛ. saṃ. 7. 83. 8) iti ca||
juṣṭī naro brahmaṇā vaḥ pitṛṇāmakṣamayyaṃ na kilā ripātha |
yacchakarīṣu bṛhatā ravendre śuṣmadadhātā vasiṣṭhāḥ ||
4 ||
juṣṭī| naraḥ| brahmaṇā| vaḥ| pitṛṇām| akṣam| avyayam| na| kila| ripātha| yata| śarkarīṣu| bṛhatā| raveṇa| indrai| śuṣmam| adhāta| vasiṣṭhāḥ||4||
he ᵥnaraḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmadīyena ᵥbrahmaṇā stotreṇa ᵥpitṛṇām ᵥjuṣṭāṃ prītirbhavati|

ahaṃ prīto bhavāmītyarthaḥ|

cālayāmītyarthaḥ|

^bhuvaneṣu pṛthivyantarikṣadyuṣu ^trayaḥ abhivāyusūryāḥ yathākramena ^retaḥ viśvasya dhāraka- mudakaṃ ^kṛṇvanti kurvanti |
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ ^jyotiragrāḥ ādityapramukhāḥ ^āryāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ ^tisraḥ ^prajāḥ bhavanti |
te ca ^trayaḥ abhivāyusūryāḥ ^gharmāsaḥ dīpyamānāḥ ^upamaṃ ^sacante samavayanti |
durjñānān ^sarvān sarvānaiva ^tān ^vasiṣṭhāḥ ^anu ^viduḥ abhijānanti |
teṣāṃ rahasya- vijñānādyamapi vasiṣṭhāneva stutiḥ |
tathā ca jñātyāyanakaṃ—'trayaḥ kṛṇvanti bhuvaneṣu reta ityabhiḥ pṛthivyāṃ retaḥ kṛṇoti vāyurantarikṣa ādityau divi tisraḥ prajā āryā jyotiragrā iti vasavo rudrā ādityāstāsāṃ jyotiryadasāvādityakhyayo gharmāsa upasaṃ sacanta ityabhirupasaṃ sacate vāyuryasaṃ sacata āditya upasaṃ sacate' iti ||

sūryasya'iva | vṛkṣaḥ | jyotiḥ | eṣām | samudrasya'iva | mahimā | gambhīraḥ | vātasya'iva | pra'jvaḥ | na | anyena | stomaḥ | vasiṣṭhāḥ | anu'etave | vaḥ || 8 ||
he vasiṣṭhāḥ eṣāṃ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ stomaḥ mahimāpi vā sūryasya jyotiḥ iva vṛkṣaḥ prakāśo'sti |
he vasiṣṭhāḥ vo yuṣmākaṃ mahimā stomo'pi vā samudrasyaiva gambhīraḥ gambhīro'sti |
tathā he vasiṣṭhāḥ eṣāṃ vāṃ yuṣmākaṃ stoma ṛksamūha mahimāpi vā vātasyaiva prajavaḥ yathā vātasya pravego'nyenānvetuṃ na śakyastadvat anyena anvetave anvetuṃ na śakyaḥ ||
ta iṇṇiyaṃ hṛdayasya praketaiḥ sahasravalśamabhi saṃ caranti |
yamena tataṃ paridhi vayanto'psurasa urṣa sedurvasiṣṭhāḥ ||
9 ||
te | ita | niyam | hṛdayasya | pra'ketaiḥ | mahaḥ'valśam | abhi | sam | caranti | yamena | tatam | pari'dhim | vayantaḥ | apsurasaḥ | urṣa | seduḥ | vasiṣṭhāḥ || 9 ||
ta it na eva vasiṣṭhāḥ niyaṃ tirohitaṃ durjñānam |
‘niyaṃ masvaḥ’ ityantarhitanāmasu pāṭhāt |
mahasvalśaṃ sahasraśākhaṃ saṃsāraṃ hṛdayasya praketaiḥ prajñānaiḥ abhi saṃ caranti |
evaṃ svācchandyena abhimaṃcarantaste vasiṣṭhāḥ yamena kāraṇātmanā sarvaniyantrā tataṃ vistṛtaṃ paridhi vastram |
paridhirityanena janmādipravāho vivakṣitaḥ |
taṃ vayantaḥ apsurasaḥ jananītvena upa seduḥ |
atra vasiṣṭhā iti bahuvacanaṃ pūjāyām |

atreyamanukramaṇikā—'pra śukrā pañcādhikā vaiśvadevaṃ hādyā ekaviṃśatirdvipadā abjāmaheradharca uttaro'- hivṛndhyāya' iti |
vasiṣṭha ṛṣiḥ |
ādyā ekaviṃśatirdvipadā viṃśatyakṣarā virājo dvāviṃśatyāśvata- sriṣṭubhaḥ |
viśve devā devatā |
vyūḻahe daśarātre caturthe'hani vaiśvadevaśastra idaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvadeva- nividdānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—'pra śukraiti vaiśvadevam' (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) iti |
ṣoḍaśini 'ā dhūrvasmā iti dvipadā' (āśva. śrau. 6. 2) iti |
mahāvrate'pyeṣā dvipadā |
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake sūtritaṃ—'ā dhūrvasmā ityekā sūddohāḥ' (ai. ā. 5. 2. 2) iti ||
pra śukraitu devī manīṣā asmatsutaṣṭo ratho na vājī ||

pra | śukrā | etu | devī | manīṣā | asmat | su'taṣṭaḥ | rathaḥ | na | vājī || 1 ||
^śukrā dīptā ^devī sarveṣāṃ kāmānāṃ pradātrī ^manīṣā stutiḥ ^asmat asmattaḥ asmin sūkte stobhyamāṇān devān ^vājī vegavān ^sutaṣṭaḥ susaṃskṛtaḥ ^ratho ^na ratha iva ^pra ^etu pragacchatu^ ||

2 ||

ᵛdevaḥ dyotanādiguṇayuktaḥ ᵛindraḥ ᵛvasubhiḥ devaiḥ sādhaṃ ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛśaṃ śāntyai bhavatu |

śaṃ no aditirbhavatu vratebhiḥ śaṃ no bhavantu murataḥ svarkāḥ | śaṃ no viṣṇuḥ śaṃyu pūṣā no astu śaṃ no bhavitraṃ śaṃrvastu vāyuḥ || 9 || śam | naḥ | aditiḥ | bhavatu | vratebhiḥ | śam | naḥ | bhavantu | murataḥ | su'arkāḥ | śam | naḥ | viṣṇuḥ | śam | ū~ iti | pūṣā | naḥ | astu | śam | naḥ | bhavitram | śam | ū~ iti | astu | vāyuḥ || 9 || ᵥaditiḥ devī ᵥvratebhiḥ vrataiḥ karmabhiḥ sārdhaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai ᵥbhavatu | ᵥsvarkāḥ śobhanastutayaḥ ᵥmarutaḥ api ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai santu | ᵥviṣṇuḥ vyāpakaḥ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntya astu | ᵥpūṣā devo'pi ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśamu śāntya eva ᵥastu | ᵥbhavitraṃ bhuvana- mantarikṣamudakaṃ vā ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntya astu | ᵥvāyuḥ api naḥ ᵥśamu śāntya eva ᵥastu ||
śaṃ no devaḥ saṃvitā trāyamāṇaḥ śaṃ no bhavantūpasauṃ vibhātīḥ | śaṃ naḥ parjanyau bhavatu prajābhyaḥ śaṃ naḥ kṣetrasya patirastu śaṃbhuḥ || 10 || śam | naḥ | devaḥ | savitā | trāyamāṇaḥ | śam | naḥ | bhavantu | upasaḥ | vi'bhātīḥ | śam | naḥ | parjanyaḥ | bhavatu | pra'jābhyaḥ | śam | naḥ | kṣetrasya | patiḥ | astu | śam'bhuḥ || 10 || ᵥdevaḥ krīḍanādiguṇayuktaḥ ᵥsavitā ᵥtrāyamāṇaḥ rakṣan ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai bhavatu | ᵥvibhātīḥ dyucchanyaḥ ᵥupasaḥ api ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai ᵥbhavantu | ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥprajābhyaḥ ᵥparjanyaḥ api ᵥśaṃ ᵥbhavatu | ᵥśaṃbhuḥ sukhasya bhāvayitā ᵥkṣetrasya ᵥpatiḥ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai ᵥastu || || 29 ||
śaṃ no devā viśvadevā bhavantu śaṃ sarasvatī saha dhībhirastu | śamabhiṣācaḥ śaṃyu rātiṣācaḥ śaṃ no divyāḥ pārthivāḥ śaṃ no apyāḥ || 11 || śam | naḥ | devāḥ | viśva'devāḥ | bhavantu | śam | sarasvatī | saha | dhībhiḥ | astu | śam | abhi'sācaḥ | śam | ū~ iti | rāti'sācaḥ | śam | naḥ | divyāḥ | pārthivāḥ | śam | naḥ | apyāḥ || 11 || ᵥviśvadevāḥ bahustotrakāḥ ᵥdevāḥ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai ᵥbhavantu | ᵥsarasvatī ca ᵥdhībhiḥ stutibhiḥ karmabhirvā ᵥsaha no'smākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai ᵥastu | ᵥabhiṣācaḥ yajñamabhitaḥ sevamānāśca naḥ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai bhavantu | ᵥrātiṣācaḥ dānaṃ sevamānā api ᵥśamu śāntya eva bhavantu | ᵥdivyāḥ divi bhavāśca ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai bhavantu | ᵥpārthivāḥ pṛthivyāṃ saṃbhūtāśca naḥ śaṃ bhavantu | ᵥapyāḥ apsvantarikṣe bhavāśca | ‘ākāśamāpaḥ’ ityantarikṣanāmasu pāṭhāt | ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥśaṃ śāntyai bhavantu ||
śaṃ naḥ satyasya patayo bhavantu śaṃ no arventaḥ śaṃyu santu gārvaḥ | śaṃ naḥ ṛbhavaḥ sukṛtaḥ suhastāḥ śaṃ nau bhavantu pitaro haveṣu || 12 || śam | naḥ | satyasya | patayaḥ | bhavantu | śam | naḥ | arventaḥ | śam | ū~ iti | santu | gārvaḥ | śam | naḥ | ṛbhavaḥ | su'kṛtaḥ | su'hastāḥ | śam | naḥ | bhavantu | pitaraḥ | haveṣu || 12 ||
^satyasya ^patayaḥ pālakāḥ satyaśīlā devāḥ ^naḥ asmākaṃ ^śaṃ śāntyai ^bhavantu | ^arbantaḥ aśvāśca ^naḥ asmākaṃ ^śaṃ śāntyai bhavantu | ^gāvaḥ api ^naḥ ^śaṃ śāntyai bhavantu | ^sukṛtaḥ sukarmāṇaḥ ^yajñiyānāṃ yajanīyānāṃ ^devānām api ^yajñiyāḥ yajanīyāḥ ^manoḥ prajāpateśca ^yajatrāḥ yajanīyāḥ ^amṛtāḥ maraṇarahitāḥ ^ṛtajñāḥ satyajñāḥ ^ye devāḥ santi ^te sarve ^uhagāyaṃ bahukīrti putram ^adha ^naḥ asmābhyaṃ ^rasantāṃ prayacchantu | siddha evoktamaḥ pādaḥ || || 30 || vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan | pumarthāṃścaturo deyādvidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ || iti nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram ||
atha pañcamāṣṭake caturtho'dhyāya ārabhyate |

pra brahma̭tu॒ sa॒danā̭t॒sasya॒ vi ra॒śmibhi̭ḥ sasṛ॒je sū॒ryo gāḥ |
vi sā॒nunā̭ pṛthi॒vī sa॒sra u॒rvī pṛ॒thu pra॒tīka̭ma॒dhye a॒gniḥ ||
1 ||
pra | brahma̭ | e॒tu | sa॒danā̭t | ṛ॒tasya̭ | vi | ra॒śmi'bhi̭ḥ | sasṛ॒je | sū॒rya̭ḥ | gāḥ | vi | sā॒nunā̭ | pṛthi॒vī | sa॒sra | u॒rvī | pṛ॒thu | pra॒tīka̭m | a॒dhi | ā | ī॒dhe | a॒gniḥ || 1 ||
^ṛtasya yajñasya ^sadanāt sthānāddevajanadeśāt ^brahma stotraṃ stutyān sūryādīn ^pra ^etu prakarṣeṇa gacchatu |
kiṃ sahasroti tadāha |
^sūryaḥ sarvasya prerakaḥ śobhanavarṇo vā devaḥ ^raśmibhiḥ ātmīyaiḥ kiraṇaiḥ |
^gāḥ apo vṛṣṭyudakāni ^vi ^sasṛje visṛjati vimuñcati |
pravarṣati |
śrūyate hi—'yābhirādityastapati raśmibhistābhiḥ parjanyo varṣati' (tai. ā. 10. 63) iti |
smṛtiśca bhavati—'ādityājjāyate vṛṣṭirvṛṣṭerannaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ' iti |

api ca ^pṛthivī prathitā bhūmiḥ ^sānunā samucchritena parvatādinā ^urvī vistīrṇā satī ^vi ^sasre visarati vyāpnoti |
tathā ^abhiḥ ^pṛthu vistīrṇaṃ ^pratīkaṃ pṛthivyā avayavaṃ devajanalakṣaṇaṃ sthānam ^adhi |
'adhiparī anarthakau' ityadheḥ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāyāṃ 'karmapravacanīyayukte' iti saptamyarthe dvitīyā |
īdṛśe sthāne ^edhe ādīpyate ||
i॒māṃ vo̭ mi॒trāva̭ruṇā su॒vakti॒miṣa̭ṃ na kṛ॒ṇve a॒surā̭ na॒vīya̭ḥ |

i॒mām | vā॒m | mi॒trāva̭ruṇā | su॒'vakti̭m | i॒ṣam | na | kṛ॒ṇve | a॒surā̭ | na॒vīya̭ḥ | i॒naḥ | vā॒m | a॒nya̭ḥ | pa॒da'vī̭ḥ | a॒dabdha̭ḥ | ja॒nam | ca̭ | mi॒traḥ | ya̭tati | bru॒vāṇa̭ḥ ||2||
he ^asurā asurau balavantau he ^mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau ^vāṃ yuvābhyāṃ ^iṣaṃ ^na havī- rūpamannamiva ^navīyaḥ navīyasīm ^imām asmadīyāṃ purovartinīṃ ^suvaktiṃ stutiṃ ^kṛṇve ahaṃ stotā karomi |

^bruvāṇaḥ asmābhiḥ stūyamānaḥ ^mitraḥ ^ca ^janaṃ sarvaṃ prāṇijātaṃ ^yatati yātayati pravartayati |
