<sup>v</sup>ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ vistīrṇasya tejaso nivāsabhūtā he <sup>v</sup>vājāḥ ṛbhavaḥ <sup>v</sup>vāhiṣṭhaḥ voḍhṛtamaḥ <sup>v</sup>stavadhyai stotumarhaḥ <sup>v</sup>amṛktaḥ kenāpyahiṃsito yuṣmadīyaḥ <sup>v</sup>rathaḥ <sup>v</sup>vaḥ yuṣmān <sup>v</sup>ā <sup>v</sup>vahatu ā samantādasmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ prāpayatu |
he <sup>v</sup>suśiprāḥ śobhanahanavaḥ yūyaṃ tena rathenāgatya <sup>v</sup>savaneṣu asmadīyayajñeṣu <sup>v</sup>made madyanimitte <sup>v</sup>tripṛṣṭhaiḥ kṣīradadhisaktumiḥ <sup>v</sup>mahabhiḥ mahadbhiḥ <sup>v</sup>saumaiḥ <sup>v</sup>abhi <sup>v</sup>pṛṇadhvaṃ yuṣmadīyaṃ jaṭharambhīpūrayata |
sa ratho yuṣmānāvahatviti pūrveṇānvayaḥ ||
yūyaṃ ha ratnaṃ maghavatsu dhattha svardarśa ṛbhukṣaṇo amṛktam |
saṃ yajñeṣu svadhāvantaḥ pibadhvaṃ vi no rādhauṃsi matibhirdayadhvam ||
2 ||
yūyam | ha | ratnam | maghavat'su | dhattha | svaḥ'darśaḥ | ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ | amṛktam | sam | yajñeṣu | svadhā'vantaḥ | pibadhvam | vi | naḥ | rādhauṃsi | mati'bhiḥ | dayadhvam ||2||
he <sup>v</sup>ṛbhukṣaṇaḥ he ṛbhavaḥ <sup>v</sup>svardarśaḥ svargaṃ paśyantaḥ <sup>v</sup>yūyaṃ <sup>v</sup>ha yūyameva <sup>v</sup>maghavatsu havi- rlakṣaṇavatsvasmāsu nimitteṣu <sup>v</sup>amṛktam ahiṃsitam |
corādibhirnāpahatamityarthaḥ |
<sup>v</sup>ratnaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ <sup>v</sup>dhattha dhārayata |
tadanantaraṃ <sup>v</sup>svadhāvantaḥ balavantaste yūyaṃ <sup>v</sup>yajñeṣu asmadīyayajñeṣu <sup>v</sup>saṃ <sup>v</sup>pibadhvaṃ samyak somaṃ 'pibata' |
api ca yūyaṃ <sup>v</sup>matibhiḥ dhanahetubhiḥ <sup>2</sup> <sup>v</sup>naḥ asmabhyaṃ <sup>v</sup>rādhauṃsi dhanāni <sup>v</sup>vi <sup>v</sup>dayadhvaṃ viśeṣeṇa datta ||
uvocitha hi maghavanddeṣṇaṃ maho arbhasya vasuno vibhāge |
ubhā te pūrṇā vasunā garbhastī na sunnatā ni yamate vasuvyā ||
3 ||
uvocitha | hi | magha'van | deṣṇam | mahaḥ | arbhasya | vasu'naḥ | vi'bhāge | ubhā | te | pūrṇā | vasunā | garbhastī | iti | na | sunnatā | ni | yamate | vasu'vyā || 3 ||
‘uvocitha’ ityādyāḥ pañcendradevatākāḥ |
he <sup>v</sup>maghavan dhanavannindra tvaṃ <sup>v</sup>mahaḥ mahataḥ <sup>v</sup>arbhasya alpasya ca <sup>v</sup>vasunaḥ dhanasya <sup>v</sup>vibhāge paricaraṇānukūle dānanimitte <sup>v</sup>deṣṇaṃ dhanam <sup>v</sup>uvocitha <sup>v</sup>hi sevase khalu |
ucatiḥ sevākarmā |
tathā <sup>v</sup>te tvadīyo <sup>v</sup>ubhā ubhau <sup>v</sup>gabhastī bāhū <sup>v</sup>vasunā dhanena <sup>v</sup>pūrṇā sampūrṇau bhavataḥ |
te tvadīyā <sup>v</sup>sunnatā vāk <sup>v</sup>vasuvyā vasūni dhanāni <sup>v</sup>ni <sup>v</sup>yamate ||
1. jha-kṣa-ta-1-4-saṃṣibata |
2. jha-ta-1-2-dhanahetubhiḥ mananīyaiḥ stotraiḥ |
^na ^ni ^dhamate na niyacchati | yadvā | vasadhyā vasuṣu dhaneṣu sādhuḥ | sūnṛtā vāk dhamena saṃpūrṇā ^vadīyau bāhū na niyacchati | nārghaṃ pradāpayatītyarthaḥ ||
tvamindra svaryaśā ṛbhukṣā vājo na sādhurastameṣyarkā |
varya nu tai dādhvāṃsaḥ syām brahma kṛṇvantau harivo vasiṣṭhāḥ ||
4 ||
tvam | indra | svaryaśāḥ | ṛbhukṣāḥ | vāje | na | sādhuḥ | astam | eṣi | arkā | vayam | nu | te | dādhvāṃsaḥ | syām | brahma | kṛṇvantaḥ | hari'vaḥ | vasiṣṭhāḥ || 4 ||
he ^indra ^svayaśāḥ asādhāraṇakīrtiḥ ^ṛbhukṣāḥ ṛbhunivāsakaḥ ṛbhunāmīśvaro vā ^tvaṃ ^sādhuḥ sādhakaḥ ^vājaḥ ^na annamiva ^ṛkkāḥ ṛkaṇaḥ stotumarham ^astaṃ gṛham ^eṣi prāpnuhi |
^nu atha ^vasiṣṭhāḥ etatsaṃjakā ṛṣayaḥ ^vayaṃ he ^harivaḥ svakīyādhipetendra ^te tvadartha
abhi | yam | devī | niḥ'ṛtiḥ | cit | īśe | nakṣante | indram | śaradaḥ | su'dṛkṣaḥ | urpa | tri'bandhuḥ | jarat'aṣṭim | eti | asva'veśam | yam | kṛṇvanta | martāḥ || 7 || devī dyotamānā niḥṛtiścit bhūmirapi īśe | kṛtyārthaṃ kenapratyayaḥ || īśitavyā svabhūtā satī yam indram abhi nakṣate vyāpnoti | sudṛkṣaḥ śobhanā'rūpetāḥ śaradaḥ saṃvatsarāśca yamindraṃ nakṣante vyāpnuvanti | martāḥ maraṇadharmāṇaḥ stotāro vayaṃ yam indram asvaveśaṃ svakīye sthāne'nupaviśantaṃ kṛṇvanta kurvanti | tribandhuḥ trayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ bandhako vidhārakaḥ sa indraḥ jaradṛṣṭi jarajīrṇamaṣṭiraśanaṃ yasya balasya hetubhūtaṃ tadbalam upa eti upagacchati ||
ā no rādhaॉsi savitaḥ stavadhyā ā rāyo yantu parvatasya rātau | sadā no divyaḥ pāyuḥ siṣaktu yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 8 || ā | naḥ | rādhaॉsi | savitariti | stavadhyai | ā | rāyaḥ | yantu | parvatasya | rātau | sadā | naḥ | divyaḥ | pāyuḥ | siṣaktu | yūyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 8 || he savitaḥ sarvasya preraka deva tvatsakāśāt stavadhyai stotuṃ yogyāni rādhaॉsi dhanāni naḥ asmān ā yantu āgacchantu | parvatasya parvata iti kaścidindrasya sakhā | eta- tsaṃjñakasya devasya rātau dāne sati rāyaḥ dhanānyasmān ā yantu | pāyuḥ sarvasya pālakaḥ divyaḥ divi bhavaḥ sa indraḥ sadā sarvadā naḥ asmān siṣaktu rakṣakatvena sevatām | asmin sūkte ye pratipāditā devāste sarve yūyaṃ naḥ asmān svastibhiḥ kalyāṇaiḥ sadā pāta pālayata || || 4 ||
'udu pya devaḥ' ityaṣṭacaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ savitṛdevatākam |
saptamyaṣṭamau vājidevatāke |
'bhagamugro'vase' ityardharco bhagadevatyaḥ sāvitro vā |
tathā cānukramaṇikā— 'udu pya sāvitramantye vājinyau bhagamiti bhāgo vārtharcaḥ' iti |
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ ||
udu pya devaḥ savitā yayāma hiraṇyayīṃmatiṃ yāmaśiśret | nūnaṃ bhago havyo mānuṣebhiryo ratnā puruvasurdhati || 1 || ut | ū~ iti | syaḥ | devaḥ | savitā | yayāma | hiraṇyayīm | amatim | yām | aśiśret | nūnam | bhagaḥ | havyaḥ | mānuṣebhiḥ | tri | yaḥ | ratnā | puruvasuḥ | dadhāti || 1 || savitā sarvasya preraka syaḥ saḥ devaḥ hiraṇyayīṃ suvarṇamayīṃ yām amatim | rūpanā- maitat | rūpaṃ prabhāmityarthaḥ | aśiśret āśrayati tāmamatim ut yayāma uccacchati udyamayati | u iti pādapūraṇaḥ | nūnam adya bhagaḥ bhajanīyo yaḥ savitā mānuṣebhiḥ manuṣyaiḥ stotṛbhiḥ havyaḥ havanīyaḥ stotavyo bhavati | puruvasuḥ bahudhanaḥ yaḥ devaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ ratnā ratnāni ramaṇī- yāni dhanāni vi dadhāti karoti | sa savitā devaḥ tāmamatimudyayāmeti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
udu tiṣṭha savitaḥ śrudhya'sya hiraṇyapāṇe prabhṛtāvatasya |
vyu'rvī pṛthvīmatiṃ sṛjāna ā nṛbhyo martobhojanaṃ suvānaḥ ||
2 ||
1. kha-jña-ta-na-bha-śa-pratipādikāḥ |
2. śa-rakṣata |
ut | ū~ iti̭ | tiṣṭha | sa॒vi॒tariti̭ | śṛ॒dhi | a॒sya | hi॒ra॒ṇya'pāṇe | pra॒bhaitau | ṛ॒tasya̭ | vi | u॒rvīm | pṛ॒thvīm | a॒martim | sṛ॒jāna̭ḥ | ā | nṛ॒bhya̭ḥ | marta'bhojanam | su॒vāna̭ḥ ||2||
he savitaḥ sarvasya prerayitardeva tvam ut tiṣṭha ūrdhvaṃ gaccha |
tato he hiraṇyapāṇe he suvarṇahasta tvamasmadabhīpsitapradānāya ṛtasya yajñasya prabhṛtau praṇayane asya asmadīyamidaṃ stotraṃ śṛdhi śṛṇu |
u iti pūraṇaḥ |
kīdṛśastvam |
urvīṃ vistīrṇāṃ pṛthvīṃ prathitām amarti rūpaṃ prabhā vi sṛjānaḥ visṛjan nṛbhyaḥ netṛbhyaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ martebhojanaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhogayogyaṃ dhanam ā suvānaḥ prerayan |
evaṃbhūtastvamidaṃ stotraṃ śṛṇviti sambandhaḥ ||
arpi ṣṭutaḥ sa̭vi॒tā de॒vo a̭stu॒ yamā ci॒dviśva̭e va॒savo̭ gṛṇanti |
sa na̭ḥ sto॒mā॒nma॒sya॒ śnau dhā॒dviśvai̭ḥ pā॒tu pā॒yubhi̭rnaḥ sū॒rīn ||
3 ||
arpi | stutaḥ | sa॒vi॒tā | de॒vaḥ | a॒stu | yam | ā | ci॒t | viśvḙ | va॒sava̭ḥ | gṛ॒ṇanti̭ | saḥ | na̭ḥ | sto॒mān | na॒ma॒sya̭ḥ | ca॒naḥ | dhā॒t | viśvai̭ḥ | pā॒tu | pā॒yu'bhi̭ḥ | ni | sū॒rīn ||3||
api ca savitā devaḥ asmābhiḥ stutaḥ astu |
asmadīyāḥ stutīḥ śṛṇvitvarthaḥ |
viśve vasavaḥ cit sarve devā api yaṃ savitāram ā gṛṇanti abhiṣṭuvanti namasyaḥ sarvairnamaskāraṇīyaḥ saḥ devaḥ stomān naḥ asmadīyāni stotrāṇi canaḥ annaṃ dhāt dadhātu |
annaphalāni karotu |
viśvebhiḥ viśvaiḥ sarvaiḥ pāyubhiḥ pālanaiḥ sūrīn stotṛnasmān ni pātu nitarāṃ pālayatu ||
abhi yaṃ de॒vyadi̭ti॒rgṛṇā̭ti॒ sava̭ṃ de॒vasya̭ sa॒vitṷrju॒ṣāṇā̭ |
abhi sa॒mrājo̭ va॒ruṇo̭ gṛṇantya॒bhi mi॒trāso̭ a॒ryamā̭ sa॒joṣā̭ḥ ||
4 ||
abhi | yam | de॒vī | a॒di॒tiḥ | gṛ॒ṇāti̭ | sa॒vam | de॒vasya̭ | sa॒vi॒tuḥ | ju॒ṣāṇā̭ | abhi | sa॒m'rāja̭ḥ | va॒ruṇa̭ḥ | gṛ॒ṇanti̭ | a॒bhi | mi॒trāsa̭ḥ | a॒ryamā̭ | sa॒'joṣā̭ḥ || 4 ||
devī dyotamānā aditiḥ adīnā devamātā yaṃ savitāram abhi gṛṇāti abhiṣṭauti |
kīdṛśī |
savituḥ devasya eva savaṃ prasavamanujñāṃ juṣāṇā sevamānā |
samrājaḥ samyagrājamānāḥ varuṇaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
varuṇādayo devāḥ yaṃ savitāram abhi gṛṇanti abhiṣṭuvanti |
mitrāsaḥ mitrādayaśca sajoṣāḥ samānaprītiḥ aryamā etatsaṃjñako devaśca yam abhi gṛṇanti |
ma naḥ stomāṃścano dhāditi pūrvayarcā sambandhaḥ ||
abhi ye mi॒tho va̭nu॒ṣaḥ sa̭rpa॒nte rā॒tiṃ di॒vo rā̭ti॒pāca̭: pṛ॒thivyā̭ḥ |
ahi̭rbu॒dhnya u॒ta na̭ḥ śṛ॒ṇotu॒ varṷṇo॒ ekḙdha॒nebhi̭rni pātu ||
5 ||
abhi | ye | mi॒thaḥ | va॒nu॒ṣaḥ | sa॒rpa॒nte | rā॒tim | di॒vaḥ | rā॒ti'sāca̭ḥ | pṛ॒thivyā̭ḥ | ahi̭ḥ | bu॒dhnya̭ḥ | u॒ta | na̭ḥ | śṛ॒ṇotṷ | varṷṇaḥ | e॒ka'dhenṷ'bhiḥ | ni | pā॒tu || 5 ||
rātiṣācaḥ dānasevinaḥ vanuṣaḥ saṃbhaktāraḥ ye yajamānāḥ mithaḥ parasparaṃ saṃhatā bhūtvā savitāramabhilakṣya saprante paricaranti |

mitrabhūtam |
ᵥuta api ca ᵥahirbudhnyaḥ |
ᵥantarīkṣe bhavo budhnyaḥ |
etītyādiḥ |
etatpada- dvayābhidho madhyamasthāno'bhirahirbudhnya ityucyate |
saviturmitrabhūtaḥ so'pi teṣāṃ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ savitṛviṣayaṃ stotraṃ ᵥśṛṇotu |
tathā ᵥvarutrī vāgdevī ca savitṛsahitā' satī ᵥekadhenubhiḥ mukhyābhirgobhiḥ ᵥni ᵥpātu nitarāmasmān pālayatu ||
anu tanno jāspatimaṃsīṣṭa ralaṃ devasya savituriyānaḥ |
bhargamugro'rvase johavīti bhagamanugro ardha yāti ratnam ||
6 ||
anu | tat | naḥ | jāḥpatiḥ | maṃsīṣṭa | ratnam | devasya | savituḥ | iyānaḥ | bhargam | ugraḥ | avase | johavīti | bhagam | anugraḥ | ardha | yāti | ratnam || 6 ||
ᵥiyānaḥ asmābhiryācyamānaḥ ᵥjāspatiḥ prajānāṃ pālakaḥ savitā devaḥ ᵥsavituḥ ᵥdevasya svasya saṃbandhvi ᵥralaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ ᵥtat prasiddhaṃ dhanaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmākam ᵥanu ᵥmaṃsīṣṭa anumanyatām |
ᵥugraḥ ojasvī stotā ᵥbhagaṃ bhajanīyaṃ savitāraṃ bhagasaṃjñakaṃ devaṃ vā ᵥavase no'smākaṃ rakṣaṇāya ᵥjohavīti bhṛśaṃ hayati |
ᵥadha api ca ᵥanugraḥ asamarthaḥ stotā ᵥbhagam etatsaṃjñakaṃ savitāraṃ vā ᵥralaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ tatprasiddhaṃ dhanaṃ ᵥyāti yācate ||
vaiśvadeve parvaṇi vājinasya haviṣaḥ 'śaṃ no bhavantu' ityādi ke dve yājyānuvākye |
sūtritaṃ ca- 'śaṃ no bhavantu vājino haveṣu vājevāje'vata vājino na ityudhvaṃrju ranavānaṃ yājyām' (āśva. śrau. 2. 16 ) iti ||
śaṃ no bhavantu vājino haveṣu devatrātā mitadravaḥ svarkāḥ |
jambhayanto'hiṃ vṛkaṃ rakṣāṃsi sanemyasmadyuyuvanmīvāḥ ||
7 ||
śam | naḥ | bhavantu | vājinaḥ | haveṣu | deva'trātā | mita'dravaḥ | su'arkāḥ | jambhayantaḥ | ahim | vṛkam | rakṣāṃsi | sanemi | asmata | yuyuvan | amīvāḥ || 7 ||
ᵥdevatrātā devatrātau yajñe ᵥhaveṣu asmadīyeṣu stotreṣu ᵥmitadravaḥ mitadravaṇā mitamārgāḥ ᵥsvarkāḥ śobhanāḥ|

1. kha-bha-savitre mahitā; gha-jha-śa-savitṛprahitā |
2. kha-na-bha-śobhanāntāḥ; gha-śobhanāgāḥ; śa-śobhamānāḥ |
3. kha-jha-jña-t-2-4-bha-pṛthakkurvati |
he vājina: etannāmakā devā: viprā: medhāvina: amṛtā: amaradharmāṇa: ṛtajñā: satyaṃ jānanta evaṃbhūtā: santo yūyaṃ vājevāje sarveṣu yuddheṣu na: asmān dhaneṣu dhananimitteṣu avata pālayata|
tato yūyam asya madhva: madhuropetamimaṃ somaṃ pibata|
somapānānantaraṃ mādayadhvaṃ yūyaṃ nṛsā bhavata|
tata: tṛsā: yūyaṃ devayānai: devagamanasādhanai: pathibhi: mārgai: yāta gacchata |

‘ūrdhvo abhi:’ iti saptarcaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vaiśvadevam|
‘ūrdhva: sasa vaiśvadevaṃ tu’ ityanukramaṇikā|
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgika:|
dvitīye chandome praugaśastre ‘ūrdhvo abhi:’ iti vaiśvadevastṛca:|
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘ūrdhvo abhi: sumatiṃ vasvo aśreduta syā na: sarasvatī juṣāṇeti praugam’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 10) iti|
ūrdhvo abhi: sumatiṃ vasvo aśa
^vasavaḥ vasusaṃjñakāḥ ^devāḥ ^atra asmin yajñe ^jmyāḥ pṛthivyāṃ bhavāḥ ^ranta ramayantām |
^urau vistīrṇe ^antarikṣe sthitāḥ ^śubhāḥ dīpyamānā marutaśca ^marjayanta paricaryante |
he ^urugrayaḥ prabhūtagamanā vasavo marutaśca yūyaṃ ^pathaḥ yuṣmadīyān mārgān ^arvāk asmadabhimukhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ^kṛṇudhvaṃ kuruta |
api ca yūyaṃ ^jagmuṣaḥ yuṣmān prati gatavataḥ' ^naḥ asmadīyasya ^asya ^dūtasya aprerāhvānaṃ ^śrota śṛṇuta |
abhirhi yajamānānāṃ dūtaḥ san devānāhvayatītyarthaḥ ||
te hi yajñeṣu yajñiyāsa ūmāḥ sadhasthaṃ viśve abhi santi devāḥ |
tāṃ adhvara uśato yakṣyagre śruṣṭī bhagaṃ nāsatyā puraṃdhim ||
4 ||
te | hi | yajñeṣu | yajñiyāsaḥ | ūmāḥ | sadha'stham | viśve | abhi | santi | devāḥ | tān | adhvare | uśataḥ | yakṣi | agne | śruṣṭī | bhagam | nāsatyā | puram'dhim || 4 ||
^yajñeṣu yāgeṣu ^te ^hi te khalu prasiddhāḥ ^yajñiyāsaḥ yajñārhāḥ ^ūmāḥ rakṣakāḥ ^viśve sarve ^devāḥ ^sadhasthaṃ sahasthānam ^abhi ^santi abhibhavanti ākrāmanti |
he ^agne ^adhvare asmadīye yajñe ^uśataḥ kāmayamānān ^tān devān ^yakṣi yaja |
tathā ^śruṣṭī |
kṣipramaitat |
kṣipraṃ ^bhagam etatsaṃjjñakaṃ devaṃ ^nāsatyā nāsatyāśvinau ca ^puraṃdhi puruṇāṃ dhyātāramindraṃ ca yaja ||
āye girau diva ā pṛthivyā mitraṃ vaha varuṇamindramagnim |
āryamaṇamaditiṃ viṣṇumeṣāṃ sarasvatī maruto mādayantām ||
5 ||
ā | agne | giraḥ | divaḥ | ā | pṛthivyāḥ | mitram | vaha | varuṇam | indram | agnim | ā | aryamaṇam | aditim | viṣṇum | eṣām | sarasvatī | marutaḥ | mādayantām || 5 ||
he ^agne tvaṃ ^divaḥ dyulokāt sakāśāt ^giraḥ garaṇīyān stutyān devānasmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ prati ^ā ^vaha āhvānaṃ kuru |
^pṛthivyāḥ antarikṣācca ^ā vaha |
kān devāniti taducyate |
^mitram etatsaṃjjñakaṃ ^varuṇaṃ ca ^indraṃ ca deveṣu devatāsvarūpeṇāvasthitaṃ ca ^agnim ^aryamaṇam etatsaṃjjñakam ^aditim ardānāṃ pṛthivīṃ ca ^viṣṇuṃ ca |
evaṃbhūtān devān ^eṣām asmākaṃ yajamānānāmarthāya ^ā vaha |
^sarasvatī vāgdevatā ca ^marutaḥ ca ^mādayantām |
asmadīyeḥ stotrairvibhinnaśca mādyantu ||
re havyaṃ matibhiryajñiyānāṃ nakṣatkāmaṃ martyānāmasinvan |
dhātā rayimavidasyaṃ sadāsāṃ sakṣīmahi yujyebhirnu devaiḥ ||
6 ||
re | havyam | mati'bhiḥ | yajñiyānām | nakṣat | kāmam | martyānām | asinvana | dhātā | rayim | avi'dasyam | sadā'sām | sakṣīmahi | yujyebhiḥ | nu | devaiḥ || 6 ||
^yajñiyānām |
caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
yajñārhebhyo devebhyaḥ ^matibhiḥ asmadīyābhiḥ stutibhiḥ saha ^havyaṃ haviḥ ^re asmābhirdīyate |
^martyānāṃ manuṣyāṇāmasmākaṃ ^kāmam abhilāṣam ^asinvan apratibandhabhiḥ ^nakṣat asmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ vyāpnotu |
he devā yūyam ^avidasyam anupakṣapaṇīyaṃ ^sadā'sām |
1. jha-gantumicchati | 2. jñāta-1-2-4 'te' nāsti | 3. ta-mu-pṛthvī | 4 na-vyāpnoti |
^sadāsāṃ sarvadā saṃbhajanīyaṃ ^rathi dhanaṃ ^dhāta asmabhyaṃ datta |
^nu agha vayaṃ ^yujyebhiḥ sahāyabhūtairaਿਹ yajñe samāgataiḥ ^devaiḥ ^sakṣīmahe |
bahu |
nvityupamārthe |
yujyebhinuṃ bandhubhiriva devaiḥ sakṣīmahe' |
nū rodasī abhiṣṭute vasiṣṭhaiṛtāvāno varuṇo mitro agniḥ | yacchantu candrā upamaṃ nau arke yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 7 || nu | rodasī iti | abhiṣṭute ityabhi'stute | vasiṣṭhaiḥ | ṛta'vānaḥ | varuṇaḥ | mitraḥ | agniḥ | yacchantu | candrāḥ | upa'mam | naḥ | arkam | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||7||
^nu agha ^rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^vasiṣṭhaiḥ asmābhiḥ |
pūjārthaṃ bahuvacanam |
^abhiṣṭute abhitaḥ sarvataḥ stute abhūtām |
tathā ^ṛtāvānaḥ asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇairyajñairupetāḥ ^varuṇaḥ ^mitraḥ ^agniḥ ca evaṃbhūtā devā asmābhirabhiṣṭutā āsan |
^candrāḥ āhlādakāḥ sarve devāḥ ^naḥ asmabhyam ^upamam upamānabhūtamutkṛṣṭam ^arkam annaṃ ^yacchantu dadatu |
asmin sūkte pratipāditā ye devāste sarve ^yūyaṃ ^naḥ asmān ^svastibhiḥ avināśaiḥ ^sadā sarvadā ^pāta pālayata |
6 |
'o śruṣṭiḥ' iti sasarca saptamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vaiśvadevam |
'o śruṣṭiḥ' ityanukrāntam |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
o śruṣṭirvidadhyā^1 sametu prati stomaṃ dadhīmahi turāṇām | yadadya devaḥ savitā suvāti syāmāsya ratirnau vibhāge || 1 || o iti | śruṣṭiḥ | vidadhyā | sam | etu | prati | stomam | dadhīmahi | turāṇām | yat | adya | devaḥ | ^savitā | suvāti | syām | asya | ratirnaḥ | vi'bhāge || 1 ||
he devāḥ ^vidadhyā vidadhena tvadīyena cittena saṃpādhā ^śruṣṭiḥ sukhamasmān ^o ā ^sametu āgacchatu |
athavā śruṣṭirvegavatī vidadhyā vidadhe yajñe kriyamāṇāmasmadīyā stutiryupamānāgacchatu |
vayaṃ ^turāṇāṃ vegavatāṃ devānāṃ ^stomaṃ stotraṃ ^prati ^dadhīmahi kurvīmahi |
^adya idānīṃ ^savitā ^devaḥ ^yat dhanaṃ ^suvāti asmabhyaṃ prerayet ^ratirnaḥ ramaṇīyadhanavataḥ ^asya savitustasya dhanasya ^vibhāge dāne ^syāma vayaṃ bhavema |
mitrastambho varuṇo rodasī ca dhubhaktamindro aryamā dadātu | dideṣṭu devyaditi rekṇo vāyuśca yanniyuvaite bhagaśca || 2 || mitraḥ | tat | naḥ | varuṇaḥ | rodasī iti | ca | dhu'bhaktam | indraḥ | ^aryamā | dadātu | dideṣṭu | devī | aditiḥ | rekṇaḥ | vāyuḥ | ca | yat | niyuvaite iti ni'yuvaite | bhagaḥ | ca ||2||
^mitraḥ devaḥ ^naḥ asmabhyaṃ ^tat prasiddhaṃ dhanaṃ ^dadātu prayacchatu |
tathā ^varuṇaḥ dadātu |
^rodasī ^ca dyāvāpṛthivyau ca dattām |
tathā ^indraḥ ^dhubhaktaṃ śubhirdhotamainaiḥ stotṛbhiḥ sevitaṃ taddhanaṃ dadātu |
^aryamā ca dadātu |
tathā ^aditiḥ ^devī ^rekṇaḥ dhanaṃ ^dideṣṭu taddhanamasmabhyaṃ diśatu |
^vāyuśca ^bhagaśca ubhau devau ^yat dhanaṃ ^niyuvaite asmābhitarāṃ yojayetāṃ taddhanamiti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ |
1. jha-sakṣīmahe saṃgatā bhavema |
sedugro a̭stu ma॒ruta॒ḥ sa śuṣmī॒ yaṃ ma॒rtyaṃ pṛ̭ṣa॒daśvā॒ avā̭tha | u॒te॒mibhi̭ḥ sa॒rasva̭tī ju॒nanti॒ na tasya̭ rā॒yaḥ pa̭ryètāsti || 3 || saḥ | it | u॒graḥ | a॒stu | ma॒ruta̭ḥ | saḥ | śuṣmī̭ | yam | ma॒rtyam | pṛ̭ṣa॒t'aśvāḥ | avā̭tha | u॒ta | ī॒m | a॒bhiḥ | sa॒rasva̭tī | ju॒nanti̭ | na | tasya̭ | rā॒yaḥ | pari̭'etā | a॒sti || 3 || he ᵥpṛṣadaśvāḥ | pṛṣacchabdena kecinmṛgaviśeṣā ucyante | ta evāśvā vāhā yeṣāṃ te | evaṃbhūtā he ᵥmarutaḥ rudraputrā devā yūyaṃ ᵥmartyaṃ maraṇadharmāṇaṃ ᵥyaṃ yajamānam ᵥavātha pālayeta ᵥset sa eva yajamānaḥ ᵥugraḥ ᵥastu ojasvī bhava
mā | atre | pūṣan | āghuṇe | irasya | varūtrī | yat | rāti'sācaḥ | ca | rāsan | bhūyaḥ'bhuvaḥ | naḥ | arvantaḥ | ni | pāntu | vṛṣṭim | pari'jmā | vātaḥ | dadātu || 6 ||
he āghuṇe prāsadapte evaṃbhūta he pūṣan deva atra asmin dāne mā irasya vighātaṃ mā kṛthāḥ |
varūtrī sarvairvaṇīyā sarasvatī rātisācaśca |
rātirdānam |
tasya saṃbhaktyo devapattyaśca yat dhanaṃ rāsan asmabhyaṃ prayaccheyuḥ |
atra mā kṛthā iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
kiṃca mayobhuvaḥ sukhasya bhāvakāḥ arvantaḥ gacchanto devāḥ naḥ asmān ni pāntu nitarāṃ pālayantu |
parijmā parito gantā vātaḥ vāyuḥ vṛṣṭi vṛṣṭilakṣaṇamudakaṃ dadātu prayacchatvasmabhyam ||
nū rodasī abhiṣṭute vasiṣṭhairṛtāvāno varuṇo mitro agniḥ |
yacchantu candrā upamaṃ no arke yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
7 ||
nu | rodasī iti | abhiṣṭute ityabhi'stute | vasiṣṭhaiḥ | ṛta'vānaḥ | varuṇaḥ | mitraḥ | agniḥ | yacchantu | candrāḥ | upa'mam | naḥ | arkam | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||7||
pūrvaṃ vyākhyāteyam |
akṣarārthastu |
dyāvāpṛthivyau varuṇadayo devāśca vasiṣṭhairasmābhira- bhihutā bhavanti |
evaṃbhūtā āhvādakā devāḥ sarvotkṛṣṭamasmabhyaṃ dadatu |
asmin sūkte prati- pāditāḥ sarve devā yūyaṃ kalyāṇairasmān sarvadā pālayata ||
7 ||
‘prātarabhima’ iti saptarcamaṃ sūktam |
atrānukramaṇikā—‘prātarbhāṃgaṃ jagatyādyā liṅgokta- devatāntyopasyā’ iti |
vasiṣṭha ṛṣiḥ |
ādyā jagatī śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |
ādyāmīndrādidevatyā dvitīyādyāḥ pañca bhagadevatyāḥ saptamyuṣodevatyā |
atra kecidāhuḥ ‘niveṣṭukāmo rogārto bhagasūktaṃ japelasadā |
niveśaṃ viśati kṣipraṃ rogaiśca parimucyate’ (ṛgviva. 2. 25) iti ||
prātaragniṃ prātarindraṃ havāmahe prātarmitrāvaruṇā prātaraśvinā |
prātarbhagaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇaspatiṃ prātaḥ somamuta rudraṃ huvema ||
1 ||
prātaḥ | agnim | prātaḥ | indram | havāmahe | prātaḥ | mitrāvaruṇā | prātaḥ | aśvinā | prātaḥ | bhagam | pūṣaṇam | brahmaṇaḥ | patim | prātariti | somam | uta | rudram | huvema || 1 ||
prātaḥ upaḥkāle agni devaṃ havāmahe vayaṃ stotāra āhvayāmaḥ |
tathā prātaḥkāle indraṃ havāmahe |
tathā mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇābahorātrābhimāninau devau prātaḥ vayaṃ havāmahe |
tathāśvinau devānāṃ bhiṣajau prātaḥ vayaṃ havāmahe |
tathā prātaḥ bhagaṃ devaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ devaṃ brahmaṇaspati mantrābhimāninametatsaṃjñakaṃ āhvayāmaḥ |
tathā prātaḥ somam etatsaṃjñakaṃ devam uta api ca rudraṃ devaṃ ca huvema āhvayāmaḥ ||
prātarjitaṃ bhagamugraṃ huvema vayaṃ putramaditiryo vidhartā |
ādhraścidyaṃ manyamānasturaścidrājā cid bhagaṃ bhakṣītyāha ||
2 ||
1. kha-sa-ta-na-bha-śa- ‘devāḥ’ nāsti |
prātaḥ'jitam | bhargam | ugram | huvem | vayam | putram | aditaiḥ | yaḥ | vi'dhartā | ādhaḥ | cit | yam | mandyamānaḥ | turaḥ | cit | rājā | cit | yam | bhargam | bhakṣi | iti | āha || 2 ||
vaḥ bhago devaḥ vidhātā viśvasya jagato dhārakaḥ jitaṃ jayaśīlam ugram udūrṇam aditeḥ putraṃ bhagaṃ devaṃ prātaḥkāla eva vayaṃ huvema āhvayāmaḥ |
ādhaśrit daridro'pi stotā yaṃ bhagaṃ devaṃ manyamānaḥ stuvan bhagaṃ bhajanīyaṃ dhanaṃ bhakṣi vibhaja mahyaṃ dehi iti āha bravīti |
turaśrit |
turatirgatikarmā |
prāptadhano'pi rājā cit samartho'pi janaḥ yaṃ bhagaṃ devaṃ bhajanīyaṃ dhanaṃ mahyaṃ bhakṣa dehītyāha |
taṃ bhagaṃ prātareva vayaṃ huvemeti sambandhaḥ ||
bhaga praṇetarbhaga satyarādho bhagemāṃ dhiyamudavā dadannaḥ |
bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhiraśvairbhaga pra nṛbhirnṛvantaḥ syāma ||
3 ||
bhaga | praṇetiti pra'nainataḥ | bhaga | satya'rādhaḥ | bhaga | imām | dhiyam | ut | ava | dadat | naḥ | bhaga | pra | naḥ | janaya | gobhiḥ | aśvaiḥ | bhaga | pra | nṛbhiḥ | nṛ'vantaḥ | syāma || 3 ||
he bhaga deva tvaṃ praṇetā prakarṣeṇa netāsi |
tādṛśa praṇetaḥ he bhaga tvaṃ satyarādhaḥ satya- dhano'si |
tādṛśa satyarādho he bhaga tvaṃ naḥ asmabhyaṃ dadat kāmān prayacchan imām asmadīyāṃ dhiyaṃ stutim udava udrakṣa phalayuktāṃ kuru |
he bhaga tvaṃ gobhiraśvaiḥ ca naḥ asmān pra janaya prokṣatān kuru |
he bhaga tvatprasādādvayaṃ nṛbhiḥ netṛbhiḥ putrādibhiḥ nṛvantaḥ manuṣyavantaḥ pra syāma prabhavema ||
utedānīṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma uta prapitva uta madhye ahnām |
utoditā maghavantsūryasya vayaṃ devānāṃ sumatau syāma ||
4 ||
uta | idānīm | bhaga'vantaḥ | syāma | uta | pra'pitve | uta | madhye | ahnām | uta | ut'ītā | magha'van | sūryasya | vayam | devānām | su'matau | syāma || 4 ||
uta api ca idānīṃ vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
he bhaga bhagena tvayā svāminā yuktā bhavema |
yadvā |
bhagavanto dhanavantaḥ syāma |
uta api ca prapitve ahnāṃ prāte pūrvāhṇe bhagavantaḥ syāma |
uta api ca ahnāṃ divasānāṃ madhye madhyāhne bhagavantaḥ syāma |
uta api ca he maghavan dhanavan bhaga deva sūryasya sarvasya prerakasya devasya uditā uditau udaye sati vayaṃ tvadanugrahāt devānām indrādīnāṃ sumatau anugrahabuddhau syāma bhavema ||
bhaga eva bhagavā~ astu devāstena vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
taṃ tvā bhaga sarva ijjohavīti sa nau bhaga puraetā bhaveha ||
5 ||
bhagaḥ | eva | bhaga'vān | astu | devāḥ | tena | vayam | bhaga'vantaḥ | syāma | tam | tvā | bhaga | sarvaḥ | it | johavīti | saḥ | naḥ | bhaga | puraḥ'etā | bhava | iha ||5||
1. kha-bhakṣi bhaja mahyaṃ dehi; śa-mahyaṃ bhārkṣa dehi |
he devāḥ bhagaḥ devaḥ eva bhagavān dhanavān astu |
tena bhagena devena dhanena vā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma dhanavanto bhavema |
he bhaga taṃ prasiddhaṃ tvā tvāṃ sarvaṃ ita sarva eva janaḥ johavīti bhṛśaṃ punaḥpunarhvayati |
he bhaga deva saḥ tvam iha asmin yajñe naḥ asmākaṃ puraetā purogantā bhava ||
sarmadhvarāyoṣasau namanta dadhikrāveva śucaye padārya |
arvācīnaṃ vasuvidaṃ bhagaṃ no rathamivāśvā vājina ā vahantu ||
6 ||
sam | adhvarāya | upasaḥ | namanta | dadhikrāvā'iva | śucaye | padārya | arvācīnam | vasu'vidam | bhagam | naḥ | ratham'iva | aśvāḥ | vājinaḥ | ā | vahantu ||6||
śucaye śuddhāya gamanayogyāya padāya sthānāya dadhikrāveva aśvo yathā tathā upasaḥ upodevatāḥ adhvarāya asmadīyāya yāgāya saṃ namanta saṃgacchantu |
vājinaḥ vegavantaḥ aśvāḥ rathamiva rathaṃ yathā tathopasaḥ arvācīnam asmadabhimukhaṃ vasuvidaṃ dhanasya prāpakaṃ bhagaṃ devaṃ naḥ asmān prati ā vahantu ānayantu ||
aśvāvatīrgomaṃtīrna uṣāso vīravatīḥ sadamucchantu bhadrāḥ |
ghṛtaṃ duhānā viśvataḥ prapītā yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
7 ||
aśvā'vatīḥ | go'matīḥ | naḥ | upasaḥ | vīra'vatīḥ | sadam | ucchantu | bhadrāḥ | ghṛtam | duhānāḥ | viśvataḥ | pra'pītāḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||7||
bhadrāḥ bhajanīyāḥ upasaḥ upodevatāḥ aśvāvatīḥ aśvavatyo'śvasahitāḥ satyaḥ gomatīḥ gomatyaśca vīravatīḥ vīravatyaḥ putrādijanopetāśca bhavantyaḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ sadaṃ sarvadā ucchantu vyucchantu naiśaṃ tamo vivāsayantu |
kīdṛśyaḥ |
ghṛtam udakaṃ duhānāḥ siñcantyaḥ viśvataḥ sarvaguṇaiḥ prapītāḥ pravṛddhāḥ |
evaṃbhūtā upasastama ucchantu |
asmin sūkte pratipāditā he sarve devāḥ yūyaṃ naḥ asmān sadā sarvadā svastibhiḥ kalyāṇaiḥ pāta pālayata ||
8 ||
‘pra brahmaṇaḥ’ iti ṣaḍcaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vaiśvadevam |
atrānukāntaṃ—‘pra brahmaṇaḥ ṣaḍśvadevaṃ tu’ iti |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
tṛtīye chandome praugaśastre ‘pra brahmaṇaḥ’ iti vaiśvadevastṛcaḥ |
sutritaṃ ca—‘pra brahmāṇo aṅgiraso nakṣanta sarasvatīṃ devayanto havante’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 11) iti ||
pra brahmāṇo aṅgiraso nakṣanta pra krandunarnabhanyasya vetu |
pra dhenarva udumṛtī nayanta yujyatāmadrī adhvarasya peśaḥ ||
1 ||
pra | brahmāṇaḥ | aṅgirasaḥ | nakṣanta | pra | krandatuḥ | nabhanyasya | vetu | pra | dhenavaḥ | ut'srutaḥ | nayanta | yujyatām | adrī | adhvarasya | peśaḥ || 1 ||
brahmāṇaḥ aṅgirasaḥ etannāmakā ṛṣayaḥ pra nakṣanta sarvatra vyāpnuvantu |
krandatuḥ parjanyaḥ nabhanyasya stotrasyāsmadīyaṃ stotraṃ pra vetu prakarṣeṇecchatu |
dhenavaḥ prīṇayitavyo nadyaḥ udasrutaḥ 1. ta-yathā aśvaḥ |
udakāni siñcantyaḥ ᵥpra ᵥnavanta sarpantu'|
ᵥagnī ādriyantau patnīyamānau ᵥadhvarasya yajñasya ᵥpeśa: rūpaṃ ᵥyujyatāṃ yojayetām ||
sugastai agne sarnavittau aśvā yuktvā sute haritau rohitaśca | ye vā sarnabharuṣā vīravāhau huve devānāṃ janimāni sattaḥ || 2 || sugaḥ | te | agne | sarna'vittaḥ | aśvā | yukṣva | sute | haritaḥ | rohitaḥ | ca | ye | vā | sarnan | aruṣāḥ | vīra'vāhaḥ | huve | devānām | janimāni | sattaḥ || 2 || he ᵥagne ᵥsanavittaḥ sanābhirakālādārabhya labdhaḥ ᵥte tvadīyaḥ ᵥaśvā mārgaḥ ᵥsugaḥ suṣṭhu gantavyo bhavatu | kiṃca ᵥharitaḥ śyāmavarṇāḥ ᵥrohitaśca lohitavarṇāścetyubhayavidhāḥ ᵥye ᵥvā ye ca te tvadīyā a
imaṃ nau agne adhvaraṃ juṣasva murutsva indrai yaśasaṃ kṛdhī naḥ | ā naktā barhiḥ sadatāmuṣāśantā mitrāvaruṇā yajeha || 5 || imam | naḥ | agne | adhvaram | juṣasva | murutsu | indrai | yaśasam | kṛdhi | naḥ | ā | naktā | barhiḥ | sadatām | uṣasā | uśantā | mitrāvaruṇā | yaja | iha || 5 ||
he 'gne tvaṃ naḥ asmadīyam adhvaraṃ yajñaṃ juṣasva sevasva |
kiṃca murutsu indrasya sakhibhūteṣu deveṣu indre ca yaśasaṃ yaśoyuktaṃ havilakṣaṇāmavantaṃ naḥ asmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ he agne tvaṃ kṛdhi kuru |
sthāpayetyarthaḥ |
tathā naktā rātriśtra upasā divasaśca |
aharniṃśo ityarthaḥ |
barhiḥ barhiṣi kuśamaye ā sadatām upaviśatām |
api ca he agne uśantā uśantau yajñamicchantau mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau devau iha asmin yajñe yaja pūjaya ||
evābhi sahasyaṃ vasiṣṭha
pra | yajñaḥ | etu | hetvaḥ | na | sartaḥ | ut | yacchadhvam | sa'manasaḥ | ghṛtācīḥ | stṛṇīta | barhiḥ | adhvarāya | sādhu | ūrdhvā | śocīṃṣi | deva'yūni | asthuḥ || 2 ||
ayasmadīyaḥ ٧yajñaḥ ٧pra ٧etu devān prati gacchatu |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
٧hetvo ٧na ٧satiḥ śīghragāmyaśvo yathā tadvat |
he ṛtvijaḥ sarve yūyaṃ ٧samanasaḥ sadṛśamanaskāḥ santaḥ ٧ghṛtācīḥ srucaḥ ٧udyacchadhvaṃ hasta udyamya dhārayata |
tathā ٧adhvarāya yāgaṃ kartuṃ ٧sādhu sādhakaṃ ٧barhiḥ kuśamayaṃ ٧stṛṇīta vedyāṃ chādayata |
he agne ٧devayūni devān kāmayamānāni tvadīyāni ٧śocīṃṣi arcīṣi ٧ūrdhvā ūrdhvamukhāni ٧asthuḥ tiṣṭhantu ||
ā putrāso na mātaraṃ vibhṛtrāḥ sānau devāsau barhiṣaḥ sadantu | ā viśvācīṃ vidadhyāmanaktvagne mā nau devatāta mṛdhaskaḥ || 3 || ā | putrāsaḥ | na | mātaram | vi'bhṛtrāḥ | sānau | devāsaḥ | barhiṣaḥ | sadantu | ā | viśvācīm | vidadhyām | anaktu | agne | mā | naḥ | deva'tātā | mṛdhaḥ | kaḥ ||3||
٧mātaraṃ jananīṃ ٧vibhṛtrāḥ viśeṣeṇa bhartavyāḥ ٧putrāso ٧na putrā iva tadvadasmākaṃ bharaṇīyāḥ |
٧devāsaḥ devāḥ ٧barhiṣaḥ kuśamayasya vedyāmāstīrṇasya ٧sānau unnate deśe ٧ā ٧sadantu upaviśantu |
he agne ٧vidadhyāṃ yajñayogyāṃ tvadīyāṃ jvālāṃ ٧viśvācī |
viśvaṃ sarvaṃ havirañjati gacchatīti viśvācī juhūḥ |
٧ā ٧anaktu ā samantāt siñcatu |
٧devatātā devatātau yuddhe ٧naḥ asmākaṃ ٧mṛdhaḥ hiṃsakān he agne tvaṃ ٧mā ٧kaḥ mā kārṣīḥ |
yajñavācako devatātaśabdo'tra saṃgrāme vartate ||
te sīṃṣapanta joṣamā yarjetrā ṛtasya dhārāḥ sudughā duhānāḥ | jyeṣṭhaṃ vo adya maha ā vastūnomā gantanu sarmanaso yati cha || 4 || te | sīṃṣapanta | joṣam | ā | yarjetrāḥ | ṛtasya | dhārāḥ | su'dughāḥ | duhānāḥ | jyeṣṭham | vaḥ | adya | mahaḥ | ā | vastūnom | ā | gantanu | sa'manasaḥ | yati | cha || 4 ||
٧yajatrāḥ yajanīyāḥ ٧te indrādayo devāḥ ٧ṛtasya udakasya ٧sudughāḥ sukhena dogdhuṃ śakyāḥ ٧dhārāḥ duhānāḥ varṣantaḥ ٧joṣaṃ paryāptaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ٧ā ٧sīpapanta |
sapatiḥ pari- caraṇārthaḥ |
stutibhiḥ ā samantāt paryacīcaran |
asmān paricaraṇaṃ kurvantu |
svīkurvantviti yāvat |
٧adya asmin dine he devāḥ ٧vasūnāṃ dhanānāṃ madhye ٧jyeṣṭhaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ ٧vaḥ yuṣmadīyaṃ ٧mahaḥ mahanīyaṃ dhanam ٧ā gacchatu |
yūyamapi ٧samanasaḥ tulyamatayaḥ santaḥ ٧ā ٧gantana āgacchata |
he devāḥ yūyaṃ ٧- - - āgantaneti sambandhaḥ ||
evā nau agne viśvā daśasya tvayā vayaṃ sahasāvannāskāḥ | rāyā yujā sadhamādo ariṣṭā yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 5 || evā | naḥ | agne | viśvā | daśasya | tvayā | vayam | sahasā'van | āskāḥ | rāyā | yujā | sadha'mādaḥ | ariṣṭāḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 5 ||
1. kha-na-bha-tathā; gha-tathā adya; jha-ta-śa-atha |
he 'gne 'eva evaṃ sutasya 'viśvu prajāsu madhye 'naḥ asmabhyam 'ā 'daśasya dhana- mabhiyaccha |
he 'sahasāvan balavadagne 'tvayā 'āskāḥ askannāḥ 'vayaṃ 'yujā nityayuktena 'rāyā dhanena 'sadhamādaḥ saha mādyantaḥ 'ariṣṭāḥ ahiṃsitā bhavema |
asmin sūkte pratipāditāḥ sarve devāḥ 'yūyaṃ 'naḥ asmān kalyāṇaiḥ sarvadā pālayata ||
10 ||
‘dadhikrāṃ baḥ’ iti pañcamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ dadhikrāvyadevatākam |
ādyā jagatī sā tu dadhikrādiliṅgoktadevatā śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |
anukramyate hi—‘dadhikrāṃ dadhikraṃ jagatyādyā liṅgoktadevatā’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
dadhikrāṃ vaḥ prathamaśvinoṣasamagniṃ samiddhaṃ bhagamūtaye huve |
indraṃ viṣṇuṃ pūṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇaspatimādityāndyāvāpṛthivī apaḥ svaḥ ||
1 ||
vdadhikrāvāṇam aśvaviśeṣaṃ vbubudhānaḥ stotreṇa bodhayajñaham vagni devam vupa vhruve upastaumi |
tathā ca vupasam upodevatāṃ vsūrya sarvasya prerakaṃ devaṃ vgāṃ bhūmim |
vāgdevatāṃ vā |
evaṃvidhān devānahamupastaumi |
vmaṃtrataḥ |
manyamānān stuvato janāṃścetayate jānātīti yadvābhimabhya- mānāṃścātayate nāśayatīti maṃtratuḥ |
tasya vvaruṇasya vbrahma mahāntaṃ vbahuṃ piṅgalavarṇamabhaṃ tasyā- nayanārthamahamupa hruve |
vte devāḥ vasmat asantaḥ vviśvā viśvāni sarvāṇi vduritā duritāni pāpāni vyavayantu' pṛthakurvantu ||
dadhikrāvā prathamo vājyagre rathānāṃ bhavati prajānan |
saṃvidāna uṣasā sūryeṇādityebhirvasubhiraṅgirobhiḥ ||
4 ||
dadhi'krāvā | prathamaḥ | vājī | arvā | agre | rathānām | bhavati | pra'jānan | sam'vidānaḥ | uṣasā | sūryeṇa | ādityebhiḥ | vasu'bhiḥ | aṅgiraḥ'bhiḥ || 4 ||
vprathamaḥ sarvepāmaśvānāṃ mukhyaḥ vvājī śīghragāmī varvā gamanaśīlaḥ vdadhikrāvā aśvarūpo devaḥ vprajānan rathasaṃyojanīyāstāṃstānasthānadhiṣṭhāya jñātavyāni samyagjānan vrathānām vagre pramukhaḥ vbhavati |
kīdṛśo'śvaḥ |
vupasā upodevatayā vsūryeṇa sarvasya prerakeṇa devena vādityebhiḥ ādityaiśca vvasubhiḥ ca vaṅgirobhiḥ devaiḥ saha stotryairṛṣibhiśca vsaṃvidānaḥ samyagjānan |
aikamatyaṃ prāpta ityarthaḥ ||
ā nau dadhikrāḥ pṛthyāmanaktutvasya panthāmanvaitvā u |
śṛṇotu no daivyaṃ śardho agniḥ śṛṇvantu viśve mahiṣā amūrāḥ ||
5 ||
ā | naḥ | dadhikrāḥ | pṛthyām | anaktu | ṛtasya | panthām | anu'etvai | ū~ iti | śṛṇotu | naḥ | daivyam | śardhaḥ | agniḥ | śṛṇvantu | viśve | mahiṣāḥ | amūrāḥ || 5 ||
vdadhikrāḥ aśvarūpo devaḥ vṛtasya yajñasya vpanthāṃ panthānaṃ mārgam vanvetvai anugantuṃ pravṛttānāṃ vnaḥ asmākaṃ vpathyāṃ padavīm vā vanaktu udakenāsiñcatu |
vu iti pūraṇaḥ |
vdaivyaṃ vśardhaḥ devasambandhi balamīdṛgrūpaḥ vagniḥ vnaḥ asmadīyaṃ havaṃ vśṛṇotu |
vamūrāḥ amūḍhāḥ vmahiṣāḥ mahāntaḥ vviśve sarve devā asmadīyaṃ havaṃ vśṛṇvantu ||
11 ||
‘ā devo yātu’ iti caturṛcaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ savitṛdevatākaṃ traiṣṭubham |
‘ā devaśratuṣkaṃ sāvitram’ ityanukrāntam |
vyūhe daśarātre caturthe'hani vaiśvadevaśastra idaṃ sāvitra- nividvānam |
sūyate hi—‘caturthe'hanyā devo yātu---’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) |
--- ityeṣā vapānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā devo yātu savitā suratnaḥ sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sahāvā’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti |
adhvamedhe'nusavanaṃ tisraḥ sāvitrya iṣṭayaḥ |
tatra dvitīyasyāmiṣṭau yājyeyam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ya imā viśvā jātānyā devo yātu’ (āśva. śrau. 10. 6) iti ||
ā devo yātu savitā suratno'ntarikṣā vahemāno aśvaiḥ |
haste dadhāno naryā puruṇi niveśayannapasuvaśca bhūma ||
1 ||
1. gha-yāvayaṃ tu |
2. kha-kṣa-ta-na-śa-ekamatyaṃ |
ā | devaḥ | yātu | suvitā | su'ratnaḥ | antarikṣa'prāḥ | vahamānaḥ | athaiḥ | hastai | dardhānaḥ | nayau | puruṇi | ni'veśayan | ca | pra'suvan | ca | bhūma || 1 ||
^su'ratnaḥ śobhanaratnopetaḥ ^antarikṣaprāḥ svakīyena tejasāntarikṣasya pūrayitā ^athaiḥ svakīyai- rvāhaiḥ ^vahamānaḥ udyamānaḥ ^savitā sarvasya prerakaḥ ^devaḥ ^ā ^yātu āgacchatu |
kīdṛśaḥ |
^nayāṃ nayāṃṣi manuṣyahitāni ^puruṇi bahūni dhanāni ^hastai pāṇau ^dadhānaḥ dātuṃ dhārayan ^bhūma bhūtāni ^niveśayaṃśa rātriṣu sve' sthāne' sthāpayaṃśa ^prasuvaṃśa ahaḥsu prerayaṃśa |
evaṃbhūtaḥ savitā deva ā yātu ||
urdasya bāhū śithirā bṛhantā hiraṇyayā divo antā~ anaṣṭām |
nūnaṃ so asya mahimā paniṣṭa śūraścidasmā anu dādapsyām ||
2 ||
^śithirā śithilau dānārtha prasāritau ^bṛhantā bṛhantau mahāntau ^hiraṇyayā hiraṇyayau suvarṇamayau ^asya savituḥ saṃbandhinī ^bāhū hastau ^divaḥ antarikṣasya ^antān paryantān ^ut ^anaṣṭām ūrdhvau santau vyāpyanuvatām |
^nūnam atha ^asya īdṛbhūtasya savituḥ ^saḥ tādṛśaḥ ^mahimā mahattvaṃ ^paniṣṭa asmābhiḥ stūyate |
^sūraśrita sūryo'pi ^asmai savitre ^apasyāṃ karmecchāṃ ^anu ^dāt anudadāt ||
sāvitre paśau puroḍāśasya haviṣo : 'sa ghā naḥ' iti dve anuvākye |
sūtraṃ pūrvamudāhṛtam |
āśvamedhikeṣu sāvitreṣu tṛtīyasyāmiṣṭāvime eva yājyānuvākye |
sūtritaṃ ca-'sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sahāveti dve' (āśva. śrau. 10. 6) iti ||
ma ghā no devaḥ savitā sahāvā sāviṣadupatirvarmūni |
viśrayamāṇo amatimurucīṃ martyabhojanamardha rāsate naḥ ||
3 ||
saḥ | gha | naḥ | devaḥ | suvitā | saha'vā | ā | sāviṣada | vas'patiḥ | varmūni | vi'śrayamāṇaḥ | amatim | urucīm | martya'bhojanam | ardha | rāsate | naḥ || 3 ||
^sahāvā |
tejo'ntarāṇyabhibhāvukaṃ tejo yasya saḥ |
^vasupatiḥ dhanānāṃ pālakaḥ ^saḥ ^savitā ^devaḥ ^naḥ asmabhyaṃ ^vasūni dhanāni ^ā ^sāviṣat ā samantāt prerayati |
^gha iti pūraṇaḥ |
sa savitā devaḥ ^urucīṃ vistīrṇagamanām ^amatiṃ rūpam |
dīptimatyarthaḥ |
^viśrayamāṇaḥ niṣevamāṇaḥ san ^adha adhunā ^naḥ asmabhyaṃ ^martyabhojanaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhogayogyaṃ dhanaṃ ^rāsate dadātu ||
imā giraḥ savitāraṃ sujihvaṃ pūrṇagarbhastimīḻate supāṇim |
citraṃ vayo bṛhadasme dadhātu yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
4 ||
imāḥ | giraḥ | savitāram | su'jihvam | pūrṇa'garbhastam | īḻate | su'pāṇim | citram | vayaḥ | bṛhat | asme iti | dadhātu | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 4 ||
1. kha-bha-manuṣyāṇāṃ hitāni |
2. kha-ta-svasthāne |
3. kha-bha-ntarasyābhibhāvukaṃ |
ᐁimāḥ īdṛgbhūtāḥ ᐁgiraḥ|
gṛṇanti stuvantīti giraḥ stotryaḥ prajāḥ|
yadvā|
imāḥ stutirūpā vācaḥ|
ᐁsujihvaṃ śobhanajihvam|
śobhanavācamityarthaḥ|
ᐁpūrṇagabhasti saṃpūrṇadhanahastaṃ ᐁsavitāraṃ devam ᐁīḻate stuvanti|
sa ca savitā ᐁcitraṃ cāyanīyaṃ ᐁvṛhat mahat ᐁvayaḥ annam ᐁasme asmāsu ᐁdadhātu|
yadvā asme asmabhyaṃ prayacchatu|
he savitṛpramukhā devāḥ ᐁyūyaṃ ᐁnaḥ asmān ᐁsvastibhiḥ kalyāṇeḥ ᐁsadā sarvadā ᐁpāta pālayata|
12 ||
‘imā rudrāya’ iti caturṛcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ rudradevatākam|
anyā triṣṭup śiṣṭā jagatyaḥ|
tathā cānukramaṇikā—‘imā raudraṃ triṣṭubantam’ iti|
śūlagavādiṣu raudrayajñeṣvanena sūktenodīcī digupastheyā|
sūyate hi—‘imā rudrāya sthiradhanvana iti sarvarudrayajñeṣu diśāmupasthānam’ (āśva. gṛ. 4. 9. 21) iti||
imā rudrāya sthiradhanvane giraḥ kṣipreṣave devāya svadhāvne|
arṣāḻahāya sahamānāya vedhase tigmāyudhāya bharatā śṛṇotu naḥ ||
1 ||
imāḥ| rudrāya| sthira'dhanvane| giraḥ| kṣipra'iṣave| devāya| svadhā'vne| arṣāḻahāya| sahamānāya| vedhase| tigma'āyudhāya| bharata| śṛṇotu| naḥ|| 1||
he asmadīyāḥ stotāraḥ yūyam ᐁimāḥ ᐁgiraḥ stutīḥ ᐁrudrāya etannāmakāya ᐁdevāya ᐁbharata dhārayata|
kīdṛśāya|
ᐁsthiradhanvane dṛḍhadhanuṣkāya ᐁkṣipreṣave śīghragāmibāṇāya ᐁsvadhāvne annavate ᐁarṣāḻahāya kenāpyanabhibhūtāya ᐁsahamānāya śatrūṇāmabhibhavitre ᐁvedhase vidhātre ᐁtigmāyudhāya tīkṣṇāstrāya|
evaṃbhūtāya rudrāya stutīḥ bharata|
sa ca rudraḥ ᐁnaḥ asmadīyāḥ stutīḥ ᐁśṛṇotu||
sa hi kṣayeṇa kṣamyasya janmaneḥ sāmrājyena divyasya cetati|
avvantīrupa no duraścarānamīvo rudra jāsu no bhava ||
2 ||
saḥ| hi| kṣayeṇa| kṣamyasya| janmaneḥ| sāmra'rājyena| divyasya| cetati| avvan| avvantīḥ| upa| naḥ| duraḥ| cara| anamīvaḥ| rudra| jāsu| naḥ| bhava|| 2||
ᐁsa ᐁhi sa khalu rudro devaḥ ᐁkṣamyasya kṣamāyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ bhavaḥ|
tasya ᐁjanmaneḥ janasya ᐁkṣayeṇa aiśvaryeṇa ᐁcetati prajñāyate|
ᐁdivyasya janasya ca ᐁsāmrājyena aiśvaryeṇa prajñāyate|
śeṣaḥ pratyakṣakṛtaḥ|
he ᐁrudra deva tvaṃ ca ᐁavvantīḥ tvāṃ stotraistarpayantīḥ ᐁnaḥ asmadīyāḥ prajāḥ ᐁavvan pālayan ᐁduraḥ duryāṇi asmadīyāni gṛhāṇi ᐁupa ᐁcara upagaccha|
kiṃca tvaṃ ᐁnaḥ asmadīyāsu prajāsu ᐁanamīvaḥ|
amīvā rogaḥ|
tāmakurvan ᐁbhava||
yā te didyudarvasṛṣṭā divaspāriṃ kṣmayā carati pari sā vṛṇaktu naḥ|
sahasraṃ te svapivāta bheṣajā mā nastokeṣu tanayeṣu rīriṣaḥ ||
3 ||
yā| te| didyut| arba'sṛṣṭā| divaḥ| pari| kṣmayā| carati| pari| sā| vṛṇaktu| naḥ| sahasram| te| su'apivāta| bheṣajā| mā| naḥ| tokeṣu| tanayeṣu| rīriṣaḥ|| 3||
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-rudrāya|
he rudra <sup>v</sup>te vaidyutātmanastva saṃbandhinī <sup>v</sup>divaspari antarikṣasakāśāt <sup>v</sup>avasṛṣṭā vimuktā <sup>v</sup>yā <sup>v</sup>diśyut aśanirūpā hetiḥ <sup>v</sup>kṣamayā kṣityā kṣitau vā <sup>v</sup>carati vartate <sup>v</sup>sā diśyut <sup>v</sup>naḥ asmān <sup>v</sup>pari <sup>v</sup>vṛṇaku parityajatu|
api ca he <sup>v</sup>svapivāta <sup>v</sup>te tava <sup>v</sup>sahasraṃ bahūni <sup>v</sup>bheṣajā bheṣajāni yānyoṣadhāni santi tānyasmabhyaṃ prayaccheti śeṣaḥ|
<sup>v</sup>naḥ asmākaṃ <sup>v</sup>tokeṣu putreṣu <sup>v</sup>tanayeṣu ca <sup>v</sup>mā <sup>v</sup>rirīṣaḥ hiṃsāṃ mā kṛthāḥ||
mā nau vadhī rudra mā parā dā mā teṃ bhūm prasitau hīḻitasya|
ā nau bhaja barhiṣi jīvaśaṃse yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ||
4||
mā | naḥ | dhnīḥ | rudra | mā | parā | dāḥ | mā | te | bhum | prasitau | hīḻitasya| ā | naḥ | bhaja | barhiṣi | jīvaśaṃse | yūyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ|| 4||
he rudra tvaṃ <sup>v</sup>naḥ asmān <sup>v</sup>mā <sup>v</sup>vadhīḥ mā hiṃsīḥ|
tathā <sup>v</sup>mā <sup>v</sup>parā <sup>v</sup>dāḥ mā ca tyākṣīḥ|
api ca <sup>v</sup>hīḻitasya kruddhasya <sup>v</sup>te tava <sup>v</sup>prasitau prakarṣeṇa bandhane vayaṃ ca <sup>v</sup>mā <sup>v</sup>bhūm|
kiṃca <sup>v</sup>jīvaśaṃse jīvairāśaṃsanīye <sup>v</sup>barhiṣi yajñe <sup>v</sup>naḥ asmān <sup>v</sup>ā <sup>v</sup>bhaja bhāginaḥ kuru|
he rudrapramukhāḥ2 devāḥ <sup>v</sup>yūyaṃ <sup>v</sup>naḥ asmān kalyāṇeḥ3 sarvadā4 pālayata||
13||
‘āpo yaṃ vaḥ’ iti caturṛcaṃ caturdaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣa traiṣṭubhamadevatākam|
‘āpo yamāpam’ ityanukrāntaṃ ca|
gato viniyogaḥ||
āpo yaṃ vaḥ prathamaṃ devayanta indrapānamūrmimakṛṇvateḻaḥ|
taṃ vo vayaṃ śucimaripramadya ghṛtamṛṣaṃ madhumantaṃ vanema||
1||
āpaḥ | yam | vaḥ | prathamam | deva'yantaḥ | indra'pānam | ūrmim | akṛṇvata | iḻaḥ | tam | vaḥ | vayam | śucim | aripram | adya | ghṛta'pṛṣam | madhu'mantam | vanema|| 1||
<sup>v</sup>devayantaḥ devānicchanto'tharvayaḥ he āpaḥ he abdevatāḥ <sup>v</sup>yaḥ yuṣmākaṃ kāryabhūtam <sup>v</sup>indrapānam indreṇa pātavyam <sup>v</sup>iḻaḥ ilāyāḥ bhūmyāḥ saṃbhūtaṃ <sup>v</sup>yam <sup>v</sup>ūrmiṃ somākhyaṃ <sup>v</sup>yaṃ rasaṃ <sup>v</sup>prathamaṃ purā <sup>v</sup>akṛṇvata abhiśavaṇapavanādibhiḥ samastakurvata <sup>v</sup>atha idānīṃ <sup>v</sup>vayam api <sup>v</sup>vaḥ yuṣmadīyaṃ <sup>v</sup>tam ūrmiṃ <sup>v</sup>vanema saṃbhajemahi|
kīdṛśam|
<sup>v</sup>śuciṃ śuddham <sup>v</sup>aripraṃ pāparahitaṃ <sup>v</sup>ghṛtapṛṣaṃ vṛṣṭilakṣaṇamudakaṃ siñcantaṃ <sup>v</sup>madhumantaṃ madhurasopetam|
evaṃbhūtaṃ taṃ vanemeti saṃbandhaḥ||
tamūrmimāpo madhumattamaṃ vo'pāṃ napādavatuśuhemā|
yasminnindro vasubhirmādayate tamaśyāma devayanto vo adya||
2||
tam | ūrmim | āpaḥ | madhumat'tamam | vaḥ | apām | napāt | avatu | āśu'hemā | yasmin | indraḥ | vasubhiḥ | mādayate | tam | aśyāma | deva'yantaḥ | vaḥ | adya|| 2||
he <sup>v</sup>āpaḥ etatsaṃjñakā devāḥ <sup>v</sup>vaḥ yuṣmadīyaṃ <sup>v</sup>madhumattamaṃ rasavattamaṃ <sup>v</sup>tamūrmi prasiddhaṃ somākhyaṃ rasam <sup>v</sup>āśuhemā śīghragatiḥ <sup>v</sup>apāṃ <sup>v</sup>napāt etadākhyo devaḥ <sup>v</sup>avatu pālayatu|
<sup>v</sup>indraḥ <sup>v</sup>yasmin
1. kha-śa-ta-na-śa-aṃtarikṣāt sakāśāt| 2. kha-na-bha-rudramukhāḥ| 3. kha-gha-śa-svastibhiḥ kalyāṇeḥ| 4. gha-śa-sadā sarvadā pāta| 5. kha-ta-ja-bha- 'somākhyaṃ...tamūrmi' nāsti| ka. 1-25
ūmā~ vvasubhi: vāsakairdevai: saha vmādyate mādyet vatha asmin dine vdevayantaḥ devakāmā vayaṃ vva: yuṣmadīyaṃ vtam ūrmim vaśyāma prāpnuyāma ||
śatapavitrā: svadhayā madantīrderdevānāmpi yanti pārtha: tā indrasya na minanti vratāni sindhuṃbhyo havyaṃ ghṛtarvajjuhota || 2 || śata'pavitrā: | svadhayā | madantī: | devī: | devānām | api | yanti | pārtha: | tā: | indrasya | na | minanti | vratāni | sindhu'bhya: | havyam | ghṛta'vat | juhota ||3|| vśatapavitrā: śataṃ bahuni pavitrāṇi pāvanāni rūpāṇi yāsāṃ tā: vsvadhayā svakāryabhūtenānnena vmadantī: janān mādayantya: vdevī: debhyo dyotamānā āpa: vdevānām indrādīnāṃ vpātha: sthānam vapi vyanti praviśanti | vtā: tādṛśya āpa: vindrasya prīṇanāni vvratāni yajñādīni karmāṇi v
saṃprathyai |
idānīṃ vātāṃ gacchatāṃ vaḥ yuṣmadīyāḥ ṛtavaḥ karmaṇāṃ kartāraḥ vibhvaḥ vibhavaḥ samarthāḥ aśvāḥ avāṃcaḥ arvāñco'smadabhimukhāḥ santaḥ naḥ manuṣyahitaṃ rathaṃ yuṣmadīyam ā vartayantu āgamayantu ||
ṛbhurṛbhurbhirabhi vaḥ syām vibhvo vibhubhiḥ śarvasā śarvāṃsi |
vājau asmā~ avatu vājasātāvindreṇa yujā taruṣema vṛtram ||
2 ||
ṛbhuḥ | ṛbhu'bhiḥ | abhi | vaḥ | syām | vi'bhvaḥ | vibhu'bhiḥ | śarvasā | śarvāṃsi | vājaḥ | asmān | avatu | vāja'sātau | indreṇa | yujā | taruṣema | vṛtram || 2 ||
he ṛbhavaḥ ṛbhubhiḥ yuṣmābhivaṃyam ṛbhuḥ uha bhavantītyabhavaḥ |
santaḥ vibhubhiḥ yuṣmābhiḥ viśvaḥ vibhavaśca santaḥ śarvāṃsi śatrūṇāṃ balāni śavasā yuṣmadīyena balena abhi syām abhivavema |
tathā vājasātau saṃgrāme vājaḥ etatsaṃhaka ṛbhuḥ asmānavatu pālayatu |
api ca yujā sahāyabhūtena indreṇa vṛtraṃ śatruṃ vayaṃ taruṣema hanāma |
prāyeṇarbhavo'pīndreṇa saha stūyanta iti ||
te ciddhi pūrvīrbhi santi śāsā viśvā~ arya upuratāti vanavan |
indro vibhvau ṛbhukṣā vājau aryaḥ śatrormithatyā kṛṇvanvi nṛmṇam ||
3 ||
te | cit | hi | pūrvīḥ | abhi | santi | śāsā | viśvān | aryaḥ | upura'tāti | vanavan | indraḥ | vi'bhvau | ṛbhukṣāḥ | vājaḥ | aryaḥ | śatroḥ | mithatyā | kṛṇvan | vi | nṛmṇam ||3||
te tādṛśāḥ indraḥ ṛbhavaśca pūrvīḥ bahvīrasmacchatrusenāḥ śāsā śāsanena svakīyayājñayā |
bahvā |
viśvasyate hiṃsyate'neneti śāsaśabda āyudhavācī |
tena abhi santi abhibhavanti |
ciddhi itīmau pūrṇau |
kiṃca upuratāti |
saptamyā luk ||
upare upalaiḥ pāṣāṇasadṛśāyudhaistāyate vistāryeta ityuparatāti yuddham |
tasmin viśvān samastān arthaḥ arīnśatrūn vanavan hiṃsanti |
viśvā ṛbhukṣāḥ vājaḥ etatsaṃhakā ṛbhavaśca indraḥ ca aryaḥ śatrūṇāmabhigantāraḥ santaḥ śatroḥ saṃbandhva nṛmṇaṃ balaṃ mithatyā |
methateridaṃ rūpam |
mithatirhiṃsā |
tayā vi kṛṇvan vikurvantu |
vināśayantvityarthaḥ ||
nū devāso varivaḥ kartanā no viśve'vase sajoṣaḥ |
samasme iṣaṃ varsavo dadīranyuyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
4 ||
nu | devāsaḥ | varivaḥ | kartanā | naḥ | bhūta | naḥ | viśve | avase | sa'joṣaḥ | sam | asme iti | iṣam | varsavaḥ | dadīran | yuyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||4||
he devāsaḥ devāḥ dyotamānā ṛbhavaḥ yūyaṃ nu atha naḥ asmabhyaṃ varivaḥ dhanaṃ kartanā kuruta prayacchata |
tathā viśve sarve ṛbhavo yūyaṃ sajoṣaḥ saha prīyamāṇāḥ santaḥ naḥ asmā- kam avase rakṣaṇāya bhūta bhavata |
api ca vasavaḥ praśasyā ṛbhavaḥ iṣam annam asme asmabhyaṃ saṃ dadīran saṃprayaccheyuḥ |
he ṛbhavaḥ yūyam asmān sarvadā kalyāṇaiḥ rakṣata ||
15 ||
'samudrajyeṣṭhāḥ' iti caturṛcaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamadevatākam|
tathā cānu- krāntaṃ—'samudrajyeṣṭhā āpam' iti|
gato viniyogaḥ ||
samudrajyeṣṭhāḥ salilasya madhyātpunānā yantyaniviśamānāḥ |
indro yā vajrī vṛṣabho rarāda tā āpo devīriha māmavantu ||
1 ||
samudra'jyeṣṭhāḥ | salilasya | madhyāt | punānāḥ | yanti | ani'viśamānāḥ | indraḥ | yāḥ | vajrī | vṛṣabhaḥ | rarāda | tāḥ | āpaḥ | devīḥ | iha | mām | avantu ||1||
ᵛsamudrajyeṣṭhāḥ|
samudra'rṇavo jyeṣṭhaḥ praśastatamo yāsāmapāṃ tāḥ|
ᵛsalilasya|
antarikṣanāmaitat|
antarikṣasya ᵛmadhyāt mādhyamikāt sthānāt ᵛyanti gacchanti|
kīdṛśyaḥ|
ᵛpunānāḥ viśaṃ śodhayantyaḥ ᵛaniviśamānāḥ sarvadā gacchantyaḥ|
ᵛvajrī vajrabhṛt ᵛvṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitā ᵛindraḥ ᵛyāḥ: niruddhā apaḥ ᵛrarāda likhati ᵛdevīḥ devyaḥ ᵛtāḥ ᵛāpaḥ ᵛiha asmin pradeśe sthitaṃ ᵛmāmavantu rakṣantu abhigacchantu vā ||
yā āpo divyā uta vā sravanti khanitrimā uta vā yāḥ svayaṃjāḥ |
samudrārthā yāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāstā āpo devīriha māmavantu ||
2 ||
yāḥ | āpaḥ | divyāḥ | uta | vā | sravanti | khanitrimāḥ | uta | vā | yāḥ | svayam'jāḥ | samudra'arthāḥ | yāḥ | śucayaḥ | pāvakāḥ | tāḥ | āpaḥ | devīḥ | iha | mām | avantu ||2||
ᵛyā ᵛāpo ᵛdivyāḥ antarikṣabhavāḥ santi|
ᵛuta ᵛvā api ca yāḥ āpaḥ nadyādi- gatāḥ satyaḥ ᵛsravanti gacchanti|
yāśca ᵛkhanitrimāḥ khananena nirvṛttāḥ|
ᵛuta ᵛvā api ca yāḥ ᵛsvayaṃjāḥ svayameva prādurbhavantyaḥ ᵛsamudrārthāḥ|
samudra evārtho gantavyo yāsāṃ tāḥ samudrārthāḥ|
ᵛśucayaḥ dīptiyuktāḥ ᵛpāvakāḥ śodhayitryaśca bhavanti|
ᵛtā ᵛāpaḥ ᵛmāmavantu iti ||
yāsāṃ rājā varuṇo yāti madhye satyānṛte avapaśyañjanānām |
madhuścutaḥ śucayo yāḥ pāvakāstā āpo devīriha māmavantu ||
3 ||
yāsām | rājā | varuṇaḥ | yāti | madhye | satyānṛte | ava'paśyan | janānām | madhu'ścutaḥ | śucayaḥ | yāḥ | pāvakāḥ | tāḥ | āpaḥ | devīḥ | iha | mām | avantu || 3 ||
ᵛvaruṇaḥ ᵛyāsām apāṃ ᵛrājā svāmī ᵛmadhye madhyamaloke ᵛyāti gacchati|
kiṃ kurvan|
ᵛjanānāṃ prajānāṃ ᵛsatyānṛte satyaṃ cānṛtaṃ ca ᵛavapaśyan|
jānannityarthaḥ|
ᵛyāḥ āpaḥ ᵛmadhu- cyutaḥ rasaṃ kṣarantyaḥ ᵛśucayaḥ dīptiyuktāḥ ᵛpāvakāḥ śodhayitryaḥ|
ᵛtā ᵛāpaḥ devyaḥ ᵛmāṃ rakṣantviti ||
yāsu rājā varuṇo yāsu somo viśve devā yāśca mardanti |
vaiśvānaro yāsvagniḥ praviṣṭā āpo devīriha māmavantu ||
4 ||
1. kha-na-bha- mādhyamikasthānāt |
2. kha-ma-vilikhati |
3. kha-sa-ta-na-bha- 'samudrārthāḥ' nāsti |
yāsṷ | rājā̭ | varṷṇaḥ | yāsṷ | soma̭ḥ | viśvḙ | devā̭ḥ | yāsṷ | ūrja̭m | madanti | vaiśvānaraḥ | yāsṷ | a॒gniḥ | pra'vi̭ṣṭaḥ | tāḥ | āpa̭ḥ | devī̭ḥ | iha | mām | ava̭ntu || 4 ||
apāṃ ᵛrājā ᵛvaruṇaḥ ᵛyāsu apsu vartate |
ᵛsomaḥ¹ ᵛyāsu apsu vartate |
ᵛyāsu apsu sthitāḥ ᵛviśve sarve ᵛdevāḥ ᵛūrjam annaṃ ᵛmadanti |
ᵛvaiśvānaraḥ ᵛagniḥ ᵛyāsu ᵛpraviṣṭaḥ |
ᵛtāḥ ᵛāpaḥ devyaḥ ᵛiha sthitaṃ ᵛmāmavantu ||
16 ||
‘ā māṃ mitrāvaruṇā²’ iti³ caturthaṃ saptadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣam |
caturthatijagatī vyūhena śakvarī vāditastisro jagatyaḥ |
prathamā maitrāvaruṇī dvitīyāgneyī tṛtīyā vaiśvadevī caturthī gaṅgādinadīdevatākā |
tathā cānukramaṇikā—‘ā māṃ maitrāvaruṇāgneyī vai
yacchalmalau bhavati yannadīṣu yadoṣadhībhyaḥ pari jāyate viṣam | viśve devā niritastatsuvantu mā māṃ padyena rapasā vidatsaruḥ || 3 || yat | śalmalau | bhavati | yat | nadīṣu | yat | oṣadhībhyaḥ | pari | jāyate | viṣam | viśve | devāḥ | niḥ | itaḥ | tat | suvantu | mā | mām | padyena | rapasā | vidat | saruḥ ||3|| ^yat viṣaṃ ^śalmalau etatsaṃjñake vṛkṣe ^bhavati | ^yat viṣaṃ ^nadīṣu tatrasthāsvapsu prādu- rbhavati | ^pari iti pañcamyarthānuvādī | ^oṣadhībhyaḥ sakāśāt ^yat viṣaṃ ^jāyate utpadyate | ^viśve sarve ^devāḥ ^tat viṣam ^itaḥ asmājjanādeśādvā ^niḥ ^suvantu niḥśeṣeṇa prerayantu | mā māmiti śiṣṭaṃ vyākhyātam ||
yāḥ pravato nivata udvata udanvatīranudakāśca yāḥ | tā asmabhyaṃ payasā pinvamānāḥ śivā devīraśipadā bhavantu sarvī nadyo aśimidā bhavantu || 4 || yāḥ | pravataḥ | nivataḥ | uta | udvataḥ | udanvatīḥ | anudakāḥ | ca | yāḥ | tāḥ | asmabhyam | payasā | pinvamānāḥ | śivāḥ | devīḥ | aśipadāḥ | bhavantu | sarvāḥ | nadyaḥ | aśimidāḥ | bhavantu || 4 || ^yāḥ nadyaḥ ^pravataḥ pravaṇadeśe gacchantyaḥ | ^yāḥ ^nivataḥ nimnadeśe gacchantyaḥ | ^yāḥ ^udvataḥ uccatadeśe gacchantyaḥ | ^yāḥ ^udanvatīḥ udakayuktāḥ | ^anudakāśca udakarahitāśca ^yāḥ nadyo yānti | ^payasā udakena ^pinvamānāḥ viśvamāpyāyayantyaḥ ^devīḥ devyo dyotamānāḥ ^tāḥ tādṛśyo nadyaḥ ^asmabhyam ^aśipadāḥ | śipadaṃ nāma rogaviśeṣaḥ | tadakurvantyaḥ satyaḥ ^śivāḥ kalyāṇyaḥ ^bhavantu | api ca ^sarvāḥ tāḥ ^nadyaḥ ^aśimidāḥ | kimivadharmāḥ | ahiṃsāpradāḥ ^bhavantu || ||17||
‘ādityānām’ iti tṛcamaṣṭādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhādityadaivatakam |
anukramyate ca—‘ādityānāṃ tṛcamādityaṃ tu’ iti |
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ |
ādityadevatāke paśau ‘ādityā- nāmavasā’ iti vapāyā anuvākyā |
sutritaṃ ca—‘ādityānāmavasā nūtanena girā ādityebhyaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti |
ādityagrahasyaiṣānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ādityānāmavasā nūtanena hotā yakṣadādityān’ (āśva. śrau. 5. 10) iti ||
ādityānāmavasā nūtanena sakṣīmahi śarmaṇā śaṃtamena | anāgāstve adititve turāsa imaṃ yajñaṃ dadhatu śroṣamāṇāḥ || 1 || ādityānām | avasā | nūtanena | sakṣīmahi | śarmaṇā | śaṃtamena | anāgāḥtve | adititve | turāsaḥ | imam | yajñam | dadhatu | śroṣamāṇāḥ || 1 ||
1. śa-pravataḥ prakarṣeṇa vahantyaḥ nivataḥ |
2. kha-sa-ta-na-bha-śa- ‘yā nivato......unnatadeśe gacchantyaḥ’ nāsti; gha-yā nivataḥ nīcairgacchantyaḥ udvataḥ yā ūrdhvaṃ gacchantyaḥ |
^ādityānām aditaḥ putrāṇāmetatsaṃjñakānāṃ devānām ^avasā rakṣaṇena |
taddhetubhūtenetyarthaḥ |
^nūtanena adyatanena ^śaṃtamena |
śaṃ sukham |
atiśayena tatkaraṇena ^śarmaṇā |
śarmeti gṛhaṇāmaitat |
gṛheṇa ^sakṣīmahi vayaṃ saṃgacchemahi |
^turāsaḥ turāsvaritā ādityāḥ ^śroṣyamāṇāḥ asma- dīyāni stotrāṇi śṛṇvantaḥ ^yajñaṃ paṣṭāram ^imaṃ janam ^anāgāsve anaparādhatve ^adititve adīnatve ^ca ^dadhatu sthāpayantu ||
‘ādityāso aditiḥ’ ityathādityagrahasya yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ādityāso aditi- rmādayantāmiti naitaṃ grahamīkṣeta hūyamānam’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 17 ) iti ||
ādityāso aditirmādayantāṃ mitro aryamā varuṇo rajīṣṭhāḥ | asmākaṃ santu bhuvanasya gopāḥ pibantu somamarvase no adya || 2 || ādityāseḥ | aditiḥ | mādayantām | mitraḥ | aryamā | varuṇaḥ | rajīṣṭhāḥ | asmākam | santu | bhuvanasya | gopāḥ | pibantu | somam | avase | naḥ | adya || 2 ||
^ādityāḥ : ādityā devāḥ ^aditiḥ teṣāṃ mātā ca |
yadvā aditiriti devaviśeṣaṇam |
aditiraditayo'dīnāḥ |
^rajīṣṭhāḥ atiśayenarjavaḥ ^mitro ^aryamā ^varuṇaḥ caitatsaṃjñakāḥ ^māda- yantāṃ tuṣṭāḥ santu |
^bhuvanasya sarvasya jagataḥ ^gopāḥ rakṣakā evaṃbhūtā devāḥ ^asmākaṃ ^santu |
asmākameva rakṣakā bhavantvityarthaḥ |
^adya asmin dine ^naḥ asmākam ^avase rakṣaṇāya ^somam asmābhirabhiṣutaṃ ^pibantu ||
ādityā viśve marutaśca viśve devāśca viśva ṛbhavaśca viśve | indro agniśśvinā tuṣṭuvānā yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 3 || ādityāḥ | viśve | marutaḥ | ca | viśve | devāḥ | ca | viśve | ṛbhavaḥ | ca | viśve | indraḥ | agniḥ | aśvinau | tuṣṭuvānāḥ | yūyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 3 ||
^ādityāḥ aditaḥ putrāḥ ^viśve sarve dvādaśasaṃkhyākā arkāḥ ^viśve ^marutaśca sarve ekona- pañcāśatsaṃkhyaupetāśca ^viśve ^devāśca ^viśve ^ṛbhavaśca ^indraḥ ^agniḥ ^aśvinā aśvinautatsaṃjakā evaṃbhūtā ye devāḥ ^tuṣṭuvānāḥ asmābhiḥ stutaḥ babhūvuḥ sarve te devāḥ ^yūyaṃ ^sadā sarvadā ^naḥ asmān ^svastibhiḥ kalyāṇaiḥ ^pāta rakṣata ||
18 ||
‘ādityāso aditayaḥ’ iti turāsamekonaviṃśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamādityadevatākam |
‘ādityāḥ’ ityanukramaṇikā |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
ādityāso aditayaḥ syāma pūrvevatrā vasavo martyatrā | sanema mitrāvaruṇā sarnanto bhavema dyāvāpṛthivī bhavantaḥ || 1 || ādityāseḥ | aditayaḥ | syāma | pūḥ | deva'trā | vasavaḥ | martya'trā | sanema | mitrāvaruṇā | sarnantaḥ | bhavema | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | bhavantaḥ || 1 ||
1. sva-gha-na-bha-‘devānāṃ’ nāsti |
2. kha-bha-‘ca’ nāsti; jha-ta-na-śa- vā |
3. jha-ta-na- 1. bha-adīnatvena; śa-adititvena ca |
he ādityāsaḥ ādityā devāḥ ||
vyatyayenāyudāttatvābhāvaḥ ||
yadvā |
ādityānāmiva ādityāḥ ||
'tasyedam' ityarthe prāgdīvyatīyo vyapratyayaḥ ||
ādityānāṃ śeṣabhūtā vayam ||
aditayaḥ akhaṇḍanīyāḥ ||
syāma bhavema |
devatrā deveṣu vasavaḥ vāsakā devā yuṣmadīyaṃ vaḥ pālanaṃ martyatrā manuṣyeṣvasmāsu bhavatu |
he mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau sanantaḥ yuvāṃ saṃbhajanto vayaṃ sanema yuvābhyāṃ dattaṃ dhanaṃ saṃbhajemahi |
he dyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau yuvayoḥ prasādāt vayaṃ bhavantaḥ bhavema bhūtimantaḥ syāma ||
mitrastambho varuṇo māmahanta śarma tokāya tanayāya gopāḥ |
mā vo bhujemānyajātameno mā tatkarma vasavo yacyadhve ||
2 ||
mitraḥ | tat | naḥ | varuṇaḥ | māmahanta | śarma | tokāya | tanayāya | gopāḥ | mā | vaḥ | bhujema | anyajātam | enaḥ | mā | tat | karma | vasavaḥ | yat | cyadhve || 2 ||
mitraḥ varuṇaḥ aharniśābhimāninau devāvetadāhāḥ sarva ādityāḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ tat prasiddhaṃ śarma sukhaṃ māmahanta |
maṃrhatidānakarmā |
dadatu |
gopāḥ viśvasya rakṣakāste devāḥ tokāya asmadīyāya putrāya tanayāya tatputrāya ca śarma prayacchantu |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |
he devāḥ vaḥ yuṣmadīyā vayam anyajātam anyenotpāditam enaḥ pāpaṃ mā bhujema mā bhuktavantaḥ syāma |
he vasavaḥ vāsakā devāḥ yat yena yuṣmadapriyaṇa karmaṇā cyadhve yūyamāsmācchādyatha tat tādṛśaṃ karma vayaṃ mā karma mā kārṣma ||
luṅi karoteruttamasya bahuvacanam |
'mantre ghasahara' ityādinā clerlopaḥ ||
turuṇyavo'ṅgiraso nakṣanta ratnaṃ devasya savituriyānāḥ |
pitā ca tanno mahānyajatro viśve devāḥ samanasojuṣanta ||
3 ||
turuṇyavaḥ | aṅgirasaḥ | nakṣanta | ratnam | devasya | savituḥ | iyānāḥ | pitā | ca | tat | naḥ | mahān | yajatraḥ | viśve | devāḥ | samanasaḥ | juṣanta || 3 ||
turuṇyavaḥ yajñādikarmasu tvaritāḥ | aṅgirasaḥ etannāmakā ṛṣayaḥ | iyānāḥ savitāraṃ yācamānāḥ santa: | savituḥ prerakasya devasya saṃbandhi ratnaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ yajñaṃ nakṣanta ābhuvanti | uttarārdha- gatasacchadāpekṣayā yacchabdo'dhyāhriyate | yajatraḥ yajanaśīlaḥ | mahān prabhūtaḥ | pitā ca vasiṣṭhasya pitṛbhūto varuṇaśca | yadvā | sarveṣāṃ pitā prajāpatiḥ | viśve sarve devāḥ samanasaḥ samāna- manaskāḥ | tat tādṛśaṃ ratnaṃ naḥ asmān juṣanta sevayantu | yadvā no'smabhyaṃ dadatu || ||11||
'pra dyāvā yajñaiḥ' iti tṛcātmakaṃ viṃśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyam |
anukramyate ca—'pra dyāvā dyāvāpṛthivyam' iti |
caturthe'hani vaiśvadevaśastra idaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyavinirvidvānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—'ā devo yātu prāveti vāsiṣṭham' (ādha. śrau. 8. 8) iti |
dyāvāpṛthivye paśau vapāpuroḍāśayoḥ 'pra dyāvā' iti dve ṛcau yājye |
sūtritaṃ ca—'pra dyāvā yajñaiḥ pṛthivī namoṃbhiriti dve' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
pra dyāvā yajñaiḥ pṛthivī namoṃbhiḥ sabārdha īḻe bṛhatī yajatre |
te dhidvi pūrve kavayo gṛṇantaḥ puro mahī dadhire devaputre ||
1 ||
1. kha-sevate; gha-kṣa-ta-na-bha-śa-sevaryata |
pra | dyāvā̭ | ya॒jñaiḥ | pṛ॒thivī̭ iti̭ | na॒rmaḥbhi̭ḥ | su'bārdha̭ḥ | ī॒ḻe | bṛhatī iti̭ | ya॒jñatre iti̭ | te iti̭ | ci॒t | hi | pū॒rvai | ka॒vaya̭ḥ | gṛ॒ṇanta̭ḥ | pu॒raḥ | ma॒hī iti̭ | da॒dhire | de॒vapṷtre iti̭ de॒va'pṷtre || 1||
ᵥyajatre yajanīye ᵥbṛhatī bṛhatyau mahatyau ᵥdyāvā ᵥpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥyajñaiḥ yāgaiḥ ᵥnamobhiḥ stotraiścāhaṃ stotā ᵥsabādhaḥ bādhāsahitaḥ |
ṛtvijāṃ saṃbādhayukta ityarthaḥ |
ᵥpra ᵥīḻe prakarṣeṇa staumi |
ᵥmahī mahatyau ᵥdevaputre |
devāḥ putrā yayoste |
ᵥte ᵥciddhi tādṛśyau khalvapi dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥpūrve purātanāḥ ᵥkavayaḥ ᵥgṛṇantaḥ stuvantaḥ ᵥpuraḥ ᵥdadhire purastāt sthāpayāmāsuḥ ||
āgrayaṇe ᵥdyāvāpṛthivyaikakapālasya ‘pra pūrvaje’ iti yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘mahī dyauḥ pṛthivī ca naḥ pra pūrvaje pitarā navyasībhiḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 9) iti ||
pra pū॒rva॒je pita̭rā na॒vyasī̭bhiḥ |
kṛ॒ṇudhva̭ṃ sa॒danḙ ṛ॒tasya̭ |
ā naṷ dyāvā̭pṛthi॒vī daivyḙna |
ja॒nena̭ yātaṃ ma॒hi vā̭ṃ va॒rūtham̭ ||
2 ||
pra | pu॒rva॒je iti̭ pu॒rva'je | pi॒tarā̭ | na॒vyasī̭bhiḥ | gīḥ'bhi̭ḥ | kṛ॒ṇudhvam | sa॒danḙ iti̭ | ṛ॒tasya̭ | ā | naḥ | dyāvā̭pṛthi॒vī iti̭ | daivyḙna | ja॒nena̭ | yā॒tam | ma॒hi | vām | va॒rūtham || 2 ||
he asmadīyāḥ stotāro yūyaṃ ᵥnavyasībhiḥ navatarābhiḥ ᵥgīrbhiḥ stutirūpābhirvāgbhiḥ ᵥṛtasya yajñasya ᵥsadane sthānabhūte ᵥpūrvaje pūrvaṃ prajāte ᵥpitarā pitarau viśvasya mātāpitṛbhūte dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥpra ᵥkṛṇudhvaṃ puraskṛta |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |
he ᵥdyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau yuvāṃ ᵥdaivyena saṃbandhinā ᵥjanena saha ᵥnaḥ asmānbhi ᵥā ᵥyātam āgacchatam |
kimartham āyātamityucyate |
ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥvarūtham asmābhirviraṇīyaṃ ᵥmahi mahat |
thaddhanamastīti śeṣaḥ |
taddhanamasmabhyaṃ dīyatāmityarthaḥ ||
u॒to hi vā̭ṃ ra॒tnadhḙyāni॒ santi̭ |
pu॒rūṇi̭ dyāvā̭pṛthi॒vī su॒dāsḙ |
a॒sme dhatta॒ṃ yada̭sa॒daskṛ̭dhoyu |
yu॒yaṃ pā̭ta sva॒stibhi̭ḥ sa॒dā na̭ḥ ||
3 ||
u॒to iti̭ | hi | vām | ra॒tna'dhḙyāni | santi̭ | pu॒rūṇi̭ | dyāvā̭pṛthi॒vī iti̭ | su॒dāsḙ | a॒sme | dha॒ttam | yat | a॒sat | a॒skṛ̭dhoyu | yu॒yam | pā॒ta | sva॒stibhi̭ḥ | sa॒dā | naḥ || 3||
ᵥuto ᵥhi api ca khalu he ᵥdyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥsudāse śobhana- havirdānāya yajamānāya deyāni ᵥpurūṇi bahūni ᵥratnadheyāni ramaṇīyāni dhanāni ᵥsanti bhavanti |
teṣāṃ madhye ᵥyat dhanam ᵥaskṛdhoyu |
kṛdhuko hrasvaḥ |
ahasvamanalpam ᵥasat bhavettaddhanam ᵥasme asmabhyaṃ ᵥdhattaṃ prayacchatam |
he dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥyūyaṃ yuvāṃ ᵥnaḥ asmān sarvadā kalyāṇaiḥ ᵥpāta pālayatam ||
20||
‘vāstoṣpate’ iti tṛcātmakamekaviṃśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vāstoṣpatyam |
tathā cānu- kramyate—‘vāstoṣpate vāstoṣpatyam’ iti |
smārte gṛhanirmāṇe ‘vāstoṣpate’ iti catasṛbhiḥ pratyṛcaṃ juhuyāt |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘vāstoṣpate prati jānīhyasmāniti catasṛbhiḥ pratyṛcaṃ hutvā’ (āśva. gṛ. 2. 9. 9) iti ||
1. ta1-4-āmnāyaṇe |
vāstoṣpate prati jānīhyasmānsvāveśo anamīvo bhava naḥ | yastvemahe prati tanno juṣasva śaṃ no bhava dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade || 1 || vāstoḥ | pate | prati | jānīhi | asmān | su'āveśaḥ | anamīvaḥ | bhava | naḥ | yat | tvā | īmahe | prati | tat | naḥ | juṣasva | śam | naḥ | bhava | dvi'pade | śam | catuḥ'pade ||1|| he vāstoṣpate gṛhasya pālayitardeva tvam asmān tvadīyān stotṝniti prati jānīhi prabhuṣvasva | tadanantaraṃ naḥ asmākaṃ svāveśaḥ śobhananiveśaḥ anamīvaḥ arogakṛcca bhava | kiṃca vayaṃ tvā tvāṃ yat dhanam īmahe yācāmahe tvamapi tat dhanaṃ naḥ asmabhyaṃ prati juṣasva prayaccha | api ca naḥ asmākaṃ dvipade putrapautrādijanāya śaṃ sukhakaraḥ bhava | catuṣpade asmadīyāya gavāśvādigavāya ca śaṃ sukhakaro bhava ||
vāstoṣpate prataraṇo na edhi gayasphāno gobhiraśvebhirindo | ajarāsaste sakhye syāma piteva putrānprati no juṣasva || 2 || vāstoḥ | pate | pra'taraṇaḥ | naḥ | edhi | gaya'sphānaḥ | gobhiḥ | aśvebhiḥ | indo iti | ajarāsaḥ | te | sakhye | syāma | pitā'iva | putrān | prati | naḥ | juṣasva || 2 || he vāstoṣpate gṛhasya pālayitardeva tvaṃ naḥ asmākaṃ prataraṇaḥ pravartakaḥ gayasphānaḥ gayasya asmadīyasya dhanasya sphāyayitā pravardhakaḥ edhi bhava | he indo somavadāhlādaka te tvayā saha sakhye sati vayaṃ gobhiḥ paśubhiḥ aśvebhiḥ aśvaiśca sahitāḥ ajarāsaḥ jarārahitāḥ syāma bhavema | vināśarahitā ityarthaḥ | piteva putrān yathā pitā putrān rakṣakatvena sevate tathā tvamapi naḥ asmān prati juṣasva sevasva ||
vāstoṣpate śagmayā saṃsadā te sakṣīmahi raṇvayā gātumatyā | pāhi kṣema uta yoge varaṃ no yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 3 || vāstoḥ | pate | śagmayā | sam'sadā | te | sakṣīmahi | raṇvayā | gātu'matyā | pāhi | kṣeme | uta | yoge | varam | naḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 3 || he vāstoṣpate deva śagmayā sukhakaryā raṇvayā ramaṇīyayā gātumatyā dhanavatyā te tvayā saṃsadā sthānena sakṣīmahi vayaṃ saṃgacchemahi | tvamapi kṣeme prāptasya rakṣaṇe uta api ca yoge aprāptasya prāptaṇe varaṃ varaṇīyaṃ vaḥ asmadīyaṃ dhanaṃ pāhi rakṣa | he vāstoṣpate yūyaṃ tvaṃ naḥ asmān sarvadā kalyāṇaiḥ pāta pāhi ||
'amīvahā' ityaṣṭarcaṃ dvāviṃśaṃ sūktam |
ādyā gāyatrī pañcamyādyāstrayo'nuṣṭubho dvitīyādyā- stisra upariṣṭādbṛhatyaḥ |
'aṣṭakā caturdaśakī bṛhatī' iti tallakṣaṇayogāt |
ādyā vāsto- ṣpatidevatākā śiṣṭā aindrāḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam—'amīvahā vāstoṣpatyā gāyatrī śeṣāsyupa- riṣṭādbṛhatvādayo'nuṣṭubhaḥ prastāpibhyaḥ' upaniṣat' iti |
gato viniyogaḥ |
gṛhadevatāyāṃ yaduktaṃ tadiha likhyate—'varuṇasya gṛhān rātrau vasiṣṭhaḥ svamamācaran |
praviveśātha taṃ tatra thā nadajamba- 1. kha-ta-ta-śa-iti prastāpibhya |
vartata |
krandantaṃ sārameyaṃ sa dhāvantaṃ dṛṣṭumudyatam |
yadajuneti ca dvābhyāṃ sāntvayitvā hyasupapat |
evaṃ prasvāpayāmāsa janamanyaṃ ca vāruṇam' (bṛhade. 6. 11-13) iti |
atra kecit punareva- māhuḥ—'āsāṃ prasvāpinitvaṃ' tu kathāsu parikalpyate |
vasiṣṭhastupito'jñārthī trirātrālabdhabhojanaḥ |
caturtharātrau caturthaṃ vāruṇaṃ gṛhametya tu |
koṣṭhāgāre' praveśāya pālakathādisusaye |
yadajuneti saptarcaṃ dadarśa ca jajāpa ca' (anu. bhā. 7. 55) iti |
amīvahā vāstoṣpate viśvā rūpāṇyāviśan | sakhā suśeva edhi naḥ || 1 || amīva'hā | vāstaḥ | pate | viśvā | rūpāṇi | ā'viśan | sakhā | su'śevaḥ | edhi | naḥ || 1 ||
he vāstoṣpate gṛhasya pālakatvatsaṃjjñaka deva amīvahā amīvānāṃ rogāṇāṃ nāśakastvaṃ viśvā viśvāni bahūni rūpāṇyāviśan praviśan |
'yadyadrūpaṃ kāmayate tattaddevatā viśati' (niru. 10.17) iti yāskaḥ |
naḥ asmākaṃ sakhā mitrabhūtaḥ suśevaḥ suṣṭhu sukhakaraḥ edhi bhava |
yadajuna sārameya dūtaḥ piśaṅga yacchase | vīva ājanta ṛṣya upa sakeṣu bapsato ni ṣu svapa || 2 ||
yata | arjuna | sārameya | dūtaḥ | piśaṅga | yacchase | vi'iva | bhrājante | ṛṣyaḥ | upa | sakeṣu | bapsataḥ | ni | su | svapa || 2 ||
he arjuna śveta he sārameya saramā nāma devaśunī |
tasyāḥ kulodbhava he piśaṅga keṣu- cidaṅgeṣu piṅgalavarṇavaṃbhūta he śunaka tvaṃ dataḥ dantān yat yadā yacchase vivṛṇoṣi |
asmān draṣṭumityarthaḥ |
tadānīṃ vīva ājanta ṛṣyaḥ pathādyudhāni viśeṣeṇa bhāsante tathā upa asmatsamīpe bapsataḥ bhakṣayatastva dantāḥ sakeṣu sakāsu bhāsante |
sakāśabda oṣṭhapradeśaviśeṣavācītyarthaḥ4 |
tathābhūtastvamidānīṃ su suṣṭhu ni svapa nitarāṃ nidrāṃ kuru |
stenaṃ rāya sārameya taskaraṃ vā punaḥsara |
stotṛnindrasya rāyasi kimusmānducchunāyase ni ṣu svapa |
3 |
stenam | rāya | sārameya | taskaram | vā | punaḥ'sara | stotṛn | indrasya | rāyasi | kim | asmān | ducchunāyase | ni | su | svapa || 3 ||
he punaḥsara gatameva deśaṃ punaḥ saratīti punaḥsaraḥ |
tādṛgbhūta he sārameya tvaṃ stenam pracchannadhanāpahārī stenaḥ |
taṃ taskaram pratyakṣadhanāpahārī taskaraḥ |
taṃ vā rāya gaccha |
indrasya stotṛn asmān kiṃ rāyasi gacchasi |
asmān ducchunāyase kiṃ bādhase |
ni ṣu svapa atyantaṃ nidrāṃ kuru |
tvaṃ śvakarasya dardi tarva dardu śvakaraḥ |
stotṛnindrasya rāyasi kimusmānducchunāyase ni ṣu svapa |

4. śa-daṃṣṭrapradeśavācī |

he sārameya vtvaṃ vsukarasya varāhasya | dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | vdarhahi vidāraya | vsukaraḥ api vtava vdarvatuṃ vidārayatu | yuvayornityavairtvāt | asmān mā dyotthaḥ | stotṛn ityarthaḥ | pūrvasyā- mucci vyākhyātaḥ ||
sastu mātā sastu pitā sastu śvā sastu viśapatiḥ | sasantu sarve jñātayaḥ sastvayambhito janaḥ | | 5 | | sastu | mātā | sastu | pitā | sastu | śvā | sastu | viśapatiḥ | | sasantu | sarve | jñātayaḥ | sastu | ayam | abhitaḥ | janaḥ | | 5 | |
he sārameya vmātā tvadīyā jananī vsastu svapatu |
vpitā ca vsastu |
vśvā sārameyo bhavān vsastu |
vviśapatiḥ jāmātā bahuā viśaṃ janānāṃ pālako gṛhī vsastu |
vsarve vjñātayaḥ bandhavo'pi vsasantu svapantu |
vabhitaḥ paritaḥ sthitaḥ vayam api sarvaḥ vjanaḥ vsastu svapatu |
ya āste yaśca carati yaśca paśyati no janaḥ | teṣāṃ saṃ hanmo akṣāṇi yathedaṃ hūmyaṃ tathā | | 6 | | yaḥ | āstai | yaḥ | ca | carati | yaḥ | ca | paśyati | naḥ | janaḥ | | teṣām | sam | hanmaḥ | akṣāṇi | yathā | idam | hūmyam | tathā | | 6 | |
vyaḥ janaḥ vāste asmin pradeśe tiṣṭhati vyaśca vcarati gacchati vyaśca vjanaḥ vnaḥ asmān vpaśyati evaṃbhūtānāṃ vteṣāṃ janānām vakṣāṇi indriyāṇi vsaṃ vhanmaḥ saṃhanam |
saṃhatirnimīla- nam |
nimīlyatāmetyarthaḥ |
vidaṃ pratyakṣopalabhyamānaṃ vhūmyaṃ prāsādādisthāvarātmakaṃ vastujātaṃ vyathā niścalaṃ bhavati vtathā ime sarve janā niścalā bhavantirvatyarthaḥ |
sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho yaḥ samudrādācarat | tenā sahasryainā vayaṃ ni janānansvāpayāmāsi | | 7 | | sahasra'śṛṅgaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | yaḥ | samudrāt | ut'ācarat | tena | sahasryaina | vayam | ni | janān | svāpayāmasi | | 7 | |
vsahasraśṛṅgaḥ sahasrakiraṇaḥ vvṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitā vyaḥ sūryaḥ vsamudrāt ambudheḥ sakāśāt vudācarat udāgacchati vsahasryena abhivavinā vtena sūryeṇa vvayaṃ stotāraḥ vjanān sarvān vni vsvāpayāmasi nitarāṃ svāpayāmaḥ |
proṣṭheśayā veśeśayā nārīyāstalpyeśvarīḥ | striyo yāḥ puṇyagandhāstāḥ sarvāḥ svāpayāmasi | | 8 | |
1. kha-gha-ma-tara-3-na-bha-śa- 'vairtvāt |

1. kha-bha-samudrūrṇa; gha-ugraṃ samudrūrṇa |
2. kha-gha-śa-kampayituṃ vegaḥ; jñāta-1-3-4-na-bha-kampayitu-vegaḥ; tara-kampayituvegaḥ; śa-kampayatu vegaḥ |
3. kha-manaśat; jñāta-na-manasān; śa-manasā |
svāyudhāsa iṣmiṇaḥ suniṣkā uta svayaṃ tanvaḥ śubhamānāḥ || 11 || su'āyudhāsaḥ | iṣmiṇaḥ | su'niṣkāḥ | uta | svayam | tanvaḥ | śubhamānāḥ || 11 || svāyudhāsaḥ svāyudhāḥ śobhanāstrāḥ iṣmiṇaḥ gantāraḥ suniṣkāḥ śobhanālaṃkārāḥ uta api ca tanvaḥ svakīyāni śarīrāṇi śubhamānāḥ svayameva śobhayanto maruta evaṃbhūtā bhavantītyarthaḥ || mārute paśau 'śucī vo havyā' iti vapāyā anuvākyā | sūtritaṃ ca—'śucī vo havyā marutaḥ śucīnāṃ nū chiraṃ marutaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
śucī vo havyā marutaḥ śucīnāṃ śuciṃ hinomyadhvaraṃ śucibhyaḥ | ṛtena satyamṛtasāpa āyuñchucijanmānaḥ śurcayaḥ pāvakāḥ || 12 || śucī | vaḥ | havyā | marutaḥ | śucīnām | śucim | hinomi | adhvaram | śuci'bhyaḥ | ṛtena | satyam | ṛta'sāpaḥ | āyan | śuci'janmānaḥ | śurcayaḥ | pāvakāḥ || 12 || he marutaḥ śucīnāṃ śuddhānāṃ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ śucī śucīni havyā havyāni havīṃṣi bhavantu | śucibhyaḥ prakāśamānebhyo yuṣmabhyaṃ śuci śuddham adhvaraṃ yāgaṃ hinomi ahaṃ prerayāmi | ṛtasāpaḥ ṛtamudakaṃ spṛśanto marutaḥ ṛtena satyenaiva satyam āyan gacchanti | kīdṛśāḥ | śucijanmānaḥ śobhanajananāḥ śucayaḥ dīpyamānāḥ pāvakāḥ śodhakāḥ | evaṃbhūtā gacchantītyarthaḥ ||
aṃseṣvā marutaḥ khādayo vo vakṣaḥsu rukmā upaśiśriyāṇāḥ | vi vidyuto na vṛṣṭibhī rucānā anu svadhāmāyudhairyacchamānāḥ || 13 || aṃseṣu | ā | marutaḥ | khādayaḥ | vaḥ | vakṣaḥsu | rukmāḥ | upa'śiśriyāṇāḥ | vi | tri'dhutaḥ | na | vṛṣṭi'bhiḥ | rucānāḥ | anu | svadhām | āyudhaiḥ | yacchamānāḥ || 13 || he marutaḥ aṃseṣu yuṣmadīyeṣu skandhapradeśeṣu khādayaḥ alaṃkāraviśeṣāḥ ā muktā bhavantīti śeṣaḥ | rukmāḥ suṣṭhu rocamānā hārāḥ vaḥ yuṣmadīyaṃ vakṣa uraḥpradeśam upaśiśriyāṇāḥ āśritā bhavanti | kiṃca he marutaḥ vṛṣṭibhiḥ varṣaiḥ sārdhaṃ vidyuto na taḍito yathā rucānāḥ rocamānāḥ tādṛbhūtā yūyam āyudhaiḥ meghatāḍanaiḥ svadhām udakam anu yacchamānāḥ prayacchantaḥ vi carayeti śeṣaḥ ||
gṛhamedhīye 'pra budhyā vaḥ' iti pradhānasya yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'pra budhyā va irte mahāṃsīti puṣṭimantau virājau saṃyājye' (āśva. śrau. 2. 18) iti ||
pra budhyā va irte mahāṃsī pra nāmāni prayajyavastiradhvam | sahasrayaṃ damyaṃ bhāgametaṃ gṛhamedhīyaṃ maruto juṣadhvam || 14 || pra | budhyā | vaḥ | irte | mahāṃsī | pra | nāmāni | pra'yajyavaḥ | tiradhvam | sahasriyam | damyam | bhāgam | etam | gṛha'medhīyam | marutaḥ | juṣadhvam || 14 || he marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmadīyāni budhyā budhyāntyantarikṣe bhavāni mahāṃsī tejāṃsi pra irte prakarṣeṇa gacchanti | kiṃca he prayajyavaḥ prakarṣeṇa yaṣṭavyā marutaḥ yūyaṃ nāmāni pāṃsūna
namayantīti nāmānyūdakāni |
ᵥpra ᵥtiraścaṃ vardhayata |
he ᵥmarutaḥ yūyaṃ ᵥsahasriyaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyākaṃ ᵥdabhyaṃ dame gṛhe bhavaṃ ᵥgṛhamedhīyaṃ gṛhamedhiguṇebhyo yuṣmabhyaṃ deyam ᵥetam etādṛśaṃ ᵥbhāgaṃ ᵥjuṣadhvaṃ sevadhvam |
ekaṃ bhāgaṃ sahasriyamiti kathamāha |
tathā ca śrūyate—'yāvadekā devatā kāmayate yāvadekā tāvadāhutiḥ prathate na hi tadasti yattāvadeva svāyāvajjuhoti' (tai. brā. 3. 2. 6. 4) iti ||
yadi̭ stu॒tasya̭ ma॒ruto॒ adha॒īthetthā vi॒prasya̭ vā॒jino̭ ha॒vīma̭n |
ma॒kṣu rā॒yaḥ su॒vīrya̭sya dā॒ta nū ci॒dyama॒nya ā̭da॒bhadrā̭vā ||
15 ||
yadi̭ | stu॒tasya̭ | ma॒ruta̭ḥ | adhi̭'itha | i॒tthā | vi॒prasya̭ | vā॒jina̭ḥ | ha॒vīma̭n | ma॒kṣu | rā॒yaḥ | su॒vīrya̭sya | dā॒ta | nu | ci॒t | yam | a॒nyaḥ | ā॒da॒bhat | a॒rā̭vā || 15 ||
ᵥvājinaḥ havilakṣaṇānnavataḥ ᵥviprasya medhāvino mama saṃbandhini ᵥhavīman haviṣmati haviṣā yukte stotre kriyamāṇe sati he ᵥmarutaḥ yūyaṃ ᵥyadi yasmāt kāraṇāt ᵥstutasya stutaṃ madīyaṃ stotram ᵥithā itthamanena prakāreṇa ᵥadhītha avagacchatha tasmāt kāraṇāt he marutaḥ yūyaṃ ᵥsuvīryasya śobhanaputropetasya ᵥrāyaḥ rāyaṃ dhanam |
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
ᵥmakṣu śīghraṃ ᵥdāta dattaḥ |
ᵥarāvā arātiḥ śatrubhūtaḥ ᵥanyaḥ janaḥ ᵥyaṃ rāyaṃ ᵥnū cit ᵥādabhat naivābhihanyāt |
taddana- masmabhyaṃ dateti saṃbandhaḥ ||
24 ||
'marudbhyaḥ krīḻibhyaḥ sasakapālam' (tai. saṃ. 1. 8. 4. 2) ityasya 'atyāsaḥ' iti yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'atyāso na ye marutaḥ svaśvo juṣṭo damūnāḥ' (āśva. śrau. 2. 18) iti ||
atyā̭so॒ na ye ma॒ruta॒ḥ svaśvo̭ ya॒kṣadṛ̭śo॒ na śu॒bhaya̭nta॒ maryā̭ḥ |
te ha॒myḙṣṭhāḥ śi॒śavo॒ na śu॒bhrā va॒tsāso॒ na pra̭krī॒ḻina̭ḥ pa॒yodhā̭ḥ ||
16 ||
atyā̭saḥ | na | ye | ma॒ruta̭ḥ | su॒'aśva̭ḥ | ya॒kṣa'dṛ̭śaḥ | na | śu॒bhaya̭nta | maryā̭ḥ | te | ha॒myḙ'sthāḥ | śi॒śava̭ḥ | na | śu॒bhrāḥ | va॒tsāsa̭ḥ | na | pra̭'krī॒ḻina̭ḥ | pa॒yaḥ'dhā̭ḥ || 16 ||
ᵥatyāso ᵥna atyāḥ satatagāmino'śvā iva ᵥsvaśvaḥ suṣṭhu gacchantaḥ ᵥye ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥyakṣa- dṛśo ᵥna ᵥmaryāḥ yajñasyotsasya draṣṭāro manuṣyā iva ᵥśubhayanta śobhante ᵥharmyeṣṭhāḥ harmye sthitāḥ ᵥśiśavo ᵥna kumārā iva ᵥśubhrāḥ śobhamānāḥ ᵥvatsāso ᵥna vatsā iva ᵥprakrīḻinaḥ prakarṣeṇa krīḻamānāḥ ᵥte marutaḥ ᵥpayodhāḥ payasa udakasya dhārayitāro dātāro vā bhavanti ||
da॒śya॒nto॒ no॒ marṷto mṛḻantu va॒riva̭syanto roda॒sī su॒mekḙ |
ā॒re go॒hā nṛ॒hā va॒dho vo̭ astu su॒mebhi̭ra॒sme va̭sa॒vo na̭ma॒dhvam ||
17 ||
da॒śya॒ntaḥ | naḥ | marṷtaḥ | mṛ॒ḻantu | va॒riva̭syantaḥ | roda॒sī iti̭ | su॒mekḙ iti su'meke | ā॒re | go॒hā | nṛ॒hā | va॒dhaḥ | vaḥ | a॒stu | su॒mebhi̭ḥ | a॒sme iti̭ | va॒sava̭ḥ | na॒ma॒dhvam || 17 ||
ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥnaḥ asmabhyaṃ ᵥdaśyantaḥ dhanāni prayacchantaḥ ᵥsumeke surūpe ᵥrodasī dyāvā- pṛthivyau ᵥvarivasyantaḥ svamahimnā pūrayantaḥ ᵥmṛḻantu mṛḻayantu |
asmān sukhayuktān kurvantu |
1. jha-ta-prakrīḻinaḥ: prakrīḻinaḥ |
api ca he vasavaḥ vāsakā marutaḥ gohā gavāṃ meghasthānāmudakānāṃ bhedakaḥ nṛhā nṛṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ hantā vaḥ yuṣmadīyaṃ vadhaḥ āyudham āre astu asmatto dūre bhavatu |
yūyamapi sujñebhiḥ sujñaiḥ sukhaiḥ saha asme asmāsu namadhvaṃ svayamevābhimukhā bhavata ||
mārute paśau 'ā vo hotā' ityeṣānuvākyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—'ā vo hotā johavīti sattaḥ pra citramarkaṃ gṛṇate turāya' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
ā vo hotā johavīti sattaḥ satrācīṃ rātiṃ maruto gṛṇānaḥ |
ya īrvato vṛṣaṇo asti gopāḥ so advayāvī havate va ukthaiḥ ||
18 ||
ā | vaḥ | hotā | johavīti | sattaḥ | satrācīm | rātim | marutaḥ | gṛṇānaḥ | yaḥ | īrvataḥ | vṛṣaṇaḥ | asti | gopāḥ | maḥ | advayāvī | havate | vaḥ | ukthaiḥ || 18 ||
he marutaḥ sattaḥ hotṛṣadane nipaṇṇo'smadīyaḥ hotā satrācīṃ sarvato gamanaśīlaṃ rātiṃ tvadīyaṃ dānaṃ gṛṇānaḥ stuvan vaḥ yuṣmān ā johavīti bhṛśamāhvayati |
he vṛṣaṇaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitāro marutaḥ yaḥ hotā īrvataḥ gacchataḥ vyāpāravato yajamānasya gopāḥ asti yuṣma- dvāhananimittena rakṣako bhavati maḥ hotā advayāvī māyārahitaḥ san vaḥ yuṣmān ukthaiḥ stotraiḥ havate stauti ||
ime turaṃ maruto ramayantīme sahaḥ sahasa ā namanti |
ime śaṃsaṃ vanuṣyato ni pānti guru dveṣo aruruṣe dadhanti ||
19 ||
ime | turam | marutaḥ | ramayanti | ime | sahaḥ | sahasaḥ | ā | namanti | ime | śaṃsam | vanuṣyataḥ | ni | pānti | guru | dveṣaḥ | aruruṣe | dadhanti || 19 ||
ime īdṛśāḥ marutaḥ turaṃ karmasu kṣipravantaṃ yajamānaṃ ramayanti krīḍayanti |
ime marutaḥ sahaḥ sahasā balena sahasaḥ balavato janān ā namanti |
ime marutaḥ vanuṣyataḥ hiṃsakāt puruṣāt śaṃsaṃ śaṃsakaṃ stotāraṃ ni pānti nitarāṃ pālayanti |
aruruṣe havirprayacchate janāya guru mahat dveṣaḥ apriyaṃ dadhanti kurvanti ||
ime radhraṃ cinmaruto junanti bhṛmiṃ cidyathā vasavo juṣanta |
arpa bādhadhvaṃ vṛṣaṇastamāṃsi dhatta viśvaṃ tanayaṃ tokamusme ||
20 ||
ime | radhram | cit | marutaḥ | junanti | bhṛmim | cit | yathā | vasavaḥ | juṣanta | arpa | bādhadhvam | vṛṣaṇaḥ | tamāṃsi | dhatta | viśvam | tanayam | tokam | asme iti || 20 ||
ime marutaḥ radhraṃ cit samṛddhamapi janaṃ junanti prerayanti |
bhṛmiṃ cit bhramaṇaśīlamapi daridraṃ junanti prerayanti |
vasavaḥ vāsakā devā yuṣmān yathā juṣanta kāmayeran he vṛṣaṇaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitāraḥ te yūyaṃ tamāṃsi apa bādhadhvaṃ nāśayata |
api ca asme asmabhyaṃ viśvaṃ bahulaṃ tokaṃ putraṃ tanayaṃ pautraṃ ca dhatta prayacchata ||
1. kha-gamanaśīlā; bha-śa-gamanaśīlāṃ |
ka 3-26
mā vo dātrānmāruto nirrāma mā paścāddhama radhyo vibhāge |
ā naḥ spārhe bhajatanā vasavye' yadīṃ sujātaṃ vṛṣaṇo vo asti ||
21 ||
mā | vaḥ | dātrāt | marutaḥ | niḥ | arāma | mā | paścāt | dadhma | radhyaḥ | vi'bhāge | ā | naḥ | spārhe | bhajata | vasavye | yat | īm | su'jātam | vṛṣaṇaḥ | vaḥ | asti ||21||
he marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ dātrāt dānāt mā nirrāma vayaṃ mā nirgamāma |
yūyamasmān parityaktvā'nyebhyo dhanaṃ mā dattetyarthaḥ |
he radhyaḥ rathavanto marutaḥ vibhāge yuṣmadīyasya dhanasya dāne paścāt mā dadhma |
dadhyatirgatikarmā |
vayaṃ paścādbhāgino mā bhūma |
yūyaṃ prathamamasmabhyameva datteti yāvat |
spārhe spṛhaṇīye vasavye dhanasamūhe yūyaṃ naḥ asmān ā bhajata bhāginaḥ kuruta |
he vṛṣaṇaḥ varṣitāro marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ sujātaṃ śobhanajanaṃ yat vasavyam asti tasmin bhāginaḥ kuruteti saṃbandhaḥ |
īm iti pūraṇaḥ ||
saṃ yaddhananta manyubhirjanāsaḥ śūrā yadvīṣvoṣadhīṣu viśva |
ardhe smā no maruto rudriyāsastrātāro bhūta pṛtanāsvaryaḥ ||
22 ||
sam | yat | hananta | manyu'bhiḥ | janāsaḥ | śūrāḥ | yat | īṣu | oṣadhīṣu | viśva | ardha | sma | naḥ | marutaḥ | rudriyāsaḥ | trātāraḥ | bhūta | pṛtanāsu | aryaḥ || 22 ||
yat yadā śūrāḥ vikrāntāḥ janāsaḥ janāḥ yat īṣu mahatīṣu oṣadhīṣu viśva prajāsu ca jetavyāsu manyubhiḥ kopairabhimānairvā saṃ hanantu saṃgacchante atha tadānīṃ he rudriyāsaḥ rudraputrā he marutaḥ yūyaṃ pṛtanāsu yuddheṣu aryaḥ areḥ śatroḥ sakāśāt naḥ asmākaṃ trātāraḥ bhūta rakṣakā bhavata ||
bhūri cakra marutaḥ pitryāṇyukathāni yā vaḥ śasyantai purā cit |
marudbhirugraḥ pṛtanāsu sāḻahā marudbhiritsanitā vājamarvā ||
23 ||
bhūri | cakra | marutaḥ | pitryāṇi | ukthāni | yā | vaḥ | śasyantai | purā | cit | marut'bhiḥ | ugraḥ | pṛtanāsu | sāḻahā | marut'bhiḥ | it | sanitā | vājam | arvā || 23 ||
he marutaḥ yūyaṃ bhūri bhūrīṇi bahūni pitryāṇi asmatpitṛsambandhīni dhanadānādīni karmāṇi cakra kṛtavanto bhavata |
purā cit pūrvakāle'pi vaḥ yuṣmākam ukthāni praśasyāni yāni karmāṇi praśasyante prakhyāyante tāni cakreti saṃbandhaḥ |
ugraḥ ojasvī pṛtanāsu yuddheṣu marudbhiḥ yuṣmābhirhetubhiḥ sāḻahā śatrūṇāmabhibhavitā bhavati |
marudbhirit marudbhiruṣmābhireva hetubhiḥ arvā stotrairabhigantā vājam ahaṃ sanitā sambhaktā bhavati |
yadvā arvātho vājaṃ yuddhaṃ sanitā bhavati ||
asme vīro marutaḥ śuṣmyastu janānāṃ yo asṷro vidhartā |
apo yenaṃ sukṣitaye taremātha khamoko abhi vaḥ syāma ||

asme iti | vīraḥ | marutaḥ | śuṣmī | astu | janānām | yaḥ | asuraḥ | vi'dhartā | apaḥ | yena | su'kṣitayai | tarema | ardha | svam | orkaḥ | abhi | vaḥ | syāma || 24 ||
he marutaḥ asme asmākaṃ vīraḥ putraḥ śuṣmī astu balavān bhavatu |
asuraḥ prajñāvān yaḥ janānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ vidhartā vidhārakaḥ |
yena putreṇa vayaṃ sukṣitaye suhu nivāsāya apaḥ āpnuvataḥ śatrūn tarema hiṃsema |
sa putro balavānastviti pūrveṇānvayaḥ |
adha api ca vaḥ yuṣmadīyā vayaṃ svamokaḥ ātmīyaṃ sthānam abhi syāma ātiṣṭhema |
tanna indro varuṇo mitro agnirāpa oṣadhīrvininau juṣanta |
śarmansyāma marutāmupasthe yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ |
25 |
tat | naḥ | indraḥ | varuṇaḥ | mitraḥ | agniḥ | āpaḥ | oṣadhīḥ | vininaiḥ | juṣanta | śarman | syāma | marutām | upasthe | yūyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 25 ||
pūrvaṃ vyākhyāteyam | akṣarārthastu | indrādayo devā asmadīyaṃ stotraṃ sevantām | marutāmupasthāne vartamānā vayaṃ sukhe syāma | he marutaḥ yūyaṃ sarvadāsmānavināśaiḥ pālayata || 26 ||
‘ madhvo vaḥ ’ iti saptarcaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ maruddevatākam |
‘ madhvaḥ sata ’ ityanukrāntam |
daśarātre ṣaṣṭhe'hanyābhimāruta idaṃ mārutanividvānam |
sūtritaṃ ca-‘ madhvo vo nāma sa pratnathetyābhimārutam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 8 ) iti |
madhvo vo nāma mārutaṃ yajatrāḥ pra yajñeṣu śavasā madanti |
ye rejayanti rodasī cidurvī pinvantyutsaṃ yadayāsurāḥ |
1 |
madhvaḥ | vaḥ | nāma | mārutam | yajatrāḥ | pra | yajñeṣu | śavasā | madanti | ye | rejayanti | rodasī iti | cit | urvī iti | pinvanti | utsam | yat | aryāsaḥ | ugrāḥ || 1 ||
he yajatrāḥ yajanīyā marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmadīyaṃ mārutaṃ marutsambandha nāma nāmadheyaṃ madhvaḥ madhvo mādayitāraḥ stotāraḥ yajñeṣu yāgeṣu śavasā balena pra madanti prakarṣeṇa stuvanti |
uccaiḥ stuvantītyarthaḥ |
ye marutaḥ urvī vistīrṇe rodasī cit dyāvāpṛthivyāvapi rejayanti kampayanti kiṃca utsaṃ meghaṃ pinvanti varṣayanti ugrāḥ utpūrṇāḥ yat ye marutaḥ ayāsaḥ yānti sarvatra gacchanti |
teṣāṃ marutāṃ yuṣmākaṃ nāma pra madantīti pūrveṇānvayaḥ |
nivetāro hi marutau gṛṇantaṃ praṇetāro yajamānasya manma |
asmākamadya vidatheṣu barhirā vītayai sadata pipriyāṇāḥ |
2 |
ni'cetāraḥ | hi | marutaḥ | gṛṇantam | pra'netāraḥ | yajamānasya | manma | asmākam | adya | vidatheṣu | barhiḥ | ā | vītayai | sadata | pipriyāṇāḥ || 2 ||
ye marutaḥ gṛṇantaṃ stuvantaṃ janaṃ nivetāro hi mṛgayamāṇā bhavanti khalu api ca yajamānasya manma abhimataṃ kāmaṃ praṇetāraḥ ca bhavanti |
aparo'rdharcaḥ pratyakṣakṛtaḥ |
he marutaḥ
1. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-prajāvān |
2. kha-udbalāḥ |
yūyaṃ ᵛpiyriāṇāḥ prīyamāṇāḥ santaḥ ᵛadya asmin divase ᵛasmākaṃ ᵛvidayeṣu yaśeṣu ᵛvītaye somasya bhakṣaṇāya ᵛbarhiḥ¹ barhiṣi kuśamaye ᵛā ᵛsadata āsīdata upaviśata ||
naitāvadnye maruto yatheme ārjante rukmairāyudhaistanūbhiḥ |
ā rodasī viśvapiśaḥ piśānāḥ saṃmānamañjryañjate śubhe kam ||
3 ||
na | etāvat | anye | marutaḥ | yathā | ime | ārjante | rukmaiḥ | āyudhaiḥ | tanūbhiḥ | ā | rodasī iti | viśva'piśaḥ | piśānāḥ | samānam | añji | añjate | śubhe | kam ||3||
ᵛime īdṛśāḥ ᵛmarutaḥ ᵛyathā yatparimāṇaṃ dhanādikaṃ dadati ᵛanye marudbhyatiriktā devāḥ ᵛetāvat dhanādikaṃ ᵛna dadhurityarthaḥ |
te ca ᵛrukmaiḥ rocamānairābharaṇaiḥ ᵛāyudhaiḥ svakīyāstraiḥ ᵛtanūbhiḥ ātmīyaiḥ kevalarajjaiśca ᵛājante sarvadā bhāsante |
kaścidekavākyatāmāha |
yatheme maruto rukmādi- bhirrājante naitāvadetadavyatiriktā ājanta iti |
api ca ᵛrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵛpiśānāḥ prakāśayantaḥ ᵛviśvapiśaḥ vyāsadīsaya evaṃbhūtāḥ marutaḥ ᵛśubhe śobhāyai ᵛsamānaṃ sadṛśarūpam ᵛañji ābharaṇam ᵛā ᵛañjate svakīyāvayaveṣvabhivyaktīkurvanti |
ᵛkam iti pūraṇaḥ ||
ṛdhaksā vo maruto dyudusta yad ārgaḥ purupatā karāma |
mā vṛtasyāmapi bhūmā yajatrā asme vo astu sumatiścaniṣṭhā ||
4 ||
ṛdhak | sā | vaḥ | marutaḥ | dyudut | astu | yat | vaḥ | ārgaḥ | purupatā | karāma | mā | vṛtasyām | api | bhūma | yajatrāḥ | asme iti | vaḥ | astu | su'matiḥ | caniṣṭhā ||4||
he ᵛmarutaḥ ᵛvaḥ yuṣmadīyā ᵛsā prasiddhā ᵛdyudut hetiḥ ᵛṛdhak ᵛastu asmattaḥ pṛthagbhavatu |
ᵛyat yadyapi vayaṃ ᵛvaḥ yuṣmākam ᵛāgaḥ aparādhaṃ ᵛpurupatā purupatayā manuṣyatvena ᵛkarāma karavāma |
manuṣyāṇāṃ hi pramādaḥ sulabha ityarthaḥ |
he ᵛyajatrāḥ yajanīyā marutaḥ ᵛvaḥ yuṣmadīyāyāṃ ᵛtasyāṃ dyudyuti ᵛapi ᵛīṣadapi ᵛmā ᵛbhrama |
ᵛcaniṣṭhā añavattamā |
atiśayenānnapradetyarthaḥ |
ᵛvaḥ yuṣmadīyā ᵛsumatiḥ anugrahabuddhiḥ ᵛasme asmāsu ᵛastu ||
kṛte cidatra marutau raṇantānavadyāsaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
pra ṇau'vata sumatibhiryajatrāḥ pra vājaibhistarata puṣyasai naḥ ||
5 ||
kṛte | cit | atra | marutaḥ | raṇantu | anavadyāsaḥ | śucayaḥ | pāvakāḥ | pra | naḥ | avata | sumati'bhiḥ | yajatrāḥ | pra | vājaibhiḥ | tirata | puṣyasai | naḥ ||5||
ᵛatra asmin ᵛkṛte ᵛcit asmadīye yajñakarmaṇyeva ᵛmaruto ᵛraṇanta ramantām |
kīdṛśā marutaḥ |
ᵛanavadyāsaḥ aninditāḥ ᵛśucayaḥ dīptiyuktāḥ ᵛpāvakāḥ śodhakā evaṃbhūtā iti |
kiṃca he marutaḥ ᵛyajatrāḥ yajanīyā yūyaṃ ᵛnaḥ asmān ᵛsumatibhiḥ anugrahabuddhibhiḥ suhṛdbhirhetubhirvā ᵛpra ᵛavata prakarṣeṇa pālayata |
tathā ᵛnaḥ asmān ᵛvājebhiḥ vājairannaiḥ ᵛpuṣyasai poṣāya ᵛpra ᵛtirata pravadharyata ||
1. kha-kṣa-ta-na-ma-śa- ‘barhiḥ’ nāsti |
2. kha-śa-śobhanāyai |
3. ta- ‘api’ nāsti |
