uta stutāsau maruto vyantu viśvebhirnāmabhirnaro havīm̐ṣi || dadāta no amṛtasya prajāyai jigata rāyaḥ sūnṛtā maghāni || 6 || uta | stutāsaḥ | marutaḥ | vyantu | viśvebhiḥ | nāmabhiḥ | naraḥ | havīm̐ṣi | dadāta | naḥ | amṛtasya | pra'jāyai | jigata | rāyaḥ | sūnṛtā | maghāni || 6 || ᵥuta api ca ᵥstutāsaḥ evamasmābhiḥ stutāḥ ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥhavīm̐ṣi ᵥvyantu bhakṣayantu | kīdṛśāḥ | ᵥviśvebhiḥ viśvaiḥ ᵥnāmabhiḥ udakaiḥ sahitāḥ ᵥnaraḥ netāraḥ | śeṣaḥ pratyakṣastutaḥ | he marutaḥ ᵥnaḥ asmadīyāyai ᵥprajāyai saṃtataye ᵥamṛtasya amṛtamudakaṃ ᵥdadāta datta | tathā haviṣo dāturyajamānasya ᵥsūnṛtā sūnṛtāni suṣṭhu nṛtyānti śobhanayogyāni ᵥmaghāni dhanāni ᵥjigata udgirata prayacchatetyarthaḥ ||
ā stutāsau maruto viśva ūtī acchā sūrīnsarvatātā jigāta | ye nastamanā śatino vardhayanti yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 7 || ā | stutāsaḥ | marutaḥ | viśvaiḥ | ūtī | acchā | sūrīn | sarvatātā | jigāta | ye | naḥ | tmanā | śatinaḥ | vardhayanti | yūyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 7 || he ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥstutāsaḥ evamasmābhiḥ stutāḥ ᵥviśve sarve yūyam ᵥūtī ūtyā rakṣayā sahitāḥ ᵥsūrīn stotṝn ᵥacchā abhilakṣya ᵥsarvatātā sarvatātā yajñe ᵥā ᵥjigāta āgacchata | ᵥye marutaḥ ᵥtmanā ātmanaiva ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥśatinaḥ śatasaṃkhyākān ᵥvardhayanti | yathā vayaṃ putrapautrādibhiḥ śatino bhavema tathā vardhayantītyarthaḥ | te yūyamā jigāteti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ | evaṃbhūtā marutaḥ yūyaṃ sarvadā asmān pālayata || || 7 ||
‘pra sākumukṣe’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ maruddevatākam |
anukrāntaṃ ca—‘pra sākumukṣe ṣaṭ ’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
pra sākumukṣe arcatā gaṇāya yo daivyasya dhāmnāstuviṣmān | uta kṣodanti rodasī mahitvā nakṣante nākaṃ niḥṛteravaṃśāt || 1 || pra | sākam'ukṣai | arcata | gaṇāya | yaḥ | daivyasya | dhāmnaḥ | tuviṣmān | uta | kṣodanti | rodasī iti | mahitvā | nakṣante | nākam | niḥ'ṛteḥ | avaṃśāt || 1 || he stotāro yūyaṃ ᵥsākamukṣe satataṃ varṣitre ᵥgaṇāya marutsamūhāya ᵥpra ᵥarcata stotraṃ proccārayata | ᵥyaḥ marudgaṇaḥ ᵥdaivyasya devasambandhinaḥ ᵥdhāmnaḥ svargasya sthānasya ᵥtuviṣmān vṛddhimān bhavati | sarvebhyo devebhyaḥ pravṛddha ityarthaḥ | tasmai ᵥgaṇāyeti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ | ᵥuta api ca marutaḥ ᵥmahitvā svakīyena mahattvena sahitāḥ ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥkṣodanti bhajanti | tathā ᵥniṛteḥ bhūmeḥ ᵥavaṃśāt antarikṣācca ᵥnākaṃ svargaṃ ᵥnakṣante vyāpnuvanti ||
1. gha-pratyakṣaḥ; jha-pratyakṣakṛtaḥ; jha-pratyakṣastutiḥ; bha- pratyakṣaḥ stutaḥ |
2. ta-śobhanayogāni |
3. ta-asmai |
janūṣdhidro marutastveṣyeṇa bhīmāsastuvimanyuva'yāsa: | pra ye mahobhirojasota santi viśvo vo yārmanbhayate svardak || 2 || janū: | cit | va: | maruta: | tveṣyeṇa | bhīmāsa: | tuvi'manyava: | ayāsa: | pra | ye | maha:'bhi: | ojasā | uta | santi | viśve | va: | yārman | bhayate | sva:'dṛk ||2|| he bhīmāsa: bhīmā: tuvimanyava: pravṛddhamataya: ayāsa: gantāra iti trīṇi saṃbodhanāni | evaṃbhūtā he maruta: va: yuṣmākaṃ janū: janma tveṣyeṇa dīptena rudreṇa babhūveti śeṣa: | uta api ca ye maruta: mahobhi: tejobhi: ojasā balena ca pra santi prabhavanti teṣāṃ va: yuṣmākaṃ yāmanū yāmani gamane viśva: svardak sūryasya draṣṭā sarvo jīvasamūha: | yadvā | svarantarikṣam | tat paśyatīti vṛkṣa: svardak | sarvadoṣicchidyartha: | bhayate

mīḻahuṣaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣituḥ rudrasya īśvarasya putrān tān marutaḥ ā vivāse ahaṃ hotā paricarāmi |
te marutaḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ kucit bahukṛtvaḥ punaḥ bhūyaḥ naṃsante namantām |
abhimukhībhavantu |
kuvicchabdenaiva punaḥśabdārthasya labdhatvāt punaḥśabdagrahaṇaṃ kimartham |
ādarārtham |
svastā antarhitenāprakāśena yat yena enasā jihīḻire marutaḥ kuṣyeyuḥ āviḥ prakāśena yat yenasā ca jihīḻire turāṇāṃ kṣiprāṇāṃ marutāṃ sambandhi tat ubhayam enaḥ aparādham ava īmahe stotreṇa vayamapanayāmaḥ ||
pra sā vāci suṣṭurmidhonāmidaṃ sūktaṃ marutau juṣanta |

pra | sā | vāci | su'stutiḥ | maghonām | idam | su'uktam | marutaḥ | juṣanta | ārāt | cit | dveṣaḥ | vṛṣaṇaḥ | yuyota | yūyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā |
mahanti ᵥhṛṣaḥ havirlakṣaṇānyajñāni ᵥvarāya nivāraṇāya' yūyaṃ nānyatra gacchata asmadīyānyeva havīṃṣi svīkuruteti nirodhanaṃ kartuṃ ᵥvi ᵥdāśati viśeṣeṇa dadāti|
ata eva sa svakīyaṃ nivāsaṃ vardhayatītyarthaḥ ||
nahi va̭śvaraṃ ca॒na va̭siṣṭhaḥ pari̭maṃsate |
asmā̭ka॒madya ma̭rutaḥ su॒te sacā॒ viśvḙ pibata kāmina̭ḥ ||
3 ||
nahi | vaḥ | ca॒ra॒mam | ca॒na | va̭siṣṭhaḥ | pa॒ri̭'maṃsate | asmā̭kam | adya | ma̭rutaḥ | su॒te | sacā̭ | viśvḙ | pi॒ba॒ta | kā॒mi॒na̭ḥ || 3 ||
ᵥvasiṣṭhaḥ ṛṣiḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ madhye ᵥcaramaṃ ᵥcana avaramapi ᵥnahi ᵥparimaṃsate varjayitvā na stauti|
kiṃtu sarvānaiva yuṣmān stautītyarthaḥ |
ᵥadya asmin dine ᵥasmākam asmadīye ᵥsute some'bhipute sati he ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥkāminaḥ somaṃ kāmayamānāḥ ᵥviśve yūyaṃ ᵥsacā saṃgatya ᵥpibata pānaṃ kuruta ||
nahi va̭ ū॒tīḥ pṛ̭ta॒nāsṷ ma̭rdhati yasmā̭ arā̭dhvaṃ na̭raḥ |
abhi̭ va॒ āva̭rtu॒su॒ma̭ti॒rnavī̭yasī tū॒yaṃ yā̭ta pi॒pīṣa̭vaḥ ||
4 ||
nahi | vaḥ | ū॒tīḥ | pṛ̭ta॒nāsṷ | ma̭rdhati | yasmai̭ | arā̭dhvam | na̭raḥ | abhi̭ | vaḥ | ā | a॒va॒rtu | su॒'ma̭tiḥ | na॒vī̭yasī | tū॒yam | yā॒ta | pi॒pīṣa̭vaḥ || 4 ||
he marutaḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmadīyā ᵥūtiḥ rakṣā ᵥpṛtanāsu yuddheṣu ᵥnahi ᵥmardhati na khalu hinasti |
śatrubhiḥ kṛtāṃ hiṃsāṃ na sahata ityarthaḥ |
he ᵥnaraḥ netāraḥ yūyaṃ ᵥyasmai janāya ᵥarādhvaṃ kāmānadadhvaṃ taṃ janaṃ na hinastīti sambandhaḥ |
ᵥnavīyasī navatarā ᵥvaḥ yuṣmadīyā ᵥsumatiḥ anugrahabuddhiḥ ᵥabhi ᵥāvartu asmāndhyāvartatām |
tathā he ᵥpipīṣavaḥ somapānakāmāḥ yūyamapi ᵥtūyaṃ kṣipraṃ ᵥyāta āgacchata ||

he ᵥghṛṣvārādhasaḥ parasparaghṛṣṭāni susaṃhatāni² rādhāṃsi yeṣāṃ te he marutaḥ yūyam ᵥandhāṃsi somalakṣaṇāni havīṃṣi ᵥpītaye bhakṣaṇārthaṃ ᵥsu suṣṭhu ᵥo ᵥyātana āyāta |
ᵥhi yasmāt kāraṇāt he ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmabhyam ᵥimā imāni ᵥhavyā havyāni havīṃṣi ᵥrare ahaṃ dadāmi ataḥ kāraṇāt yūyam ᵥanyatra ᵥmo ᵥṣu gantana mā gacchata ||

he mitrāvaruṇau ^vāṃ yuvayoḥ ^yajñaṃ ^namobhiḥ namaskāraiḥ stutibhiḥ ^samu ^mahayaṃ saṃpūja- yāmyaham |
tadartha he ^mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau ^vāṃ ^sabādhaḥ bādhāyukto'haṃ ^huve āhvayāmi bādhāparihārāya |
^vāṃ yuvām ^ṛcase sevituṃ ^narvāni nūtanāni stutyāni vā ^manmāni stotrāṇi ^pra bhavantvityadhyāhāraḥ |
^kṛtāni mayā samūhīkṛtāni ^imāni idānīṃ kriyamāṇāni ^brahma parivṛḍhāni stotrāṇi yuvāṃ ^jujuṣan prīṇayantu ||
iyaṃ deva purohitiryuvabhyāṃ yajñeṣu mitrāvaruṇāvakāri |
viśvāni durgā pipṛtaṃ tiro no yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
7 ||
iyam | devā | puraḥ | hitiḥ | yuva'bhyām | yajñeṣu | mitrāvaruṇau | akāri | viśvāni | duḥ'gā | pipṛt | tiraḥ | naḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||7||
‘iyaṃ deva’ iti saptamī gatā ||
3 ||
‘utsūryaḥ’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ saptamaṃ sūktam |
atrānukramaṇikā—‘utsūryaḥ ṣaḻādyāstisraḥ sauryeḥ’ iti |
vasiṣṭha ṛṣiḥ |
triṣṭup chandaḥ |
ādyāstisraḥ sūryadevatyāḥ śiṣṭā mitrāvaruṇadevatyāḥ |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-yajñārthamatimān |
ut sūryo bṛhadarciṣyaśreturu viśvā janim mānuṣāṇām | sumo divā dadṛśe rocamānaḥ kratvā kṛtaḥ sukṛtaḥ kartṛbhirbhūt || 1 || ut | sūryaḥ | bṛhat | arcīṃṣi | aśret | puru | viśvā | janim | mānuṣāṇām | sum | divā | dadṛśe | rocamānaḥ | kratvā | kṛtaḥ | su'kṛtaḥ | kartṛbhiḥ | bhut || 1 || vsūryaḥ sarvasya prerako devaḥ vbṛhat atyadhikaṃ vpuru puruṇi bahūni varcīṃṣi tejāṃsi vut vaśret ūrdhvaṃ śrayati | kiṃ prati | vmānuṣāṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ vviśvā sarvāṇi vjanima janimāni janān 1 | janaśabdaḥ saṃghavacanaḥ | tān pratyudaśret | sa devaḥ vdivā ahani vrocamānaḥ san vsamaḥ vdadṛśe | ekarūpaḥ pratiniyataḥ san dṛśyate | 'tasmātpuruṣaṃ puruṣaṃ pratyādityo bhavati' (ai. ā. 3.2.3) iti hi śrutiḥ | sa devaḥ vkratvā sarvasya kartā vkṛtaḥ saṃpāditaḥ prajāpatinā vkartṛbhi
dyāvābhūmī aditeṃ trāsīthāṃ no ye vāṃ jajñuḥ sujanimāna ṛṣve | mā helai bhūm varuṇasya vāyormā mitrasya priyatamasya nṛṇām || 4 || dyāvābhūmī iti | aditeṃ | trāsīthām | naḥ | ye | vām | jajñuḥ | su'janimānaḥ | ṛṣve iti | mā | helai | bhūm | varuṇasya | vāyoḥ | mā | mitrasya | priya'tamasya | nṛṇām || 4 || he ॰dyāvābhūmī dyāvāpṛthivyau he ॰adite akhaṇḍanīye | etat dyāvābhūmyorevaikavacanena saṃbo- dhanam | he ॰ṛṣve | mahanmāmetat | he ॰mahatyau ॰naḥ | asmān ॰trāsīthāṃ rakṣatam | ॰ye vayaṃ ॰sujanimānaḥ śobhanajanmānaḥ ॰vāṃ yuvāṃ ॰jajñuḥ jñātavantaḥ sma | kiṃca vayaṃ ॰varuṇasya ॰hele krodhe ॰mā ॰bhūma | tathā ॰vāyoḥ mā bhūma | tathā ॰nṛṇāṃ stutinetṝṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ॰priyatamasya ॰mitrasya hele ॰mā bhūma || ‘pra bāhavā’ iti pañcamī maitrāvaruṇe paśau paśupuroḍāśasya yājyā | sūtritaṃ ca—‘pra bāhavā sisṛtaṃ jīvase no yadaṃhihaṃ nātividhe sudānu’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
pra bāhavā sisṛtaṃ jīvaseṃ na ā no gavyūtimukṣataṃ ghṛtena | ā no janai śravataṃ yuvānā śrutaṃ meṃ mitrāvaruṇā havemā || 5 || pra | bāhavā | sisṛtam | jīvaseṃ | naḥ | ā | naḥ | gavyūtim | ukṣatam | ghṛtena | ā | naḥ | janai | śravatam | yuvānā | śrutam | me | mitrāvaruṇā | havemā || 5 || he ॰mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau devau ॰bāhavā yuvābhyāṃ bāhū ॰pra ॰sisṛtaṃ prasārayataṃ haviḥ- svīkārāya dhanapradānāya vā | kimarthamiti | ॰naḥ ॰jīvase asmākaṃ jīvanāya | kiṃca ॰naḥ asmākaṃ ॰gavyūtim | gāvo yanti gacchantyatreti gavyūtimārgabhūtabhūmiḥ | tāṃ tṛṇādiprarohāya ॰ghṛtena udakena ॰ā samantāt ॰ukṣataṃ siñcatam | kiṃca ॰naḥ asmān ॰jane asmatsamāne manuṣya- samūhe vā no'smān ॰śravataṃ viśrutaṃ kurutam | he ॰yuvānā nityayauvanau sarvatra vyāptau vā yuvāṃ ॰me mama ॰imā imāni ॰havā āhvānāni ॰śrutaṃ śṛṇutam ||
n mitro varuṇo aryamā nustamaneṃ tokāya varivo dadhantu | sugā no viśvā supathāni santu yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 6 || nu | mitraḥ | varuṇaḥ | aryamā | naḥ | tamaneṃ | tokāya | varivaḥ | dadhantu | su'gā | naḥ | viśvā | su'pathāni | santu | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||6|| ॰mitraḥ ॰varuṇaḥ ॰aryamā caite trayo devāḥ ॰nu adya ॰naḥ asmākaṃ ॰tamaneṃ ātmane ātmahitāya ॰tokāya putrāya ca ॰varivaḥ dhanaṃ ॰dadhantu prayacchantu | ॰naḥ asmākaṃ ॰viśvā sarvāṇi gantavyāni ॰sugā sugamanāni ॰supathāni ca ॰santu bhavantu | śiṣṭaṃ gatam || || 4 || ‘udeti’ iti ṣaḍṛcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham | ādyāstataḥ pañcamyāḥ pūrvārdhaśca sūryadevatyā avaśiṣṭāstayo'rdharcā mitrāvaruṇadevatyāḥ | tathā cānukrāntam—‘udetīti cārdhapañcamāḥ |’ iti | viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ || 1. kha-jha-ta-śa-mahe |
uddeti subhago viśvacakṣāḥ sādhāraṇaḥ sūryo mānuṣāṇām | cakṣurmitrasya varuṇasya devaścarmeva yaḥ samaviivyaktamauṃsi || 1 || ut | ū~ iti | eti | subhagaḥ | viśvacakṣāḥ | sādhāraṇaḥ | sūryaḥ | mānuṣāṇām | cakṣuḥ | mitrasya | varuṇasya | devaḥ | carmeva | yaḥ | samaviivyaktam | tamāṃsi || 1 || ^uddeti ^ullaṅghayan ^sūryaḥ ^subhagaḥ ^śobhanabhāgyaḥ ^suṣṭhu ^bhajanīyo ^vā ^viśvacakṣāḥ ^sarvasya draṣṭā ^mānuṣāṇāṃ ^sarveṣāṃ ^manuṣyāṇāṃ ^sādhāraṇaḥ | ^sādhāraṇatvapratipādakaśrutiḥ ^pūrvamudāhṛtā | ^mitrasya ^varuṇasya ^ca ^cakṣuḥ ^prakāśakaḥ ^devaḥ ^dyotamānaḥ | ^yaḥ ^devaḥ ^carmeva ^carmāṇīva ^tamāṃsi ^samaviivyakta ^saha ^vicarati ^saṃveṣṭayati ^sa ^mahānubhāvo ^deva ^uddeti ||
uddeti prasavītā janānāṃ mahān
atha sūryaḥ ᐁrukmaḥ rocamānaḥ ᐁurukṣāḥ prabhutatejāśca san ᐁdivaḥ antarikṣāt ᐁudeti |
yadvā divo'ntarikṣasya rukma ābharaṇasthānīyaḥ |
kīdṛśo'yam |
ᐁdūrearthaḥ dūre gantā |
artho'taiḥ |
dūre prārthyamāno vā |
ᐁtaraṇiḥ tārakaḥ ᐁājamānaḥ dīpyamānaḥ sanudeti |
ᐁnūnaṃ niścayaṃ ᐁjanāḥ sarve prāṇinaḥ ᐁsūryeṇa prerakena devena ᐁprasūtāḥ anujñātāḥ preritāḥ santaḥ ᐁarthāni gantavyāni anuṣṭheyāni ᐁapāṃsi karmāṇi ᐁkṛṇvan kurvanti ||
yatrā̭ ca॒kruramṛ̭tā॒ gātṷma॒smai॒ śye॒no na dīya॒nva̭ti॒ pārtha̭: |
pra॒ti vā॒ṃ sūra॒ udi̭te॒ vidhḙma॒ namo̭bhi॒rmitrā̭varu॒ṇo॒ta ha॒vyaiḥ ||
5 ||
yatra̭ | ca॒kruḥ | a॒mṛ॒tāḥ | gātṷm | a॒smai̭ | śye॒naḥ | na | dīya̭n | anṷ | e॒ti | pārtha̭: | pra॒ti | vā॒m | sū॒re | u॒ta | i̭te | vi॒dhe॒ma | namo̭bhiḥ | mi॒trā॒varu॒ṇau | u॒ta | ha॒vyaiḥ || 5 ||
ᐁyatra yasminnantarikṣe ᐁamṛtāḥ amaradharmāṇaḥ pūrve devāḥ ᐁasmai sūryāya ᐁgātuṃ mārgaṃ ᐁcakruḥ akurvan tat ᐁpāthaḥ antarikṣam ᐁanveti anugacchati |
ka iva |
ᐁdīyan gacchan ᐁśyeno ᐁna śaṃsanīyagamano gṛdhra iva |
ayamartharcaḥ saurya ityuktam |
he ᐁmitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau ᐁvāṃ yuvāṃ ᐁsūre sūrye ᐁudite sati prātaḥsavane ᐁnamobhiḥ namaskāraiḥ stutibhiḥ ᐁuta api ca ᐁhavyaiḥ havirbhiñca ᐁprati ᐁvidhema paricarema ||
nū mi॒tro varṷṇo॒ arya॒mā nūsta̭ma॒ne to॒kāya॒ varī̭vo dadhatu |
su॒gā no॒ viśvā̭ su॒pathā̭ni santu॒ yūya̭ṃ pā॒ta sva॒stibhi॒ḥ sadā̭ naḥ ||
6 ||
nu | mi॒traḥ | varṷṇaḥ | arya॒mā | naḥ | ta॒ne | to॒kāya̭ | varī̭vaḥ | da॒dha॒tu | su॒gā | naḥ | viśvā̭ | su॒pathā̭ni | sa॒ntu | yūya̭m | pā॒ta | sva॒stibhi̭ḥ | sadā̭ | naḥ || 6 ||
‘nū mitraḥ’ iti ṣaṣṭhī gatā ||
5 ||
‘divi kṣayantā’ iti pañcamaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ maitrāvaruṇam |
‘divi pañca’ ityanukramaṇikā |
tṛtīye chandome praugaśastra ādyasṛco maitrāvaruṇasya |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘divi kṣayantā rajasaḥ pṛthivyāmā viśvavārāśvinā gataṃ naḥ’ (ādha. śrau. 8. 11) iti ||
di॒vi kṣaya̭ntā॒ raja̭saḥ pṛthi॒vyāḥ pra vā̭ṃ ghṛ॒tasya॒ nirṇi̭jau dadīran |
ha॒vyaṃ nau mi॒tro arya॒mā sujā̭to॒ rājā̭ su॒kṣatro varṷṇo juṣanta ||
1 ||
di॒vi | kṣaya̭ntā | raja̭saḥ | pṛ॒thi॒vyām | pra | vā॒m | ghṛ॒tasya̭ | ni॒ḥ'ni॒rjau | da॒dī॒ran | ha॒vyam | nau | mi॒traḥ | arya॒mā | su'jā̭taḥ | rājā̭ | su॒kṣatraḥ | varṷṇaḥ | ju॒ṣa॒nta || 1 ||
ᐁdivi dyuloke'ntarikṣe ᐁpṛthivyāṃ ca vartamānasya ᐁrajasaḥ udakasya ᐁkṣayantā |
kṣayati- raiśvaryakarmā |
svāminau bhavataḥ |
he mitrāvaruṇau ᐁvāṃ yuvābhyāṃ preritā meghāḥ ᐁghṛtasya ᐁnirṇijaḥ udakasya rūpāṇi ᐁdadīran dadate prayacchanti |
athavā ᐁvāṃ yuvābhyāṃ ghṛtasya nirṇijo rūpāṇi |
ghṛtānītyarthaḥ |
tāni dadīran dīyante |
ᐁnaḥ asmākaṃ saṃbandha ᐁhavyaṃ ᐁmitraḥ ᐁsujātaḥ suṣṭhu prādurbhūtaḥ ᐁaryamā ᐁrājā sarvasya svāmī ᐁsukṣatraḥ śobhanabalaḥ ᐁvaruṇaḥ caite ᐁjuṣanta sevantām ||
1. kṣa-ta-na-bha-śa-ghṛtasya ca |
ā rājānā maha ṛtasya gopā sindhupatī kṣatriyā yātam‌arvāk‌ |
iḻāṃ no mitrāvaruṇota vṛṣṭimarva diva invataṃ jīradānū ||
2 ||
ā | rājānā | mahaḥ | ṛtasya | gopā | sindhupatī iti sindhu'patī | kṣatriyā | yātam | arvāk | iḻām | naḥ | mitrāvaruṇā | uta | vṛṣṭim | arva | divaḥ | invatam | jīradānū iti jīra'dānū || 2 ||
he rājānā sarvasya svāminau he mahaḥ mahataḥ ṛtasya udakasya yajñasya vā gopā gopāthi- tārau |
subāmantritaṃ ‘parājñavasvare’ iti parājñavadbhāvāt‌ ṣaṣṭhāmantritasamudāyasya nighātatvam‌ |
he sindhupatī nadyāḥ pālayitārau he kṣatriyā balavantau yuvām arvāk asmadabhimukham ā yātam āgacchatam |
kiṃca he mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau he jīradānū kṣipradānau yuvāṃ naḥ asma- bhyam iḻām annam uta api ca puṣṭiṃ tatsādhikāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ ca divaḥ antarikṣāt ava avasthāt invataṃ prerayatam ||
mitrastanno varuṇo devo aryaḥ pra sādhiṣṭhebhiḥ pṛthubhirnayantu |
bravadharyā na ādriḥ sudāsa iṣā madema saha devagopāḥ ||
3 ||
mitraḥ | tat | naḥ | varuṇaḥ | devaḥ | aryaḥ | pra | sādhiṣṭhebhiḥ | pṛthu'bhiḥ | nayantu | bravat | yathā | naḥ | āt | ariḥ | su'dāsai | iṣā | madema | saha | deva'gopāḥ ||3||
mitraḥ varuṇaḥ devaḥ aryaḥ aryamā caite trayo'pi naḥ asmān tat tadā yadāsmākama- pekṣitaṃ tadā sādhiṣṭhebhiḥ sādhakatamaiḥ pathibhiḥ mārgaiḥ nayantu prāpayantu |
kiṃca naḥ asmān sudāse śobhanadānāya janāya ariḥ aryamā yathā bravat asāvanukampya iti brūyāttathā kurvantu |
aryamṇaḥ punarabhidhānamādarārtham |
devagopāḥ devā yūyaṃ gopāyitāro yeṣāsmākaṃ te vayam iṣā yuṣmābhiradātteṣyanānnena saha putrādisahitāḥ madema hṛpyema ||
yo vāṃ gartaṃ manasā takṣadetamurdhvāṃ dhītiṃ kṛṇavat‌dhārayacca |
ukṣethāṃ mitrāvaruṇā ghṛtena tā rājānā sukṣitīstarpayethām ||
4 ||
yaḥ | vām | gartam | manasā | takṣat | etam | ūrdhvam | dhītim | kṛṇvat | dhārayat | ca | ukṣethām | mitrāvaruṇā | ghṛtena | tā | rājānā | su'kṣitīḥ | tarpayethām || 4 ||
he mitrāvaruṇau yaḥ vāṃ yuvayoḥ etaṃ gataṃ rathaṃ manasā takṣat stomena saṃkalpayet tathā kṛtvā ūrdhvām unnatāṃ dhītiṃ karma stutirūpaṃ kṛṇvat kuryāt uccaiḥ stuyāt evaṃ kṛtvā dhārayacca dhāge dhārayet he rājānā svāminau mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau tā tau yuvāṃ janaṃ ghṛtena udakena ukṣethāṃ siñcatam |
tasmai sukṣitīḥ śobhananivāsāḥ prajāḥ tarpayethām |
yathā sukṣitayo bhavanti tathā tarpayethāmiti ||
1. kha-sa-tanna-bha-śa-’yaḥ’ nāsti |
eṣa stomo varuṇa mitra tubhyaṃ somaḥ śukro na vāyavai'yāmi |
aviṣṭaṃ dhiyau jigataṃ puraṃdhīryūṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
5 ||
eṣaḥ | stomaḥ | varuṇa | mitra | tubhyam | somaḥ | śukraḥ | na | vāyave | ayāmi | aviṣṭam | dhiyaḥ | jigatam | puram'dhīḥ | yuyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||5||
anvayā stutimupasaṃharati | he varuṇa he mitra tubhyaṃ yuvayoḥ vāyave | vāyurgantādityaḥ | sa evāyāmā | tasmai ca eṣa stomaḥ stavaḥ ayāmi akāri | kimiba | śukraḥ dīptaḥ somaḥ na yuṣmabhyaṃ prītikaraḥ somo yathā dīyate tadvat | dhiyaḥ karmāṇyasmadīyāni aviṣṭaṃ rakṣatam | puraṃdhīḥ stutīḥ jigataṃ prabudhyatam | anyadratam || || 6 ||
‘prati vāṃ sure’ iti pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ maitrāvaruṇam |
‘prati vām’ ityanukrāntam |

tā | bhūri̭'pāśau | a̭nṛtasya | setṷ iti̭ | du॒ḥstyetū iti̭ du॒ḥstyetū | ri॒pavai̭ | ma॒tyaṷya | ṛ॒tasya̭ | mi॒trāva̭ruṇā | pṛ॒thā | vām | a॒paḥ | na | nā॒vā | du॒ḥ'itā | tarḙma || 3 || ᵛtā tau mi॒trāva̭ru॒ṇau ᵛbhūri̭pāśau pra॒bhūta̭bandhanasādhanapāśopetau ᵛa̭nṛtasya yāga̭rahitasya ᵛsetū setṷva॒ndhakau ᵛri॒pavḙ ᵛma॒rtyāya̭ vai॒rinā̭ya ᵛdu॒styetū dṷratikra॒mau bha̭vataḥ | he ᵛmi॒trāva̭ruṇā tādṛ॒śau mi॒trāva̭ru॒ṇau ᵛvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵛṛ॒tasya̭ ya॒jñasya̭ yuva॒yorvā̭yānuṣṭhīyamānasya yāga̭sya ᵛpathā mā॒rgeṇa̭ ᵛdu॒ritā̭ du॒ḥkhāni̭ ᵛtarḙma ᵛnā॒vā ᵛa॒po ᵛna pra̭bhūta॒myuda̭kānīva || maitrāvaruṇe paśau 'ā no mitrāvaruṇā' ityeṣā caturthyanuvākyā| sūtritaṃ ca—'ā no mitrā- varuṇā havyajuṣṭiṃ yuva~ vastrāṇi pīvasā vasāye' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||

19 ||
'prati vāṃ ratham' iti daśacaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktam |

1 ||
prati | vām | ratham | nṛpatī iti nṛ'patī | jaradhyai | haviṣmatā | manasā | yajñiyena | yaḥ | vām | dūtaḥ | na | dhiṣṇyā | ajīgaḥ | accha | sunuḥ | na | pitarā | vivakkim ||1||

śīghramāgacchatītyarthaḥ |

ā | śuśrā | yātum | aśvinā | su'aśvā | giraḥ | dastrā | jujuṣāṇā | yuvākau | havyāni | ca | prati'bhṛtā | vītam | naḥ || 1 ||

'ā vāṃ rathaḥ' ityaṣṭacaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃbhāśvinam |
anukramyate ca—'ā vāṃ ratho'ṣṭau' iti |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayorviniyoga uktaḥ ||
ā vāṃ ratho roṭasī baddhaghāno hiraṇyayo vṛṣabhiratvaśvaiḥ | ghṛtarvartaniḥ pivibhī rucāna iṣā voḻahā nṛpatirvājinīvān || 1 || ā | vām | rathaḥ | roṭasī iti | baddhaghānaḥ | hiraṇyayaḥ | vṛṣabhaiḥ | yātu | aśvaiḥ | ghṛta'vartaniḥ | pivi'bhiḥ | rucānaḥ | iṣām | voḻahā | nṛ'patiḥ | vājinī'vān ||1||
he aśvinau ᵥvāṃ ᵥrathaḥ ᵥvṛṣabhaiḥ yuvabhiḥ ᵥaśvaiḥ yuktaḥ san ᵥā ᵥyātu yajñamasmadīyam |
kīdṛśo rathaḥ |
ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥbaddhaghānaḥ bādhamānaḥ ᵥhiraṇyayaḥ hiraṇmayaḥ ᵥghṛtavartaniḥ ghṛtodakaṃ vartanyāṃ yasya tādṛśaḥ ᵥpivibhiḥ rathanemibhirvā ᵥrucānaḥ dīpyamānaḥ ᵥiṣāṃ ᵥvoḻahā yajamānairdattānāṃ haviṣāṃ vāhako dātavyānāṃ vājñānāṃ ᵥvoḻahā ᵥnṛpatiḥ nṛṇāṃ yaja- mānānāṃ svāmī ᵥvājinīvān annavān ||

2. kha-ta-na-bha-cānnānāṃ |
sa pa̭prathā॒no a॒bhi pa̭śca॒ bhūmā̭ tri॒vandhṷro ma॒nasā॒ yātu॒ yukta̭ḥ |
vi॒śo yena॒ gaccha̭tho de॒va॒yantī̭ḥ ku॒trā ci॒dyāma̭ma॒śvinā॒ dadhā̭nā ||
2 ||
saḥ | pa॒prathā॒naḥ | a॒bhi | pa॒śca | bhūma̭ | tri॒'vandhṷraḥ | ma॒nasā̭ | ā | yā॒tu॒ | yukta̭ḥ | vi॒śaḥ | yena̭ | gaccha̭thaḥ | de॒va॒'yantī̭ḥ | ku॒tra | ci॒t | yāma̭m | a॒śvinā̭ | dadhā̭nā ||2||
ᵛsaḥ rathaḥ ᵛpañca ᵛbhūma bhūtāni sarvaprāṇinaḥ ᵛpaprathānaḥ prathamānaḥ ᵛtrivandhuraḥ |
vandhuramuccāvacaṃ sārathyavasthānaṃ kāṣṭhamayam |
tādṛśaistribhiryuktaḥ ᵛmanasā asmatastutyā ᵛyuktaḥ ᵛabhi ᵛā ᵛyātu |
ᵛyena rathena ᵛdevayantīḥ ᵛviśaḥ yajamānān prati ᵛgacchathaḥ |
he ᵛaśvinā aśvinau ᵛkutra ᵛcit yatra kāpi ᵛyāmaṃ gamanaṃ ᵛdadhānā dhārayantau yena viśo gacchathaḥ sa yātviti ||
svaśvā̭ yu॒śasā॒ yā॒ta॒marvā̭gda॒strā ni॒dhiṃ ma॒dhuma̭ntaṃ pi॒batha̭ḥ |
vi vā॒ṃ rathaṷ va॒dhvā yā̭da॒māno॒'ntāndi॒vo bā̭dhate॒ varta̭ni॒bhyām ||
3 ||
su'aśvā̭ | yu॒śasā̭ | ā | yā॒ta॒m | a॒rvāk | da॒strā | ni॒'dhim | ma॒dhu'ma̭ntam | pi॒batha̭ḥ | vi | vā॒m | rathaṷ | va॒dhvā | yā॒da॒māna̭ḥ | a॒ntān | di॒vaḥ | bā॒dha॒te॒ | varta̭ni॒'bhyām || 3 ||
he devau ᵛsvaśvā śobhanāśvena ᵛyaśasā ca ᵛarvāk asmadabhimukhaṃ ᵛyātam āgacchatam |
he ᵛdastrā śatrūṇāmupakṣapayitārau ᵛmadhumantaṃ madhurasapetaṃ ᵛnidhiṃ nidhivihitaṃ somaṃ ᵛpibathaḥ pibatam |
ᵛvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵛrathaḥ ᵛvadhvā sūryayā saha ᵛyādamānaḥ gantavyān prati gacchan |
gamayannityarthaḥ |
evaṃ kurvan ᵛvartanibhyāṃ svacakrābhyāṃ ᵛdivaḥ ᵛantān paryantapradeśān ᵛbādhate śīghragamanena pīḍayati ||
yu॒voḥ śriya॒ṃ pari॒ yoṣā̭vṛṇīta॒ sūraṷ du॒hitā॒ parita̭kmyāyām |
yadde॒va॒yanta̭mavatha॒ḥ śacī̭bhi॒ḥ pari̭ ghra॒saṃma̭nā॒ vāṃ va॒yo gā̭t ||
4 ||
yu॒voḥ | śriya̭m | pari̭ | yoṣā̭ | a॒vṛṇīta̭ | sūra̭ḥ | du॒hitā̭ | pari̭'takmyāyām | yat | de॒va॒'yanta̭m | a॒vatha̭ḥ | śacī̭bhiḥ | pari̭ | ghra॒sam | o॒ma॒nā | vā॒m | va॒yaḥ | gā̭t ||4||
ᵛyuvoḥ yuvayoḥ ᵛśriyam |
śrayata iti śrī rathaḥ |
taṃ sevāmeva vā ᵛyoṣā sarvadā miśrayantī yoṣit ᵛsūraḥ sūryasya ᵛduhitā ᵛpari ᵛavṛṇīta |
kadā |
ᵛparitakmyāyāṃ rātrau paritastaknavatī saṃgrāme yajñe vā gantavye |
kiṃca ᵛyat yadā ᵛdevayantaṃ devakāmaṃ yajamānaṃ yajñaṃ vā ᵛśacībhiḥ yuvayorgamanādilakṣaṇaiḥ karmabhiḥ ᵛavathaḥ rakṣathaḥ tadānīṃ ᵛghrasaṃ dīptaṃ ᵛvayaḥ annaṃ somādilakṣaṇam ᵛomanā avanena rakṣaṇena nimittena ᵛvāṃ ᵛpari ᵛgāt paryagāt ||
yo hu sya vā॒ṃ ratha̭rā va॒sta u॒strā ratha॒ṃ yujā̭na॒ḥ pari̭yāti॒ vṛti̭ḥ |
tena̭ na॒ḥ śaṃ yo॒ruṣo॒ vyṷṣṭau॒ nyaśvi̭nā va॒hata॒ṃ ya॒jñe a॒smin ||
5 ||
yaḥ | hu | sya | vā॒m | ra॒thi॒rā | va॒stai | u॒strā | ratha̭m | yu॒jā॒naḥ | pari̭'yāti | vṛti̭ḥ | tena̭ | na॒ḥ | śam | yoḥ | u॒ṣaḥ | vi'ṷṣṭau | ni | a॒śvi॒nā | va॒ha॒tam | ya॒jñe | a॒smin ||5||
^ya: ratha:|
^ha iti pūraṇa:|
^sya: sa prasiddho rayo he ^rathirā rathinau|
matvarthīyo ra:|
^ustrā: tejāṃsi ^vaste ācchādayati|
yaśca ^ratha: ^yujāna: aśvairyukta: san ^varti: mārgaṃ yajamānagṛhaṃ vā ^pariyāti parigacchati|
^tena rathena he ^aśvinā aśvinau ^na: asmākam ^asmin ^yajñe ^upaso ^vyuṣṭau prāta:kāle ^śaṃ śamanāya pāpānāṃ ^yo: miśranāya ca sukhānāṃ ^ni ^vahataṃ nitarāṃ prāpnutam||
narā gauraiva vidyutaṃ tṛṣāṇāsmākamadya savanopa yātam|
purutrā hi vāṃ matibhirhavante mā vāṃmanye ni yamandeyantaḥ||
6||
narā| gaurā'iva| vi'dyutam| tṛṣāṇā| asmākam| adya| sarvanā| upa| yātam| puru'trā| hi| vām| matibhi:| havante| mā| vām| anye| ni| yaman| deva'yantaḥ||6||
he ^narā netārāvaśvinau ^gauraiva gaurā mṛgīva ^vidyutaṃ viśeṣeṇa dīpyamānaṃ somaṃ prati ^tṛṣāṇā tṛṣṇāyuktau ^adya ^asmākaṃ ^savanā savanāni ^upa ^yātam upāgacchatam|
^purutrā bahuṣu yajñeṣu ^vāṃ yuvāṃ yajamānā: ^matibhi: stutibhi: ^havante ^hi stuvanti|
ata: ^vāṃ yuvām ^anye yaṣṭāra: ^devayantaḥ devau kāmayamānā vāṃ yuvāṃ ^mā ^ni ^yaman mā niyacchantu||
yuvaṃ bhujyumarvividvaṃ samudra ūrddhathurarṇaso asridhānai:|
patatribhiraśramairavyathibhirdasanābhiraśvinā pārayantā||
7||
yuvam| bhujyum| arvā'vidvam| samudre| ut| ūhathu:| arṇasa:| asridhānai:| patatribhi:| aśramai:| avyathibhi:| daṃsanābhi:| aśvinā| pārayantā|| 7||
he ^aśvinā aśvinau ^yuvaṃ ^bhujyum etannāmakam ^avividvaṃ vikṣiptaṃ sakhibhi: ^samudre tanmadhye nimagnam ^arṇasa: udakāt ^ūhathu:|
^kiṃ kurvantāviti taducyate|
^asridhānai: akṣīya- māṇai: ^aśramai: ^avyathibhi: ca ^patatribhi: patanavadbhirmanasvī rathe niyuktairathai: ^daṃsanābhi: śārīrai: karmabhiśca ^pārayantā pārayantau samudramarṇasa ūhathuriti|
‘nāsatyā bhujyumūhatu:|
patatrai:’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 116. 4) iti muktam||
nū me havamā śṛṇutaṃ yuvānā yāsiṣṭaṃ vartiraśvināvirāvat|
dhattaṃ ratnāni jarataṃ ca sūrīn‌yuyaṃ pāta svastibhi: sadā na:||
8||
nu| me| havam| ā| śṛṇutam| yuvānā| yāsiṣṭam| varti:| aśvinau| irā'vat| dhattam| ratnāni| jaratam| ca| sūrīn| yuyam| pāta| svastibhi:| sadā| na:||8||
‘nū me havam’ ityaṣṭamī siddhā||
16||
‘ā viśvavārā’ iti saptacaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāśvinam|
anukramyate ca— ‘ā viśvavārā sapta’ iti|
prātaranuvāka āśvinaśastre ca viniyoga ukta:|
ādyastṛcastṛtīye chandome praugaśastre viniyukta:|
‘ā viśvavārāśvinā gataṃ no'yaṃ soma indra’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 11) iti sūtritatvāt||
yajñasya yajñasya śiraḥsthānīyatvādasya |
yaḥ gharmaḥ vāṃ yuvāṃ prāpnavaditi śeṣaḥ |
samudrān saritaḥ ca piparti pūrayati vṛddhidvārā |
etagvā citra |
ciditi pūraṇaḥ |
aśvāviva yathā suyujā suṣṭhu yuktau rathe bhavatastathetyarthaḥ |
netyupamārthe |

ca॒niṣṭa̭ṃ de॒vā oṣa̭dhī॒ṣpsu ya॒dyo॒gyā a॒śvavai̭the ṛ॒ṣī॒ṇām | pu॒rū॒ṇi ra॒tnā da̭dha॒tau nya̭sme a॒nu pū॒rvāṇi̭ ca॒ra॒thyu॒gā॒ni || 4 || ca॒niṣṭa̭m | de॒vau | oṣa̭dhīṣu | a॒psu | yat | yo॒gyāḥ | a॒śvavai̭the iti | ṛ॒ṣī॒ṇām | pu॒rū॒ṇi | ra॒tnā | da̭dha॒tau | ni | a॒sme iti | a॒nu | pū॒rvāṇi̭ | ca॒ra॒thyuḥ | yu॒gā॒ni ||4|| he devā devau yuvām oṣadhīṣu oṣadhivikārāṃścapuroḍāśādikān apsu somarasān caniṣṭam | atyantakamanīyatamaṃ kāmayethāmityarthaḥ | yat yasmāt yogyāḥ yuvayorucitā oṣadhī- rapsu ṛṣīṇāṃ sambandhinīḥ abhavaithe vyāpnuthaḥ tasmādasmadīyā api kāmayethāmityarthaḥ | yadvā | ṛṣīṇāmasmākamiti pūjārthaṃ bahuvacanam | yadyasmādoṣadhīṣvapsu ca caniṣṭa yogyāḥ stutīścabhāvaithe tasmāt asme asmāsu puruṇi bahūni ratnā ramaṇīyāni dhanāni ni dadhatau pūrvāṇi yugāni mithunāni jāyāpatirūpāṇi anu carathyuḥ dhyātavantau | anukṛṣvantāvanugrahārtham ||
śu॒śru॒vāṃsā̭ ci॒daśvi̭nā pu॒rū॒ṇya॒bhi bra॒hmāṇi̭ ca॒kṣāthe ṛ॒ṣī॒ṇām | pra॒ti pra॒ yā̭ta॒ṃ vara̭mā ja॒nāya॒sme vā̭mastu su॒mati̭śca॒niṣṭhā || 5 || śu॒śru॒vāṃsā̭ | ci॒t | a॒śvi॒nā | pu॒rū॒ṇi | a॒bhi | bra॒hmāṇi̭ | ca॒kṣāthe iti | ṛ॒ṣī॒ṇām | pra॒ti | pra | yā॒tam | vara̭m | ā | ja॒nāya | a॒sme iti | vā॒m | a॒stu | su॒matiḥ | ca॒niṣṭhā ||5|| he adhvanā aśvinau cit iti pūraṇaḥ | yuvāṃ śuśruvāṃsā śrutavantau santo puruṇi bahūni brahmāṇi parivṛḍhāni karmāṇi stutilakṣaṇāni ṛṣīṇām asmākaṃ sambandhīni abhi cakṣāthe abhipaśyetho yuvām | ataḥ janāya janasya yajamānasya mama varaṃ yajñaṃ prati pra yātam | vāṃ yuvayoḥ caniṣṭhā uttalakṣaṇā sumatiḥ anugrahameti | asme asmāsu astu bhavatu ||
yo vā̭ṃ ya॒jño nā̭satyā ha॒viṣma̭nkṛ॒tabra̭hmā sa॒maryo̭ bha॒vāti̭ | u॒pa pra॒ yā̭ta॒ṃ vara̭mā va॒siṣṭha॒mimā bra॒hmāṇyṛ̭cyante yu॒vabhyā̭m || 6 || yaḥ | vā॒m | ya॒jñaḥ | nā॒sa॒tyā | ha॒viṣma̭ān | kṛ॒ta'bra̭hmā | sa॒marya̭ḥ | bha॒vāti̭ | u॒pa | pra | yā॒tam | vara̭m | ā | va॒siṣṭha̭m | i॒mā | bra॒hmāṇi̭ | ṛ॒cyante | yu॒vabhyā̭m ||6|| he nāsatyā aśvinau vāṃ yuvayoḥ yaḥ yajñaḥ yajamānaḥ samaryaḥ ṛtvigbhiramaryaiḥ sahitaḥ san haviṣmān haviṣā yuktaḥ kṛtabrahmā kṛtastotrarūpakarmā bhavati bhavati taṃ varaṃ varaṇīyaṃ vasiṣṭham ā upa pra yātam prakarṣeṇopagacchatam | imā imāni brahmāṇi mantrajātāni yuvabhyāṃ yuvābhyāmarrthāyāgamanāya ṛcyante stūyante | kriyanta ityarthaḥ ||
i॒yaṃ ma̭nī॒ṣā i॒yama̭śvinā gī॒rimāṃ su॒vṛktiṃ vṛṣa̭ṇā juṣethām | i॒mā bra॒hmāṇi̭ yu॒vayṷnya॒gmanpa॒ta sva॒stibhi̭ḥ sa॒dā na̭ḥ || 7 || i॒yam | ma॒nī॒ṣā | i॒yam | a॒śvi॒nā | gīḥ | i॒mām | su॒'vṛktim | vṛṣa̭ṇā | ju॒ṣe॒thām | i॒mā | bra॒hmāṇi̭ | yu॒va'yṷni | a॒gman | pa॒ta | sva॒sti'bhi̭ḥ | sa॒dā | na̭ḥ || 7 ||
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa- 'janāya' nāsti |
2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-bhavati |
atha saptamyā stutimupasaṃharati |
he ᵥaśvinā aśvinau ᵥiyaṃ ᵥmanīṣā stutiryuvayoḥ kṛteti śeṣaḥ |
tadevādārarthaṃ punarucyate |
ᵥiyaṃ ᵥgīḥ stutiḥ kṛtā |
he ᵥvṛṣaṇā kāmānāṃ varṣakau ᵥimām asmatkṛtāṃ ᵥsuvṛktiṃ śobhanāṃ stutiṃ ᵥjuṣethāṃ sevethām |
ᵥimā imāni ᵥbrahmāṇa karmāṇi stutirūpāṇi ᵥyuvayūni yuvāṃ kāmayamānāni santi ᵥagman gacchantu yuvām |
yūyaṃ pāteti siddham ||
17||
4||
pañcame'nuvāke ekonaviṃśatisūktāni |
tatra ‘apa svasuḥ’ iti bahucaṃ prathamaṃ sūktaṃ traidhabha- māśvinam |
anukramyate ca—‘apa svasuḥ ṣaṭ’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
apa svasṷrupa॒so narji̭hīte riṇa॒kti kṛ॒ṣṇīra̭ru॒ṣāya॒ panthām̭ |
aśvā̭maghā॒ goma̭ghā॒ vāṃ hṷvema di॒vā nakta̭ṃ śa॒ruma̭sma॒dyuyo̭tam ||
1 ||
ap | svasuḥ | uṣasaḥ | nak | jihīte | riṇakti | kṛṣṇīḥ | aruṣāya | panthām | aśvaṃmaghā | goḍameghā | vām | huvema | divā | naktam | śarum | asmat | yuyotam ||1||
ᵥsvasuḥ svasṛsthānīyāyāḥ ᵥuṣasaḥ sakāśāt ᵥnak naktaṃ rātriḥ ᵥapa ᵥjihīte apagacchati |
tasyā avakāśaṃ datvā svayamapagatetyarthaḥ |
‘svasā svase jyāyasyai yonimāraika’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 124. 8) ityuktam |
ᵥkṛṣṇīḥ kṛṣṇavarṇā rātriḥ ᵥaruṣāya ārocamānāyāhne sūryāya vā ᵥpanthāṃ panthānaṃ mārgaṃ ᵥriṇakti recayati |
yasmādevaṃ tasmādyuvayorāgamanasamayatvāt he ᵥaśvamaghā aśvadhanau he ᵥgomaghā godhanau |
ubhayoḥ pradātāvāvityarthaḥ |
īdṛśau ᵥvāṃ yuvāṃ ᵥhuvema stumaḥ |
āhvayāmaḥ |
ᵥdivā ᵥnaktaṃ sarvadā ᵥśaruṃ hiṃsakam ᵥasmat asmattaḥ ᵥyuyotaṃ pṛthakurutam ||
u॒pāyā̭taṃ dā॒śuṣe॒ martyā̭ya॒ rathḙna॒ vāma̭ma॒śvinā॒ vaha̭ntā |
yu॒yuta॒masma̭dani॒rāma̭mī॒vāṃ di॒vā nakta̭ṃ mā॒dhvī trāsī̭thāṃ naḥ ||
2 ||
upa'āyātam | dāśuṣe | martyāya | rathena | vāmam | aśvinā | vahantā | yuyutam | asmat | anirāmam | amīvām | divā | naktam | mādhvī iti | trāsīthām | naḥ ||2||
he aśvinau yuvām ᵥupāyātam upāgacchatamasmadāhvānaṃ prati |
kimartham |
ᵥdāśuṣe haviṣāṃ dātre yajamānāya tadarthaṃ ᵥrathena ᵥvāmaṃ bananīyaṃ dhanaṃ ᵥvahantā vahantau |
ᵥasmat asmattaḥ ᵥyuyutaṃ pṛthakurutam |
kim |
ᵥanirāmam |
irām |
tadabhāvaṃ śāridrāmityarthaḥ |
ᵥamīvāṃ rogaṃ ca |
he ᵥmādhvī madhumantau yuvāṃ ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥdivā ᵥnaktaṃ sarvadā ᵥtrāsīthāṃ rakṣatam ||
ā vā̭ṃ ra॒thama̭va॒syāṃ vyṷṣṭau su॒mnāya̭vo vṛṣa̭ṇo॒ varta̭yantu |
syu॒rmaga̭bhasti॒mṛta̭yu॒bhirdhai॒raśvi̭nā॒ vasṷmanta॒ṃ vahḙthām ||
3 ||
ā | vām | ratham | avasyām | vi'uṣṭau | sumna'yavaḥ | vṛṣaṇaḥ | vartayantu | syurmagabhastim | ṛtayu'bhiḥ | aśvaiḥ | ā | aśvinā | vasu'mantam | vahethām || 3 ||
ᵥavasyām āsannāyāṃ ᵥvyuṣṭau vyucchan uṣasi ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥrathaṃ ᵥsumnāyavaḥ sukhena yoja- yanto'śvāḥ ᵥvṛṣaṇaḥ varṣakā ᵥyuvām ᵥā ᵥvartayantu |
ᵥsyumagabhastiṃ subaraśmi syutaraśmiṃ ᵥvasumantaṃ pradeyadhanayuktaṃ rathaṃ he ᵥaśvinā aśvinau ᵥṛtayubhiḥ udakayutaiḥ ᵥaśvaiḥ ᵥā ᵥvahethām ||
1. kha-sūkta vasiṣṭhasyārthaḥ; gha- sūkta vasiṣṭha ṛṣiḥ |
2. jha- varṣakau |
yo vāṃ rathau nṛpatī asti voḻhā trivandhuro vasumā~ ustrayāmā | ā na enā nāsatyopa yātamibha yadvau viśvapsanyo jigāti || 4 || yaḥ | vām | rathaḥ | nṛpatī iti nṛ'patī | asti | voḻhā | tri'vandhuraḥ | vasu'mān | ustra'yāmā | ā | naḥ | enā | nāsatyā | upa | yātam | abhi | yat | vām | viśva'psanyaḥ | jigāti ||4||
he nṛpatī nṛṇāṃ yajamānānāṃ pālakauaśvinau vāṃ yuvayoḥ yaḥ rathaḥ voḻhā yuvayo- rvāhakaḥ asti sarvadā saṃnihito vartate |
kīdṛśo'sau |
trivandhuraḥ sārathyadhiṣṭhānasthānatrayopetaḥ vasumān dhanavān ustrayāmā ustaṃ divasaṃ prati gantā |
enā etena rathena he nāsatyā aśvinau naḥ asmān upa ā yātam |
yat rathaḥ yaśca rathaḥ vāṃ viśvapsanyaḥ vyāsarūpaḥ abhi jigāti abhigacchati |
anya āha |
yadyasmādviśvapsanyo vasiṣṭho vāṃ jigāti stauti ata upāyātam ||
yuvaṃ cyavānaṃ jaraso'mumuktaṃ ni pedavaṃ ūhathuḥ āśumaśvam | niraṃhasastamasaḥ spartamatriṃ ni jāhuṣaṃ śithire dhātamantaḥ || 5 || yuvam | cyavānam | jarasaḥ | amumuktam | ni | pedave | ūhathuḥ | āśum | aśvam | niḥ | aṃhasaḥ | tamasaḥ | spartam | atrim | ni | jāhuṣam | śithire | dhātam | antaḥ ||5||

tathā atriṃ maharṣim aṃhasaḥ ṛbīsādameḥ sakāśāt tamasaḥ ca guhāntaḥsthitācca sakāśāt niḥ spataṃ nyapārayatam |

ᵥye ᵥaśvāsaḥ aśvāḥ ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ svabhūtāḥ ᵥdāśuṣaḥ havirdātuḥ ᵥgṛhaṃ ᵥyuvāṃ ᵥbibhrataḥ dhārayantaḥ ᵥdīyanti |
gamayantītyarthaḥ |
ᵥmakṣyubhiḥ śīghragantubhiḥ ᵥhyaibhiḥ hyaiḥ aśvair ᵥnarā netārī ᵥaśvinā aśvinau ᵥdevā devau ᵥasmyū asmān kāmayamānau ᵥā ᵥyātam asmadyajñamāgacchatam ||
ardhā ha yanto ᵥaśvinā pṛkṣaḥ sa canta sūryaḥ |
tā yaṃsato maghavandyo dhruvaṃ yaśa̭cchardirasmabhyaṃ nāsatyā ||
5 ||
ardha | ha | yantaḥ | aśvinā | pṛkṣaḥ | sa canta | sūryaḥ | tā | yaṃsataḥ | maghavat'bhyaḥ | dhruvam | yaśaḥ | chardiḥ | asmabhyam | nāsatyā || 5 ||
ᵥadhā ᵥha api ca ᵥaśvinā aśvinau ᵥyantaḥ stutibhirgacchanto yajamānāḥ ᵥsūryaḥ medhāvinaḥ stotāraḥ ᵥpṛkṣaḥ annaṃ prabhūtaṃ ᵥsa canta sevante saṃyanti vā |
ᵥtā tau yuvāṃ ᵥmaghavadbhyaḥ annavadbhyaḥ ᵥasmabhyaṃ ᵥdhruvam avicalitaṃ ᵥyaśaḥ annaṃ yaśa eva vā ᵥchardiḥ gṛhaṃ ᵥyaṃsataḥ prayacchataṃ he ᵥnāsatyā aśvinau ||
pra ye ᵥyuryuktāso rathā iva nṛpātaro janānām |
uta svena ᵥśarvasā ᵥśūśuvur ᵥuta kṣiyanti sukṣitim ||
6 ||
pra | ye | yuyu | avṛkāsaḥ | rathāḥ'iva | nṛ'pātāraḥ | janānām | uta | svena | śarvasā | śṛśuvuḥ | naraḥ | | uta | kṣiyanti | su'kṣitim || 6 ||
ᵥye yajamānāḥ ᵥavṛkāsaḥ parakīyadhanasyānādātāraḥ ᵥjanānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ madhye ᵥnṛpātāraḥ ṛtvigbhūpāṇāṃ nṛṇāṃ rakṣitāraḥ santaḥ ᵥyuyuḥ yuvāṃ prāpnuvanti havirbhiḥ |
prāsau dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵥrathā iva vrīhmādipūrṇā³ rathā yathā prāpnuvanti svāmigṛham |
utetyayamuttaravākyāpekṣaḥ |
api ca te ᵥnaraḥ yajamānāḥ ᵥsvena ᵥśavasā svīyena balena ᵥśūśuvuḥ vardhante |
ᵥuta api ca ᵥsukṣitiṃ sunivāsaṃ ᵥkṣiyanti gacchanti prāpnuvanti ||
21 |
‘dyuṣā āvaḥ’ ityaṣṭarcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣam |
annānukramaṇikā—‘dyuṣā aṣṭāvaṣaṣṭyaṃ⁴ tu vai’ iti |
tu vai ityuktvāttuhyādiparibhāṣayā idamādīni sapta sūktānyupodevatyāni |
prātara- nuvāka upasthe kratau tairbhume chandasyāśvinaśastre vedamādīni ṣaṭ sūktāni |
tathā ca sūyate—‘dyuṣā 1. jha-ta1-2-varṣakau kāmānāṃ |
2. jha-hiṃsāṃ; śa-ahiṃsā |
3. śa-dravyādi |
4. jha-ta1-2- anu.-aṣṭā upasyaṃ |
āvo divijā iti pakti traiṣṭubham' (āśva. śrau. 4. 14) iti|

vyuṣā ityupatiṣṭheta ṣaḍbhiḥ sūktaiḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ|

gā aśvān puruṣān dhānyaṃ striyo vāsasyajāvikam' (ṛgvi. 2. 317-18) iti|
vyṷṣā॒ āvo̭ di॒vijā̭ ṛ॒tenā॒viṣkṛ̭ṇva॒ānā ma̭hi॒māna̭mā॒gāt |
apa̭ du॒hastama̭ āva॒rjuṣṭa̭ma॒rjista̭mā pṛ॒thvyā a̭jīgaḥ ||
1 ||
vi | upāḥ | āvaḥ | divi'jāḥ | ṛtena | āviḥ'kṛṇvānā | mahimānam | ā | agāt | apa | duhaḥ | tamaḥ | āvaḥ | arjuṣṭam | arjiḥ'tamā | pṛthvyāḥ | ajīgariti || 1 ||
iyam uṣāḥ divijāḥ divyantarikṣe prādurbhūtā satī vi āvaḥ vyaucchat |
vimānaṃ kṛtavatītyarthaḥ |
vasinivāsavācyatra vipūrvo vyucchane bhavet |

halṅyābhyaḥ iti lopaḥ |
saivauṣāḥ ṛtena tejasā mahimānaṃ svamahattvam āviṣkṛṇvānā āgāt āgatavatī |

gantṛtamā pathyāḥ padavīḥ ajīgaḥ udgirati |
prāṇināṃ vyavahārāya prakāśayatītyarthaḥ ||

2 ||
mahe | naḥ | adya | sutritāya | bodhi | uṣaḥ | mahe | saubhagāya | pra | yandhi | citram | rayim | yaśasam | dhehi | asme iti | devi | marteṣu | mānuṣi | śravasyum || 2 ||
adya naḥ asmākaṃ mahe mahate sutritāya sukhaprāptaye sukhagamanāya vā bodhi bhava |
kiṃca he uṣaḥ mahe mahate saubhagāya saubhāgyāya pra yandhi prayacchāsmān |

he mānuṣi manuṣya- hite devi marteṣu asmāsu śravasyum annavantaṃ putraṃ dhehītyanuṣaṅgaḥ ||
e॒te tye bhā॒navo̭ da॒rśatāyā॒ścitrā॒ uṣa̭so॒ amṛtā̭ āguḥ |

darśatāyāḥ darśanīyāyāḥ prakāśayuktāyāḥ uṣasaḥ ete puro dṛśyamānāḥ ye te prasiddhāḥ citrāḥ pūjyā āścaryabhūtā vā amṛtāsaḥ amarā anaśvarāḥ bhānavaḥ raśmayaḥ āguḥ āgacchanti |
kiṃ kurvantaḥ |

tadanukūlaprakāśapradānāttadutpādakatvameṣām |

^me mayā ^devayānā: devaprāpakā: ^panthā: panthāna: ^pra ^ahaśran pradṛśyante| kīdṛśā: panthāna:| ^amardhantaḥ ahiṃsantaḥ ^vasubhi: tejobhi: ^iṣkṛtāsa: saṃskṛtā:| ^purastāt pūrvasyāṃ diśi ^uṣasa: prati tvā stomairīḻate vasiṣṭhā uparbudhaḥ| subhage tuṣṭuvāṃsaḥ| gavāṃ netrī vājapatnī na ucchorṣaḥ sujāte prathamā jarasva || 6 || prati| tvā| stomaiḥ| īḻate| vasiṣṭhāḥ| upaḥ'budhaḥ| su'bhage| tuṣṭuvāṃsaḥ| gavāṃm| netrī| vāja'patnī| naḥ| uccha| uṣaḥ| su'jāte| prathamā| jarasva || 6 ||

ᵥeṣā ᵥuṣāḥ ᵥrādhasaḥ stotuḥ ᵥsūnṛtānāṃ stutīnāṃ ᵥnetrī satī ᵥucchantī tamo vivāsayantī ᵥvasiṣṭhaiḥ vasiṣṭhagotrotpannaiḥ ᵥribhyate stūyate|

vāre viśvaiḥ saṃbhajanīye devi naḥ asmabhyam iṣam annaṃ ca gomat gobhirbahubhiryutam aśvāvat aśvaiśvopetaṃ rathavat rathairupetaṃ rādhaḥ dhanaṃ ca dadhatī vi bhāhiti ||

prati ketavaḥ prathamā adṛśrannūrdhvā asyā añjayo vi śrayante | uṣo arvācā bṛhatā rathena jyotiṣmatā vāmasmabhyaṃ vakṣi || 1 || prati | ketavaḥ | prathamāḥ | adṛśran | ūrdhvāḥ | asyāḥ | añjaryaḥ | vi | śrayante | uṣaḥ | arvācā | bṛhatā | rathena | jyotiṣmatā | vāmam | asmabhyam | vakṣi || 1 || asyāḥ prathamāḥ prathamotpannāḥ ketavaḥ prajñāpakā raśmayaḥ prati adṛśran pratidṛśyante | asyāḥ añjaryaḥ vyañjakā raśmayaḥ ūrdhvāḥ ūrdhvamukhāḥ vi śrayante vividhaṃ sarvatra śrayanti | he uṣaḥ devi arvācā asmadabhimukhenāgacchatā bṛhatā mahatā jyotiṣmatā tejovatā rathena asmabhyaṃ vāmaṃ vanīyaṃ dhanaṃ vakṣi vahasi ||
prati bhīmarjirate samiddhaḥ prati viprāso matibhirgṛṇantaḥ | uṣā yāti jyotiṣā bādhamānā viśvā tamāṃsi duritāpa devī || 2 || prati | mīm | agniḥ | jarate | sam | iddhaḥ | prati | viprāsaḥ | matibhiḥ | gṛṇantaḥ | uṣāḥ | yāti | jyotiṣā | bādhamānā | viśvā | tamāṃsi | duḥitā | apa | devī || 2 || agniḥ samiddhaḥ san sīṃ sarvataḥ prati jarate abhivardhate | viprāsaḥ viprā medhāvina ṛtvijaśca matibhiḥ stutibhirupasaṃ gṛṇantaḥ stuvanto jarante | uṣāḥ ca devī jyotiṣā viśvā sarvāṇi tamāṃsi duritā asmaduritāni apa bādhamānā yāti ūrdhvaṃ gacchati ||
etā u tyāḥ pratyadṛśranpurastājjyotiryacchantīruṣaso vibhātīḥ | ajījanantasūryaṃ yajñamagnimapācīrnaṃ tamo agādajuṣṭam || 3 || etāḥ | ū~ iti | tyāḥ | prati | adṛśran | purastāt | jyotiḥ | yacchantīḥ | uṣasaḥ | vi'bhātīḥ | ajījanan | sūryam | yajñam | agnim | apācīrnam | tamaḥ | agāt | ajuṣṭam || 3 ||

ᵛtyā: tā: prasiddhā etā: ᵛvibhātī: vibhātyo vimānaṃ kurvatya: ᵛjyoti: teja: ᵛyacchantī: prayacchantya: ᵛuṣasa: ᵛpurastāt pūrvasyāṃ diśi ᵛpratyadṛśan pratidṛśyante |
tā uṣasa: ᵛsūrya ᵛyajñamagniṃ ca ᵛajījanan prādurakurvan |
uṣasa udayānantaraṃ teṣāṃ saṃbhavāttajjana- katvamupacaryate |
kiṃca ᵛapācīnaṃ nīcīnam ᵛajuṣṭam apriyam |
sarveṣāṃ dṛṣṭinirodhakatvādapriyatvam |
tādṛśaṃ ᵛtama: ᵛagāt apagatamabhūt ||
arcaiti divo duhitā maghonī viśvai paśyantyuṣasaṃ vibhātīm |
āsthādrathaṃ svadhayā yujyamānamā yamaśvāsa: suyujo vahanti ||
4 ||
arcaiti | diva: | duhitā | maghonī | viśvai | paśyanti | uṣasam | vi'bhātīm | ā | asthāt | ratham | svadhayā | yujyamānam | ā | yam | aśvāsa: | su'yuja: | vahanti ||4||
ᵛdivo ᵛduhitā ᵛmaghonī dhanavatyuṣā: ᵛaceti sarvairjñāyate |
ᵛviśve sarve'pi prāṇina: ᵛvibhātīm ucchantīm ᵛupasaṃ ᵛpaśyanti |
tādṛśī devī ᵛsvadhayā annena ᵛyujyamānaṃ ᵛratham ᵛāsthāt atiṣṭhat ārohati |
ᵛyaṃ rathaṃ ᵛsuyuja: śobhanayojanā: ᵛaśvāsa: aśvā: ᵛā ᵛvahanti abhimatadeśaṃ prāpayanti taṃ rathamāsthāditi ||

tilvilāyadharvamupaso vibhātīryaṃ pāta svastibhi: sadā na: ||

25 ||
‘ḍyupā āva:’ iti pañcamaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣamupasaṃ traiṣṭubham |
‘ḍyupā:’ ityanu- kramaṇikā |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayokto viniyoga: ||
vyuṣā āva: pṛthyā janānāṃ pañca kṣitīmānuṣīrbodhayantī |
susaṃdagbhirṛkṣabhirbhāṃnumeśredi sūryo rodasī cakṣasāva: ||
1 ||
vi | uṣā: | āva: | pṛthyā | janānām | pañca | kṣitī: | mānuṣī: | bodhayantī | susaṃdṛk'bhi: | ṛkṣa'bhi: | bhānum | aśret | vi | sūrya: | rodasī iti | cakṣasā | āvirityava: || 1 ||
ᵛjanānāṃ sarvaprāṇināṃ ᵛpathyā pathi hitā ᵛuṣā: ᵛvi ᵛāva: vyaucchat |
yadvā |
janānāṃ hitāya vyaucchaditi yojyam |
kiṃ kurvantī |
ᵛmānuṣī: manuṣyarūpā: ᵛpañca ᵛkṣitī: niṣāda- pañcamāṃścaturo varṇān ᵛbodhayantī |
īdṛśyā ᵛsusaṃdṛbhi: |
saṃdṛśyate saṃdarśyatīti vā saṃdṛk teja: |
sutejobhiḥ vukṣabhiḥ gobhiḥ vbhānumaśret |
'aruṇeśā ājimadhāvat' (ai. brā. 4. 9) iti hi śrutiḥ |
'aruṇyo gāva upasām' (ni. 1. 15. 7) iti niruktam |
vsūryaḥ ca vrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau tamoyukte vcakṣasā prakāśena tejasā vvi vāvaḥ vivṛṇoti ||
vyajyate divo anteṣvaktviṃśo na yuktā upasau yatante |
saṃ te gāvustama ā vartayanti jyotiryacchanti saviteva bāhū ||
2 ||
vi | añjate | divaḥ | anteṣu | aktūn | viśaḥ | na | yuktāḥ | upasaḥ | yatante | sam | te | gāvaḥ | tamaḥ | ā | vartayanti | jyotiḥ | yacchanti | savitā'iva | bāhū iti ||2||
vuṣasaḥ vaktūn tejāṃsi vdivaḥ antarikṣasya vanteṣu paryantapradeśeṣu vvyajyate vyaktī- kurvantītyarthaḥ |
vyuktāḥ parasparaṃ saṃyuktāḥ vviśo vna prajā iva senā iva vyatante yatante tamonāśanāya atra gamanāya vā |
atha pratyakṣavādaḥ |
he uṣaḥ vte tava vgāvaḥ raśmayaḥ vtamaḥ andhakāraṃ vsam vā vvartayanti nāśayanti vjyotiḥ tejo vyacchanti prayacchanti |
vsavitā sūryaḥ vbāhū viva ||
abhūduṣā indratamā maghonījījanatsuvitāya śravāṃsi |
vi divo devī duhitā dadhātyaṅgirastamā sukṛte vasūni ||
3 ||
abhūt | uṣāḥ | indra'tamā | maghonī | ajījanat | suvitāya | śravāṃsi | vi | divaḥ | devī | duhitā | dadhāti | āṅgiraḥ'tamā | sukṛte | vasūni || 3 ||

vdivaḥ vduhitā vaṅgirastamā gantṛtamā |
yadvā |
aṅgirogotrairbhāradvājaiḥ saha rātrerapyutpatte rātryavasānasyoṣā rūpatvādāṅgirastametyucyate |
bhāradvājai rātreḥ sahotpattiranukramaṇyāmuktā—' rātrī kuśikaḥ saubharo rātrirvā bhāradvājī ' (anu. 10. 127) iti |
tādṛyuṣāḥ vsukṛte yajamānāya vvasūni dhanāni vvi vdadhāti karoti ||
tārvaduṣo rādho asmabhyaṃ rāsva yārvattastotṛbhyo arado gṛṇānā |
yāṃ tvā jajñurvṛṣabhasyā raveṇa vi duhasya duro adrairaurṇoḥ ||
4 ||
tāvat | upaḥ | rādhaḥ | asmabhyam | rāsva | yāvat | stotṛbhyaḥ | aradaḥ | gṛṇānā | yām | tvā | jajñuḥ | vṛṣabhasya | raveṇa | vi | duhasya | duraḥ | adraiḥ | aurṇoḥ || 4 ||
he vupaḥ vyāvat vrādhaḥ dhanaṃ vstotṛbhyaḥ pūrvam varadaḥ dattavatyasi vtāvat rādho dhanaṃ vgṛṇānā stūyamānā vasmabhyam api vrāsva dehi |
vyāṃ vtvā tvāṃ vvṛṣabhasya vraveṇa |
lusopamaiṣā |
vṛṣabha- syeti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī |
vṛṣabhaṃ raveṇaiva tvāṃ prakāśena vjajñuḥ jānanti prāṇinaḥ |
athavā vṛṣabhasya pravṛddhasya stotrasya raveṇa śabdena jajñuḥ jñāpayanti |
vdṛhasya dṛḍhasya vadreḥ vduraḥ dvārāṇi paṇibhirgāḥ praveśya pihitāni vvi vaurṇoḥ : vivṛtānyakaroḥ ||
devaṃdevaṃ rādhase codayantyasmadyaksūnṛtā īrayantī |
vyucchantī naḥ sunaye dhiyo dhā yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
5 ||
devam'devam | rādhase | codayantī | asmadyak | sūnṛtāḥ | īrayantī | vi'ucchantī | naḥ | sunaye | dhiyaḥ | dhāḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||5||
śrīgaṇeśāyanamaḥ ||
yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat |
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram ||
atha ṣaṣṭho vyākhyāyate |
saptame maṇḍale pañcame'nuvāke daśa sūktāni vyākhyātāni |
‘pratyu adarśi’ iti ṣaṭkamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣamupastham |
prathamāyā ayujo bṛhatyo dvitīyāyā yujaḥ satobṛhatyaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘pratyu adarśi ṣaṭ prāgātham’ iti |
prātaranuvāka uṣasye kratau bārhate chandasthāśvinaśastre cedaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pratyu adarśi saha vāmeneti bārhatam’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 14) iti ||
pratyu adarśīyatyucchantī duhitā divaḥ |
apo mahi vyayati cakṣase tamo jyotiṣkṛṇoti sūnarī ||
1 ||
prati | ū~ iti | adarśi | ā'yatī | ucchantī | duhitā | divaḥ | apo iti | mahi | vyayati | cakṣase | tamaḥ | jyotiḥ | kṛṇoti | sūnarī || 1 ||
^āyatī āgacchantī vyucchantī tamāṃsi vivāsayantī varjayantī ^divaḥ dyulokasya sūryasya vā ^duhitā putrī evaṃbhūtoṣāḥ ^prati ^adarśisarvaiḥ pratidṛśyate |
^u iti pūrakaḥ |
^saiṣā ^mahi mahat ^tamaḥ naiśamandhakāram |
apa u iti nipātadvayasamudāyo'pelastyārthe |
^apo ^vyayati apavṛṇoti |
kimartham |
^cakṣase darśanārtham |
evaṃ kṛtvā ^sūnarī janānāṃ suṣṭhu nenyupāḥ ^jyotiḥ prakāśaṃ ^kṛṇoti karoti ||
udusriyāḥ sṛjate sūryaḥ sacā udyannakṣatramarcivat |
taveduṣo vyuṣi sūryasya ca saṃ bhaktena gamemahi ||
2 ||
ut | usriyāḥ | sṛjate | sūryaḥ | sacā | ut'yat | nakṣatram | arci'vat | tava | ita | uṣaḥ | vi'uṣi | sūryasya | ca | sam | bhaktena | gamemahi || 2 ||
^sūryaḥ sarvasya preraka ādityaḥ ^usriyāḥ raśmīn ^sacā saha yugapadeva ^ut ^sṛjate udgamayati |
tathā ^udyat ucchat prādurbhavat ^nakṣatraṃ nabhasi dṛśyamānaṃ grahanakṣatrādikam ^arcivat dīsimat karoti |
saureṇa tejasā hi naktaṃ candraprabhṛtīni nakṣatrāṇi bhāsante |
‘suputraḥ sūryaśmiścandramā gandharvaḥ’ (vā. saṃ. 18. 40) iti hi nigamaḥ |
evaṃ ca sati he ^uṣaḥ upodevate ^tava ^sūryasya ^ca ^vyuṣi vivāsane prakāśane sati ^bhaktena agnena ^saṃ ^gamemahi vayaṃ saṃgacchemahi |
icchabdaḥ pūrakaḥ ||
prati tvā duhitardiva uṣo jīrā abhutsmahi |
yā vahasi puru spārhaṃ vananvati ralaṃ na dāśuṣe ||
3 ||
prati | tvā | duhitaḥ | divaḥ | uṣaḥ | jīrāḥ | abhutsmahi | yā | vahasi | puru | spārham | vanan'vati | ratnam | na | dāśuṣe || 3 ||
he ^divaḥ ^duhitaḥ divo'ntarikṣājjāyāmāne he ^uṣaḥ upodevate ^tvā tvāṃ ^jīrāḥ kṣiprakāriṇo vayaṃ ^prati ^abhutsmahi pratibodhvāro bhavema |
haṃ ^vananvati |
vananaṃ saṃbhajanaṃ saṃbhaktavyaṃ dhanaṃ vā |
tadvati
^yā tvaṃ ^puru bahu ^spāhaṃ spṛhaṇīyaṃ dhanaṃ ^vahasi prāpayasi ^dāśuṣe havīṃṣi dattavate yajamānāya ^ratnaṃ ^na ramaṇīyaṃ dhanamiva ^mayaḥ sukhaṃ ca thā tvaṃ vahasi tāṃ tvāṃ pratyabhyutsmahītyanvayaḥ || uccantī yā kṛṇoṣi maṃhanā mahi prakhyai devi svarhaśe | tasyāste ratnabhāja īmahe vayaṃ syāma māturna sūnavaḥ || 4 || uccantī | yā | kṛṇoṣi | maṃhanā | mahi | prakhyai | devi | svaḥ | dṛśe | tasyāḥ | te | ratna'bhājaḥ | īmahe | vayam | syāma | mātuḥ | na | sūnavaḥ || 4 || he ^mahi mahati ^devi dānādiguṇayukta upodevate vyucchantī tamāṃsi varjayantī ^maṃhanā mahimnā yuktā | yadvā | maṃhatirdānakarmā | dānayuktā | ^yā tvaṃ ^svaḥ sarvaṃ jagat ^prakhyai prabodhanārthaṃ ^dṛśe darśanāthaṃ ca ^kṛṇoṣi karoṣi ^tasyāḥ tādṛśyāḥ ^ratnabhājaḥ ratnānāṃ ramaṇīyānāṃ dhanānāṃ bhājayitryāḥ bhavanti tadvat || taccitraṃ rādha ā bharoṣo yadīrghaśruttamam | yattai divo duhitarmartyabhojanaṃ tadrāsva bhunajāmahai || 5 || tat | citram | rādhaḥ | ā | bhara | ūrpaḥ | yat | dīrghaśrut'tamam | yat | te | divaḥ | duhitaḥ | martya'bhojanam | tat | rāsva | bhunajāmahai || 5 || he ^uṣaḥ ^taccitraṃ cāyanīyaṃ ^rādhaḥ dhanam ^ā ^bhara āhara | asmabhyaṃ prayaccha | ^yat ^dhanaṃ ^dīrghaśruttamaṃ deśakālayorviprakarṣe'pyatiśayena śrotavyam | api ca he ^divo ^duhitaḥ ^te tava tvayā deyaṃ ^martabhojanaṃ martānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhogayogyaṃ ^yat annamasti ^tat ^rāsva dehyasmabhyam | vayaṃ ca svadattaṃ dhanamannaṃ ca ^bhunajāmahai abhyavaharema || śravaḥ sūribhyo amṛtaṃ vasutvanaṃ vājā~ asmabhyaṃ gormataḥ | codayitrī maghonaḥ sūnṛtāvyuṣā uccedapa sirdhaḥ || 6 || śravaḥ | sūri'bhyaḥ | amṛtam | vasu'tvanam | vājān | asmabhyam | go'mataḥ | codayitrī | maghonaḥ | sūnṛtā'vatī | upāḥ | uccat | arpa | sirdhaḥ || 6 || he uṣaḥ ^sūribhyaḥ stotṛbhyo'smabhyam ^amṛtaṃ maraṇarahitaṃ nityaṃ ^vasutvanaṃ vāsakaṃ vasutvayuktaṃ vā ^śravaḥ śravaṇīyaṃ yaśo rāsvetyanuṣaṅgaḥ | tathā ^gomataḥ bahubhirgobhiryuktān ^vājān annāni ca ^asmabhyaṃ rāsva | śiṣṭaḥ parokṣakṛtaḥ | ^maghonaḥ haviṣmato yajamānasya ^codayitrī prerayitrī ^sūnṛtāvatī | priyasatyātmikā vāksūnṛtā | tadvatī ^uṣāḥ ^sirdhaḥ śoṣakācchatrūn ^apa ^uccat apagamayatu || 1 || ‘indrāvaruṇā’ iti daśarcaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ jāgatam | idamādīni catvāri sūktānīndrāvaruṇadevatāni | tathā cānukrāntam—‘indrāvaruṇā darśandrāvaruṇaṃ ha jāgataṃ tu’ iti | tṛtīye savana ukthye praśastaḥ śastra etatsūktam | sūtritaṃ ca—‘indrāvaruṇā yuvamā vāṃ rājānau’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 1) iti || 1. jña-ta1-tat|
indrāvaruṇā yuvama̭dhvarāya no viśe janā̭ya mahi śarma̭ yacchatam | dīrghapra̭yajjumati yo va̭nuṣyati va॒yaṃ ja̭yema pṛtanāsu dū॒rdhaḥ || 1 || indrāvaruṇā | yuvam | adhvarāya | naḥ | viśe | janāya | mahi | śarma | yacchatam | dīrgha'prayajjum | ati | yaḥ | vanuṣyati | vayam | jayema | pṛtanāsu | duḥ'dhaḥ || 1 || he vindrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau vyuvaṃ yuvāṃ vnaḥ asmākaṃ vviśe niveśayitre paricārakāya vjanāya putrapautrādilakṣaṇāya vadhvarāya yajñānuṣṭhānārthaṃ vmahi mahat vśarma gṛhaṃ sukhaṃ vā vyacchataṃ prayacchatam | api ca vdīrghaprayajjuṃ dīrghapratatayajñasmadīyaṃ janaṃ vyaḥ śatruḥ vati vvanuṣyati atijighāṃsati vpṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu vdūdhayaḥ durdhiyo duṣṭābhisaṃdhistānchatrūn vvayaṃ vjayema abhibhavema ||
samrā̭ḻanyaḥ svarā̭ḻanya ucyate vāṃ ma॒hantā॒vindrā̭varuṇā ma॒hāva̭sū | viśvḙ de॒vāsa̭ḥ pa॒rame॒ vyoma̭ni saṃ vā॒mojo̭ vṛṣaṇā॒ saṃ bala̭ṃ dadhuḥ || 2 || sam'rāṭ | anyaḥ | sva'rāṭ | anyaḥ | ucyate | vām | mahāntau | indrāvaruṇā | mahā'vasū iti mahā'vasū | viśve | devāsaḥ | parame | vi'omani | sam | vām | ojaḥ | vṛṣaṇā | sam | balam | dadhuḥ ||2|| dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ parokṣakṛtaḥ śiṣṭāḥ pratyakṣakṛtāḥ | he indrāvaruṇau vvāṃ yuvayormadhye vanyaḥ eko varuṇaḥ vsamrāṭ samyagrājamāna iti vucyate | ‘tā samrājā ghṛtāsutī’ (ṛ. saṃ. 2. 41. 6) ityādiṣu kīrtyate | vanyaḥ indraḥ vsvarāṭ svayamevānyanirapekṣayaiva rājamāna ityucyate | ‘svarā- ścindro dame’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 61. 9) ityādiṣu kīrtyate | tathāvidhau vindrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau vmahāntau guṇairadhikau vmahāvasū mahādhanau ca bhavataḥ | he vvṛṣaṇā kāmānāṃ varṣitārāvindrāvaruṇau vvāṃ yuvāṃ vparame vyomani utkṛṣṭe ākāśe vviśve vdevāsaḥ sarve devāḥ vojaḥ vsaṃ vdadhuḥ samayojanam śarīradāḍharyāya | tadetadbhūtaṃ tadoja ityucyate | smaryate ca—‘ojo nāmāṣṭamī daśā’ iti | tathā vbalaṃ vṛtravadhādeḥ kāryasya hetubhūtaṃ sāmarthyaṃ ca yuvābhyāṃ vsaṃ dadhuḥ samayojanam ||
anvā̭ pāṃ khānya̭tṛṇo॒jasā॒ sūrya̭mairayataṃ di॒vi pra॒bhum | indrā̭varuṇā ma॒de a̭sya mā॒yino̭'rpi॒nvatama॒piḥ pi॒nvataṃ dhiya̭ḥ || 3 || anu | apām | khāni | atṛṇat | ojasā | ā | sūryam | airayatam | divi | pra'bhum | indrāvaruṇā | made | asya | māyinaḥ | arpinavatam | apitaḥ | pinvatam | dhiyaḥ || 3 || he indrāvaruṇau vapām udakānāṃ vkhāni dvārāṇi vṛtreṇa pihitāni vojasā balena vanu vatṛṇat anvavidhyatam | āvarakasya vṛtrasya vadhena vṛṣṭipratibandhaṃ nirākṛtavantā vityarthaḥ | tathā vsūrya sarvasya prerakādityaṃ vdivi antarikṣe vprabhuṃ prabhūtaṃ santama vā vairayatam abhyagamayatam | svabhānunāvṛtaṃ sūryaṃ tadvadhena prakāśitavantā vityarthaḥ | he vindrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau vmāyinaḥ prajñākarasya vasya somasya pānena vmade harṣe sati vapitaḥ jalarahitā nadīḥ vapinvataṃ jalenāpūrayatam | tathā ca niruktapadmāmnāyate—‘asya made jaritarindro'jinavadajubo'pinadapitaḥ’ (śā. śrau. 8. 25. 1) iti | api ca vdhiyaḥ karmāṇyāsmābhiranuṣṭhitāni karmāṇi vpinvataṃ siddhyataṃ phalena pūrayatam ||
yuvāmidyutsu pṛtanāsu vahvayo yuvāṃ kṣemasya prasave mitajñvaḥ | īśānā vasvaṃ ubhayasya kārava indrāvaruṇā suhavā havāmahe || 4 || yuvām | it | yut'su | pṛtanāsu | vahvayaḥ | yuvām | kṣemasya | pra'save | mita'jñvaḥ | īśānā | vasvaḥ | ubhayasya | kāravaḥ | indrāvaruṇā | su'havā | havāmahe || 4 ||
he indrāvaruṇau ᵥvahvayaḥ haviṣāṃ stotrāṇāṃ vā voḍhāra ṛtvijaḥ ᵥyutsu yuddheṣu ᵥpṛtanāsu śatrusenāsu rakṣaṇāthaṃ ᵥyuvāmit yuvāmeva havante āhvayanti |
ᵥmitajñvaḥ saṃkucitajānukā aṅgi- raso'pi ᵥkṣemasya rakṣaṇasya ᵥprasave utpādane nimittabhūte sati ᵥyuvām eva havante |
ataḥ kāraṇāt he indrāvaruṇau ᵥkāravaḥ stotāro vayamapi ᵥubhayasya divyasya pārthivasya ca ᵥvasvaḥ vasuno dhanasya ᵥīśānā īśvarau ᵥsuhavā sukhena hrātavyau yuvāmeva ᵥhavāmahe āhvayāmahe ||
indrāvaruṇā yadimāni cakrathurviśvā jātāni bhuvanasya majmanā | kṣemeṇa mitro varuṇaṃ duvasyati murudbhirugraḥ śubhamanya īyate || 5 || indrāvaruṇā | yat | imāni | cakrathuḥ | viśvā | jātāni | bhuvanasya | majmanā | kṣemeṇa | mitraḥ | varuṇam | duvasyati | murut'bhiḥ | ugraḥ | śubham | anyaḥ | īyate || 5 ||
he indrāvaruṇau ᵥyat yau yuvāṃ ᵥbhuvanasya lokasya sambandhīni ᵥimāni paridṛśyamānāni ᵥviśvā sarvāṇi ᵥjātāni janminti bhūtajātāni ᵥmajmanā ātmīyena balena ᵥcakrathuḥ kṛtavantau tayoryavayormadhya ekaṃ ᵥvaruṇaṃ ᵥkṣemeṇa rakṣaṇahetunā ᵥmitraḥ devaḥ ᵥduvasyati paricarati |
mitrā- varuṇau hi parasparaṃ prāptasakhyau |
ata eva sahacarau dṛśyate |
ᵥanyaḥ eka indraḥ ᵥmarudbhiḥ marudgaṇaiḥ ᵥugraḥ udbhaṇabalaḥ san ᵥśubhaṃ śobhanamalaṃkāram ᵥīyate prāpnoti |
yadvā |
marudbhirmadhyasthānedevagaṇaiḥ sārthamugra ojasvīndraḥ śubhamudakamīyate prerayati ||
2 ||
muhe śulkāya varuṇasya nu tviṣa ojau mimāte dhruvasya yatsvam | ajāmimanyaḥ śrathayantamātiṃrdubhrebhiranyaḥ pra vṛṇoti bhūyasaḥ || 6 || muhe | śulkāya | varuṇasya | nu | tviṣe | ojaḥ | mimāte iti | dhruvam | asya | yat | svam | ajāmim | anyaḥ | śrathayantam | ā | atirat | dubhrebhiḥ | anyaḥ | pra | vṛṇoti | bhūyasaḥ || 6 ||
ᵥvaruṇasya |
upalakṣaṇametat |
indrasya varuṇasya ca ᵥtviṣe dīptyartham ᵥojaḥ balaṃ ᵥnu kṣipraṃ ᵥmimāte stotreṇa nirmimāte yajamānapatnyau |
stotreṇa hi balaṃ jāyate |
kimartham |
ᵥmuhe mahate ᵥśulkāya dhanāya |
īdṛśasya dhanasya lābhārtham |
ᵥasya indrasya varuṇasya ca ᵥdhruvaṃ nityaṃ ᵥsvaṃ svakīya- masādhāraṇaṃ ᵥyat ojaḥ vidyate tadojo mimāta ityanvayaḥ |
tayorindrāvaruṇayoḥ ᵥanyaḥ eko varuṇaḥ ᵥajāmim abandhumastuvantaṃ ᵥbhayayantaṃ hiṃsantaṃ karmāṇi kurvantamstotāramayajamānaṃ ca ᵥātirat abhihanti |
ᵥanyaḥ eka indraḥ ᵥdubhrebhiḥ alpairaivopāthaiḥ ᵥbhūyasaḥ bahutarānchatrūn ᵥpra ᵥvṛṇoti prakarṣeṇāvṛtān bādhitān karoti |
yadvā |
bhūyaso yajamānān pra vṛṇoti pravarānutkṛṣṭān karoti ||
na tamaṃho na duritāni martyamindrāvaruṇā na tapaḥ kurtaścana |
yasya devā gacchatho vītho arvvaraṃ na taṃ martyasya naśate parihṛtiḥ ||
7 ||
na | tam | aha̭: | na | dṷ:i॒tāni̭ | ma॒rtya̭m | i॒ndrā॒varṷṇā | na | ta॒rpaḥ | ku॒taḥ | ca॒na | yasya̭ | de॒vā | ga॒ccha̭thaḥ | vī॒thaḥ | a॒dhva॒ram | na | tam | ma॒rtya̭sya | na॒śa॒te | pari̭'i॒hṛtiḥ || 7 ||
he indrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau taṃ martyaṃ manuṣyam ahaḥ pāpaṃ na naśate na vyāpnoti |
na ca duritāni durgamanāni pāpaphalāni ca prāpnuvanti |
kutaścana kasmādapi nimittāt tapaḥ saṃtāpaśca na prāpnoti |
he devā devau dānādiguṇayuktāvindrāvaruṇau yasya martyasya manuṣyasya adhvaraṃ yajñaṃ gacchathaḥ prāpnutho yuvāṃ vīthaḥ kāmayethe ca yasya havīṃṣi taṃ manuṣyaṃ parihṛtiḥ paribādhā na naśate uktaprakāreṇa na vyāpnoti ||
a॒rvā॒ṅ‌rā̭ dai॒vyḙnā॒vasā̭ gataṃ śṛṇutaṃ ha॒vaṃ ya॒di mḙ ju॒joṣa̭thaḥ |
yu॒vorhi̭ sa॒khyamu॒ta vā॒ yadā̭pyaṃ mā॒rḍī॒kami̭ndrāvaruṇā ni ya̭cchatam ||
8 ||
arvāṅ | narā | daivyena | avasā | ā | gatam | śṛṇutam | havam | yadi | me | jujoṣathaḥ | yuvoḥ | hi | sakhyam | uta | vā | yat | āpyam | mārḍīkam | indrāvaruṇā | ni | yacchatam || 8 ||
he narā netārāvindrāvaruṇau daivyena devasambandhinā avasā rakṣaṇena saha arvāk asma- dabhimukham ā gatam āgacchatam |
āgatya ca madīyaṃ havaṃ stotraṃ śṛṇutam |
yadi me mama jujoṣathaḥ prīyathe |
mayi prītirhasti cet yuvābhyāmāgantavyaṃ madīyaṃ stotraṃ śrotavyaṃ ca bhavatītyarthaḥ |
yuvorhi yuvayoḥ khalu yat sakhyaṃ sakhitvam uta vā api ca yat āpyam āpitvaṃ bāndhavaṃ mārḍīkaṃ mṛḍīkasya sukhasya sādhanaṃ tadubhayaṃ he indrāvaruṇau ni yacchatam asmabhyaṃ prayacchatam ||
a॒smā॒kami̭ndrāvaruṇā bha॒raibha̭re pu॒ro॒yo॒dhā bha̭vataṃ kṛ॒ṣṭyoja̭sā |
ya॒dvāṃ ha॒vanta॒ ubha̭ye a॒rdha spṛdhi॒ nara̭stokasya॒ tana̭yasya sā॒tiṣṷ ||
9 ||
asmākam | indrāvaruṇā | bharai'bhare | puraḥ'yodhā | bhavatam | kṛṣṭi'ojasā | yat | vām | havante | ubhaye | ardha | spṛdhi | naraḥ | tokasya | tanayasya | sātiṣu || 9 ||
he kṛṣṭyojasā śatrūṇāṃ karṣakamojo balaṃ yayostādṛśau he indrāvaruṇau bharaibhare saṃgrāme saṃgrāme asmākaṃ puroyodhā purastādyoddhārau bhavatam |
yat yau vām yuvām ubhaye ubhayavidhāḥ pūrvakālīnā idānīṃ- tanāśca naraḥ netāraḥ stotāraḥ spṛdhi yuddhe yau yuvāṃ havante |
adha api ca tokasya putrasya tanayasya pautrasya sātiṣu saṃbhajanīyeṣu nimittabhūteṣu ca yau yuvāṃ havante |
tāvasmākaṃ puroyodhā bhavatamityanvayaḥ ||
a॒sme indro̭ varu॒ṇo mi॒tro a̭rya॒mā ghumnaṃ ya̭cchantu ma॒hi śarma̭ sa॒pratha̭ḥ |
a॒va॒dyaṃ jyoti̭radi॒te ṛ॒tāvṛ̭dho de॒vasya̭ ślo॒kaṃ sa̭vi॒turma̭nāmahe ||
10 ||
asme iti | indraḥ | varuṇaḥ | mitraḥ | aryamā | ghumnam | yacchantu | mahi | śarma | saprathaḥ | avadyam | jyotiḥ | aditaḥ | ṛta'vṛdhaḥ | devasya | ślokam | savituḥ | manāmahe || 10 ||
asme asmabhyamindrādayaḥ ghumnaṃ dyotamānaṃ dhanaṃ yacchantu prayacchantu |
tathā mahi mahat saprathaḥ sarvataḥ pṛthu vistīrṇaṃ śarma gṛhaṃ ca prayacchantu |
api ca ṛtāvṛdhaḥ ṛtasya yajñasya vardhayitavyāḥ 1. śara-matasya |
^aditeḥ adīnāyā devamātuḥ ^jyotiḥ tejaśca no'smākam ^avabhram ahiṃsakamastu |
vayaṃ ca ^devasya dānādiguṇayuktasya ^savituḥ sarvasya prerakasya ^ślokaṃ stotraṃ ^manāmahe jānīmaḥ |
kurma ityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
devena savitrāsmabhyaṃ deyaṃ ślokaṃ yaśo manāmahe yācāmahe ||
3 ||
'yuvāṃ narā' iti daśarcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ jāgatamaindrāvaruṇam |
'yuvāṃ narā' ityanukrāntam |
ābhivivikepruktheyeṣu stomavṛddhau praśāsturidaṃ sūktamāvāpārtham |
sūtritaṃ ca—'yuvāṃ narā punīye vām' (āśva. śrau. 7. 9) iti ||
yuvāṃ narā paśyamānāḥ āpyaṃ prācā guyantaḥ pṛthuparśivo yayuḥ |
dāsā ca vṛtrā hatamāryāṇi ca sudāsaṃmindrāvaruṇāvasāvatam ||
1
he indrāvaruṇau vbhūmyā: vantā: paryantā: vdhvasirā: sainikairdhvastā: vsam vadakṣata saṃdahyante |
tathā vdivi dyuloke vghoṣa: sainikānāṃ śabdaśca vāruhat ārūḍho'bhūt |
vjanānām asmadīyānāṃ bhaṭānām varātaya: śatrava: vmām vupa vasthu: upasthitā: |
evaṃ pravartamāne'smin yuddhe he vhavanaśrutā āhvānaśīlāvindrāvaruṇau varvāk asmadabhimukham vavasā rakṣaṇena saha vā vgatam āgacchatam ||
indrāvaruṇā vṛdhanābhiraprati bhedaṃ vanvantā pra sudāsamāvatam |
brahāṇyeṣāṃ śṛṇutaṃ havīmani satyā tṛtsūnāmabhavatpurohitaḥ ||
4 ||
indrāvaruṇā | vṛdhanābhi: | aprati | bhedam | vanvantā | pra | sudāsam | āvatam | brahāṇi | eṣām | śṛṇutam | havīmani | satyā | tṛtsūnām | abhavat | pura:'hita: || 4 ||
he vindrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau vvṛdhanābhi: vardhakarairāyudhairapratigatamaprāptaṃ vbhedam etatsaṃjaṃ sudāsa: śatruṃ vvanvantā hiṃsantau yuvāṃ vsudāsam |
śobhanaṃ dadātīti sudā: |
etatsaṃjaṃ mama yājyaṃ rājānaṃ vpra vāvataṃ prakarṣeṇārakṣatam |
veṣāṃ tṛtsūnāṃ mama yājyānāṃ vbrahāṇi stotrāṇi vśṛṇutam aśṛṇu- tam |
kadā |
vhavīmani |
āhūyante'smin yuddhārthaṃ parasparamiti havīmā saṃgrāma: |
tasmin |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāt vtṛtsūnām etatsaṃjyānāṃ mama yājyānāṃ vpurohita: mama purodhānaṃ vsatyā satya- phalam vabhavat |
teṣu yanmama paurohityaṃ tat saphalaṃ jātamityartha: ||
indrāvaruṇāvabhyā taṃpanti māghānyaryo vṛnuṣāmarātayaḥ |
yuvaṃ hi vastva ubhayasya rājatho'dha sma no'vataṃ pāryye divi ||
5 ||
indrāvaruṇau | abhi | ā | taṃpanti | mā | aghāni | arya: | vṛnuṣām | arātaya: | yuvam | hi | vastva: | ubhayasya | rājatha: | ardha | sma | na: | avatam | pāryye | divi || 5 ||
he vindrāvaruṇau varya: are: śatro: sambandhīni vaghāni āhantṛṇyāyudhāni vmā mām vabhyā vtapanti abhito bādhante |
api ca vvanuṣāṃ hiṃsakānāṃ madhye varātaya: abhigamanaśīlā: śatravaśca māmabhitapanti |
vyuvaṃ vhi yuvāṃ khalu vubhayasya pārthivasya divyasya vvastra: vasuno dhanasya vrājatha: īśāthe |
rājatireśvaryakarmā |
vadha vsma ataḥ kāraṇāt vpāryye taraṇīye vdivi divase yuddhadine vna: asmān vavataṃ rakṣatam ||
4 ||
yuvāṃ havanta ubhayāsa ājiṣvindraṃ ca vastvaṃ varuṇaṃ ca sātaye |
yatra rājabhirdaśabhinirbādhitaṃ pra sudāsamāvataṃ tṛtsubhiḥ saha ||
6 ||
yuvām | havante | ubhayāsa: | ājiṣu | indram | ca | vastva: | varuṇam | ca | sātaye | yatra | rājabhi: | daśabhi: | ni:'bādhitam | pra | sudāsam | āvatam | tṛtsubhi: | saha || 6 ||
vubhayāsa: ubhayavidhā: sudā:saṃjño rājā tatsahāyabhūtāstṛtsuścaiva dvikārā janā: vājiṣu saṃgrāmeṣu vindraṃ vca vvaruṇaṃ vca vyuvāṃ vhavante āhūyante |
kimartham |
vvastva: dhanasya vsātaye saṃbhajanārtham |
vyatra yeṣvājiṣu vdaśabhi: daśasaṃkhyākai: vrājabhi: śatrubhūtairnṛpai: vnibādhitaṃ nitarāṃ hiṃsitaṃ vsudāsaṃ vtṛtsubhi: vsaha vartamānaṃ vpra vāvataṃ yuvāṃ prakarṣeṇārakṣatam |
teṣvājiṣvityanvaya: ||
1. kha-na-bha-vartamāne |

stotraṃ manāmahe kurmahe || ||5|| ‘ā vām’ iti pañcarcaṃ caturdaśaṃ sūktaṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindrāvaruṇam | anukrāntaṃ ca— ‘ā vāṃ pañca’ iti | ukthye tṛtīyasavane maitrāvaruṇaśastra idaṃ sūktam | sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā vāṃ rājānāvindrāvaruṇā madhumattamasyeti yājyā’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 1) iti || ā vā~ rājānāvadhvare vaṃvṛtyāṃ havyobhirindrāvaruṇā namaubhiḥ | pra vā~ ghṛtācīṃ bāhvordadhānā pari tmanā vipa~rūpā jigāti || 1 || ā | vām | rājānau | adhvare | vatryām | havyebhiḥ | indrāvaruṇā | namaḥ'bhiḥ | pra | vām | ghṛtācīm | bāhvoḥ | dadhānā | pari | tmanā | vipa~'rūpā | jigāti || 1 || he rājānau rājamānāvīśvaro vendrāvaruṇau adhvare hisārahite'smin yāge vāṃ yuvāṃ havyebhiḥ havirbhiḥ namaubhiḥ stotraiśca ā vavṛtyām āvartayāmi | api ca bāhvoḥ hastayoḥ dadhānā dhāryamāṇā vipa~rūpā | rūpya iti dyauryau setṛbhirrjubhaiḥ : sinīthaḥ | pari no heḻo varuṇasya vṛjyā uruṃ na indraḥ : kṛṇavadu lokam || 2 || yuvoḥ | rāṣṭram | bṛhat | invati | dyauḥ | yau | setu'bhiḥ | aruju'bhiḥ | sinīthaḥ | pari | yuvāṃ setubhiḥ bandhakaiḥ arujubhiḥ rajjurāhitaiḥ rogādibhiḥ sinīthaḥ pāpakṛto badhnīthaḥ | ‘vis bandhane’ iti dhātuḥ | tayormadhye varuṇasya vārayituṃrdevasya heḻaḥ krodhaḥ naḥ asmān pari vṛjyāḥ parivṛṇaktu | parityajyānyatra gacchatu | indraḥ u indraśca uruṃ vistīrṇaṃ lokaṃ sthānaṃ kṛṇavat karotu || kṛtaṃ nau yajñaṃ vidatheṣu cāruṃ kṛtaṃ brahmāṇi sūriṣu praśastā | upau rayirdevajūṃto na etu pra rṇaḥ : spārhābhirūtibhiḥstiretam || 3 || kṛtam | naḥ | yajñam | vidatheṣu | cārum | kṛtam | brahmāṇi | sūriṣu | pra'śastā | upo iti | rayiḥ | deva'jūtaḥ | naḥ | etu | pra | naḥ | spārhābhiḥ | ūti'bhiḥ | tiretam ||3|| 1. ta-na-ma-śa- 'dhanaṃ' nāsti | 2. kha-na-bha-vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ |
he indrāvaruṇau ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥvidayeṣu gṛheṣu kriyamāṇaṃ ᵥyajñaṃ ᵥcāhaṃ śobhanaṃ' phalasahitaṃ' ᵥkṛtaṃ kurutam |
tathā ᵥsuriṣu stotṛvasmāsu vidyamānāni ᵥbrahmāṇi parivṛḍhāni stotrāṇi ᵥpraśastā praśastānyutkṛṣṭāni phalabhāñji ᵥkṛtaṃ kurutam |
api ca ᵥdevajūtaḥ devābhyāṃ yuvābhyāṃ preritaḥ ᵥrathiḥ dhanaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥupaitu prāpnotu |
tathā ᵥsvāhābhiḥ spṛhaṇīyābhiḥ ᵥūtibhiḥ rakṣābhiḥ ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥpra ᵥtiretaṃ yuvāṃ vardhayethām |
prapūrvistaratirvardhanārthaḥ ||
asme indrāvaruṇā viśvavāraṃ rayiṃ dhattaṃ vasuṃmantaṃ purukṣum |
pra ya ādityo anṛtā minātyarmitā śūrau dayate vasūni ||
4 ||
asme iti | indrāvaruṇā | viśva'vāram | rayim | dhattam | vasu'mantam | puru'kṣum | pra | yaḥ | ādityaḥ | anṛtā | mināti | armitā | śūrau | dayate | vasūni || 4 ||
he indrāvaruṇau ᵥasme asmabhyaṃ ᵥrathiṃ dhanaṃ ᵥdhattaṃ prayacchatam |
kīdṛśam |
ᵥviśvavāraṃ viśvaiḥ sarvairvareṇīyaṃ saṃbhajanīyaṃ ᵥvasuṃmantaṃ nivāsayuktaṃ ᵥpurukṣuṃ bahulaṃ purubhirbahubhiḥ praśasyaṃ vā |
ᵥādityaḥ aditeḥ putraḥ ᵥyaḥ varuṇaḥ ᵥanṛtā anṛttāni satyarahitāni bhūtāni ᵥpra ᵥmināti prahinasti |
'mīñ hiṃsāyām' iti dhātuḥ |
ᵥśūraḥ śauryavān sa varuṇaḥ ᵥamitā amitānyaparimitāni ᵥvasūni dhanāni ᵥdayate stotṛbhyo dadāti ||
iyamindraṃ varuṇamaṣṭa me gīḥ prāvattoke tanaye tūtajānā |
suralāso devavītiṃ gamema yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
5 ||
iyam | indram | varuṇam | aṣṭa | me | gīḥ | pra | āvat | toke | tanaye | tūtajānā | su'ratnāsaḥ | deva'vītim | gamema | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 5 ||
ᵥme madīyā ᵥiyaṃ ᵥgīḥ stutiḥ ᵥindraṃ varuṇaṃ ca ᵥaṣṭa abhyutāṃ vyāpnotu |
tathā ᵥtūtajānā mayā preryamāṇā sā ᵥtoke putre ᵥtanaye pautre ca viṣaye ᵥprāvat prakṣatvasmān |
vayaṃ ca ᵥsuratnāsaḥ śobhanadhanāḥ santaḥ ᵥdevavītiṃ devaiḥ kāmayitavyaṃ yajñaṃ ᵥgamema prāpnuyāma |
he indrāvaruṇādayaḥ sarve devāḥ ᵥyūyaṃ ᵥsvastibhiḥ kalyāṇaiḥ ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥsadā sarvadā ᵥpāta rakṣata ||
6 ||
'punīṣe vām' iti pañcadaśaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindrāvaruṇam |
'punīṣe' ityanukrāntam |
ābhilavikeṣūpadheyeṣu tṛtīyasavane stomapṛṣṭhau praśāsturidamāvāpārtham |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'yuvāṃ narā punīṣe vām' (āśva. śrau. 7. 9) iti ||
punīṣe vāmarakṣasaṃ manīṣāṃ somamindrāya varuṇāya juhvat |
ghṛtapratīkāmuṣasaṃ na devīṃ tā no yāmaṃruṣyatāmabhīkeṃ ||
1 ||
punīṣe | vām | arakṣasam | manīṣām | somam | indrāya | varuṇāya | juhvat | ghṛta'pratīkām | uṣasam | na | devīm | tā | naḥ | yāman | uruṣyatām | abhīke || 1 ||
he indrāvaruṇau ᵥvāṃ yuvābhyāṃ yuvayortham ᵥarakṣasaṃ rakṣorahitāṃ rākṣasairasaṃsṛṣṭāṃ ᵥmanīṣāṃ stutiṃ ᵥpunīṣe śodhayāmi |
kiṃ kurvan |

somamabhau prakṣipan |
kīdṛśīṃ manīṣām |

śiṣṭaḥ' parokṣakṛtaḥ |
tā tāvindrāvaruṇau abhīke abhigate yuddhe dhāman dhāmani yuddhārthaṃ gamane sati naḥ asmān uruṣyatāṃ rakṣatām ||
spardhante vā u devahūye atra yeṣu dhvajeṣu didyavaḥ patanti |
yuvaṃ tā~ indrāvaruṇāvamitrānhataṃ parācaḥ śarvā viṣūcaḥ ||
2 ||

devahūye |

hasvaḥ | yaḥ namasvān namasā namaskāreṇa stotreṇa vā yuktaḥ san śavasā balena yuktau vāṃ yuvāṃ paricaratīti śeṣaḥ | sa sukṛturityanvayaḥ | api ca yaḥ haviṣmān havirbhiryuktaḥ san avase tarpaṇārthaṃ uteti vicikitsāyām | uta kiṃ svayā tanvā svīyenātmīyena śarīreṇa saṃ vande sahavadanaṃ karomi | āho svit tat tena varuṇena saha saṃvade | kadā nu kadā khalu varuṇe deve antaḥ bhuvāni antarbhūto kena hetunā ᐁjuṣeta seveta|
ᐁsumanaḥ śobhanamanaskaḥ sannahaṃ ᐁkadā kasmin kāle ᐁmṛḻīkaṃ sukhayitāraṃ varuṇam ᐁabhi ᐁhavyam abhipayeyam ||
pṛcche tadenoṃ varuṇa didṛkṣupo emi cikituṣo vipṛccham |

3 ||

he varuṇa ᐁtadenaḥ pāpaṃ ᐁpṛcche tvāṃ pṛcchāmi |
ᐁdidṛkṣuḥ |
chāndasaḥ sulopaḥ |
draṣṭumicchannaham |

ahaṃ ᐁvipṛcchaṃ vividhaṃ praṣṭuṃ ᐁcikituṣaḥ viduṣo janān ᐁupo ᐁebhi upāgām |
te ᐁkavayaścit krāntadarśino janāśca ᐁme mahyaṃ ᐁsamānamit samānamevaikapameva ᐁāhuḥ akathayan |
yadāhastadāha |
he stotaḥ ᐁtubhyam ᐁayaṃ ᐁha ayameva ᐁvaruṇaḥ ᐁhaṇīte kṛṣyatīti |
ataḥ krodhaṃ parityajyāsmān pāśebhyo mocaya ||
kimāgaṃ āsa varuṇa jyeṣṭhaṃ yatstotāraṃ jighāṃsasi sakhāyam |
pra tanme voco dūlabha svadhāvo'rva tānenā namasā tura īyām ||

kim | āgaḥ | āsa | varuṇa | jyeṣṭham | yat | stotāram | jighāṃsasi | sakhāyam | pra | tat | me | vocaḥ | duḥ'dabha | svadhāvaḥ | arva | tvā | anenāḥ | namasā | turaḥ | īyām || 4 ||
he varuṇa ᐁjyeṣṭham adhikaṃ ᐁkimāgaṃ ᐁāsa ko''parādho mayā kṛto babhūva |
ᐁyat yena āgasā ᐁsakhāyaṃ mitrabhūtaṃ santaṃ ᐁstotāraṃ ᐁjighāṃsasi hantumicchasi |
he ᐁdūlabha durbhānyārbādhituma- śakya ᐁsvadhāvaḥ tejasvin he varuṇa ᐁtat āgaḥ ᐁme mahyaṃ ᐁpra ᐁvocaḥ prabrūhi |
evaṃ sati tasya prāyaścittaṃ kṛtvā ᐁanenāḥ apāpaḥ sannahaṃ ᐁturaḥ tvaramāṇaḥ śīghraḥ ᐁnamasā namaskāreṇa haviṣā vā tvām ᐁava ᐁhyām upagaccheyam ||
arve dugdhāni pitryā sṛjā no'va yā vayaṃ cakrumā tanūbhiḥ |
arva rājanapaśutṛpaṃ na tāyuṃ sṛjā vatsaṃ na dāmno vasiṣṭham ||

arve | dugdhāni | pitryā | sṛja | naḥ | arve | yā | vayam | cakruma | tanūbhiḥ | arva | rājan | paśu'tṛpam | na | tāyum | sṛja | vatsam | na | dāmnaḥ | vasiṣṭham || 5 ||
he varuṇa ᐁpitryā pitṛtaḥ prāptāni ᐁnaḥ asmadīyāni ᐁdugdhāni drohān bandhanahetubhūtān ᐁava ᐁsṛja vimuñca |
asmatto viśleṣaṇyaḥ |
ᐁvayaṃ ca ᐁyā yāni drohajātāni ᐁtanūbhiḥ śarīraiḥ ᐁcakruma kṛtavantaḥ sma tāni ca ᐁava sṛja |
he ᐁrājan rājamāna varuṇa ᐁpaśutṛpaṃ ᐁna ᐁtāyuṃ stenya- prāyaścittaṃ kṛtvāvasāne ghāsādibhiḥ paśūnāṃ tarpayitāraṃ stenamiva ᐁdāmnnaḥ rajjoḥ ᐁvatsaṃ ᐁna vatsamiba ca ᐁvasiṣṭhaṃ māṃ bandhakāt pāpāt ᐁava ᐁsṛja vimuñca ||
1. kha-kṣa-ta-na-bha-śa-āga | 2. kha-pitryāṇi; bha-pitryā pitryāṇi | 3. kha-hetūn | 4. kha-kṣa-ta-na-bha-śa-viśleṣaṇya ca |

na | saḥ | svaḥ | dakṣaḥ | varuṇa | dhṛtiḥ | sā | surā | manyuḥ | vibhīdakaḥ | acittiḥ | asti | jyāyān | kanīyanaḥ | upa'are | svarmaḥ | cana | ita | anṛtasya | pra'yotā || 6 ||
he varuṇa vaḥ svo dakṣaḥ puruṣasya svabhūtaṃ tadbalaṃ pāpapravṛttau kāraṇaṃ na bhavati |
kiṃ tarhi dhṛtiḥ sthirā utpattisamaya eva nirmitā daivagatiḥ kāraṇam |
'dhu gatisthairyayoḥ' iti dhātuḥ |
sā ca dhṛtirvakṣyamāṇarūpā |
surā pramādakāriṇī manyuḥ krodhaśca gurvādiviṣayaḥ sañjanahetuḥ |
vibhīdakaḥ dyūtasādhano'kṣaḥ |
sa ca dyūteṣu puruṣaṃ prerayananarthaheturbhavati |
acittiḥ ajñānamaviveka- kāraṇam |
ata īdṛśī daivatsireva puruṣasya pāpapravṛttau kāraṇam |
api ca kanīyasaḥ alpasya hīnasya puruṣasya pāpapravṛttau upāre upāgate samīpe niyantṛtvena sthitaḥ jyāyān adhika īśvaraḥ asti |
sa eva taṃ pāpe pravartayati |
tathā cāmnātam—'eṣa hyevāsādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamagho ninīṣate' (kau. u. 3.8) iti |
evaṃ ca sati svapraśna svamo'pi anṛtasya pāpasya prayotā prakarṣeṇa miśrayitā bhavati |
ita iti pūrakaḥ |
svame kṛtairapi karmabhirbahūni pāpāni jāyante kimu vaktavyaṃ jāgrati kṛtaiḥ karmabhiḥ pāpānyutpadyanta iti |
ato mamāparādho daivāgata iti he varuṇa tvayā kṣantavya iti bhāvaḥ ||
araṃ dāso na mīḻahuṣe karaṇyahaṃ devāya bhūryadenāgāḥ |
acetayadacito devo aryo gṛtsaṃ rāye kavitaro junāti ||
7 ||
aram | dāsaḥ | na | mīḻahuṣe | karāṇi | aham | devāya | bhūryade | anāgāḥ | acetayat | acitaḥ | devaḥ | aryaḥ | gṛtsam | rāye | kavi'taraḥ | junāti || 7 ||
mīḻahuṣe sektre kāmānāṃ varṣitre bhūryade jagato bhartre devāya dānādiguṇayuktāya varuṇāya anāgāḥ tatprasādātpāpaḥ san aham aram alaṃ paryāptaṃ karāṇi paricaraṇaṃ karavāṇi |
dāso na yathā bhṛtyaḥ svāmine samyak paricarati tadvat |
aryaḥ svāmī sa ca devaḥ acitaḥ ajānato'smān acetayat cetayatu prajñāpayatu |
gṛtsaṃ stotāraṃ ca kavitaraḥ prājñataro devaḥ rāye dhanāya dhanaprāptyarthaṃ junāti junātu prerayatu ||
ekādaśine vāruṇe paśau 'ayam' iti puroḍāśasyānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'ayaṃ su tubhyaṃ varuṇa svabhāva evā vandasva varuṇa bṛhantam' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
ayaṃ su tubhyaṃ varuṇa svadhāvo hṛdi stoma upaśritaścidastu |
śaṃ naḥ kṣeme śamu yoge no astu yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
8 ||
ayam | su | tubhyam | varuṇa | svadhāvaḥ | hṛdi | stomaḥ | upa'śritaḥ | cit | astu | śam | naḥ | kṣeme | śam | ūṃ iti | yoge | naḥ | astu | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ ||8||
he svadhāvaḥ annavan varuṇa tubhyaṃ tvadarthaṃ kriyamāṇaḥ ayam etatsūktātmakaṃ stomaḥ stotraṃ hṛdi tvadīye hṛdaye su suṣṭhu upaśritaḥ upagataḥ samavetaḥ astu |
cit iti pūrakaḥ |
śaṃ naḥ kṣeme śam ūṃ iti |
yoge naḥ astu yūyam pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
1. i-sa-tara-4-devagatiḥ | 2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-pramodakāriṇī | 3. gha-ta3-śa-kṣantavyaṃ | 4. kha-bha-tsūktātmakaḥ |
aprāsasya prāpaṇaṃ yogaḥ prāsasya rakṣaṇaṃ kṣemaḥ |
॰naḥ asmadīye ॰kṣeme rakṣaṇe ॰jham upadravāṇāṃ śamanam ॰astu |
॰yoge ca ॰naḥ asmadīye prāpaṇe ॰jhamu śamanamevāstūpadravāṇām |
he varuṇadyo devāḥ ॰naḥ asmān sarvadā ॰svastibhiḥ avināśaiḥ ॰pāta rakṣata ||

‘radatpathaḥ’ iti saptarcaṃ saptadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vāruṇam |
anukrāntaṃ ca—‘radatpasa’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
radatpatho varuṇaḥ sūryāya arṇaॉṃsi sudriyāṃ nadīnām | sargo na sṛṣṭo arvetīrṛtāyaścakāra mahīravanīrahamyaḥ || 1 || radat | pathaḥ | varuṇaḥ | sūryāya | pra | arṇaॉṃsi | sudriyāṃ | nadīnām | sargaḥ | na | sṛṣṭaḥ | arvetīḥ | ṛta'yan | cakāra | mahīḥ | avanīḥ | aha'bhyaḥ || 1 ||

sūtritaṃ ca- 'ava sindhuṃ varuṇo dhauriva sthāpayat su tumyaṃ varuṇa svabhāvaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 3.7) iti ||
ava sindhuṃ varuṇo dhauriva sthāpso na śveto mṛgasturviṣmān |
gambhīraśa~so rajaso vimānaḥ supārakṣatraḥ suto asya rājā ||

ava | sindhum | varuṇaḥ | dhauḥ'iva | sthāt | drapsaḥ | na | śvetaḥ | mṛgaḥ | turviṣmān | gambhīra'śa~saḥ | rajasaḥ | vimānaḥ | supāra'kṣatraḥ | sutaḥ | asya | rājā || 6 ||
dhauriva sūrya iva dīptaḥ varuṇaḥ sindhuṃ samudram ava sthāt velāyāmavasthāpayati |
yathā velāṃ nātikrāmati tathā karotītyarthaḥ |
kīdṛśo varuṇaḥ |
drapso na dravaṇaśīla udabinduriva śvetaḥ śubhravarṇaḥ mṛgaḥ |
lusopametat |
gauramṛga iva tuviṣmān balavān pravṛddho vā gambhīraśa~saḥ |
gambhīro mahān śaṃsaḥ stotraṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
rajasaḥ udakasya vimānaḥ nirmātā supārakṣatraḥ suṣṭhu duḥkhatvāt pārakaṃ kṣatraṃ balaṃ dhanaṃ vā yasya tādṛśo varuṇaḥ sataḥ vidyamānasya asya jagataḥ rājā īśvaro bhavati ||
yo mṛḻayāti cakruṣe cidāgau vayaṃ syāma varuṇe anāgāḥ |
anu vratānyaditeṛdhantau yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
7 ||
yaḥ | mṛḻayāti | cakruṣe | cit | āgaḥ | vayam | syāma | varuṇe | anāgāḥ | anu | vratāni | aditeḥ | ṛdhantaḥ | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 7 ||
āgaḥ aparādhaṃ cakruṣe cit kṛtavate'pi stotre yaḥ varuṇaḥ mṛḻayāti upadayāṃ karoti tasmin varuṇe anāgāḥ anāgaso'naparādhāḥ santaḥ vayaṃ syāma vartamānā bhavema |
kiṃ kurvantaḥ |
aditeḥ adīnasya varuṇasya saṃbandhīni vratāni karmāṇi anu ānupūrvyeṇa ṛdhantaḥ samarthayantaḥ |
he varuṇā- dyo devā yūyamasmān sarvadā kalyāṇaiḥ pālayata ||
9 ||
'pra śundhyuvam' iti saptamamaṣṭādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vāruṇam |
'pra śundhyuvam' ityanukrāntam |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
pra śundhyuvaṃ varuṇāya praiṣṭāṃ matiṃ vasiṣṭha mīḻahuṣe bharasva |
ya īṃrvāñcaṃ karate yajatraṃ sahasrādhaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ bṛhantam ||
1 ||
pra | śundhyuvam | varuṇāya | praiṣṭām | matim | vasiṣṭha | mīḻahuṣe | bharasva | yaḥ | īm | arvāñcam | karate | yajatram | sahasra'dham | vṛṣaṇam | bṛhantam || 1 ||
ṛṣirātmānameva pratyakṣīkṛtya stutau niyuṅkte |
he vasiṣṭha tvaṃ mīḻahuṣe sektre varuṇāya śundhyuvaṃ śodhayitrīṃ yadvā svata eva śuddhāṃ praiṣṭāṃ priyatamāṃ matiṃ mananīyāmīdṛśīṃ stutiṃ pra bharasva prahara prāpaya |
yaḥ varuṇaḥ īm enaṃ sūryam arvāñcam asmadabhimukhaṃ karate antarikṣe karoti tasmai varuṇāye- tyanvayaḥ |
kīdṛśaṃ sūryam |
yajatraṃ yaṣṭavyaṃ sahasrādhaṃ bahudhanaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣakaṃ bṛhantaṃ mahāntam ||
adhā nvasya saṃdṛśaṃ jaganvānmeranīkaṃ varuṇasya maṃsi |
svaryadaśamadhipā u andho'bhi mā vardhuryai ninīyāt ||
2 ||
ardha | nu | asya | samu'darśam | jaganvān | agneḥ | anīkam | varuṇasya | maṃsti | sveḥ | yat | aśman | adhi'pāḥ | oṃ iti | andhaḥ | abhi | mā | vapuḥ | dṛśyai | niniyāt ||2||
paatha adhunā paasya pavaruṇasya pasaṃdṛśaṃ saṃdarśanaṃ panu kṣipraṃ pajaganvān gatavānaham paagreḥ paanīkaṃ jvālāsaṃghaṃ pamaṃsi stavāni |

abhiṣutabhityarthaḥ |

adhi yadapāṃ stubhiścarāva pra preha īhyāvahai śubhe kam ||

ā | yat | ruhāva | varuṇaḥ | ca | nāvam | pra | yat | samudram | īrayāva | madhyam | adhi | yat | apām | snu'bhiḥ | carāva | pra | pra'ihve | īhyāvahai | śubhe | kam || 3 ||
payat yadā varuṇe prasanne sati ahaṃ pavaruṇaśca ubhau panāvaṃ dumamayīṃ taraṇasādhanabhūtām paā pahuhāva ubhāvāruḍhāvabhūva |
tāṃ ca nāvaṃ payat yadā pasamudraṃ pamadhyaṃ samudrasya madhyaṃ prati papra paīrayāva prakarṣeṇa gamayāva |
payat yadā ca paapām udakānām paadhi upari pastubhiḥ gantrībhiranyābhirapi naubhiḥ pacarāva vatāṃvahai |
tadānīṃ paśubhe śobhārthaṃ praprehe naupayāṃ dolāyāmeva papra paīhyāvahai |
nimnonnataistairitaraiścaiva pravicalantau saṃkrīḍāvaha |
pakama iti pūrakaḥ |
yadvā kriyāviśeṣaṇam |
kaṃ sukhaṃ yathā bhavata ityetyarthaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaṃ ha varuṇo nāvyādhādṛṣiṃ cakāra svapā mahobhiḥ |
stotāraṃ vipraḥ sudinatve ahnāṃ yānu dhāvastanuyādupārsaḥ ||

vasiṣṭham | ha | varuṇaḥ | nāvi | ā | adhāt | ṛṣim | cakāra | su'apaḥ | mahaḥ'bhiḥ | stotāram | vipraḥ | sudina'tve | ahnām | yāt | nu | dhāvaḥ | tatarnan | yāt | upasaḥ ||4||
evaṃ vasiṣṭhamātmanoke yadvaruṇena kṛtaṃ tadarśayati |
pavasiṣṭhaṃ ha vasiṣṭhaṃ khalu pavaruṇaḥ panāvi svakīyāyām paādhāt ārohayat |
tathā tam paṛṣim paavobhiḥ rakṣaṇaiḥ pasvapāṃ svapasaṃ śobhanakarmāṇaṃ pacakāra varuṇaḥ kṛtavān |

kiṃ kurvan |

he varuṇa ⱽtyāni tāni purātanāni ⱽnau āvayoḥ ⱽsakhyā sakhyāni sakhitvāni ⱽka kutra ⱽbabhuvuḥ|

mo iti̭ | su | va̭ruṇa | mṛ॒ta'ma̭ryam | gṛ॒ham | rā॒jan | a॒ham | ga॒mam | mṛ॒ḻa | su॒'kṣa॒tra | mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 1 || he vrājan īśvara vvaruṇa tvadīyaṃ vmṛnmayaṃ mṛdādibhirnirmitaṃ vgṛhaṃ vmo mā ' maiva vahaṃ vgamaṃ gato'smi | api tu vsu śobhanaṃ suvarṇamayameva tvadīyaṃ gṛhaṃ prāpvāni | sa tvaṃ māṃ vmṛḻa sukhaya | he vsukṣatra śobhanadhana varuṇa vmṛḻaya upadyāṃ ca kuru ||
yade॒mi pra॒sphura̭nniva ha॒tirna ॒dhmāto̭ a॒driva̭: | mṛ॒ḻā su॒kṣa॒tra mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 2 || yat | e॒mi | pra॒sphura̭n'i̭va | ha॒tiḥ | na | dhmā॒taḥ | a॒dri'va̭: | mṛ॒ḻa | su॒'kṣa॒tra | mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 2 || he vadriva: āyudhavan varuṇa vyat yadā vprasphuranniva śaityena pravicalanniva vtvadvayādvepamāna: vhatirna hatiriva vdhmātaḥ vāyunā pūrṇa: saṃstathā baddho'ham vemi gacchāmi tadānīṃ vmṛḻa sukhaya | he vsukṣatra sudhana vmṛḻaya upadyāṃ kuru ||
kra॒tvaḥ sa॒maha̭ dī॒natā̭ pra॒tīpa̭ṃ ja॒gamā̭ śu॒ce | mṛ॒ḻā su॒kṣa॒tra mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 3 || kra॒tvaḥ | sa॒maha̭ | dī॒natā̭ | pra॒ti'ī̭pam | ja॒gam | śu॒ce | mṛ॒ḻa | su॒'kṣa॒tra | mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 3 || he vsamaha sadhana vśuce svabhāvato nirmala varuṇa vdīnatā dīnatayākṛtatayā vkrava: karmaṇa: kartavyatvena vihitasya śrautasmāṃtādilakṣaṇasya vpratīpaṃ pratikūlamanuṣṭhānaṃ vjagama prāptavānasmi | ata eva tvayā baddha: | tādṛśaṃ māṃ vmṛḻa sukhaya | anyadratam ||
a॒pāṃ ma॒dhye ta̭sthi॒vāṃsa̭ṃ tṛ॒ṣṇāvi̭da॒jari̭tāram | mṛ॒ḻā su॒kṣa॒tra mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 4 || a॒pām | ma॒dhye | ta॒sthi॒'vāṃsa̭m | tṛ॒ṣṇā | a॒vi॒da॒t | ja॒ri̭tāra̭m | mṛ॒ḻa | su॒'kṣa॒tra | mṛ॒ḻaya̭ || 4 || vapāṃ samudrāṇāmudakānāṃ vmadhye vtasthidvāṃsaṃ sthitavantamapi vjaritāraṃ tava stotāraṃ māṃ vtṛṣṇā pipāsā vavidat āsavatī | lavaṇotkaṭasya sāmudrajalasya vpānānarhatvāt | atastādṛśaṃ māṃ vmṛḻa sukhaya | anyadratam ||
devasuvāṃ havi:ṣu vāruṇasya haviṣa: 'yatkiṃ cedam' iti yājyā |

yatkiṃ cedaṃ va̭ruṇa dai॒vye ja̭ne॒'bhidro̭haṃ ma॒nuṣyā̭śca॒rāma̭si | aci̭ttī yatta॒va dha॒rmā yu॒yopi̭ma mā na॒stasmā̭dena॒so de॒va rī̭riṣaḥ || 5 || yat | kim | ca | i॒dam | va॒ruṇa | dai॒vye | ja॒ne | a॒bhi'dro̭ham | ma॒nuṣyā̭: | ca॒rāma̭si | aci̭ttī | yat | ta॒va | dha॒rma | yu॒yopi̭ma | mā | naḥ | tasmā̭t | ena̭sa: | de॒va | rī̭riṣaḥ || 5 || he vvaruṇa vdaivye devasamūharūpe vjane vyat vidaṃ kiṃcana vabhidroham apakārajātaṃ vmanuṣyā: vayaṃ vcarāmasi carāma: nirvartayāma: | tathā vacittī acityājñānena vtava tvadīyaṃ vyat vdharma dhārakaṃ karma vyuyopima vayaṃ vimohitavantaḥ | he vdeva vtasmādenasa: pāpāt vna: asmān vmā vrīriṣa: mā hiṃsī: || 11 || 5 ||

paḍhe'nuvāke pañcadaśa sūktāni|
tatra 'pra vīrayā' iti saptarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vāyavyam|
yāsu pañcamyādyāsu dvivacanamasti tā aindravāyavyaḥ|
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—'pra vīrayā sapta vāyavyaṃ haindrāśca yā dvivaduktāḥ' iti|
tṛtīye chandome praugaśastre 'pra vīrayā' iti vāyavya- sūktaḥ|
sūtritaṃ ca—'pra vīrayā śucayo dadhire vāṃ te satyena manasā dīdhyānāḥ' (āśva. śrau. 8. 11) iti||
pra vīrayā śucayo dadhire vāmaśvyurbhimṛdhumantaḥ sutāsaḥ|
vaha vāyo niyuto yāhyacchā pibā sutasyāndhaso madāya ||
1 ||
pra| vīra'yā| śucayaḥ| dadhire| vām| adhvaryu'bhiḥ| marṣu'mantaḥ| sutāsaḥ| vaha| vāyo iti| ni'yutaḥ| yāhi| acchā| piba| sutasya| andhasaḥ| madāya || 1 ||
he vāyo 'vīrayā vīrāya vividhamīrayitre|
'supāṃ suluk' iti caturthyā yājādeśaḥ|
'vāṃ te|
vyatyayena dvivacanam|
tumyaṃ 'śucayaḥ śuddhāḥ 'madhumantaḥ mādhuryopetāḥ 'sutāsaḥ abhi- ṣutāḥ somā aitareyabhirbhavame'hani prauge 'adhvaryubhiḥ adhvarasya netṛbhirṛtvigbhiḥ 'pra 'dadhire pradīyante|
'dada dāne' ityasyaitadrūpam|

kimartham|
'madāya madotpatyartham||
śunāsīrīye parvaṇi vāyavyasya haviṣaḥ 'īśānāya' iti yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—'sa tvaṃ no deva manaseśānāya prahutiṃ yasta ānaṭ' (āśva. śrau. 2. 20) iti|
vāyavye paśāveva paśupuroḍāśasya yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—'īśānāya prahutiṃ yasta ānaṭ pra vo vāyuṃ rathayujaṃ kṛṇudhvam' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti||
īśānāya prahutiṃ yasta ānaṭ śuciṃ somaṃ śucipāstumyaṃ vāyo|
kṛṇoṣi taṃ matyeṣu praśastaṃ jātojāto jāyate vājyasya ||
2 ||
īśānāya| pra'hutim| yaḥ| te| ānaṭ| śucim| somam| śuci'pāḥ| tumyam| vāyo iti| kṛṇoṣi| tam| matyeṣu| pra'śastam| jātaḥ'jātaḥ| jāyate| vājī| asya || 2 ||
he 'vāyo 'īśānāya īśvarāya 'te tumyaṃ tvadarthaṃ 'prahutiṃ prakṛṣṭāmāhutiṃ carupuroḍāśādisādhyāṃ 'yaḥ yajamānaḥ 'ānaṭ prāpnot|
dadyādityarthaḥ|
tathā he 'śucipāḥ śuddhasya somasya pātarvāṃco 'tumyaṃ 'śuci śuddhaṃ 'somaṃ ca yaḥ prayacchati 'matyeṣu manuṣyeṣu madhye 'taṃ yajamānaṃ 'praśastaṃ mukhyaṃ 'kṛṇoṣi karoṣi|
sa ca 'jātojātaḥ sarvatra prādurbhūtaḥ prakhyāta: san 'vājyasya prāptavyasya dhanasya prāptaye 'jāyate avakalpyate|
sarvaṃ dhanaṃ labhata ityarthaḥ||
niyutvadguṇaviśiṣṭavāyudevatāke paśau 'rāye nu yam' iti puroḍāśasya yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—'rāye nu yaṃ jajñatū rodasīme pra vāyumacchā bṛhatī manīṣā' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti||
rāye nu yaṃ jajñatū rodasīme rāye devī dhiṣaṇā dhāti devam|
adha vāyuṃ niyutaḥ saścata svā uta śvetaṃ vasudhiti nireke ||
3 ||
1. kha-śuceḥ śuddhasya; bha-śuddhasya śuceḥ|
2. kha-bha-prāptyai|
rāye | nu | yam | jajñatuḥ | rodasī iti | ime iti | rāye | devī | dhiṣaṇā | dhāti | devam | ardha | vāyum | ni'yutaḥ | saścata | svāḥ | uta | śvetam | vasu'dhitim | nireke || 3 ||
ime rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau yaṃ vāyuṃ rāye dhanārthaṃ nu kṣipraṃ jajñatuḥ janayāmāsatuḥ |
taṃ devaṃ dānādiguṇayuktaṃ vāyuṃ devī dyotamānā dhiṣaṇā stutiḥ rāye dhanārthaṃ dhāti dhārayati |
dhanaṃ yathā labhyate tathā prerayatītyarthaḥ |
adha adhunaiva stutau pravṛttāyāṃ svāḥ svakīyāḥ niyutaḥ vaḍavā rathavāhāḥ vāyuṃ saścata sañcante sevante |

nitarāṃ reko riktatā nirekaḥ |
dāridryamityarthaḥ |

īśānāso ye dadhate svarṇī gobhiraśvibhirvasubhirihiraṇyaiḥ | indravāyū sūrayo viśvamāyurvaidabhirviraiḥ | pṛtanāsu sadhuḥ || 6 || īśānāsaḥ | ye | dadhate | svaḥ | naḥ | gobhiḥ | aśvibhiḥ | vasubhiḥ | hiraṇyaiḥ | indravāyū iti | sūrayaḥ | viśvam | āyuḥ | arvadbhiḥ | vīraiḥ | pṛtanāsu | sadhuḥ || 6 || he indravāyū īśānāsaḥ īśvarāḥ prabhavaḥ ye janāḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ gobhiḥ aśvibhiḥ aśvaiḥ vasubhiḥ nivāsakaiḥ hiraṇyaiḥ ca sahitaṃ svaḥ suṣṭuvaraṇīya sukhaṃ dadhate dadati prayacchanti | yadvā | hiraṇyavyatiriktāni dhanāni vasūni | tairhiraṇyaiśca sahitairdhanaiḥ | te sūrayaḥ dātāraḥ viśvaṃ vyāptam āyuḥ annaṃ jīvanaṃ vā śatrūṇāṃ svabhūtaṃ pṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu arvadbhiḥ aśvaiḥ vīraiḥ śūramaiśca sādhanabhūtaiḥ sadhuḥ abhibhaveyuḥ | yadvā sahāyye tṛtī
he indravāyū yuvayoḥ tanvaḥ śarīrasya taraḥ vegaḥ yāvat asti yāvat ca ojaḥ balaṃ yāvat ca naraḥ karmaṇāṃ netāra ṛtvijaḥ vakṣasā jñānena dīdhyānāḥ dīpyamānā bhavanti tasya sarvasyānurūpaṃ śucipā śuceḥ somasya pātāvindravāyū śuciṃ śuddhaṃ somam asme asmadīyaṃ pātaṃ pibatam |
idaṃ vedyāṃ stīrṇaṃ barhiḥ ca ā sadataṃ pānārthamāsīdatam |
barhiṣyupaviśatamityarthaḥ ||
niyuvānā niyutaḥ spārhavīrā indravāyū sarathaṃ yātam arvāk |
idaṃ hi vāṃ prabhṛtaṃ madhvo agramadha prīṇānā vi mumuktamasme ||
5||
ni'yuvānā | ni'yutaḥ | spārha'vīrāḥ | indravāyū iti | su'ratham | yātam | arvāk | idam | hi | vām | pra'bhṛtam | madhvaḥ | agram | adha | prīṇānā | vi | mumuktam | asme iti ||5||
he indravāyū spārhavīrāḥ spṛhaṇīyastotrakān niyutaḥ ātmīyānaśvān saratham ubhayoḥ samānamekaṃ rathaṃ niyuvānā nimiśrayantau yuvām arvāk asmadabhimukhaṃ yātaṃ gacchatam |
idaṃ hi idaṃ khalu madhvaḥ madhurasya somasya agraṃ praheṣvādyamendravāyavākhyaṃ grahaṃ vāṃ yuvayortha prabhṛtaṃ prakaryeṇa hataṃ homāryamuttaravedi prati nītam |
adha atha tādṛśasya somasya pānānantaraṃ prīṇānā priyamāṇau yuvām asme asmān vi mumukaṃ pāpādvimocayatam ||
prathame chandome praḍagaśastra aindravāyavatrucasya 'thā vāṃ śatam' iti tṛtīyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'thā vāṃ śataṃ niyuto yāḥ sahasramityepātinyaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti ||
yā vāṃ śataṃ niyuto yāḥ sahasramindravāyū viśvavārāḥ sacante |
ābhiryātaṃ suvidatrābhirarvāk narā pratibhṛtasya madhvaḥ ||
6||
yāḥ | vām | śatam | ni'yutaḥ | yāḥ | sahasram | indravāyū iti | viśva'vārāḥ | sacante | ā | ābhiḥ | yātam | su'vidatrābhiḥ | arvāk | pātam | narā | prati'bhṛtasya | madhvaḥ ||6||
he indravāyū yāḥ niyutaḥ śataṃ śatasaṃkhyākāḥ satyaḥ vāṃ yuvāṃ sacante sevante |
yāḥ ca viśvavārāḥ viśvairvarṇīyā niyutaḥ sahasraṃ sahasrasaṃkhyākāḥ satyo yuvāṃ sacante |
suvidatrābhiḥ śobhanadhanapradābhiḥ ābhiḥ niyudbhiḥ arvāk asmadabhimukham ā yātam āgacchatam |
he narā netārau pratibhṛtasya uttaravediṃ prati nītasya madhvaḥ madhurasya somasya |
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
īdaśaṃ somaṃ pātaṃ pibatam ||
arvano na śravaso bhikṣamāṇā indravāyū suṣṭubhirvasiṣṭhāḥ |
vājayantaḥ svarvase huvema yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||
