‘ā vāyo’ iti pañcarcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ vāyavyam|
anukrāntaṃ ca—‘ā vāyo pañca’ iti|
śunāsīrīye parvaṇi vāyoniyutvato yāgasya ‘ā vāyo’ ityanuvākyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā vāyo bhūṣa śucipā upa naḥ pra yābhiryāṃsi dāśvāṃsamaccha’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 20) iti|
niyutvadvāyudevatāke paśāveṣaiva vapāyā anuvākyā (āśva. śrau. 3. 8)|
prathame chandome praugaśastre vāyavya-tṛcasyaiṣaivādyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘samudrādūrbhirityājyamā vāyo bhūṣa śucipāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti||
ā vāyo bhūṣa śucipā upa naḥ sahasraṃ te niyutoṃ viśvavāra |
upau te andho madyamayāmi yasya deva dadhiṣe pūrvapeyam ||
1 ||
ā | vāyo iti | bhūṣa | śuci'pāḥ | upa | naḥ | sahasram | te | ni'yutaḥ | viśva'vāra | upau | te | andhaḥ | madyam | ayāmi | yasya | deva | dadhiṣe | pūrva'peyam || 1 ||
he ॰śucipāḥ śuceḥ śuddhasya somasya pātaḥ ॰vāyo ॰naḥ asmākam ॰upa ॰samīpe ॰ā ॰bhūṣa āgaccha |
॰bhū prāptau’ ityasyaitadrūpam |
he ॰viśvavāra viśvairvareṇya te tava vāhanabhūtāḥ ॰niyutaḥ vaḍavāḥ ॰sahasraṃ sahasrasaṃkhyākā vidyante |
yata evamataḥ śīghramāgaccha |
॰te tava ॰madyaṃ madakaraṃ somalakṣaṇam ॰andhaḥ annam ॰upau upa u upa ॰ayāmi |
upayata pātre gṛhītamāsīt |
he ॰deva vāyo ॰yasya somasya ॰pūrvapeyaṃ prathamapānaṃ ॰dadhiṣe dadhāsi dhārayasi |
aindravāyavagrahe prathame vaṣaṭkāre kevalāya vāyave hūyate dvitīye tvindrāvāyubhyāmiti vāyoḥ prathamapānam |
tādṛśamandha upāyāmītyanvayaḥ ||
prathame chandome praugaśastra aindrāvāyavatvasya ‘pra sotā’ ityādyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pra sotā jīro adhvareṣvātsomamindrāya vāyave pibadhyai’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti||
pra sotā jīro adhvareṣvātsomamindrāya vāyave pibadhyai |
pra yadvāṃ madhvo agriyaṃ bharantyadhvaryavo devayantaḥ śacībhiḥ ||
2 ||
pra | sotā | jīraḥ | adhvareṣu | āt | somam | indrāya | vāyave | pibadhyai | pra | yat | vām | madhvaḥ | agriyam | bharanti | adhvaryavaḥ | deva'yantaḥ | śacībhiḥ || 2 ||
॰jīraḥ kṣiprakārī ॰sotā abhiṣotādhvaryuḥ ॰indrāya ॰vāyave ca ॰pibadhyai pānārtham ॰adhvareṣu yāgeṣu ॰somaṃ ॰pra ॰asthāt prātiṣṭhipat|
purastāduttaravediṃ prāpitavān |
he indrāvāyū ॰yat yeṣu yajñeṣu ॰madhvaḥ somasya ॰agriyam agrabhavaṃ prathamabhāgaṃ ॰devayantaḥ devakāmāḥ ॰adhvaryavaḥ ॰śacībhiḥ karmabhi-rabhiṣavādilakṣaṇaiḥ ॰vāṃ yuvayo rathaṃ ॰pra ॰bharanti prakarṣeṇa bharanti saṃpādayanti |
teṣvadhvareṣvityanvayaḥ||
śunāsīrīye parvaṇi niyutvadvāyoryāgasya ‘pra yābhiḥ’ iti yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pra yābhiryāṃsi dāśvāṃsamaccha sa tvaṃ no deva manasā’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 20) iti |
tahevatye paśāveṣaiva puroḍā-śasyānuvākyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pra yābhiryāṃsi dāśvāṃsamacchā no niyudbhiḥ śatinībhiḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti |
prathame chandome praugaśastra eṣaiva vāyavyatṛcasya dvitīyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā vāyo bhūṣa śucipā upa naḥ pra yābhiryāṃsi dāśvāṃsamaccha’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti||
pra yābhiryāṃsi dāśvāṃsamacchā niyudbhirvāyaviṣṭhaye duroṇe |
ni nau rayiṃ subhojasaṃ yuvasva ni vīraṃ gavyamaśvyaṃ ca rādhaḥ ||

1. kha-sa-ta-na-bha-śa- ‘upa samīpe’ nāsti |
2. kha-bha-prātiṣṭhapat; na-pratiṣṭhat |

pra | yāmiḥ | yāsi | dāśvāṃsam | accha | niyuta'bhiḥ | vāyo iti | iṣṭayai | duroṇe | ni | naḥ | rayim | su'bhojasam | yuvasva | ni | vīram | gavyem | aśvyam | ca | rādhaḥ ||3|| he vāyo duroṇe yajñagṛhe sthitaṃ dāśvāṃsaṃ haviṣā dātāraṃ yajamānam iṣṭaye yāgāya yābhiḥ niyudbhiḥ vaḍavābhiḥ accha yāsi abhigacchasi tābhirasmān pratyāgaccheti śeṣaḥ | āgatya ca naḥ asmabhyaṃ subhojasaṃ śobhanābhiyuktaṃ rathi dhanaṃ ni yuvasva nitarāṃ miśraya prayaccha | tathā vīraṃ putraṃ gavyaṃ gosaṃgamam aśvyam aśvasaṃgametadubhayātmakaṃ rādhaḥ dhanaṃ ca ni yuvasva prayaccha || prathame chandome praugaśastra aindravāyavatṛcasya 'ye vāyava indra' iti dvitīyā | sutritaṃ ca— 'ye vāyava indramādanāso yā vāṃ śataṃ niyuto yāḥ sahasram' (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti ||

kiṃ kurvantaḥ |

ā nau niyudbhiḥ śatinībhiradhvaraṃ sahasriṇībhirupa yāhi yajñam | vāyo asmintsavane mādayasva yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||5|| ā | naḥ | niyuta'bhiḥ | śatinībhiḥ | adhvaram | sahasriṇībhiḥ | upa | yāhi | yajñam | vāyo iti | asmin | savane | mādayasva | yūyam | pāta | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 5 || he vāyo naḥ asmākam adhvaraṃ hiṃsārahitaṃ yajñaṃ śatinībhiḥ śatasaṃkhyāvatībhiḥ saha- sriṇībhiḥ sahasrasaṃkhyāvatībhiśca niyudbhiḥ vaḍavābhiḥ upa ā yāhi upāgaccha | tadanantaram asmin savane prātaḥsavane mādayasva somena tṛpyasva | anyadatram || || 14 || 'śuci nu' ityaṣṭacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindrāpram | anukramyate hi—'śuci nvaṣṭā- vaindrāmaṃ tu' iti | gataḥ sūkaviniyogaḥ | aindrāmasya paśaurvapāthāḥ : 'śucim' ityeṣā mājyā | sutritaṃ ca —'śuci nu stomaṃ navajātamadya gīrbhirvapraḥ pramatimicchamānaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
1. kha-bha-mevamubhayā | 2. ta-rāyo | 3. kha-bha-varṣakā | 4. kha-bha-yuddhaiḥ |
śuciṃ nu stomaṃ navajātamugramindrāya vṛtrahaṇā juṣethām | ubhā hi vāṃ suhavā johavīmi tā vājaṃ sadya uśate dheṣṭhā || 1 || śucim | nu | stomam | nava'jātam | adya | indrāgnī iti | vṛtra'hanā | juṣethām | ubhā | hi | vām | su'havā | johavīmi | tā | vājam | sadyaḥ | uśate | dheṣṭhā || 1 || he vṛtrahaṇā vṛtrāṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ hantārau indrāgnī śuciṃ śuddhaṃ niravadyaṃ navajātam idānīmutpannaṃ stomam asmadīyaṃ stotram adya asmin kāle nu kṣipraṃ juṣethāṃ sevethām | hi yasmāt suhavā sukhamāhvātuṃ śakyau ubhā ubhau vāṃ yuvāṃ johavīmi punaḥpurāhvayāmi | ata āgatya sevethāmityarthaḥ | kiṃca tā tau tathāvidhau yuvām uśate kāmayamānāya yajamānāya vājam annaṃ balaṃ vā sadyaḥ tadānīmeva śīghraṃ dheṣṭhā dhātṛtamau bhavatam ||
tā sānasī śavasānā hi bhūtaṃ sākaṃ'vṛdhā śavasā śuśuvāṃsā | kṣayantau rāyo yavasasya bhūreḥ puṣṭaṃ vājasya sthavirasya ghṛṣṇaiḥ || 2 || tā | sānasī iti | śavasānā | hi | bhūtam | sākam'vṛdhā | śavasā | śuśu'vāṃsā | kṣayantau | rāyaḥ | yavasasya | bhūreḥ | puṣṭam | vājasya | sthavirasya | ghṛṣṇaiḥ || 2 || he indrāgnī tā tau tādṛśau sānasī savaiḥ saṃbhajanīyau yuvāṃ śavasānā | śavo balam | taddvā- carantau hi khalu bhūtam | balamiva śatrūṇāṃ bhakṣakāvāstāmityarthaḥ | kīdṛśau santau | sākaṃ'vṛdhā saha pravṛddhau śavasā balena śuśuvāṃsā vardhamānau tathā rāyaḥ dhanasya bhūreḥ bahulasya yavasasya annasya kṣayantau īśvarau | evaṃvidhau yuvaṃ yuvāṃ sthavirasya sthūlasya ghṛṣṇaiḥ śatrūṇāṃ gharṣakasya vājasya annasya | 'kriyāgrahaṇamapi kartavyam' iti karmaṇaḥ saṃpradānatvāccaturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī | īdṛśamannaṃ puṣṭaṃ saṃyojayatam | asmabhyaṃ prayacchatamityarthaḥ ||
upau hu yadidarthaṃ vājino gudhībhirviprāḥ pramatimicchamānāḥ | arvento na kāṣṭhāṃ nakṣamāṇā indrāgnī johuvato naraste || 3 || upau | hu | yat | tridartham | vājinaḥ | guḥ | dhībhiḥ | viprāḥ | pra'matim | icchamānāḥ | arventaḥ | na | kāṣṭhām | nakṣamāṇāḥ | indrāgnī iti | johuvataḥ | naraḥ | te || 3 || vājinaḥ haviṣmantaḥ viprāḥ medhāvinaḥ pramatiṃ prakṛṣṭāṃ matimindrāgnyoranugrahabuddhim iccha- mānāḥ ātmana icchantaḥ yat ye yajamānāḥ vidatham | vidanti jānanti devanatra yaṣṭavyatveneti vidatho yajñaḥ | taṃ dhībhiḥ karmabhirbuddhibhirvā upau guḥ upagacchanti | u iti pūrakaḥ | te naraḥ karmaṇāṃ netāro janāḥ arvento na kāṣṭhāṃ yathāśvāḥ śīghraṃ yuddhabhūmiṃ vyāpnuvanti tathā nakṣamāṇāḥ aindrābhāni karmāṇi vyāpnuvantaḥ indrāgnī indramagniṃ ca johuvataḥ punaḥpurāhvayantaḥ bhavanti ||

ekācetsarasvatī nadīnāṃ śuciryatī giribhya ā samudrāt |
rāyaścetantī bhuvanasya bhūraighṛtaṃ payo duduhe nahuṣāya ||
2 ||
ekā | acetat | sarasvatī | nadīnām | śuciḥ | yatī | giribhyaḥ | ā | samudrāt | rāyaḥ | cetantī | bhuvanasya | bhūraiḥ | ghṛtam | paryaḥ | duduhe | nahuṣāya || 2 ||
sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa2 ṛtunā yakṣyamāṇo nahuṣo nāma rājā sarasvatīṃ nadīṃ prārthayāmāsa |
sā ca tasmai sahasrasaṃvatsaraparyāptaṃ payo ghṛtaṃ ca pradadau |
ayamartho'tra pratipādyate |
1 nadīnām anyāsāṃ madhye 2 śuciḥ śuddhā 3 giribhyaḥ sakāśāt 4 ā 5 samudrāt samudraparyantaṃ 6 yatī gacchantī 7 ekā 8 sarasvatī nadī 9 acetat |
nahuṣasya prārthanāmajñāsīt |
tathā 10 bhuvanasya bhūtajātasya 11 bhūraiḥ bahulasya 12 rāyaḥ dhanāni 13 cetantī prajñāpayantī prayacchantī 14 nahuṣāya rājñe 15 ghṛtaṃ 16 payaḥ ca sahasrasaṃvatsarataḥ paryāptaṃ 17 duduhe dugdhavatī dattavatī ||
sarasvadevatāke paśau 'sa vāvṛdhe' iti puroḍāśasya yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'sa vāvṛdhe naryo yoṣaṇāsu yasya vrataṃ paśavo yanti sarve' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
sa vāvṛdhe naryo yoṣaṇāsu vṛṣā śiśurvṛṣabho yajñiyāsu |
sa vājinaṃ maghavadbhyo dadhāti vi sātaye tanvaṃ māmṛjīta ||
3 ||
saḥ | vavṛdhe | naryaḥ | yoṣaṇāsu | vṛṣā | śiśuḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | yajñiyāsu | saḥ | vājinam | maghavadbhyaḥ | dadhāti | vi | sātaye | tanvam | māmṛjīta || 3 ||
1. jha-ta- 'sarasvatyā' nāsti |
2. kha-bha-sahasrasaṃvatsareṇa |
madhyasthāno vāyuḥ sarasvatī |
ᐞnarthaḥ nṛbhyo hitaḥᐞ ᐞvṛṣā sevanasamarthaḥᐞ ᐞśiśuḥ ampaḥ prādurbhāvasamaye 'sayatayā dṛśyamānaḥ ᐞvṛṣabhaḥ varṣitā evaṃbhūtaḥ ᐞsaḥᐞ sarasvatī ᐞyajñiyāsu yajñāīāṃsu ᐞyoṣaṇāsu yoṣistvātmanaḥ kalatrabhūtāsu madhyasthānāsvapsu madhye ᐞvavṛdhe vardhate |
ᐞsaḥ tādṛśaḥ sarasvatī ᐞmaghavadbhyaḥ haviṣmadbhayo yajamānebhyaḥ ᐞvājinaṃ balinaṃ putraṃ ᐞdadhāti dadāti |
tathā ᐞsātaye lābhārthaṃ ᐞtanvaṃ teṣāṃ śarīraṃ ᐞvi ᐞmāmajīta vimāṣṭi |
lābhārthaṃ saṃskarotītyarthaḥ |
yadyapyeṣā sarasvatīḥ stutistathāpi sarasvatyāḥ prīṇanārthaṃ tatstavanamiti chāndomike sārasvate ᐞtuce'syā ukto viniyogo na virudhyate ||
daśarātre'ṣṭame'hani prauge ‘uta syā naḥ’ iti sārasvataḥ saptamastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘uta syā naḥ sarasvatī juṣāṇeti praugam’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 10) iti |
sārasvate paśau puroḍāśasya ‘uta syā naḥ’ ityanuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘uta syā naḥ sarasvatī juṣāṇā sarasvatībhi no neṣi vasyaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
uta syā naḥ sarasvatī juṣāṇopa śravatsubhagā yajñe asmin | mitadrubhirnamasyairiyānā rāyā yujā ciduttarā sakhibhyaḥ || 4 || uta | syā | naḥ | sarasvatī | juṣāṇā | upa | śravat | subhagā | yajñe | asmin | mitadrubhiḥ | namasyaiḥ | iyānā | rāyā | yujā | cit | uttarā | sakhibhyaḥ || 4 || ᐞuta api ca ᐞjuṣāṇā prīyamāṇā ᐞsubhagā śobhanadhanā ᐞsyā sā ᐞsarasvatī ᐞnaḥ asmākam ᐞasmin ᐞyajñe ᐞupa ᐞśravat | asmadīyāḥ stutīrupaśṛṇotu | kīdṛśī sā | ᐞmitadrubhiḥ prahrairjānubhiḥ ᐞnamasyaiḥ namaskārairdevaiḥᐞ ᐞiyānā upagamyamānā | vicchabdārthye | ᐞyujā nityayuktena ᐞrāyā dhanena ca saṃgatā ᐞsakhibhyaḥ ᐞuttarā utkṛṣṭatarā | īdṛśyasmadīyāḥ stutīrupaśṛṇotvityanvayaḥ || pavitreṣṭyāṃᐞ sārasvatasya haviṣaḥ ‘imā juhvānāḥ’ iti yājyā | sūtritaṃ ca—‘imā juhvānāḥ yuṣmadā namobhirvadhikāṇṇe akāriṣam’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 12) iti ||
imā juhvānā yuṣmadā namobhiḥ prati stomaṃ sarasvati juṣasva | tava śarminriyatame dadhānā upa stheyāma śaraṇaṃ na vṛkṣam || 5 || imā | juhvānāḥ | yuṣmat | ā | namaḥ | bhiḥ | prati | stomam | sarasvati | juṣasva | tava | śarman | priyatame | dadhānāḥ | upa | stheyāma | śaraṇam | na | vṛkṣam || 5 || he ᐞsarasvati ᐞimā imānyasmadīyāni havīṃṣi ᐞjuhvānāḥ tumyaṃ juhvato vayaṃ ᐞnamobhiḥ tvadvi- cayaiḥᐞ ᐞyuṣmat tvatsakāśāt ᐞā | upasargaśruteryogakriyāvyāhāraḥ | ādadīmahi dhanānīti śeṣaḥ | ᐞstomaṃ cāsmadīyaṃ stotraṃ ᐞprati ᐞjuṣasva pratisevasva | vayaṃ ca ᐞpriyatame atiśayena priye ᐞtava tvadīye ᐞśarman śarmaṇi sukhe ᐞdadhānāḥ nidhīyamānāḥ santaḥ ᐞśaraṇaṃ ᐞna ᐞvṛkṣam āśrayabhūtaṃ vṛkṣamiva ᐞupa ᐞstheyāma tvāmupatiṣṭhema saṃgacchemahi ||
ayamu te sarasvati vasiṣṭhe dvārāvṛtasya subhage vyāvaḥ | vardha śubhe stuvate rāsi vājānyūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 6 || 1. ta-yoṣaṇāsu yataḥ | 2. kha-jha-jha-na- saḥ ’ nāsti | 3. jha-ta-na-bha-dā-sārasvataṃ | 4. tha- namaskairye | 5. jha-ta-na-pariveṣṭyāṃ | 6. kha-jha-ta-bha-tupadadviṣayeḥ |
ayam | ū~ iti | te | sarasvati | vasiṣṭhaḥ | dvārau | ṛtasya | su'bhage | vi | āvaḥ | rityāvaḥ | vardha | śubhre | stuvate | rāsi | vājān | yūyam | pāt | svasti'bhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 6 ||
he subhage śobhanadhane sarasvati ayaṃ vasiṣṭhaḥ te tvāṃ yantuḥ ṛtasya yajñasya sambandhinyau dvārau pūrvāpare vyāvaḥ vivṛṇoti |
u iti pūrakaḥ |
he śubhre śubhavarṇe devi vardha vardhasva |
tathā stuvate stotraṃ kurvate vasiṣṭhāya vājān annāni rāsi pradehi |
anyadatsam ||

‘bṛhadu gāyiṣe’ iti ṣaḍcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣam |
ādyā bṛhatī dvitīyā satobṛhatī tṛtīyā prastārapaṅktiḥ śiṣṭāstisro gāyatryaḥ |
ādyastucaḥ sarasvatīdevatāko'nyaśca sarasvatīdevatākaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘bṛhadu pragāthaḥ prastārapaṅktiḥ parāstisro gāyatryaḥ sarasvatīte’ iti |
pañcame'hani praugaśastra ādyaḥ pragāthaḥ sārasvatastucaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘bṛhadu gāyiṣe’ iti bārhataṃ praugaṃ pragāthāneke ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 12 ) iti ||
bṛhadu gāyiṣe vaco'suryāṃ nadīnām |
sarasvatīminmaṃhayā suvṛktibhiḥ stomairvasiṣṭha ||
1 ||
bṛhat | ū~ iti | gāyiṣe | vacaḥ | asuryāṃ | nadīnām | sarasvatīm | it | mahaya | suvṛkti'bhiḥ | stomaiḥ | vasiṣṭha | rodasī iti || 1 ||
anayarvirātmānaṃ saṃbodhya sarasvatyāḥ stutau prerayati |
he vasiṣṭha tvaṃ bṛhadu bṛhadeva mahadeva vacaḥ stotraṃ gāyiṣe gāyasi |
kimartham |
nadīnāṃ madhye asuryāṃ |
asurāśabdāccaturthyekavacanasya vyādeśaḥ |
asurāyai balavatyai nadīrūpāyai sarasvatyai |
asyāḥ prīṇanārthamityarthaḥ |
tathā rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ sthitāṃ divi devatārūpeṇa bhūyasā vāmpeṇa nivasantīṃ sarasvatīmit sarasvatīmeva suvṛktibhiḥ suṣṭhu doṣavarjitaiḥ stomaḥ stotraiḥ mahaya pūjaya |
sarvadā sarasvatīmeva stuhi bhāmyāṃ devatāmiti bhāvaḥ ||
ubhe yattai mahiṃnā śubhre andhasī adhiśriyanti pūrvaḥ |
sā nau bodhyavitrī marutsakhā coda rādho maghonām ||
2 ||
ubhe iti | yat | te | mahiṃnā | śubhre | andhasī iti | adhi'śriyanti | pūrvaḥ | sā | naḥ | bodhi | avitrī | marut'sakhā | coda | rādhaḥ | maghonām || 2 ||
he śubhre śubhavarṇe sarasvati yat yasyāḥ te tava mahinā mahimnā ubhe andhasī ubhaya- vidhaṃ divyaṃ pārthivaṃ cānni grāmyāraṇyaṃ vā pūravaḥ pūrayitavyā manuṣyāḥ adhiśriyanti adhi- gacchanti sā tvam avitrī rakṣitrī satī naḥ asmān bodhi budhyasva |
api ca marutsakhā |
maruto mādhyamikā devagaṇāḥ |
te sakhāyo yasyā mādhyamikāyā vācastādṛśī tvaṃ maghonāṃ havirlakṣaṇa- dhanopetānāmasmākaṃ rādhaḥ dhanaṃ coda preraya ||

‘yajñe divaḥ’ iti daśarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham |
prathamaindrī tṛtīyānavamyorindrābrahmaṇa- spatī devatā daśamyā indrābṛhaspatī śiṣṭānāṃ tu bṛhaspatiḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘yajñe daśamyādi bārha- spatyamanyaindrī ca tṛtīyānavamyāvaindrābrāhmaṇaspatye’ iti |
ābhipluvikeṣuyajñeṣu tṛtīyasavane stomapṛṣṭhau brāhmaṇācchaṃsina idamuttaraṃ ca sūktamāvāpārtham |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘yajñe divaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 9) iti ||
ya॒jñe | di॒vaḥ | nu॒ṣa॒danḙ | pṛ॒thi॒vyā | na॒ro | yatra̭ | de॒va॒yava̭ḥ | ma॒rda॒nti | i॒ndrāya̭ | yatra̭ | sa॒rva॒nāni̭ | su॒nve | ga॒man'dā̭ya | pra॒tha॒mam | va॒yaḥ || 1 ||
1. jña-bhavati |
2. kha-na-bha-puroḍāśaya haviṣāṃ |
3. kṣa-śa-sarasvadeva; gha-deva |
4. jña- bhakṣīmahi prāpnuyāmaḥ; na-bhakṣīmahi khile-yasya vrata paśavo yanti sarve yasya vratamupatiṣṭhata āpaḥ |
yasya vrate puṣṭirnirviśiṣṭaṃ sarasvatīmavase huvema ||
(ṛ. khai. 15)

'yatra yasmin yajñe 'devayavaḥ devān kāmayamānāḥ 'naraḥ netāra ṛtvijaḥ 'madanti hṛṣyanti 'yatra yasminna 'savanāni abhiṣotavyāḥ somāḥ 'indrāya indrārtha 'sunve abhiṣūyante 'pṛthivyāḥ sambandhini 'nuṣadane nṛṇāṃ netṛṇāṃ' sadanabhūte tasmin 'yajñe 'prathamaṃ sarvebhyo devebhyaḥ pūrva 'divaḥ dyulokāt 'gamata āgacchatu |
kimartham |
'madāya madāyartham |
somaṃ pātumityarthaḥ |
tathā 'vayaśa gantārastadīyā aśvāśca gamatu āgacchantu ||
ā daivyā vṛṇīmahe'vā~si bṛhaspatirno maha ā sakhāyaḥ | yathā bhavema mīḻahuṣe anāgā yo nau dātā parāvataḥ piteva || 2 || ā | daivyā | vṛṇīmahe | avā~si | bṛhaspatiḥ | naḥ | mahe | ā | sakhāyaḥ | yathā | bhavema | mīḻahuṣe | anāgāḥ | yaḥ | naḥ | dātā | parā'vataḥ | pitā'iva || 2 ||
he 'sakhāyaḥ samānasthānāḥ stotāro vayaṃ 'daivyā debyāni devasambandhīni 'avā~si rakṣaṇāni 'ā vṛṇīmahe abhibhajāmahe' prārthayāmahe |
'naḥ asmākaṃ haviḥ 'bṛhaspatiḥ bṛhatāṃ pālayitā devaḥ 'ā 'mahe |
mahitadarthāyaḥ |
āmahate ādatte |
'lopasta āsmānepadeṣu 'iti talopaḥ |
'yathaḥ bṛhaspatiḥ 'parāvataḥ dūradeśādanānyahṛtya putrebhyaḥ 'piteva 'naḥ asmabhyaṃ 'dātā bhavati tasmai 'mīḻahuṣe sektre bṛhaspataye 'anāgāḥ anāgasa anaparādhāḥ 'yathā vayaṃ 'bhavema he sakhāyastathā yūyaṃ paricarateti śeṣaḥ ||
tamu jyeṣṭhaṃ namasā havirbhiḥ suśevaṃ brahmaṇaspatiṃ gṛṇīṣe | indraṃ śloko mahi daivyaḥ siṣaktu yo brahmaṇo devakṛtasya rājā || 3 || tam | ū~ iti | jyeṣṭham | namasā | haviḥ'bhiḥ | su'śevam | brahmaṇaḥ | patim | gṛṇīṣe | indram | ślokaḥ | mahi | daivyaḥ | siṣaktu | yaḥ | brahmaṇaḥ | deva'kṛtasya | rājā || 3 ||
'jyeṣṭhaṃ praśastatamaṃ 'suśevaṃ susukhaṃ 'brahmaṇaḥ mantrasya 'patiṃ pālayitārametatsaṃjñaṃ 'tamu tameva devaṃ 'namasā namaskāreṇa 'havirbhiḥ carupuroḍāśādibhiśca sārdhaṃ 'gṛṇīṣe gṛṇe stuve |
api ca 'mahi mahāntam 'indraṃ 'daivyaḥ devārhaḥ 'ślokaḥ asmadīyaḥ stāvakaḥ mantraḥ 'siṣaktu sevatām |
'brahmaṇaḥ abrahma mantrasya vā 'devakṛtasya devaiḥ stotṛbhiḥ kṛtasya 'yaḥ indro brahmaṇaspatīvāṃ 'rājā īśvaraḥ bhavati |
tamindraṃ taṃ brahmaṇaspatimiti sambandhaḥ ||
sa ā no yoniṃ sadatu preṣṭho bṛhaspatirviśvavāro yo asti | kāmo rāyaḥ suvīryasya taṃ dātparṣat arta saśvato ariṣṭān || 4 || saḥ | ā | naḥ | yonim | sadatu | preṣṭhaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ | viśva'vāraḥ | yaḥ | asti | kāmaḥ | rāyaḥ | suvīryasya | tam | dāt | parṣat | naḥ | arti | saśvataḥ | ariṣṭān || 4 ||
1. kha-gha-śa-'netṛṇā' nāsti |
2. śa-abhijāmīmahe |
^preṣṭhaḥ priyatamaḥ ^vasaḥ ^bṛhaspatiḥ ^naḥ asmākaṃ ^yoniṃ sthānaṃ vedilakṣaṇam ^ā ^sadatu āsī- datu āgatyopaviśatu |
^yaḥ bṛhaspatiḥ ^viśvavāraḥ viśvairvarṇanīyaḥ ^asti bhavati |
api ca ^rāyaḥ bhanasya ^suvīryasya śobhanavīryasya ca yaḥ ^kāmaḥ asmākamabhilāṣo'sti ^taṃ kāmamasmabhyaṃ ^dāt dadātu |
kāmyamānaṃ prayacchatvityarthaḥ |
tathā ^saśrataḥ upadravaiḥ saṃsaktān ^naḥ asmān ^ariṣṭān aṃhisitān kṛtvā ^ati ^parvat atipārayati śatrūn ||

5 ||
tam | ā | naḥ | arkam | amṛtāya | juṣṭam | ime | dhāsuḥ | amṛtāsaḥ | purā'jāḥ | śuciṃ'krandam | yajatam | pustyānām | bṛhaspatim | anavarṇam | huvema || 5 ||
^taṃ sarvatra bhoktavyatayā prasiddham ^amṛtāya amaraṇāya jīvanāya ^juṣṭaṃ paryāptam ^arkam arcanasādhanamannaṃ ^purājāḥ purā jātāḥ ^ime ^amṛtāsaḥ amaraṇā devā bṛhaspatirājñayā ^naḥ asma- bhyam ^ā ^dhāsuḥ pradadhuḥ |

pastyamiti gṛhanāma |

21||

^śgmāsaḥ śagmāḥ sukhakarāḥ śakkā vā ^aruṇāsaḥ ārocamānāḥ ^sahavāhaḥ saṃhatya vāhakāḥ ^aśvāḥ ^taṃ ^bṛhaspatiṃ ^vahanti vahantu |

sa | hi | śuciḥ | śata'patraḥ | saḥ | śundhyuḥ | hiraṇya'vāśīḥ | iṣiraḥ | svaḥ'sāḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ | saḥ | su'āveśaḥ | ṛṣvaḥ | puru | sakhi'bhyaḥ | ā'sutim | kariṣṭhaḥ || 7 ||

1. kha-stūyema; jha-ta3-śa1-stuvema; śara-cusuyema |

3. kha-jha-ta-na- bha-śa-hitaramaṇīyā vāk |
^sa eva ^bṛhaspatiḥ ^svāveśaḥ sunivāsaḥ ^ṛṣvaḥ darśanīyaḥ |
īdṛśo devaḥ ^sakhibhyaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ ^guru bahulam ^āsutim ahaṃ ^kariṣyaḥ kartṛtamo dātṛtamo bhavati ||

8 ||
devī iti | devasya | rodasī iti | janitrī iti | bṛhaspatim | vavṛdhatuḥ | mahi'tvā | dakṣāyyāya | dakṣata | sakhāyaḥ | karat | brahaṇe | su'tarā | su'gādhā || 8 ||
^devī devyau dānādiguṇayukte ^devasya bṛhaspateḥ ^janitrī janayitryau ^rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^mahiravā maharavena yuktaṃ ^bṛhaspatiṃ ^vavṛdhatuḥ vardhayāmāsatuḥ |
he ^sakhāyaḥ yūyamapi ^dakṣāyyāya vardhanīyāya |
dvitīyārthe caturthī |
vardhanīyaṃ taṃ bṛhaspatiṃ ^dakṣata vardhayata |
sa ca bṛhaspatiḥ ^brahaṇe bṛṃhitāya prabhūtāyājñāya tadartha ^sutarāṃ sutaraṇāni' sukhena taraṇīyāni ^sugādhā sukhenavagāhanīyā- nyudakāni ^karat karotu ||
iyaṃ vāṃ brahmaṇaspate suvṛktirbrahendrāya vajriṇe akāri |
aviṣṭaṃ dhiyau jigutaṃ puraṃdhīrjastamaryo vanuṣāmarātīḥ ||
9 ||
iyam | vām | brahmaṇaḥ | pate | su'vṛktiḥ | brahma | indrāya | vajriṇe | akāri | aviṣṭam | dhiyaḥ | jigutam | puram'dhīḥ | ^jajastam | aryaḥ | vanuṣām | arātīḥ || 9 ||
he ^brahmaṇaspate tumyaṃ ^vajriṇe vajravate ^indrāya ca ^vāṃ yuvābhyām |
tādarthye caturthī |
^brahma mantrarūpā ^iyaṃ ^suvṛktiḥ supravṛttā stutiḥ ^akāri mayā kṛtābhūta |
tau yuvāṃ ^dhiyaḥ asmadīyāni karmāṇi ^aviṣṭaṃ rakṣatam |
tathā ^puraṃdhīḥ pururdhīḥ stutīḥ ^jigutaṃ nigiratam |
śṛṇuta miti yāvat |
^aryaḥ arīrabhigantrīḥ ^vanuṣāṃ saṃbhaktānāmasmākam ^arātīḥ śatrusenāḥ ^jajastam upakṣapayatam ||
tṛtīyasavana ukthye brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ 'bṛhaspate yuvam' iti śastrājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasva iti yājyā' (āśva. śrau. 6. 1) iti |
vājapeye'tiriktokthasyaiṣaiva paridhānīyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasva iti paridhānīyā' (āśva. śrau. 9. 9) iti ||
bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasvau divyasyeśāthe uta pārthivasya |
dhattaṃ rayiṃ stuvate kīraye cidyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ||

abhyadratam ||

1. kha-jha-bha-śa-sutarā sutarāṇi; gha-'sutarā sutaraṇāni' nāsti; na-sutarāṇi |
2. kha-ta- upalakṣyata; gha-upalayata; jha-śa-upakṣayata; na-bha-upalakṣaṇaṃ yata |
‘adhvaryavaḥ’ iti saptarcaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindraṃ saptamyaindrābārhaspatyā |
tathā cānukrāntam—‘adhvaryavaḥ saptarcadevatāntyā’ iti |
pūrvasūktena sahokto viniyogaḥ ||
adhvaryavo'ruṇaṃ dugdhamaṃśuṃ juhotana vṛṣabhāya kṣitīnām | gaurādedīyā~ ava'pānamindro viśvāhādeti sutasomamicchan || 1 || adhvaryavaḥ | aruṇam | dugdham | aṃśum | juhotana | vṛṣabhāya | kṣitīnām | gaurāta | vedīyān | ava'pānam | indraḥ | viśvāhā | ita | yāti | suta'somam | icchan ||1||
he adhvaryavaḥ adhvarasya netāra ṛtvijaḥ kṣitīnāṃ janānāṃ madhye vṛṣabhāya śreṣṭhāya- yendrāya aruṇam ārocamānaṃ dugdham abhiṣutam aṃśuṃ somaṃ juhotana juhuta |
avapānam avakramya sthitaṃ dūrasthaṃ pātavyaṃ somaṃ gaurāta gauramṛgādapi vedīyān atiśayena vidvān indraḥ sutasomam abhiṣutasomaṃ yajamānam icchan anvicchan viśvāhā viśvānyahāni it eva sarvadaiva yāti gacchati |
atastasmā indrāya somaṃ juhutetyanvayaḥ ||
yaddhiṣe pradivi cārunnaṃ divedive pītimidasya vakṣi | uta hṛdota manasā juṣāṇa uśannindra prasthitanpāhi somān || 2 || yat | dadhiṣe | pra'divi | cāru | annam | dive'dive | pītim | ita | asya | vakṣi | uta | hṛdā | uta | manasā | juṣāṇaḥ | uśan | indra | pra'sthitān | pāhi | somān ||2||
he indra pradivi pragateṣu divaseṣu pūrvasmin kāle cāru śobhanaṃ yat somalakṣaṇam annaṃ dadhiṣe pānenodare dhārayasi asya somasya pītim pānameva divedive pratidivasamidānīmapi vakṣi kāmayase |
‘vaṣa kāntau’ ityasya sipi śapo luki plavakarūpatvevedhetadrūpam |
uta api ca he indra hṛdā hṛdayena |
utaśabdaścārthe |
manasā ca juṣāṇaḥ sevamānaḥ uśan asmān kāmaya- mānastvaṃ prasthitān purastātprītānuttaravedisthān somān pāhi piba ||
jajñānaḥ somaṃ sahase papātha pra te mātā mahimānamuvāca | endraṃ paprathorva'ntarikṣaṃ yudhā devebhyo varivaścakartha || 3 || jajñānaḥ | somam | sahase | papātha | pra | te | mātā | mahimānam | uvāca | ā | indra | papratha | uru | antarikṣam | yudhā | devebhyaḥ | varivaḥ | cakartha || 3 ||
he indra tvaṃ jajñānaḥ jāyamāna eva sahase balāya somaṃ papātha pītavānasi |
te tava mahimānaṃ mahattvaṃ mātā tvadīyā jananyaditiḥ pra uvāca proktavatī |
saṃvādasūkte ‘ayaṃ panthāḥ’ ityādike ‘nahī nvasya’ (ṛ. saṃ. 4. 18. 4) ityartharcādārabhya ādityendramahattvasyoktatvāt |
ataḥ kāraṇāt he indra tvam uru vistīrṇam antarikṣam ā papratha svatejasāpūritavānasi |
api ca yudhā yuddhena devebhyaḥ stotṛbhyo devebhya eva vā varivaḥ dhanaṃ cakartha kṛtavānasi ||
yadyodhā mahato manyamānāntsākṣāt tānbāhubhiḥ śāśadānān |
yadvā nṛbhirvṛte indrābhiyuṣpāstaṃ tyāji sauśruvasaṃ jayema ||
4 ||
yat | yodhyāḥ | mahataḥ | manyamānān | sākṣām | tān | bāhu'bhiḥ | śarśadānān | yat | vā | nṛ'bhiḥ | vṛtaḥ | indra | abhi'yudhyāḥ | tam | tvayā | ājim | sauśravasam | jayema||4||
he indra mahataḥ prabhūtān manyamānān śatrūn yat yadā yodhyāḥ asmābhiryodhayeḥ |
taiḥ saha yoddhuṃ balaṃ prayacchetyarthaḥ |

śravo'nnaṃ yaśo vā |

prendrasya vocaṃ prathamā kṛtāni pra nūtanā maghavā yā cakāra | yadedadaivīrasahiṣṭa māyā arthābhavatkevalaḥ somo asya ||5|| pra | indrasya | vocam | prathamā | kṛtāni | pra | nūtanā | maghavā | yā | cakāra | yadā | it | adaivīḥ | asahiṣṭa | māyāḥ | artha | abhavat | kevalaḥ | somaḥ | asya ||5||
indrasya kṛtāni vīryakarmāṇi prathamā prathamāni purātanāni pra vocaṃ prabravīmi |

he indra paśyam |
paśavo dvividhā dvipādaścatuṣpādaśca |

sūryasya prerakasyādityasya cakṣasā tejasā yat viśvaṃ paśyasi tvaṃ prakāśayasi |
api ca he indra ekaḥ eva tvaṃ gopatiḥ asi |

dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |

bṛhaspate yuvamindraśca vasvo divyasyaiśāthe uta pārthivasya | dhattaṃ rayiṃ stuvate kīraye cidyuyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ || 7 || bṛhaspate | yuvam | indraḥ | ca | vasvaḥ | divyasya | īśāthe iti | uta | pārthivasya | dhattam | rayim | stuvate | kīraye | cit | yuyam | pāta | svastibhiḥ | sadā | naḥ || 7 ||
vyākhyāteyam |
akṣarārthastu |

tau yuvāṃ stuvate stotre dhanaṃ dattamiti ||
23 ||
1. śa-śastranirapekṣaiḥ |
‘paro mātrayā’ iti saptarcaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham |
‘uruṃ yajñāya’ ityādyāstisra aindrāvaiṣṇavyaḥ śiṣṭāḥ kevalaviṣṇudevatākāḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘paro vaiṣṇavaṃ turamistyaindryaśca tisraḥ’ iti |
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ |
viṣṇudevatāke paśau ‘paro mātrayā’ iti puroḍāśasya yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘paro mātrayā tanvā vṛdhānerāvatī dhenamatī hi bhūtam’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
paro mātrayā tanvā vṛdhāna na tai mahitvamanvaśruvanti |
ubhe tai vidma rajasī pṛthivyā viṣṇo deva tvaṃ paramasya vitse ||
1 ||
paraḥ | mātrayā | tanvā | vṛdhāna | na | te | mahitvam | anu | aśnavanti | ubhe iti | te | vidma | rajasī iti | pṛthivyāḥ | viṣṇo iti | deva | tvam | paramasya | vitse ||1||
para iti sakārāntaṃ parastādityasyārthe |
paraśabdācchāndasa 'sipratyayaḥ : ‘paro divā para enā pṛthivyā’ (ṛ. saṃ. 10. 82. 5) iti yathā |
mātrayeti vyatyayena tṛtīyā |
‘mātrayā paraḥ parastādvartamānayāparimitayā ‘tanvā śarīreṇa ‘vṛdhāna vardhamāna he viṣṇo ‘te tava ‘mahitvaṃ mahattvaṃ ‘na ‘anvaśruvanti nānuvyāpnuvanti |
traivikramasamaye yattava māhātmyaṃ tatsarvairapi janairjñātuṃ na śakyata ityarthaḥ |
‘te tava ‘ubhe ‘rajasī ubhau lokau ‘pṛthivyāḥ ārabhya pṛthivīmantarikṣaṃ ca ‘vidma jānīmaḥ |
vayaṃ cakṣuṣopalabhāmahe nānyat |
he ‘deva dyotamāna ‘viṣṇo ‘tvam eva ‘paramasya svargāderurukalokasya’|
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
paramaṃ lokaṃ ‘vitse jānāsi |
atastava mahattvaṃ na kenāpi vyāptuṃ śakyamiti bhāvaḥ ||
pūrvokta eva paśau ‘na te viṣṇo’ iti vapāyā anuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘na te viṣṇo jāyamāno na jātasvaṃ viṣṇu sumatiṃ viśvajanyām’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
na tai viṣṇo jāyamāno na jāto deva mahimnaḥ paramantamāpa |
udastaścā nārkamuṣṭaṃ bṛhantaṃ dādhartha prācīṃ kakubhaṃ pṛthivyāḥ||
2 ||
na | te | viṣṇo iti | jāyamānaḥ | na | jātaḥ | deva | mahimnaḥ | param | antam | āpa | ut | astabhāḥ | nārkam | ṛṣvam | bṛhantam | dādhartha | prācīm | kakubham | pṛthivyāḥ ||2||
he ‘deva dānādiguṇayukta ‘viṣṇo ‘te tava ‘mahimnaḥ mahattvasya ‘paraṃ viprakṛṣṭam ‘antam avasānaṃ ‘jāyamānaḥ prādurbhavañjanaḥ ‘na ‘āpa na prāpnoti |
tathā ‘jātaḥ prādurbhūto'pi janaḥ ‘na eva prāpnoti |
tava mahattvasyāvasānaṃ nāsti |
ata eva sarvairna jñāyata iti bhāvaḥ |
ko'sau mahimā tamāha |
‘ṛṣvaṃ darśanīyaṃ ‘bṛhantaṃ mahāntaṃ ‘nākaṃ dyulokam ‘udastabhāḥ tvamūrdhvamadhāreyaḥ |
yathādho na patati tathā |
‘pṛthivyāḥ bhūmeḥ saṃbandhinīṃ ‘prācīṃ ‘kakubhaṃ ca ‘dādhartha dhāritavānasi |
upalakṣaṇametatsarvasya bhūtajātasya |
tathā ca mantrāntaraṃ—‘ya u tridhātu pṛthivīmuta dyāmeko dādhāra bhuvanāni viśvā’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 154. 4) iti ||
pūrvokta eva paśau ‘irāvatī dhenamatī hi bhūta viśvakarmahaviṣā vāvṛdhāna iti dve’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
irāvatī dhenamatī hi bhūta sūryyavāsinī manuṣe daśasyā |
vyastabhā rodasī viṣṇavete dādhartha pṛthivīmabhito mayūkhaiḥ ||
3 ||
1. kha-śa-°ityukttasya lokasya |
2. ta1-śa-māhātmya
irāvatī itīrā'vatī| dhenumatī iti dhenu'matī| hi| bhutam| suyavasinī iti su'yavasinī| manuṣe| daśasyā| vi| astambhāḥ| rodasī iti| viṣṇo iti| ete iti| dādhartha| pṛthivīm| abhitaḥ| mayūkhaiḥ||3||
he dyāvāpṛthivyau manuṣe stuvate manuṣyāya daśasyā ditsayā yukte yuvām irāvatī annavatyau dhenumatī gomatyau suyavasinī śobhanayavase ca bhutam abhūtam|
hiśabdaḥ prasiddhaḥ|
viṣṇunā vikrāntatvād yuvām evameva khalu pūrvamabhūtamityarthaḥ|
he viṣṇo ete ime rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau vyastambhāḥ vividhamadhārayaḥ|
pṛthivīmūrdhvamukhatvena dhāmadhomukhatveneti vividhatvam|
api ca pṛthivīṃ prathitāmimāṃ bhūmim abhitaḥ sarvatra sthitaiḥ mayūkhaiḥ parvataiḥ dādhartha dhāritavānasi|

'viṣṇuḥ parvatānāmadhi- patiḥ' (tai. saṃ. 3. 4. 5. 1) iti śruteḥ||

4||
urum| yajñāya| cakrathuḥ| ūṃ iti| lokam| janayantā| sūryam| upasam| agnim| dāsasya| cit| vṛśiśaprasya| māyāḥ| jaghnathuḥ| narā| pṛtanājyeṣu || 4 ||
he indrāviṣṇū yajñāya yajamānāya uru vistīrṇaṃ lokaṃ svargākhyaṃ cakrathuḥ kṛta- vantau khalu yuvām|
kiṃ kurvantau|
sūrya sarvasya prerakamādityam upasaṃ tamoni vārakamuṣaḥkālam agniṃ cāsurairāvṛtaṃ janayantau punaḥ prādurbhāvayantau|
he narā netārāvindrāviṣṇū vṛśiśaprasya eta- tsaṃjñasya dāsasya cit upakṣapathiturasurasya māyāḥ pṛtanājyeṣu saṃgrāmeṣu jaghnathuḥ jahiṃsathuḥ|
yuvāṃ sūryādikaṃ janayantā vityucyate||
indrāviṣṇū dṛṃhitāḥ śambarasya nava puro navatiṃ ca śrathiyam|
śataṃ varcinaḥ sahasraṃ ca sākaṃ hatho apratisurasya vīrān ||
5||
indrāviṣṇū iti| dṛṃhitāḥ| śambarasya| nava| puraḥ| navatim| ca| śrathiyam| śatam| varcinaḥ| sahasram| ca| sākam| hathaḥ| aprati| asurasya| vīrān || 5 ||

'atha hiṃsāyām'|
api ca śataṃ sahasraṃ ca varcinaḥ asurasya vīrān aprati pratidvandvino yathā na bhavanti tathā sākaṃ saha saṃghaśa eva hathaḥ ahiṃsi- tam|
'yo varcinaḥ śatamindraḥ sahasram' |

triḥ | devaḥ | pṛthivīm | eṣaḥ | etām | tri | cakrame | śata'rcasam | mahitvā | pra | viṣṇuḥ | astu | tavasaḥ | tavīyān | tveṣaḥ | hi | asya | sthavirasya | nāma || 3 ||
eṣaḥ devaḥ dānādiguṇayukto viṣṇuḥ |

‘parjanyāya’ iti tṛcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ gāyatram |
pūrvavadṛṣidevate |

vaiśvānarapārjanyāyāmanvārambhaṇīyāyāṃ pārjanyasya caro: ‘parjanyāya’ ityanuvākyā |

parjanyāya pra gāyat divasputrāya mīḻahuṣe | sa no yavasamicchatu || 1 || parjanyāya | pra | gāyat | divaḥ | putrāya | mīḻahuṣe | saḥ | naḥ | yavasam | icchatu || 1 ||

yo garbhamoṣadhīnāṃ gavāṃ kṛṇotyarvatām | parjanyaḥ puruṣīṇām || 2 || yaḥ | garbham | oṣadhīnām | gavām | kṛṇoti | arbatām | parjanyaḥ | puruṣīṇām || 2 ||
ᵥyaḥ parjanyaḥ ᵥoṣadhīnāṃ vrīhyādīnāṃ ᵥgavām ᵥarvatām avatīnāṃ vaḍavānāṃ ᵥpuruṣīṇāṃ nārīṇāṃ ca yaḥ ᵥparjanyaḥ ᵥgarbhaṃ prasūta hetuṃ bījamudakarūpaṃ ᵥkṛṇoti karoti tasmai parjanyāyetyuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ ||
tasmā idāsyai havirjuhotā madhumattamam | iḻāṃ naḥ saṃyataṃ karata || 3 || tasmai | it | āsyai | haviḥ | juhota | madhumat'tamam | iḻām | naḥ | sam'yatam | karata || 3 ||

‘ indrāsomā ’ iti pañcaviṃśatyṛcaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vasiṣṭhasyārṣam |
ādyā: ṣaṭ jagatya: saptamī jagatī triṣṭupvāṣṭādaśyekaviṃśatayoviṃśatyo jagatyo'nyā ‘ prati cakṣva ’ ityanuṣṭup śiṣṭāścaturdaśa triṣṭubha: |
navamīdvādaśītrayodaśya: somadevatyā ekādaśī devadevatyāṣṭamīṣoḍaśyāvindradevatāke saptadaśī grāvadevatyāṣṭādaśī maruddevatākā daśamīcaturdaśyāvagnidevatāke ‘ pra vartaya ’ ityādyā: pañcarca indra- devatākā: |

uttara'rdharca: pṛthivyantarikṣadevatya: |
śiṣṭānāṃ rakṣoghnāvindrāsomau devate |
tathā cānukrāntam— ‘ indrāsomā pañcābhikaindrāsomaṃ rakṣoghnaṃ śāpābhiśāpaprāyaṃ ṣaṭ sapta vādhā jagatya ekaviṃśatyayo- viṃśayau cāṣṭādaśī mārutī ca daśamīcaturdaśyāvābhyeyyau daivyekādaśyanvānuṣṭupnavamī dvādaśī trayodaśī saumya: saptadaśī prāṇyaṣṭamīṣoḍaśyāvaindrau pra vartayeti pañcarcyo mā no rakṣa ityupairātmana āśī-
1. kha-na-bha-pūrṇe |
2. na-pravardhayaṃtu khila:-upalabdha maṇḍūki varṣa māvada tāduri |
madhye hradasya ^sva viguhā catura: pada: (ṛ. lai. 16) |
3. jha-ta4-catasra aindrā:; jha-catasra aindro vā; ta3-ma-śa-pañcaindrā vā |
ma. 7 a. 6. sū. 104 ] pañcamo'ṣṭaka: 509
ruttaro'dherthaḥ pṛthivyantarikṣadevataḥ' iti |
atra bṛhadevatāyāmanukramyate—'saṃvatsaraṃ tu maṇḍūkainaindrā- somaṃ paraṃ tu yat ||
ṛṣivadarśa rakṣoghnaṃ putraśokapariplutaḥ |
hate putraśate kruddhaḥ saudāsairduḥkhitastadā' ||
(bṛhade. 6. 27-28) iti |
ato rakṣonibarhaṇāyametatsūktaṃ japyam ||
indrāsomā tapataṃ rakṣa ubjataṃ nyarpayataṃ vṛṣaṇā tamovṛdhaḥ |
parā śṛṇītamacito nyoṣataṃ hataṃ nudethāṃ ni śiśītamatṛṇaḥ ||
1 ||
indrāsomā | tapatam | rakṣaḥ | ubjatam | ni | arpayatam | vṛṣaṇā | tamaḥ'vṛdhaḥ | parā | śṛṇītam | acitāḥ | ni | oṣatam | hatam | nudethām | ni | śiśītam | atṛṇaḥ || 1 ||
he indrāsomā |
indraśca somaścendrasomau |
'devatādvandve ca' iti pūrvapadasthān |
āma- ntritāyudāttatva
51
ṛgvedaḥ
he indrāsomau duṣkṛtaḥ duṣkarmakāriṇaḥ rākṣasān name vārake antaḥ madhye anārambhaṇe ālambanarahite tamasi andhakāre pra vidhvyataṃ praveśya tāḍayatama |
yathā yena prakāreṇaṣāṃ madhye ekaśca eko'pi rākṣasaḥ ataḥ asmāttamasaḥ punaḥ na udayat utcchet |
tathā vidhvyata- mityarthaḥ |
eterlyaḍāgamaḥ |
itaśca lopaḥ ' itīkāralopaḥ |
guṇāyādeśau |
tat prasiddhaṃ manyumat krodhayuktaṃ vāṃ yuvayoḥ śavaḥ balaṃ sahase rakṣasāmabhibhavāya astu bhavatu ||
indrāsomā vartayataṃ divo vadhaṃ saṃ pṛthivyā adhaśa~sāya tarhaṇam |
uttakṣataṃ svaryaṃ parvatebhyo yena rakṣo vāvṛdhānaṃ nijūrvathaḥ ||
4 ||
indrāsomā | vartayatam | divaḥ | vadham | sam | pṛthivyāḥ | adha'śa~sāya | tarhaṇam | ut | takṣatam | svaryam | parvatebhyaḥ | yena | rakṣaḥ | vāvṛdhānam | ni'jūrvathaḥ || 4 ||
he indrāsomau divaḥ antarikṣāt dyulokādvā vadhaṃ hananasādhanamāyudhaṃ saṃ vartayatam utpā- dayatam |
pṛthivyāḥ asmādapi lokāt tarhaṇaṃ hiṃsakamāyudham adhaśa~sāya adhaśaṃsamanarthasyā- śaṃsakaṃ rākṣasaṃ hantumutpādayatam |
tathā parvatebhyaḥ parvavadbhyo meghebhyaḥ sakāśāt svaryam |
‘svṛ śabdotāpayoḥ ’ |
upatāpakamśanim utkṣatam uddhṛtaṃ kurutam |
yena aśaninā vāvṛdhānaṃ vardha- mānaṃ pravṛddhaṃ vā rakṣaḥ rākṣasaṃ nijūrvathaḥ nihathaḥ |
‘jurvī hiṃsāyām ’ |
tamaśanimuttakṣatamityarthaḥ ||
indrāsomā vartayataṃ divasparyabhitasebhiryuvamaśamehanmabhiḥ |
tapurvadhebhirajarairbhiratriṇo ni parśāne vidhvyataṃ yantu nisvaram ||
5 ||
indrāsomā | vartayatam | divaḥ | pari | agni'tasebhiḥ | yuvam | aśmahanma'bhiḥ | tapuḥ'vadhebhiḥ | ajaraibhiḥ | atriṇaḥ | ni | parśāne | vidhvyatam | yantu | ni'svaram || 5 ||
he indrāsomau divaḥ antarikṣāt paritaḥ sarvataḥ vartayatam āyudhāni prerayatam |
yuvaṃ tau yuvām abhitasebhiḥ abhinā saṃtasaiḥ tapurvadhebhiḥ tāpakaraprahāraiḥ ajaraibhiḥ jarārahitairhadaiḥ aśma- hanmabhiḥ aśmamārabhūtasyāyaso vikārarhainasādhanaistairāyudhaiḥ atriṇaḥ rākṣasasya parśāne pārśvasthāne ni vidhvyataṃ nihatam |
te ca rākṣasāḥ niḥsvaraṃ niḥśabdaṃ yantu apayantu nirgacchantu ’ ||
5||
indrāsomā pāri vāṃ bhūtu viśvata iyaṃ matiḥ kaśyāśvaiva vājinā |
yāṃ vāṃ hotrāṃ parihinomi medhayemā brahmāṇi nṛpatīva jinvatama ||
6 ||
indrāsomā | pari | vām | bhūtu | viśvataḥ | iyam | matiḥ | kaśyā | aśvā'iva | vājinā | yām | vām | hotrām | pari'hinomi | medhayā | imā | brahmāṇi | nṛpatī'iveti nṛpatī'iva | jinvatama || 6 ||
he indrāsomau iyam asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇā matiḥ mananīyā stutiḥ vājinā vājinau balavantau vāṃ yuvāṃ viśvataḥ sarvataḥ pari bhūtu parigṛhṇātu vyāpnotu vā |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
kaśyā kaṣabhandhanau rajjuḥ aśveva yathāśvaṃ parigṛhṇāti tadvat |
yāṃ hotrāṃ vācaṃ vāṃ yuvābhyāṃ medhayā imā |
brahmāṇi |
nṛpatī iveti nṛpatī'iva |
jinvatama ||
6 ||
he indrāsomau iyam asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇā matiḥ mananīyā stutiḥ vājinā vājinau balavantau vāṃ yuvāṃ viśvataḥ sarvataḥ pari bhūtu parigṛhṇātu vyāpnotu vā |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
kaśyā kaṣabhandhanau rajjuḥ aśveva yathāśvaṃ parigṛhṇāti tadvat |
yāṃ hotrāṃ vācaṃ vāṃ yuvābhyāṃ medhayā
1. jha-kṣa-ta-1-2-4-śa-nirgacchantu apagacchantu |
2. jha-kakṣarbadhanī |
^parihinomi prerayāmi|
seyaṃ matiriti saṃbandhaḥ|
api ca ^imā imānyasmābhiḥ kṛtāni ^brahmāṇa stotrāṇi - - - yathā dhanaiḥ pūrayanti tathā ^jinvaṃtaṃ phalaiḥ pūrayatm' |
prati smarethāṃ tujaryadvirervairhataṃ duho rakṣaso bhaṅgurāvataḥ | indrāsomā duṣkṛte mā sugaṃ bhūyo naḥ kadā cidabhidāsati duhā || 7 || prati | smarethām | tujaryadabhiḥ | evaiḥ | | hatam | duhaḥ | rakṣasaḥ | bhaṅgura'vataḥ | indrāsomā | duḥ'kṛtai | mā | su'gam | bhūya | yaḥ | naḥ | kadā | cit | abhidāsati | duhā || 7 ||
he indrāsomau ^tujaryadbhiḥ svaramāṇaiḥ ^evaiḥ gantubhiraśvaiḥ ^prati ^smarethām abhigacchatam|
upasargavaśena smaratiratrārthāntare vartate |
yathā prasmaraṇaṃ prasthānamiti |
abhigatya ca ^duhaḥ drohvan^ ^bhaṅgurāvataḥ bhajanakarmavataḥ ^rakṣasaḥ rākṣasān ^hataṃ histam |
he indrāsomau ^duṣkṛte pāpakāriṇe rākṣasāya ^sugaṃ sukhaṃ ^mā ^bhūta mā bhavatu |
^duhā droheṇa yuktaḥ ^yaḥ ^naḥ asmān ^kadā ^cit api ^abhidāsati abhihanti tasmai duṣkṛta ityanvayaḥ |
yo mā pākena manasā caraṃtamabhicaṣṭe anṛtebhirvacobhiḥ | āpaiva kāśinā saṃgṛbhītā asanbastvārsata indra vṛktā || 8 || yaḥ | mā | pākena | manasā | carantam | abhi'caṣṭe | anṛtebhiḥ | vacaḥ'bhiḥ | āpaḥ'iva | kāśinā | sam'gṛbhītāḥ | asan | astu | asataḥ | indra | vṛktā || 8 ||
^pākena pakkena śuddhena ^manasā ^carantaṃ vartamānaṃ satyavādinaṃ ^yaḥ māṃ rākṣasaḥ ^anṛtebhiḥ anṛtairasatyaiḥ ^vacobhiḥ vacanaiḥ ^abhicaṣṭe abhiśaṃsati mayyasatyavacanamāropayati he ^indra ^kāśinā muṣṭinā ^saṃgṛbhītāḥ samyaggṛhītāḥ ^āpaiva yathāpo viśīrṇā bhavanti tathā ^asataḥ asatyasya^ ^vaktā^ sa rākṣasaḥ ^asanbastu avidyamāno bhavatu |
naśyatvityarthaḥ |
ye pākaśaṃsaṃ viharantu evairvā bhadraṃ dūṣayanti svadhāmiḥ | ahye vā tānpradadātu soma ā vā dadhātu nirṛterupasthe || 9 || ye | pāka'śaṃsam | vi'harante | evaiḥ | ye | vā | bhadram | duṣyanti | svadhāmiḥ | ahye | vā | tān | pra'dadātu | somaḥ | ā | vā | dadhātu | niḥ'ṛteḥ | upa'sthe || 9 ||
^ye rākṣasāḥ ^pākaśaṃsaṃ paripakvavacanaṃ satyabhāṣiṇaṃ mām ^evaiḥ etasyaiḥ prāsagyairātmīyaiḥ kāmahetubhūtaiḥ ^viharante viśeṣeṇa haranti upakṣapayanti |
yathākāmaṃ parivadantītyarthaḥ^ |
^ye ^vā ^svadhāmiḥ balairyuktāḥ ^bhadraṃ kalyāṇavartanaṃ ^māṃ ^dūṣayanti duṣṭaṃ kurvanti ^tān sarvān ^somaḥ ^ahye ^vā sapāṃtha vā ^pradadātu |
^nirṛteḥ pāpadevatāyāḥ ^upasthe utsaṅge ^vā ^bhā ^dadhātu prakṣipatu |
1. gha-pūrayataṃ | dṛṣṭataḥ nṛpatīva rājānau iva tau yathā pūrayataḥ tadvat | 2. janta3-dveṣṭṛn | 3. jña-tta1-2-4-na-asatyavaktā | 4. ta3-paribhavantītyarthaḥ | 5. kha-na-bha-śa-kalyāṇaṃ vartamānaṃ |
512
ṛgveda:
yo no rasaṃ dipsati pitvo agne yo akṣṇāṃ yo gavāṃ yastanūnām |
ripuḥ stenaḥ staiyakṛdabhrametu ni ṣa hīyatāṃ tanvā tarnā ca ||
10||
yaḥ | naḥ | rasam | dipsati | pitvaḥ | agne | yaḥ | akṣṇām | yaḥ | gavām | yaḥ | tanūnām | ripuḥ | stenaḥ | staiya'kṛt | dubhram | etu | ni | saḥ | hīyatām | tanvā | tarnā | ca ||10||
he agne yaḥ rākṣasaḥ naḥ asmākaṃ pitvaḥ annasya rasaṃ sāraṃ dipsati jighāṃsati |
yaḥ ca akṣṇām asmadīyānāṃ rasaṃ dipsati |
yaḥ ca gavāṃ rasaṃ dipsati |
yaḥ ca tanūnām asmadīyānāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ rasaṃ dipsati |
ripuḥ bādhakaḥ stenaḥ cauraḥ staiyakṛt dhanasyāpahartā saḥ sarvo janaḥ dubhraṃ hiṃsāṃ etu prāpnotu |
api ca saḥ bādhakaḥ tanvā svakīyena śarīreṇa tanā ca tanayena ca ni hīyatāṃ nihīno bhavatu ||
6 ||
puraḥ mo astu tanvā tarnā ca tisraḥ pṛthivīradho astu viśvāḥ |
prati śuṣyatu yaśo asya devā yo no divā dipsati yaśca naktam ||
11 ||
puraḥ | seḥ | astu | tanvā | tarnā | ca | tisraḥ | pṛthivīḥ | adhaḥ | astu | viśvāḥ | prati | śuṣyatu | yaśaḥ | asya | devāḥ | yaḥ | naḥ | divā | dipsati | yaḥ | ca | naktam ||11||
saḥ rākṣasaḥ tanvā tanā ca |
vyatyayena tṛtīyā |
tanvāḥ śarīrasya tanayasya ca paraḥ parastāt astu vartamāno bhavatu |
ubhābhyāṃ viyukto bhavatvityarthaḥ |
viśvāḥ vyāptāḥ tisraḥ pṛthivīḥ trīllokān adho astu adhastādbhavatu |
lokatrayādapi pracyuto bhavatvityarthaḥ |
he devāḥ asya śatroḥ yaśaḥ annaṃ kīrtirvā prati śuṣyatu |
yaḥ rākṣasaḥ naḥ asmān divā ahani dipsati jighāṃsati yaśca naktaṃ rātrau jighāṃsati asyobhayavidhasya yaśaḥ prati śuṣyatviti saṃbandhaḥ||
suvijñānaṃ cikituṣe janāya sacāsacca varcasī paspṛdhāte |
tayoryatsatyaṃ yataradṛjīyastaditsomo'vati hantyāsat ||
12 ||
su'vijñānam | cikituṣe | janāya | sat | ca | asat | ca | varcasī iti | paspṛdhāte iti | tayoḥ | yat | satyam | yatarat | ṛjīyaḥ | tat | it | somaḥ | avati | hanti | asat ||12||
prāyeṇedamādibhirṛgbhī rākṣasena saharṣiṇā śapathaḥ kriyate |
atra kecidāhuḥ—'hatvā putraśataṃ pūrvaṃ vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaṃ rākṣaso'si tvaṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ rūpamāsthitaḥ ||
ahaṃ vasiṣṭha ityevaṃ jighāṃsū rākṣaso'bravīt |
atrottarā ṛcau dṛṣṭā vasiṣṭheneti naḥ śrutam' ||
iti |
cikituṣe viduṣe janāya idaṃ suvijñānaṃ vijñātuṃ suśakaṃ bhavati |
kiṃ tat |
saca satyaṃ ca asacca asatyaṃ ca |
varcasī satyā- satyarūpe vacane paspṛdhāte mithaḥ spardhete |
tayoḥ sadasatoramadhye yatsatyaṃ yathārthaṃ vacanaṃ yatarat yacca ṛjīyaḥ ṛjutamamakuṭilaṃ tadit tadevākuṭilaṃ satyabhāṣaṇaṃ somaḥ avati rakṣati |
asat ukta- vilakṣaṇamasatyaṃ hanti hinasti |
evaṃ satyānayormadhye kataro'nṛtabhāṣīti vidvadbhiḥ suvijñānamityarthaḥ ||
na vā u somau vṛjinaṃ hinoti na kṣatriyaṃ mithuyā dhārayantam |
hanti rakṣo hantyāsaddhadantamubhāvindrasya prasitau śayāte ||
13 ||
1. kha-sa-ta-na-bha-śa-adhogho |
na | vai | ū~ iti | somaḥ | vṛjinam | hinoti | na | kṣatriyam | mithuyā | dhārayantam | hanti | rakṣaḥ | hanti | asat | vadantam | ubhau | indrasya | pra'sitau | śayāte iti ||13||
vṛjinaṃ pāpakāriṇaṃ rākṣasaṃ somaḥ devaḥ na vā u na khalu hinoti prerayati |
gaccha tvamiti na muñcati |
tathā kṣatriyam |
kṣatraṃ balam |
tadvantaṃ mithuyā mithyābhūtaṃ vacanaṃ dhārayantaṃ bibhratamasatyavādinaṃ puruṣaṃ na ca hinoti na visṛjati |
api tu rakṣaḥ rākṣasaṃ hanti |
asat asatyaṃ vadantaṃ ca hanti hinasti |
ubhau rākṣasānṛtavādinau tau somena hatau indrasya saṃbandhini prasitau bandhane śayāte nivasata |
yadvā |
indrasyeti tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
indreṇaśvareṇa somena prasitau baddhau |
'bij bandhane' |
asmāt karmaṇi niṣṭhā |
'gatiranantaraḥ' iti gateḥ
yaḥ rākṣaso mām ayātam arākṣasaṃ santaṃ he ghātudhāna he rākṣasa iti saṃbodhaya āha brūte yo vā yaśca rakṣāḥ rākṣasaḥ śucirasmi śuddho bhavāmi na rākṣaso'smi ityāha brūte tam ubhayavidhaṃ rākṣasam indraḥ mahatā prauḍhena vadhena āyudhena vajreṇa hantu hinastu |
sa ca viśvasya sarvasya jantoḥ janasya adhamaḥ nikṛṣṭaḥ san padīṣṭa patatu ||
pra yā jigāti khargaleva naktamarpa duhā tunvaṃ 1 gṛhamānā |
vatrā~ anantāṃ ava sā padīṣṭa grāvāṇo ghrantu rakṣasa upabdaiḥ ||
17 ||
pra | yā | jigāti | khargaleva | naktam | arpa | duhā | tunvam | gṛhamānā | vatrān | anantān | ava | sā | padīṣṭa | grāvāṇaḥ | ghrantu | rakṣasaḥ | upabdaiḥ || 17 ||
yā rākṣasī naktaṃ rātrau duhā droheṇa yuktā khargaleva ullūkīva pra jigāti pragacchati |
kiṃ kurvantī |
tanvaṃ svakīyaṃ śarīram apa gṛhamānā apavṛṇvatī prakāśayantī |
sā rākṣasī ana- ntān aparyantān vatrān gatārn ava padīṣṭa avāṅmukhī patatu |
grāvāṇaḥ somābhiṣavārthāḥ pāṣāṇāśca upabdaiḥ abhiṣavaśabdaiḥ rakṣasaḥ rākṣasān ghrantu hiṃsantu ||
vi tiṣṭhadhvaṃ maruto viśvi cchata gṛhāyata rakṣasaḥ saṃ pinaṣṭana |
vayo ye bhūtvī putayanti nuktabhirye vā ripoṃ dadhire deve adhvare ||
18 ||
vi | tiṣṭhadhvam | marutaḥ | viśva | icchata | gṛhāyata | rakṣasaḥ | sam | pinaṣṭana | vayaḥ | ye | bhūtvī | putayanti | nuktabhiḥ | ye | vai | ripaḥ | dadhire | deve | adhvare || 18 ||
he marutaḥ yūyaṃ viśva prajāsu vi tiṣṭhadhvaṃ vividhaṃ tiṣṭhata |
tatra gūḍhān rākṣasān hantum icchata anvicchata |
tadanantaraṃ rakṣasaḥ tān rākṣasān gṛhāyata gṛhṇīta |
gṛhītvā ca saṃ pinaṣṭana cūrṇayata |
ye rākṣasāḥ vayaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ bhūtvī bhūtvā nuktabhiḥ rātribhī rātriṣu pata- yanti āgacchanti |
ye vā ye ca deve dīpte adhvare yāge ripaḥ hiṃsāḥ dadhire vidadhire |
tān rākṣasān saṃ pinaṣṭanetyanvayaḥ ||
pra vartaya divo aśmānamindra somaśitaṃ maghavantaṃsaṃ śiṣādhi |
prāktādapāktādadharādudaktādbhi jahi rakṣasaḥ parvatena ||
19 ||
pra | vartaya | divaḥ | aśmānam | indra | somaśitam | maghavan | sam | śiṣādhi | prāktāt | apāktāt | adharāt | udaktāt | abhi | jahi | rakṣasaḥ | parvatena || 19 ||
he indra divaḥ antarikṣāt aśmānam aśaniṃ pra vartaya preraya rākṣasān hantum |
tathā somaśitaṃ somena tīkṣṇībhūtaṃ yajamānaṃ he maghavan dhanavannindra saṃ śiṣādhi saṃskuru |
api ca prāktāt prācyāḥ apāktāt pratīcyāḥ adharāt avācyāḥ udaktāt uttarataḥ sarvasmādapi digbhāgāt rakṣasaḥ rākṣasān parvatena parvatavavā vajreṇa abhi jahi māraya ||
1. kha-gha-bha-mā māṃ |
2. jha-ta-1-2-4-aśanaṃ ; na-prāvāṇaṃ |
eta u tye patayanti śaryātava indraṃ dipsanti dipsavo'dābhyam | śiśīte śakraḥ piśunebhyo vadhaṃ nūnaṃ sṛjadaśaniṃ yātumadbhyaḥ || 20 || ete | ū~ iti | tye | patayanti | śaryātavaḥ | indram | dipsanti | dipsavaḥ | adābhyam | śiśīte | śakraḥ | piśunebhyaḥ | vadham | nūnam | sṛjat | aśanim | yātumadbhyaḥ || 20 || ete rākṣasāḥ śaryātavaḥ śabhiḥ parikarabhūtairhiṃsantaḥ śabhiḥ saha yānto vā patayanti patanti | ye dipsavaḥ jighāṃsavaḥ santaḥ adābhyam ahiṃsyam indraṃ dipsanti jighāṃsanti tebhyaḥ piśunebhyaḥ piśunān kapaṭān hantuṃ śakraḥ śakta indraḥ vadham āyudhamaśanirūpaṃ śiśīte tīkṣṇī- karoti | yātumadbhyaḥ rākṣasebhyaḥ nūnaṃ kṣipram aśaniṃ sṛjat visajatu hananātham || 8 ||
indro yātunāmabhavatparāśaro havirmathīnāmabhyāvivāsatām | abhīdu śakraḥ paraśuryā vane pātreva bhindansata eti rakṣasaḥ || 21 || indraḥ | yātunām | abhavat | parāśaraḥ | haviḥmathīnām | abhi | ā'vivāsatām | aṃbhi | ita | ū~ iti | śakraḥ | paraśuḥ | yathā | vanam | pātra'iva | bhindan | sata | eti | rakṣasaḥ || 21 || yātūnāṃ hiṃsakānāṃ rakṣasāmayam indraḥ parāśaraḥ parāśātayitā hiṃsitā abhavat | kīdṛśānām | havirmathīnāṃ havīṃṣi mathnātamabhimukham āvivāsatām āgacchatām | api cāyaṃ śakraḥ indraḥ vanaṃ vṛkṣajātaṃ paraśuryā chinadan kuṭhāra iva pātreva manmathāni pātrāṇi bhindan mudgara iva sata : prāsanāmaitat | yadāha yāskaḥ—‘tiraḥ sata iti prāsasya’ ( niru. 3. 20 ) iti | prāsān rakṣasaḥ rākṣasān bhindan hiṃsan abhi eti abhigacchati | idū pūraṇau ||
ulūkayātuṃ śuśulūkayātuṃ jahi śaryātamuta korkayātum | suparṇayātumuta gṛdhrayātuṃ dṛṣadeva pra mṛṇa rakṣa indra || 22 || ulūka'yātum | śuśulūka'yātum | jahi | śaryātam | uta | korka'yātum | suparṇa'yātum | uta | gṛdhra'yātum | dṛṣadā'iva | pra | mṛṇa | rakṣaḥ | indra || 22 || ulūkayātum | ulūkaiḥ parikarabhūtaiḥ saha yātayati hinastīti yāti gacchatīti volūkayātuḥ | yadvā ulūkarūpī yātītyulūkayātuḥ | he indra tādṛśaṃ rākṣasaṃ jahi vināśāya | tathā ca bṛhadevatāyā- muktam—‘ulūkayātuṃ jahyetānārūpāścarācarān || strīpuṃrūpāṃśca tiryañco jighāṃsūnindra me jahi ’ ( bṛhade. 6. 32 ) iti | evamuttaratrāpi yojyam | śuśulūkayātum | ulukā dvividhā bṛhadulkā alpolkāśceti | tatrolūkayātumiti bṛhadulkābhiprāyeṇoktam | śiśurūpa ulūkaḥ śuśulūkaḥ | tadrūpeṇa vartamānaṃ rākṣasaṃ śaryātam rūpeṇa vartamānaṃ rākṣasam uta api ca korkayātum | kokaścakravākaḥ | tadrūpeṇa vartamānaṃ rākṣasaṃ suparṇayātum | suparṇaḥ | iyenaḥ | tadākāraṃ yātudhānam uta api ca gṛdhrayātuṃ gṛdhrarūpaṃ ca yātudhānam | etān sarvānākārān he indra jahi | kiṃ bahunā dṛṣadeva pāṣāṇeneva vajreṇa rakṣaḥ rākṣasamātraṃ pra mṛṇa māraya ||
1. jha-śa-yātumadbhyo hisāvadbhyaḥ |
2. kha-gha-jha-ta-bha-śa-kīdṛśīnāṃ |
3. śa-mathnātāmabhyā- vivāsatāmabhi |
mā no rakṣo'bhi ndadyātumārvatāmpaucchatu mithunā yā kimīdinā | pṛthivī naḥ pārthivātpātvaṃhaso'ntarikṣaṃ divyātpātvasmān || 23 || mā | naḥ | rakṣaḥ | abhi | naṭ | yātu'mārvatām | apa | ucchatu | mithunā | yā | kimīdinā | pṛthivī | naḥ | pārthivāt | pātu | aṃhasaḥ | antarikṣam | divyāt | pātu | asmān || 23 || ^rakṣaḥ rākṣasajātiḥ ^naḥ asmān ^mā ^abhi ^naṭ mābhivyāpnotu | naśatervyāptikarmaṇo luṅi ‘mantre ghasa’ iti cleluk | ^na ^mādyoge ^ityaḍabhāvaḥ | tathā ^yātumāvatāṃ yātanāvatāṃ rākṣasānāṃ ^mithunā mithunāni strīpuṃsarūpāṇi yuglāni ^apocchatu | uṣā api vivāsayatu apa- varjayatu | ^yā yāni mithunāni ^kimīdinā kimīdināni kimidaṃ kimidamiti jighāṃsayā vartamānāni bhavanti | api ca ^pṛthivī prathivyaṃ bhūmimaśca ^naḥ asmān ^pārthivāt pṛthivyā antari- kṣasya saṃbandhanaḥ ^aṃhasaḥ pāpāt ^pātu rakṣatu | ^antarikṣaṃ ca ^divyāt divibhavāt pāpāt ^asmān ^pātu rakṣatu ||
indra̭ jahi pumā̭saṃ yātudhāna̭muta striyaṃ mā॒yayā॒ śāśa̭dānām | vigrī̭vāso mṷ̄radevā ṛ̭dantu mā te dṛ̭śantasūryamura॒cara̭ntam || 24 || indra | jahi | pumāṃsam | yātudhānam | uta | striyam | māyayā | śāśadānām | vigrīvāsaḥ | mūradevāḥ | ṛdantu | mā | te | dṛśan | sūryam | uta | carantam || 24 || he ^indra ^pumāṃsaṃ puṃrūpadhāriṇaṃ ^yātudhānaṃ rākṣasaṃ ^jahi māraya | ^uta api ca ^māyayā vañcanayā ^śāśadānāṃ hiṃsantīṃ ^striyaṃ rākṣasīṃ ca jahi | api ca ^mūradevāḥ māraṇakrīḍā rākṣasāḥ ^vigrīvāsaḥ vicchinnagrīvāḥ santaḥ ^ṛdantu naśyantu | ^te tathāvidhā rākṣasāḥ ^uccarantam udyantaṃ ^sūryam ādityaṃ ^mā ^dṛśan mā drākṣuḥ || upākaraṇotsarjanayormaṇḍalādyantahome ‘prati cakṣva’ ityeṣā | sūtritaṃ ca—‘yo naḥ svo araṇaḥ prati cakṣva’ (āśva. gṛ. 3. 5. 7) iti ||
prati cakṣva vi cakṣvendratha soma jāgṛtam | rakṣo̭bhyo va॒dhama̭syata॒maśa̭niṃ yātu॒madbhya̭ḥ || 25 || prati | cakṣva | vi | cakṣva | indra | ca | soma | jāgṛtam | rakṣaḥ'bhyaḥ | vadham | asyatam | aśanim | yātumadbhyaḥ || 25 || he ^soma ^tvam ^indraśca ^prati ^cakṣva pratyekaṃ paśya rākṣasān | tathā ^vi ^cakṣva vividhaṃ paśya | yathāsmān bādheran tathā paśyetyarthaḥ | ^yuvāṃ ca saṃhatau ^jāgratam | jāgarūkau rakṣovadhodyuktau bhavatam | ^yātumadbhyaḥ hiṃsāvadbhyaḥ ^rakṣobhyaḥ rākṣasebhyaḥ ^aśanim aśanirūpaṃ ^vadham āyudham : ^asyataṃ kṣipatam || || 9 || || iti saptame maṇḍale ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ | saptamaṃ maṇḍalaṃ samāptam ||
oṃ
athāṣṭamaṃ maṇḍalam ||
aṣṭame maṇḍale daśānuvākāḥ |
tatra prathame'nuvāke pañca sūktāni |
teṣu ‘mā cidanyat’ iti caturviṃśadṛcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
atrānugamyate—‘mā ciccaturviṃśanmedhātithimedhātithī aindraṃ bārhataṃ dvipragāthādi dvitriṣṭubantamādyaṃ dvacaṃ pragātho'pyasya ghauraḥ sanbhrātṛḥ kaṇvasya putratāmagāt plāyogi-śvāsaṅgo yaḥ strī bhūtvā pumānbhūtsa medhātithaye dānaṃ datvā stuhi stuhīti catasṛbhiramānaṃ tuṣṭāva patnī cāsyāṅgirasī śaśvatī puṃsavamupalabhyainaṃ prītāntayā tuṣṭāva’ iti |
asyāyamarthaḥ |
asya sūktasya medhātithimedhātithināmānau dvāvṛṣī tau ca kaṇvagotrau |
ṛṣiśchando'nugotraḥ: ‘prāṅmatasyāt kāṇvaḥ’ iti paribhāṣitatvāt |
ādyasya dvacasya tu ghorasya putraḥ svakīyabhrātuḥ kaṇvasya putratāṃ prāptatvāt kāṇvaḥ |
pragāthākhya ṛṣiḥ |
playoganāmno rājñaḥ putra āsaṅgābhidhāno rājā devaśāpāt strītvamanubhūya paścāttapobalena medhātitheḥ prasādāt pumān bhūtvā tasmai bahu dhanaṃ datvā svakīyamantrālānaṃ dattadānaṃ stuhi stuhītyādibhiścatasṛbhirbharastaut |
atastāsāmāmāsaṅgāsyo rājā ṛṣiḥ |
asyāsaṅgāsya bhāryāṅgirasaḥ sutā śaśvatyākhyā bhartuḥ puṃsavamupalabhya prītā satī svabhartāram ‘anvasya sthūram’ ityanayā stutavatī |
atastasyā ṛcaḥ śaśvatyṛṣikā |
anye dve triṣṭubhau dvitīyācaturthyau satībṛhatī śiṣṭā bṛhatyaḥ |
kṛtsnasya sūktasyendro devatā |
‘stuhi stuhi’ ityādyāstatra ātmakṛtasya dānasya stūyamānatvāttaddevatākāḥ |
‘anvasya’ ityasyā āsaṅgākhyo rājā devatā |
‘yā tenocyate sā devatā’ iti nyāyāt |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye bārhatatacāśītāvādita ekonatriṃśaddinayuktāḥ |
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake sūtritaṃ—‘mā cidanyaddhi śaṃsatetyekayā na triṃśat’ (ai. ā. 5. 2. 4) iti |
cāturviṃśe'hani mādhyandina-savane maitrāvaruṇasya ‘mā cidanyat’ iti vaikalpikaḥ stotriyaḥ pragāthaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘mā cidanyaddhi śaṃsata yacciddhi tvā janā ima iti stotriyānurūpau’ (āśva. śrau. 7.4) iti |
prāvastogre'pyāyā viniyuktā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā tū na indra dyumantaṃ mā cidanyaddhi śaṃsata’ (āśva. śrau. 5. 12) iti |
upākaraṇotsarjanayoḥ: ‘maṇḍalādihīme'pyeṣā |
sūyate hi—‘mā cidanyadāse yāhi svādiṣṭhayā’ iti ||
mā cidanyadvi śaṃsata sakhāyo mā riṣaṇyata |
indramidastotā vṛṣaṇaṃ sacā sute muhurukthā ca śaṃsata ||
1 ||
mā | cit | anyat | vi | śaṃsata | sakhāyaḥ | mā | riṣaṇyata | indram | it | stota | vṛṣaṇam | sacā | sute | muhuḥ | ukthā | ca | śaṃsata || 1 ||
he sakhāyaḥ samānakhyānāḥ stotāraḥ indrastotrāt anyat stotraṃ mā cit vi śaṃsata maivoccārayata |
mā riṣaṇyata mā hiṃsitāro bhavata |
anyadīyastotroccāraṇeṇa vṛthopakṣīṇā mā bhavata |
sute abhiṣute some vṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāram indramit indrameva he prastotrādayaḥ sacā saha saṃghībhūya stota stuta |
he praśāstrādayaḥ ukthā ca ukthāni śastrāṇi cendraviṣayāṇi yūyaṃ muhuḥ punaḥpunaḥ śaṃsata ||
avakṛṣiṇaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ yathājuraṃ gāṃ na carṣaṇīsaham |
vidveṣaṇaṃ saṃvananobhayaṃkaraṃ mahiṣṭhamugramabhyāvinām ||
2 ||
1. kha-kṣa-ta-śa-upākarmotsarjanayoḥ |
ava'krakṣiṇam | vṛṣabham | yathā | ajuram | gām | na | carṣaṇi'saham | vi'dveṣaṇam | su'nvartanā | ubhayam'karam | maṃheṣṭham | ubhayāvinam || 2 ||
^vṛṣabhaṃ ^yathā ^vṛṣabhamiva ^avakrakṣiṇam avakarṣaṇaśīlaṃ śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsitāram ^ajuraṃ jarārahita- maṃhisitaṃ vā ^gāṃ ^na gāmiva vṛṣabhamiva ^carṣaṇīsahaṃ carṣaṇīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ śatrubhūtānāmabhibhavitāraṃ ^vidveṣaṇaṃ vidveṣṭāraṃ śatrūṇāṃ ^saṃvanānā saṃvananaṃ samyaksaṃbhajanīyaṃ stotṛbhiḥ ^ubhayaṃkaraṃ nigrahā- nugrahayorubhayoḥ kartāraṃ ^maṃhiṣṭhaṃ dātṛtmam ^ubhayāvinaṃ divyapārthivalakṣaṇenobhayavidhadhanenopetam |
yadvā |
sthāvarajaṅgarūpeṇa dvipakāreṇa rakṣitavyenopetam |
athobhayavidhaiḥ stotṛbhiryaṣṭṛmiśropetam |
evaṃvidhamindramita stotetyanvayaḥ ||
cāturviṃśike'hani mādhyandinasavane 'yacciddhi tvā' iti praśāstṛvai kalpiko'nupaḥ pragāthaḥ |
sūtraṃ tadāhatam ||
yacciddhi tvā janā ime nānā harvanta ūtaye | asmākaṃ brahmadindra bhūtu te'hā viśvā ca vardhanam || 3 || yat | cit | hi | tvā | janāḥ | ime | nānā | harvante | ūtaye | asmākam | brahma | idam | indra | bhūtu | te | arhā | viśvā | ca | vardhanam || 3 ||
^ime dṛśyamānāḥ sarve ^janāḥ he indra tvām ^ūtaye rakṣaṇāya tarpaṇāyā vā ^nānā pṛthakpṛthak ^yaccit yadyapi ^havante stuvanti |
^hi iti pūraṇaḥ |
tathāpi ^asmākam ^idaṃ ^brahma stotrameva he ^indra ^te tava ^vardhanaṃ vardhakaṃ ^bhūtu bhavatu |
na kevalamidānīmeva api tu ^viśvā ^ahā sarvāṇya- hāni sarveṣvahaḥsu ^ca idameva stotraṃ tvāṃ vardhayatvityarthaḥ ||
vi tarturyante maghavanvipaśrito'ryo vipo janānām | upa kramasva purumpamā bhara vājaṃ nediṣṭhamūtaye || 4 || vi | turyante | mṛ'dvan | vipaḥ'citaḥ | aryaḥ | vipaḥ | janānām | upa | kramasva | puru'rūpam | ā | bhara | vājam | nediṣṭham | ūtaye || 4 ||
he ^maghavan dhanavannindra ^vipaśritaḥ vidvāṃsastvādiyāḥ stotāraḥ ^aryaḥ abhigantāraḥ ^janānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ ^vipaḥ vepayitāraḥ santaḥ ^vi ^tarturyante bhṛśamāpado vitaranti atikrāmanti |
tādṛśastvam ^upa ^kramasva upagacchāsmān |
^pururūpaṃ bahurūpam ^nediṣṭham antikatamaṃ ^vājam annam ^ūtaye tarpaṇāyā ^ā ^bhara asmabhyam ||
mahe cana tvāmadrivaḥ parā śulkāya deyām | na sahasrāya nāyutāya vajrivo na śatārdha śatamgha || 5 || mahe | cana | tvām | adri'vaḥ | parā | śulkāya | deyām | na | sahasrāya | na | ayutāya | vajrivaḥ | na | śatārdha | śata'magha || 5 ||
1. ta4-vṛṣabhamiva |
